  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨模态医学图像翻译方法受两个耦合限制：仅处理2D切片或3D patch而非整卷，以及每个翻译任务需单独训练模型。根本原因在于缺乏强大的体积先验，导致生成模型需同时学习解剖外观和跨模态映射，这在配对数据规模下是病态问题。

Method: 1) 使用大规模预训练3D变分自编码器（VAE）提供紧凑的体积外观潜在表示；2) 将翻译任务简化为潜在空间中的条件流匹配问题；3) 整卷压缩处理使计算可行；4) 分辨率感知采样策略保持原生解剖尺度；5) 在三个多中心数据集上联合训练单一模型覆盖跨模态和模态内多个翻译任务。

Result: 所有任务中整卷处理优于patch方法；多任务模型匹配任务特定基线但用1个网络替代N个网络；联合训练实现零样本泛化到训练中未见解剖区域（SSIM差距在0.15以内）；支持组合式跨数据集翻译，沿从未直接监督的路径进行翻译。

Conclusion: 结合强体积先验与多任务训练是实现可超越训练分布泛化的医学图像合成系统的可扩展路径，有望减轻多模态采集负担。

Abstract: Cross-modality medical image translation can reduce the burden of multi-modal acquisitions, yet the field remains constrained by two coupled limitations: methods operate on 2D slices or 3D patches rather than whole volumes, and train a separate model for each translation task. Both stem from a single cause, the absence of a sufficiently strong volumetric prior, which forces generative models to learn anatomical appearance and cross-modality mapping simultaneously, an ill-posed problem at the scale of available paired datasets. We propose to decouple these objectives. A large-scale pretrained 3D variational autoencoder provides a compact latent representation of volumetric appearance, reducing translation to a conditional flow-matching problem. This compression makes whole-volume processing tractable, while a resolution-aware sampling strategy preserves native anatomical scale. We train a single model jointly across inter-modality (MRI$\to$CT, CBCT$\to$CT) and intra-modality (MRI$\to$MRI) tasks over three multi-center datasets. Across all tasks, whole-volume processing outperforms its patch-based counterpart, and the multi-task model matches task-specific baselines while replacing $N$ networks with one. Crucially, joint training unlocks capabilities inaccessible to task-specific approaches: zero-shot generalization to anatomical regions unseen during training, within 0.15 SSIM of the fully supervised model, and compositional cross-dataset translation along paths never directly supervised. These results suggest that combining a strong volumetric prior with multitask training is a scalable route toward synthesis systems that generalize beyond their training distribution. Code is available at https://github.com/arco-group/Whole-Volume-Latent-FM.

</details>


### [77] [EFFEKT: Efficient Federated Knowledge Transfer to Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08138)
*Matteo Caligiuri,Francesco Barbato,Pietro Zanuttigh,Francesco Restuccia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EFFEKT是一种多域联邦学习框架，通过轻量级客户端代理模型与服务器端基础模型（FM）协作，利用双向交叉蒸馏策略训练领域特定的LoRA适配器，在保护数据隐私的同时实现高效的服务器端训练，并在低功耗边缘设备上验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据保护法规推动了联邦学习（FL）的采用，但不断增大的模型规模对客户端设备造成了巨大的计算负担，限制了FL在资源受限场景下的应用。

Method: 提出EFFEKT框架：客户端使用轻量级代理模型，服务器端使用基础模型（FM）进行训练；通过新颖的双向交叉蒸馏策略保持FM与代理提取器之间的特征空间对齐；在服务器端高效训练领域特定的LoRA适配器。

Result: 在多个真实世界数据集和低功耗边缘设备上的部署实验表明，EFFEKT在大多数测试领域中优于现有最先进基线方法，同时保持了客户端侧的轻量计算开销。

Conclusion: EFFEKT成功解决了FL中客户端计算资源受限的问题，通过代理模型与基础模型的协作以及双向交叉蒸馏，在不共享私有数据的前提下实现了高效的多域联邦学习。

Abstract: Recent data protection laws have accelerated the adoption of Federated Learning (FL) for privacy-preserving decentralized training. Nevertheless, increasing model sizes impose substantial computational demands on client devices, limiting FL applicability in resource-constrained settings. We introduce a novel multi-domain federated learning framework in which lightweight client-side proxy models collaborate with a server-side Foundation Model (FM) to learn new concepts without sharing private data. Our approach, EFFEKT, enables efficient server-side training of domain-specific LoRA adapters while preserving feature-space alignment between the FM and proxy extractors via novel bi-directional cross-distillation strategies. Experiments on multiple real-world datasets and deployments on low-power edge devices demonstrate improvements over state-of-the-art baselines in most considered domains while maintaining lightweight computation at the client side.

</details>


### [78] [Learning Structural Illumination for Unsupervised Low-light Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08153)
*Tianle Du,Peiyuan He,Hainuo Wang,Tianxiu Yu,Xiaojie Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RISE框架，解耦相对光照结构与绝对曝光水平，从可靠亮区域推断光照，并通过双测光曝光参考实现场景自适应增强，在无监督低光图像增强中达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督低光图像增强方法直接从整个低光输入估计光照，未分离空间变化的相对光照结构与绝对曝光水平，也未防止低信噪比区域对估计产生偏差；此外，固定曝光目标缺乏场景自适应性，无法适应多样光照条件。

Method: 1) 提出RISE框架，受光空间传播启发，解耦相对光照结构与绝对曝光；2) 从可靠的亮区域推断相对光照结构，实现可解释且鲁棒的增强；3) 提出Dual-Metering Exposure Reference，从每个输入推导场景自适应曝光参考，调整增强强度以适应个体场景。

Result: 在多个基准数据集和真实世界泛化实验中，RISE在无监督LLIE方法中达到最先进性能，同时生成视觉上自然的结果。

Conclusion: 解耦相对光照结构与绝对曝光、从可靠区域推断光照以及场景自适应曝光调整是提升无监督低光图像增强性能的有效策略。

Abstract: Existing unsupervised low-light image enhancement (LLIE) methods often estimate illumination directly from the entire low-light input, without separating its spatially varying illumination pattern, termed relative illumination structure, from the absolute exposure level or preventing unreliable low signal-to-noise ratio regions from biasing the estimate. Moreover, fixed exposure targets impose a scene-agnostic enhancement criterion, limiting adaptation across diverse lighting conditions. Inspired by the spatial propagation of light, we propose a Relative Illumination Structure Estimation (RISE) framework that decouples relative illumination structure from absolute exposure and infers it from reliable bright regions, enabling interpretable and robust enhancement. For scene-adaptive exposure adjustment, we further propose a Dual-Metering Exposure Reference derived from each input, allowing RISE to adapt the enhancement strength to individual scenes and generalize across diverse lighting conditions. Extensive benchmark and real-world generalization experiments show that RISE achieves state-of-the-art performance among unsupervised LLIE methods while producing visually natural results.

</details>


### [79] [Wiener Representation Filtering for VLM Hallucination Suppression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08167)
*Ameen Ali,Tamim Zoabi,Lidor Brami,Lior Wolf*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需训练的、事后表示编辑技术，通过离线校准估计协方差结构，以闭式最优增益的维纳滤波形式，在语言骨干的表示空间中衰减与幻觉相关的成分，从而降低视觉语言模型中的对象幻觉，且推理时速度不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在开放字幕和视觉问答中常描述图像中不存在的对象、属性或关系（即对象幻觉）。现有方法通常需要训练或微调，成本高昂；作者希望提供一种轻量、无需梯度更新的解决方案。

Method: 将隐藏状态建模为"真实成分"与"幻觉关联成分"的叠加，在小型配对数据集上进行一次性离线校准（仅前向传播与经验二阶统计量），估计所需协方差结构；推导出闭式最优增益的维纳型估计器；通过特征分解得到逐模式衰减，并满足稳定性准则（滤波对估计噪声连续响应）；修正被吸收进模型现有权重，仅施加于选定较深层的FFN输出投影，推理时模型不变、速度不变。

Result: 在LLaVA-1.5、MiniGPT-4、Gemma3和mPLUG-Owl2上，于CHAIR、POPE、MME基准上一致降低对象幻觉，同时保持字幕流畅度和整体响应质量。在TempCompass视频理解基准和离散扩散语言模型（grounded dialogue）上验证了泛化性，表明表示过滤在时序视频推理和多步序列级去噪场景中同样有效。

Conclusion: 该训练无关、事后编辑的表示过滤方法能有效且普适地降低各类VLM及其下游任务中的幻觉，推理开销为零，且不损害生成质量。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) excel at open-ended captioning and visual QA but often describe objects, attributes, or relations absent from the image, a phenomenon known as object hallucination. We propose a {training-free, post-hoc representation editing technique} that operates in the representation space of the language backbone. The method performs a lightweight, one-time offline calibration on a modest paired dataset to estimate the required covariance structures, using only forward passes and empirical second-order statistics with no gradient updates or fine-tuning, after which the correction is absorbed directly into the model's existing weights. By modeling hidden states as a superposition of truthful and hallucination-associated components, we derive a Wiener-type estimator whose optimal gains are given in closed form from the covariances of paired truthful and hallucinated representations. An eigendecomposition yields mode-wise attenuation that respects a stability criterion, i.e., the filter responds continuously to estimation noise. The correction is applied once to the feed-forward output projections of selected deeper layers, at inference time, the model runs unchanged and at the same speed. Experiments on LLaVA-1.5, MiniGPT-4, Gemma3, and mPLUG-Owl2 demonstrate consistent reductions in object hallucination on CHAIR, POPE, and MME while maintaining caption fluency and overall response quality. We further demonstrate the generality of our approach on the TempCompass video understanding benchmark and on discrete diffusion language models for grounded dialogue, showing that representation filtering reduces hallucinations even in temporal video reasoning and multi-step, sequence-wide denoising settings.

</details>


### [80] [BAP-MOS: Bandit-Based Adaptive Prompting for Boundary-Sensitive Multi-Organ Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08191)
*Satvik Praveen,Shengji Jin,Ahmed Lamidi,Xin Qian,Yi Sheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BAP-MOS是一个闭环自适应提示框架，用于多器官超声分割。它通过将提示选择建模为器官特定的多臂老虎机问题（外层TPE循环选择提示参数向量，内层UCB-Tuned循环在微调期间自适应每个器官的提示偏好），并引入器官缩放负提示环来适应跨解剖尺度的稀疏提示几何形状。在冻结图像/提示编码器、仅更新掩码解码器的条件下，在前列腺TRUS数据集上达到Dice 0.982、HD95 0.482、MSD 0.204，相较最强传统基线降低了约48%的HD95和45%的MSD，并在外部PFUS1骨盆底超声语料上验证了泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多器官超声分割中，解剖上相邻的结构需要联合描绘，即使Dice分数很高，局部边界错误仍然可能持续存在。传统方法难以有效解决这类边界敏感问题。

Method: 提出BAP-MOS框架：1) 将提示选择形式化为器官特定的多臂老虎机问题，涵盖box、point和组合提示；2) 外层Tree-structured Parzen Estimator (TPE)循环选择提示选择参数向量；3) 内层UCB-Tuned循环使用有界的Dice-MSD-HD95验证探针奖励来适应微调期间每个器官的提示偏好；4) 引入器官缩放负提示环以适应不同解剖尺度下的稀疏提示几何形状；5) 保持图像和提示编码器冻结，仅更新掩码解码器。

Result: 在联合前列腺TRUS队列上，BAP-MOS达到Dice 0.982、HD95 0.482、MSD 0.204，相较最强传统基线分别降低约48%的HD95和45%的MSD。在外部PFUS1骨盆底超声语料上使用MedSAM及其自适应策略变体验证了泛化能力，结果良好。

Conclusion: 自适应提示分配是一种有效机制，能够在不修改基础模型主干的情况下改善边界敏感的多器官超声分割性能。

Abstract: Multi-organ ultrasound segmentation remains challenging when anatomically adjacent structures must be delineated jointly, as localized boundary errors can persist even when Dice scores are high. To address these challenges, we propose Boundary-Adaptive Prompting for Multi-Organ Segmentation (BAP-MOS), a closed-loop adaptive prompting framework. BAP-MOS formulates prompt selection as an organ-specific multi-armed bandit problem over box, point, and combined prompts. An outer Tree-structured Parzen Estimator (TPE) loop selects the prompt-selection parameter vector, while an inner UCB-Tuned loop adapts per-organ prompt preferences during fine-tuning using a bounded Dice--MSD--HD95 validation-probe reward. The framework further introduces an organ-scaled negative prompt ring to adapt sparse prompt geometry across anatomical scales, while keeping the image and prompt encoders frozen and updating only the mask decoder. We evaluate BAP-MOS on pooled prostate-region TRUS cohorts against U-Net, nnU-Net, MedSAM, fixed-prompt SAM/MedSAM, and adaptive policy variants. On this benchmark, BAP-MOS achieves Dice 0.982, HD95 0.482, and MSD 0.204, reducing HD95 by approximately 48% and MSD by 45% relative to the strongest conventional baseline. To verify the generalization ability of the framework, we tested it on the external PFUS1 pelvic-floor ultrasound corpus using MedSAM and its adaptive strategy variants, and the results were good. These results support adaptive prompt allocation as an effective mechanism for improving boundary-sensitive multi-organ ultrasound segmentation without modifying the foundation-model backbone. Source Code is available at: https://github.com/SatvikPraveen/BAP-MOS

</details>


### [81] [VTO: Visual Tool Orchestration for Video Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08219)
*Rui Wang,Yeteng Wu,Xianling Zhang,Mengshi Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VTO，一个过程监督强化学习框架，用于视频异常检测中的多模态智能体工具编排，通过基础模型驱动的认知评估器提供细粒度逐步监督，结合层次化视觉工具集VAD-Tool，在工具调度上取得了高达10.2%的绝对准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频异常检测（VAD）面临现实场景复杂多样的挑战，传统深度学习方法泛化能力差。多模态智能体虽提供了工具学习范式，但现有系统依赖监督微调难以处理复杂编排，而标准强化学习因粗粒度结果奖励导致过早终止问题。

Method: 提出VTO（Process-Supervised Reinforcement Learning框架），包含三个核心组件：(1) 基础模型驱动的认知评估器，提供上下文感知的语义反馈；(2) 过程监督认知对齐（Process-Supervised Cognitive Alignment），提供细粒度逐步监督；(3) 显式惩罚逻辑截断并奖励完整因果链，优化多步推理策略。同时构建了VAD-Tool，包含12个专业化视觉工具的层次化工具集及对应基准。

Result: 在VAD-Tool基准上的大量实验表明，VTO显著优于基线方法，在工具调度上实现了高达10.2%的绝对准确率提升。

Conclusion: VTO通过过程监督的强化学习框架有效解决了多模态智能体在VAD任务中工具编排复杂和粗粒度奖励导致的过早终止问题，代码和数据已开源。

Abstract: Video anomaly detection (VAD) is a critical yet challenging task due to the complex and diverse nature of real-world scenarios. Traditional deep learning approaches are fundamentally limited by poor generalization across diverse scenarios. While multimodal agents offer a promising tool-learning paradigm for VAD, current systems relying on supervised fine-tuning struggle with complex orchestration, and standard reinforcement learning often causes premature termination due to coarse-grained outcome rewards. To address these challenges, we propose VTO, a process-supervised reinforcement learning framework. Moving beyond static tool usage, VTO enables the agent to dynamically explore and interact with the environment. Specifically, we introduce a foundation model-driven cognitive evaluator to provide context-aware semantic feedback, which is seamlessly integrated into a Process-Supervised Cognitive Alignment that delivers fine-grained, step-wise supervision. By explicitly penalizing logical truncation and rewarding complete causal chains, the agent optimizes its multi-step reasoning policy for interrelated tool orchestration. To support our proposed framework, we meticulously crafted VAD-Tool, a hierarchical visual tool set comprising 12 specialized vision tools spanning from entity tracking to high-stakes hazard detection, and established the corresponding benchmark for rigorous multi-step reasoning evaluation. Extensive experiments on VAD-Tool demonstrate that VTO significantly outperforms baselines, achieving up to a 10.2\% absolute accuracy improvement in tool scheduling. Code and data are available at https://github.com/MICLAB-BUPT/VTO.

</details>


### [82] [Ego-OSCAR: Egocentric Open source Stereo CAptuRe System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08285)
*Gunjan Paul,Senthil Palanisamy,Satpal Singh Rathore,Pratyush Kumar Patnaik,Shubhanshu Khatana,Abhishek Anand*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Ego-OSCAR 是一个开源硬件、低成本的头部安装双目惯性采集设备，用于野外自我中心数据收集。每台成本低于200美元，包含同步全局快门双目相机、6轴IMU、嵌入式Linux单板计算机和实时微控制器。同时发布了完整软件栈和约550小时的同步双目视频数据，配有动作标注和3D手部重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究级自我中心数据采集系统（如Project Aria）成本高昂，难以大规模部署。作者旨在降低自我中心数据采集的门槛，使任何团队都能以低成本进行大规模众包式数据收集。

Method: 设计并实现了一款开源硬件设备，结合硬件同步全局快门双目相机与6轴IMU，使用嵌入式Linux SBC进行设备端视频编码，配备实时微控制器用于用户反馈和看门狗功能。全部使用商业可用组件和3D打印部件，成本低于200美元。同时发布了完整软件栈和带注释的数据集。

Result: 成功构建了低成本采集设备，收集了约550小时（每相机）的自我中心双目视频，配有同步IMU数据，由分布式贡献者网络在多种室内环境中采集。数据集包含开放词汇的动作字幕标注和逐帧3D手部重建，以及逐会话立体标定。

Conclusion: Ego-OSCAR 不追求与研究级系统匹配单台设备的保真度，而是旨在成为规模众包自我中心数据采集的最经济可行方案，降低大规模数据收集的启动成本。所有硬件设计、软件和数据集均开源。

Abstract: We present Ego-OSCAR, an open-hardware, low-cost, head-mounted stereo-inertial capture device for egocentric data collection in the wild. EgoOSCAR pairs a hardware-synchronized global-shutter stereo camera with a 6- axis IMU, an embedded Linux SBC for on-device video encoding, and a realtime microcontroller for user feedback and watchdog functions. The complete bill of materials is under USD 200 per unit, using only commercially available components and 3D-printed parts. Alongside the device, we release a complete software stack (hardware-accelerated recording pipeline, IMU sampling daemon, time-synchronization tooling, and watchdog firmware) and roughly 550 hours of egocentric stereo video per camera with synchronized IMU, collected by a distributed contributor network across everyday indoor environments. The release is annotated rather than raw: free-form action captions cover essentially the entire recorded timeline with an open vocabulary, and per-frame 3D hand reconstructions ship alongside per-session stereo calibration. Ego-OSCAR does not aim to match the per-unit fidelity of research-grade systems such as Project Aria; it aims to be the cheapest defensible substrate for crowdsourced egocentric capture, and to lower the activation energy for any team that wants to collect egocentric data at scale. All hardware designs, software, and the dataset are open-sourced

</details>


### [83] [What Irregularity Costs: CUDA C++, Rust, and Triton on a Hash-Blocked GPU Workload](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08287)
*Petr Korolev*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过在GPU上实现相同的hash-blocked TSDF融合内核，对比了CUDA C++、Rust（cuda-oxide）和Triton三种语言在规则与不规则工作负载下的性能。规则阶段三者差距不大，但irregular阶段Triton比手写CUDA慢一个数量级以上，Rust则与CUDA接近。作者将性能差距归因于语言无法表达特定操作（而非比率差异），并发现Triton的有界探测循环还会导致正确的fusion问题（静默丢失重建表面）。同时报告并修复了cuda-oxide中scoped atomic load/store无法调用的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GPU语言对比几乎都基于tiled dense linear algebra，这类工作负载下所有工具链都表现良好，差异很小。作者希望在相反特征的工作负载上进行比较：开放寻址哈希表、compare-exchange插入、数据相关的每lane探测深度以及contended scatter，以揭示语言在这些不规则负载上的真实表达能力差异。

Method: 在CUDA C++、Rust（通过NVIDIA的cuda-oxide）和Triton中实现相同的hash-blocked TSDF融合内核，分为规则阶段（遍历截断带并累加）和不规则阶段（探测并插入哈希表）。使用硬件计数器分析指令数、compare-exchange数量、寄存器占用和L1缓存驻留等指标。

Result: 规则阶段三种语言性能接近；不规则阶段Rust接近手写CUDA C++，而Triton慢一个数量级以上。Rust虽指令数更少、compare-exchange更少、寄存器占用相同却更慢，硬件计数器定位为L1驻留问题——GPU-scope atomic load因NVIDIA L1不跨SM一致而绕过缓存。Triton的代价源于探测循环必须运行到编译期上界以及tl.atomic_cas不支持mask导致需要CUDA中没有的scratch结构。此外Triton的有界探测在常规负载因子下静默丢弃块，导致重建丢失表面。还发现并修复了cuda-oxide中scoped atomic load/store在真实kernel构建模式下无法调用的缺陷。

Conclusion: 语言选择在通常被基准测试的规则工作负载上几乎免费，但在不规则工作负载上代价高昂。性能差距源于具体的表达能力限制（而非简单ratio），且Triton的有界探测不仅是性能问题，还是正确性缺陷。这提示GPU语言设计需要在表达能力（尤其是不规则控制流和原子操作）上投入更多关注。

Abstract: GPU language comparisons are almost always run on tiled dense linear algebra, where every toolchain is good and the differences are small. We implement the same hash-blocked TSDF fusion kernel in CUDA C++, in Rust through NVIDIA's cuda-oxide, and in Triton, and measure it on a workload with the opposite character: an open-addressed hash table with compare-exchange insertion, data-dependent per-lane probe depth, and contended scatter.
  The result is a split. On the regular stage, which walks a truncation band and accumulates, all three languages land within a small factor of each other. On the irregular stage, which probes and inserts, Rust stays close to hand-written CUDA C++ while Triton is more than an order of magnitude slower. Language choice is nearly free on the work that is usually benchmarked and expensive on the work that is not.
  We attribute both gaps to specific things the languages cannot express, not to ratios. Triton's cost follows from a probe loop that must run to a compile-time bound and from tl.atomic_cas taking no mask, which forces a scratch structure with no counterpart in CUDA. Rust's cost was invisible in every instruction count: its kernel issues fewer instructions, fewer compare-exchanges and fewer registers at identical occupancy, yet was slower. Hardware counters located it in L1 residency. A GPU-scope atomic load must be coherent across SMs, no NVIDIA L1 is, so the type-correct way to read a shared location bypasses the cache on every access.
  Triton's bounded probe is also a correctness problem for fusion: at load factors an ordinary depth trajectory reaches, it silently discards blocks and the reconstruction loses patches of surface with nothing reported. We also report a defect found and fixed in cuda-oxide itself, now merged upstream: its scoped atomic load and store could not be called at all in the build mode that produces real kernels.

</details>


### [84] [Test-Time Prototype Adaptation for Open-Vocabulary Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08290)
*Haozhe Wang,Jintao Cheng,Weibin Li,Xiaoyu Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TPA（Test-time Prototype Adaptation），一种训练无关的即插即用模块，在输出层面通过transductive adaptation从无标注部署域图像中构建基于DINO特征的类别原型，与宿主模型logits线性融合，无需修改宿主前向传播和权重即可提升开放词汇语义分割精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OVSS方法要么重新设计CLIP内部注意力机制，要么注入辅助视觉基础模型的特征，都需要访问宿主模型内部计算并针对其特定前向传播进行定制，缺乏通用性和即插即用性。

Method: TPA包含三个阶段：1) 宿主模型在少量无标注部署域图像上推理，识别置信的anchor patches；2) 提取这些patch的冻结DINO特征，聚合成每类原型存入银行；3) 推理时通过余弦相似度查找获得辅助分数，与宿主logits线性融合。整个过程中宿主前向传播和权重均不修改。

Result: TPA在5种代表性OVSS宿主（涵盖attention-redesign和VFM-injection两类设计）、3种CLIP骨干、8个基准、多种内部VFM选择上，使用单一超参数且无需逐宿主调参或参数更新，一致提升分割精度；在多数基准上仅需约10%的无标注部署域图像即可有效构建原型银行。

Conclusion: TPA证明了在输出层面利用transductive adaptation和冻结DINO特征即可有效增强OVSS，无需访问宿主内部结构，是一种通用、训练无关、零参数更新的性能提升方案，为OVSS提供了一个轻量级且可广泛组合的增强范式。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary semantic segmentation (OVSS) repurposes a pretrained CLIP encoder for dense prediction without additional labeled supervision. Existing methods improve CLIP's spatial behavior either by redesigning its internal attention or by injecting features from auxiliary vision foundation models; both require access to the host's internal computation and are tailored to its specific forward pass. In this work, we propose Test-time Prototype Adaptation (TPA), a training-free plug-in that operates at the output level, leaving the host's forward pass and weights unmodified. By leveraging a lightweight transductive adaptation phase, TPA identifies confident anchor patches from the host's own output predictions on a small pool of unlabeled deployment-domain images, and aggregates their frozen DINO features into per-class prototypes; at inference, a single cosine similarity lookup against this frozen bank provides an auxiliary score fused linearly with the host's logits. TPA composes with five representative OVSS hosts spanning attention-redesign and VFM-injection designs, across three CLIP backbones, eight benchmarks, and multiple internal VFM choices. Under a single set of hyper-parameters and without per-host tuning or parameter updates, TPA consistently improves segmentation accuracy, with as few as approximately 10% of unlabeled deployment-domain images sufficing for effective bank construction on most benchmarks.

</details>


### [85] [Frequency-Domain Dual-Branch Fusion for Medical Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08307)
*Yusra Tariq,Rakesh Chandra Joshi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种双分支频域融合模块用于医学视觉问答（VQA），通过在频域中根据问题条件自适应滤波，融合低频结构与高频细节，结合冻结的BiomedCLIP编码器和BioBART解码器，在VQA-RAD和SLAKE基准上提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学VQA的多模态融合方法主要在空间域操作，未能充分利用视觉和文本表示中互补的频率信息（如病灶纹理、边界锐度、弥散密度变化等细微视觉证据），限制了融合效果。

Method: 1) 提出双分支频域融合模块，以输入问题为条件进行频谱滤波，自适应选择全局低频结构与细粒度高频细节；2) 从冻结的BiomedCLIP编码器的早期纹理敏感层和最终语义层提取互补特征，提供更丰富的频谱；3) 使用对称InfoNCE目标将两个分支特征与问题表示对齐；4) 采用分阶段联合训练，配合BioBART解码器。

Result: 在PMC-VQA上预训练，在VQA-RAD和SLAKE基准上微调，频域感知的多模态融合提升了医学VQA性能，同时保持轻量高效的架构。

Conclusion: 频域融合是医学VQA中有效且高效的多模态对齐策略，能够充分利用频率信息提升细粒度医学视觉推理能力。

Abstract: Medical Visual Question Answering (VQA) requires aligning subtle visual evidence, including lesion texture, boundary sharpness, and diffuse density changes, with clinical language. Existing multimodal fusion approaches operating in the spatial domain may not fully exploit complementary frequency information present in visual and textual representations. We introduce a dual-branch frequency-domain fusion module that conditions spectral filtering on the input question, enabling adaptive selection of global low-frequency structure and fine-grained high-frequency detail before reconstructing the spatial representation for answer generation. To provide a richer spectrum for filtering, we extract complementary features from early texture-sensitive and final semantic layers of a frozen BiomedCLIP encoder and align both with the question representation using a symmetric InfoNCE objective prior to staged joint training with a BioBART decoder. We pretrain the proposed model on PMC-VQA and fine-tune it on the VQA-RAD and SLAKE benchmarks, demonstrating that frequency-aware multimodal fusion improves medical VQA performance while maintaining a lightweight and efficient architecture.

</details>


### [86] [Open-World Semantic Segmentation with Sensitivity Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08308)
*Anastasios Romanos Varvarigos,Nikos Giakoumoglou,Tania Stathaki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于开放世界语义分割的三解码器统一编码器-解码器架构，在闭集分割基础上增加灵敏度解码器，检测语义不确定性以识别和分组未知/异常内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统语义分割模型基于闭集假设，遇到未见或异常内容时会产生过度自信的错误分类，无法适应开放世界场景。

Method: 在双解码器基线上增加第三个互补解码器：第一解码器用高斯原型进行闭集分割；第二解码器通过对比特征学习在嵌入空间中隔离未知区域；第三解码器（核心贡献）捕获细粒度纹理不规则性和激活不稳定性以指示语义不确定性。三个解码器分别提供logit空间的类级OOD距离、嵌入空间的全局特征能量、以及跨编码器尺度的局部激活不稳定性，形成互补信号。

Result: 在Cityscapes和BDD-Anomaly上的实验表明，该方法在异常分割和新类别发现上表现更好，同时保持有竞争力的闭集精度，在BDD-Anomaly上相比基线AUROC提升+2.4%，FPR@95TPR降低2.5个百分点。

Conclusion: 语义原型和对比范数无法可靠检测的细粒度纹理/激活不稳定性信号，可作为第三种互补信号提升开放世界语义分割中的异常检测与新类别发现性能。

Abstract: Modern vision systems must operate in "open-world" settings, where models must recognize known categories and detect unseen or anomalous content. Conventional semantic segmentation models operate under a "closed-world" assumption, often producing overconfident misclassifications on novel content. We address open-world semantic segmentation, the joint task of segmenting known classes while detecting and grouping novel or anomalous content without additional supervision, by extending a dual-decoder baseline with a third, complementary decoder within a unified encoder-decoder design. The first decoder performs closed-set segmentation using Gaussian prototypes for known categories. The second uses contrastive feature learning to isolate unknown regions in embedding space. The third, our key contribution, is a sensitivity decoder that captures fine-grained texture irregularities and activation instabilities indicative of semantic uncertainty, which neither semantic prototypes nor contrastive norms can reliably detect. The three decoders provide genuinely complementary signals: class-level OOD distance in logit space, global feature energy in embedding space, and local activation instability across encoder scales. Experiments on Cityscapes and BDD-Anomaly show that our method improves anomaly segmentation and novel-class discovery while maintaining competitive closed-set accuracy, with gains of +2.4% AUROC and a 2.5 pp. reduction in FPR@95TPR on BDD-Anomaly over the baseline.

</details>


### [87] [Three Necessary Principles for Self-Supervised Visual Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08309)
*Nikos Giakoumoglou,Paschalis Giakoumoglou,Tania Stathaki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文主张无标签视觉表征学习需要三个互补的训练信号：跨视图语义不变性（观察）、块级空间预测（预测）和表征非退化（正则化）。作者证明仅结合观察与预测而不加正则化会收敛到常数编码器，且动量编码器在收敛时无法保证防坍缩。所有主流自监督方法都是一个统一能量分解的特例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督视觉表征学习中，各种方法（对比学习、掩码图像建模等）在设计目标上分歧巨大，缺乏统一的理论框架来解释为何某些目标组合有效而其他无效、坍缩现象何时发生以及各组件的确切作用。

Method: 将自监督学习目标形式化为三个原则：观察（跨视图语义不变性）、预测（块级空间预测）和正则化（表征非退化）。通过理论证明分析目标间的关系：观察+预测在无正则化时以常数编码器为全局极小值；两个目标在编码器输出处梯度互补且结构不冲突；动量编码器与在线编码器收敛到相同不动点且不提供坍缩保证。对比对齐仅提供自限性的坍缩抵抗。此外构建统一能量分解框架，将所有主流方法纳入其中，并通过对照实验验证每个理论主张。

Result: 理论证明部分：(i)观察+预测无正则化时，常数编码器是全局极小值；(ii)两个目标梯度互补且不冲突；(iii)动量编码器不提供收敛时的坍缩保证。对比对齐的坍缩抵抗是自限性的，通过梯度衰减论证形式化。删去预测会丧失空间训练信号，删去观察会丧失跨视图语义不变性，任何两两组合都无法替代第三个目标。所有主流自监督方法均是该统一能量分解的特例。实验部分包括块检索评估以验证预测的空间后果。

Conclusion: 无标签视觉表征学习需要三个不可替代的互补目标共同作用：观察、预测和正则化。这项工作的统一能量分解框架揭示了所有主流自监督方法的共性结构，并提供了关于何时发生坍缩、动量编码器的作用局限以及对比对齐的自限性抵抗的理论洞见，为该领域提供了统一的理论基础。

Abstract: We argue that learning visual representations without labels requires a training signal jointly complete across three non-overlapping objectives: semantic invariance across augmented views, patch-level spatial prediction, and representational non-degeneracy. We formalize these as the observation, prediction, and regularization principles and prove (i) that combining observation and prediction without regularization admits the constant encoder as a global minimizer under negative-free alignment; (ii) that the two objectives are gradient-complementary and structurally non-conflicting at the encoder output; and (iii) that the momentum encoder converges to the same fixed point as the online encoder and provides no collapse guarantee at convergence. Contrastive alignment provides only self-limiting collapse resistance, formalized via an explicit gradient-decay argument. Dropping prediction withholds the spatial training signal by construction; dropping observation forfeits cross-view semantic invariance by construction; at the scale we study, no pair substitutes for the third. Every major self-supervised method is a special case of a single unified energy decomposition. We pair every theoretical claim with a controlled experiment, including a patch-retrieval evaluation for the spatial consequence of prediction.

</details>


### [88] [Your VLM Already Knows When: Training-Free Temporal Grounding by Asking Yes or No](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08315)
*Ji Huang,Barry Devereux,Hui Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 多模态大语言模型在识别事件上表现可靠，但在预测时间戳上严重失败。作者发现这并非感知问题而是任务接口问题：将时间戳回归改为粗到细的二进制问题扫描，仅使用首词概率排序，无需训练即可将R@0.5提升28-50个点。提出的FV-Action方法在Charades-STA上达到56.8% R@0.5，超越原生grounding pipeline和最强训练-free方法，并在多个基准上优于直接预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强视觉语言模型（VLMs）虽然能够可靠地识别事件，但在回答时间戳问题时表现极差（Charades-STA上仅3.8% R@0.5），甚至在错误预测中77-80%具有低输出熵（即模型自信地犯错），传统的基于熵的错误检测方法完全失效。作者旨在揭示这一失败的根源并设计解决方案。

Method: 将时间戳回归任务重构为粗到细的二进制问题扫描（coarse-to-fine scan of binary questions），仅利用模型的首词预测概率作为排序依据，不进行任何训练。作者进一步将残余失败分解为两个可测量的维度：感知轴（随骨干网络变化）和几何轴（可从输出窗口与事件宽度之比解析预测）。基于此分析提出FV-Action（training-free方法）。

Result: FV-Action在Charades-STA上达到56.8% R@0.5，超越同一骨干网络的本地化grounding管线及该基准上最强的训练-free结果；在TACoS上优于所有零样本评估的TVG训练模型；在ActivityNet Captions和QVHighlights上相较于直接预测均有改进，且全程无需任何时间监督。

Conclusion: 时间定位失败源于任务接口而非感知能力，通过适当的推理协议（粗到细的二进制扫描+排序）可以大幅释放冻结VLMs的时序定位潜力，为无需训练的视频时序定位提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Multimodal LLMs that recognise events reliably still fail to say when they happen. Prompted for timestamps, strong VLMs reach as little as $3.8\%$ R@0.5 on Charades-STA, and $77$ to $80\%$ of their wrong predictions carry low output entropy: the models are confidently wrong, and entropy-based error detection stays below a random classifier. We show that this failure lives in the task interface, not in perception. Holding the weights fixed, replacing timestamp regression with a coarse-to-fine scan of binary questions, whose first-token probabilities are consumed only as a ranking, raises R@0.5 by $28$ to $50$ points across four frozen backbones. The residual failures decompose into two measurable axes: a perception axis that moves with the backbone, and a geometry axis that is analytically predictable from the ratio of the output-window and event widths. FV-Action, the training-free method built on this analysis, reaches $56.8\%$ R@0.5 on Charades-STA, above the same backbone's native grounding pipeline and the strongest training-free result on this benchmark; it surpasses every TVG-trained model evaluated zero-shot on TACoS, and improves over direct prediction on ActivityNet Captions and QVHighlights, with no temporal supervision at any stage.

</details>


### [89] [A continually expandable foundation model for brain MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08319)
*Michail Mamalakis,Carmen Jimenez-Mesa,Yonghao Li,Hao Chen,Chao Li,Antonios Mamalakis,John Suckling,Richard Bethlehem,Stephen J. Price,Richard J. Gilbertson,Pietro Lio*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Alcmaeon，一个基于超过425,000个体积和影像图谱预训练的三维脑MRI基础模型，可通过图蓝图剪枝（GBP）跨临床领域顺序扩展，在保持早期能力的同时适应新任务，实现比连续适应和弹性权重巩固更少的遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑MRI模型通常针对单一疾病、人群或成像协议开发，缺乏通用性。现有基础模型虽然能提供更通用的表示，但通常只预训练一次，用新数据更新时会丢失早期能力（灾难性遗忘）。因此需要一种能随新数据增长同时保留旧能力的脑MRI基础模型方法。

Method: Alcmaeon结合三维体积编码和潜在扩散生成，采用图蓝图剪枝（GBP）技术：通过蓝图识别并保护对早期领域重要的网络模块，同时保留其余容量供新领域训练。模型从健康老龄化和神经退行性变扩展到发育、精神及肿瘤影像领域。

Result: 在从健康老龄化和神经退行扩展到发育、精神和肿瘤影像的过程中，GBP在所有体素级重建指标上均优于连续适应和弹性权重巩固方法，在肿瘤影像适应后优势最大。蓝图提供了模型能力保护和重用的可检查记录。不同模型层次的表示分别支持图像合成、疾病分类、生存建模和术后预测，但没有任何单一表示对所有任务最优。

Conclusion: 该研究为脑MRI基础模型提供了一条可持续增长的路径，使模型能够随新兴数据扩展而保持早期能力，展示了蓝图剪枝在缓解灾难性遗忘和提供模型可解释性方面的潜力。

Abstract: Brain magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) is central to neuroscience and clinical assessment, but models are commonly developed for individual diseases, populations or imaging protocols. Foundation models promise more general representations, yet they are usually pretrained once and can lose earlier capabilities when updated with new data. Here we show that Alcmaeon, a three-dimensional brain MRI foundation model pretrained without manual labels on more than 425,000 volumes and derived imaging maps, can be expanded sequentially across clinical domains. Alcmaeon combines volumetric encoding and latent diffusion generation with Graph-Blueprint Pruning (GBP), which protects network modules important to earlier domains while leaving the remaining capacity trainable. Across expansion from healthy ageing and neurodegeneration to developmental, psychiatric and tumour imaging, GBP showed less forgetting than sequential adaptation and elastic weight consolidation across voxel-level reconstruction measures, with its largest advantage after adaptation to tumour imaging. The blueprints provided an inspectable record of how model capacity was protected and reused. Representations from different model levels supported image synthesis, disease classification, survival modelling and postoperative prediction, although no single representation was optimal for every task. These findings provide a route towards brain MRI foundation models that can grow with emerging data while retaining earlier capabilities.

</details>


### [90] [Circuit Fine-Tuning for Compute-Efficient Transformer Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08336)
*Uri Z. Kialy,Gil Ben-Artzi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Circuit Fine-Tuning (CFT)，一种计算高效的微调框架。CFT利用电路发现技术（传统上用于解释训练模型）在训练前选择需要微调的模块，仅微调子网络。与传统的基于训练好的任务头的归因不同，CFT使用接近零初始化的探针头，隔离骨干网络对目标分布的响应。CFT无需学习率预热，平均约20个epoch达到峰值精度（对比PEFT基线需44-96个epoch），减少2.3-6.6倍训练FLOPs和最多16倍墙钟时间，且不增加参数和推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PEFT方法以参数数量作为主要效率指标，但参数稀疏并不等于计算高效——参数稀疏方法仍可能产生全模型训练成本，且通常需要较长的训练周期才能达到最优精度。需要一种真正计算高效的微调框架。

Method: CFT利用电路发现技术，在训练前选择需要微调的模块。关键创新在于：将归因公式化建立在接近零初始化的探针头上（而非传统训练好的任务头），从而隔离骨干网络对目标分布的响应而非特定分类器的偏好。然后仅微调恢复出的子图。

Result: CFT无需学习率预热，平均约20个epoch达到峰值精度（对比强PEFT基线的44-96个epoch）。实现2.3-6.6倍更少的训练FLOPs和最多16倍更少的墙钟时间，同时零参数增加和零推理操作。在VTAB-1k、Swin层次骨干、CBIS-DDSM医学影像和Gemma-3 on CUB-200等基准上验证了有效性。

Conclusion: CFT展示了一种将电路发现（原本用于解释模型的工具）用于训练前模块选择的创新思路，通过近零初始化探针头的归因设计，实现了计算效率与性能的平衡，为PEFT领域提供了新的效率视角（从参数效率转向计算效率）。

Abstract: Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) has become the de facto standard for adapting Vision Transformers (ViTs) to downstream tasks. While parameter count has been the dominant efficiency metric in PEFT, it does not imply \textit{compute efficiency}: parameter-sparse methods can still incur full-model training cost per step, and typically need long schedules to reach peak accuracy. We introduce Circuit Fine-Tuning (CFT), a compute-efficient framework that uses circuit discovery---conventionally used to explain trained models---to select modules for fine-tuning before training. Whereas attribution is conventionally formulated against a trained task head, we formulate it against a near-zero-initialized probe head, which isolates the response of the backbone to the target distribution rather than the preferences of a particular classifier. CFT then fine-tunes only the recovered subgraph. CFT needs no learning-rate warmup and reaches peak accuracy in ${\sim}20$ epochs on average---versus $44$--$96$ for strong PEFT baselines---yielding $2.3$--$6.6\times$ fewer training FLOPs and up to $16\times$ less wall-clock time, while adding zero parameters and no inference operations. Experiments across a standard visual transfer benchmark (VTAB-1k), hierarchical backbones (Swin), domain-shifted medical imaging (CBIS-DDSM), and a vision-language model (Gemma-3 on CUB-200) demonstrate the effectiveness of CFT. Code is available at https://github.com/UriKialy/CFT

</details>


### [91] [Tropical Cyclone Forecasting via Latent Rectified Flow using Satellite Imagery and Atmospheric Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08354)
*Meheru Zannat,Sk. Md. Masudul Ahsan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个单程深度生成模型，联合预测热带气旋的卫星红外图像和四个ERA5大气场（U风、V风、气温、地表气压），最长预报9小时。模型采用五通道变分自编码器压缩至潜空间，配合条件整流流UNet和因子化时域注意力机制，并通过可微分的引导流轨道误差进行奖励微调（DRaFT）。在2022年留出风暴上达到16.35 dB PSNR和0.759 SSIM，采样速度比级联扩散基线快约30倍，+9小时轨道误差62.4 km，比基线低15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气候变化使热带气旋更具破坏性，需要高效预报。数值天气预报计算成本高；现有深度生成模型要么只生成卫星图像、要么只生成大气场，无法兼得；需要大量采样步数，对普通硬件不友好；风暴轨道来自回归头，与生成的大气没有物理联系。

Method: 1) 五通道变分自编码器将5×256×256帧压缩为4×64×64潜变量；2) 条件整流流UNet配合因子化时域注意力模块，从三个过去帧预测未来三帧，输入包含最佳轨迹坐标和时间戳；3) 使用DRaFT（奖励微调）方法，通过可微分的引导流计算从预测风场推导轨道误差作为奖励信号进行微调。

Result: 在2022年留出风暴上：PSNR 16.35 dB，SSIM 0.759，优于复现的级联扩散基线的所有预报时效（+9小时时+0.84 dB）；采样快约30倍（56 ms vs 1673 ms）；+9小时轨道误差62.4 km，比基线低15%；奖励微调研究进一步将轨道误差降低8-11%。

Conclusion: 单程生成模型能够在普通硬件上同时高效预报卫星图像和大气场，且通过将轨道误差物理地耦合到生成的大气场中，实现了比级联扩散基线更优的预报精度和更快的采样速度。

Abstract: Tropical cyclones are growing more destructive in a changing climate, and efficient forecasting of their structure and track has become a necessity. Deep generative models promise an alternative to computationally expensive numerical weather prediction (NWP), yet current systems produce either satellite imagery or atmospheric fields, never both; they need many sampling steps, putting them out of reach of modest hardware; and their storm tracks come from regression heads with no physical link to the generated atmosphere. This work presents a single-pass model that jointly forecasts GRIDSAT-B1 infrared imagery and four ERA5 atmospheric fields (U-wind, V-wind, air temperature, and surface pressure) out to nine hours. A five-channel variational autoencoder compresses each 5 x 256 x 256 frame to a 4 x 64 x 64 latent, and a conditional rectified-flow UNet with a factorized temporal-attention module predicts the next three frames from three past frames, their best-track coordinates, and timestamps. The model is then reward-fine-tuned (DRaFT) against a differentiable track error derived from the predicted winds through a steering-flow calculation. On held-out 2022 storms the model reaches 16.35 dB PSNR and 0.759 SSIM, ahead of a reproduced cascaded-diffusion baseline at every lead time (+0.84 dB at +9 h) while sampling ~30x faster (56 ms vs. 1673 ms). Track error at +9 h is 62.4 km, 15% below the baseline, and a reward fine-tuning study demonstrates a further 8-11% track-error reduction across sampler budgets.

</details>


### [92] [VOICE: A Vision-Omics Foundation Model Integrating Direct and Retrieval-Based Prediction of In-situ Single-Cell Gene Expression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08366)
*Xin Luo,Yicheng Tao,Haoxuan Zeng,Suyuan Wang,Chenzi Ouyang,Meiqi Zhu,Kai Liu,Shuibing Chen,Jie Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VOICE是一个多模态基础模型，通过对比学习对齐H&E组织学形态与单细胞转录组表达，利用双分支（直接回归+参考细胞检索）结合逐基因权重融合，从H&E图像预测单细胞基因表达，并泛化到未见患者、切片及部分重叠基因面板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间转录组学虽能解析单细胞分辨率基因表达，但成本高、限于靶向数百至数千基因的面板、且仅适用于少量样本。而H&E成像便宜且常规大规模采集，因此直接从形态学预测单细胞表达是向大规模组织档案引入分子分析的一种实用途径。

Method: VOICE首先使用病理基础模型提取细胞中心的H&E形态特征，与转录组基础模型的单细胞表达嵌入通过对比学习在2300万细胞上对齐。随后通过两个分支预测表达：一个分支直接从形态学回归表达，另一个分支从相似参考细胞中检索测量表达以恢复缺乏形态信号的基因。由于基因形态可预测性不同，VOICE以逐基因权重融合两个分支。

Result: VOICE在未见患者、切片以及部分重叠基因面板（Xenium）上均表现良好，并在七项指标上持续优于先前的单细胞表达预测方法。

Conclusion: VOICE证明了基于H&E形态学预测单细胞基因表达的可行性，通过双分支与逐基因权重融合的架构，兼顾形态学可预测基因与需参考检索的基因，为大规模组织档案的分子分析提供了一种低成本、可扩展的方案。

Abstract: Spatial transcriptomics can resolve gene expression at single-cell resolution, but it is costly, limited to targeted panels of a few hundred to a few thousand genes, and applicable to only a small number of samples. H&E imaging, by contrast, is cheap and collected routinely at scale. This makes predicting single-cell expression directly from morphology a practical way to bring molecular analysis to large tissue archives. We therefore present VOICE, a multimodal foundation model that predicts single-cell gene expression from H&E images using paired Xenium data. VOICE first aligns cell centered H&E morphology from a pathology foundation model with single-cell expression embeddings from a transcriptome foundation model, trained using contrastive learning over 23 million cells. Next it predicts expression through two branches. One branch directly regresses expression from morphology. The other branch retrieves measured expression from similar reference cells, recovering genes that do not have morphological signal. Because genes vary in morphological predictability, VOICE fuses the two branches with a per-gene weight. After training, VOICE generalizes to heldout patients, slides, and partially overlapping gene panels from Xenium, and it consistently outperforms prior single-cell expression prediction methods on seven metrics.

</details>


### [93] [PARAGraph: Pathology-Anatomy-Aware Hierarchical Graph for Diabetic Retinopathy Grading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08368)
*Ziyang Zhang,Yuankai Huo,Yalin Zheng,He Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PARAGraph是一个用于糖尿病视网膜病变（DR）分级的三层病理-解剖感知层次图框架，它通过构造视盘-中心凹锚定的坐标系统编码病变类型、面积和空间位置等医学先验，并采用双融合策略（全局视觉上下文+决策级预测分支）来缓解病变分割噪声的影响，在Messidor-2、APTOS和DDR数据集上取得优于现有方法的分级性能，同时展现出临床可解释性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度模型大多将DR分级视为图像级分类任务，未能显式建模临床依据（如病变类型和空间关系），导致模型缺乏临床可解释性，且对病变分割噪声敏感。

Method: 提出PARAGraph框架：(1) 将每张图像建模为三层层次图，包含病变级节点、中间类别/区域节点、全局解剖和语义节点；(2) 构建视盘-中心凹锚定的坐标框架，提供尺度与旋转归一化的视网膜参考系统，用于编码病变节点的类别、归一化面积和解剖坐标；(3) 采用双融合策略，将全局视觉上下文引入图语义节点和决策级预测分支，增强对不可靠病变证据的鲁棒性。

Result: 在Messidor-2、APTOS和DDR三个数据集上，PARAGraph取得了优于当前最先进方法的一致DR分级性能；可解释性分析表明预测结果与病变证据密切关联，鲁棒性分析显示其对病变分割噪声具有良好的抵抗能力。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模病理-解剖层次结构和医学先验，PARAGraph不仅提升了DR分级精度，还增强了模型的临床可解释性与对噪声的鲁棒性，为可解释的医学图像分析提供了一个有价值的框架。

Abstract: Diabetic retinopathy (DR) remains a leading cause of vision loss among working-age adults worldwide, making reliable severity grading clinically important. Despite strong performance, most deep models formulate DR grading as image-level classification and do not explicitly model clinically grounded evidence, such as lesion types and spatial relations. In this paper, we propose PARAGraph, a Pathology-Anatomy-Aware Hierarchical Graph framework for DR grading. PARAGraph represents each image as a three-level hierarchical graph with lesion-level nodes, intermediate category and region nodes, and global anatomical and semantic nodes. To incorporate medical priors into nodes, we construct an optic disc-fovea-anchored coordinate frame that provides a scale- and rotation-normalized retinal reference system. Within this frame, lesion nodes are encoded with category, normalized area, and anatomical coordinates. To mitigate noisy lesion segmentation, PARAGraph uses a dual-fusion strategy that introduces global visual context into a graph semantic node and a decision-level prediction branch, improving robustness when lesion evidence is unreliable. Extensive experiments on Messidor-2, APTOS, and DDR show that PARAGraph achieves consistent DR grading performance over state-of-the-art methods. Interpretability and robustness analyses further demonstrate that its predictions are clinically grounded, closely associated with lesion evidence and robust to lesion segmentation noise.

</details>


### [94] [Gated Spatial Redundancy Projection for Pathology Transformer Attentions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08374)
*Zhiyuan Yang,Jiahao Cheng,Vincent Quoc-Huy Trinh,Mahdi S. Hosseini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Gated SRP（门控空间冗余投影）模块，针对病理全切片图像中自注意力机制受局部空间冗余影响而削弱细微诊断信号的问题，通过投影并几何校正冗余成分，在五类TCGA生存分析和五类分类数据集上显著提升性能，仅增加+0.02%参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer模型用于全切片图像分析时，WSI与自然图像有本质区别：相邻图块常含有高度相似的组织类型、染色、纹理和细胞组成。这种局部空间冗余是病理场景特有的自注意力失效模式——占主导的邻域特征会被反复混合进图块token，削弱细微的诊断或预后偏差信号。

Method: 提出Gated Spatial Redundancy Projection（Gated SRP），一种轻量级即插即用的自注意力层校正模块。对每个patch token和注意力头，先从相邻value向量估计局部冗余轴，将注意力输出投影到该轴上，再通过学习的有符号门控对冗余对齐分量进行几何校正。

Result: 在五个TCGA生存队列中，Gated SRP在所有队列中取得最高平均C-index，相比base attention平均有提升，仅增加约+0.02%参数；在五个切片级分类数据集上，在16项报告指标中改善12项，并在三个数据集上取得最佳AUC。

Conclusion: Gated SRP精准识别并抑制病理WSI中的局部空间冗余干扰，以极小的参数开销有效增强了自注意力对细微病理信号的捕捉能力，为计算病理学中的Transformer建模提供了通用且高效的改进方案。

Abstract: Transformer models are increasingly used for whole-slide image analysis in computational pathology. Yet, WSIs differ fundamentally from natural images: neighbouring patches often contain highly similar tissue type, stain, texture, and cellular composition. We identify this local spatial redundancy as a pathology-specific failure mode of self-attention, where dominant neighbourhood features can be repeatedly mixed into patch-tokens and weaken subtle diagnostic or prognostic deviations. We propose Gated Spatial Redundancy Projection (Gated SRP), a lightweight drop-in correction module for self-attention layers. For each patch token and attention head, Gated SRP estimates a local redundancy axis from neighbouring value vectors, projects the attention output onto this axis, and applies a learned signed gate to correct the redundancy-aligned component geometrically. Across five TCGA survival cohorts, Gated SRP obtains the highest mean C-index among the compared attention variants in all cohorts, with an average improvement over the base attention, while adding only +0.02% parameters. Across five slide-level classification datasets, it improves the base attention on 12 of 16 reported metrics and achieves the best AUC on three datasets. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/AtlasAnalyticsLab/GatedSRP.

</details>


### [95] [DoRF++: Spherical Representation Learning over Doppler Radiance Fields for Robust Wi-Fi Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08381)
*Navid Hasanzadeh,Shahrokh Valaee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DoRF（Doppler Radiance Fields），将神经辐射场（NeRF）概念从计算机视觉引入Wi-Fi感知领域，用于基于CSI的多普勒速度投影进行被动、无设备的人类活动识别（HAR）。DoRF将CSI提取的多普勒观测建模为虚拟相机视图，推断潜在3D运动序列，并将其投影到单位球面上的等角网格，生成球形运动表示。在此基础上提出DoRF++，采用球形Transformer进行活动分类，在跨用户泛化实验中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wi-Fi CSI用于被动、无设备、隐私保护的HAR受到广泛关注，多普勒速度投影已被证明能提升鲁棒性和跨用户泛化能力。然而在真实世界变异性下可靠泛化仍是重大挑战，阻碍了Wi-Fi感知的实际应用部署。

Method: 1) DoRF：借鉴NeRF思想，将CSI提取的多普勒速度投影建模为稀疏且多样的虚拟相机视图，推断潜在的3D运动序列，其投影沿学习到的有效多普勒方向解释CSI观测；2) 将恢复的运动投影到单位球面上的等角方向网格，得到球形运动表示；3) DoRF++：引入球形Transformer架构对球形表示进行分类，完成活动识别任务。

Result: 在自采集的手势数据集上，DoRF++在跨用户泛化准确率上显著优于现有最先进的Wi-Fi HAR方法，尤其是在单多天线接收AP设置下的困难手势识别上表现突出。

Conclusion: DoRF/DoRF++通过将NeRF概念引入Wi-Fi感知并利用球形学习架构，有效提升了跨用户泛化能力，为真实场景下Wi-Fi感知应用（受IEEE 802.11bf标准推动）提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Motivated by the IEEE 802.11bf effort to standardize advanced WLAN sensing, interest in Wi-Fi Channel State Information (CSI) for passive, device-free, and privacy-preserving activity and gesture recognition has grown rapidly. Recent studies have shown that Doppler velocity projections extracted from CSI, which directly reflect human-motion velocity, enable more robust human activity recognition (HAR) and stronger generalization across users and unseen conditions. Nevertheless, reliable generalization under real-world variability remains a major challenge, hindering the adoption of Wi-Fi sensing in real-world applications. To address this challenge, we introduce Doppler Radiance Fields (DoRF), bringing the concept of neural radiance fields (NeRF) from computer vision into Wi-Fi sensing. DoRF models Doppler velocity projections extracted from Wi-Fi CSI as sparse and diverse virtual-camera views of human motion. It then infers a latent 3D motion sequence whose projections along learned effective Doppler directions explain the CSI-derived Doppler observations. The recovered motion is subsequently projected onto an equiangular grid of directions on the unit sphere, producing a spherical representation of the underlying motion. Since DoRF naturally defines the Doppler representation on spheres, we further introduce DoRF++, a spherical-learning design that applies spherical Transformers for activity classification. Experiments on our collected hand-gesture dataset show that DoRF++ significantly outperforms state-of-the-art Wi-Fi-based HAR methods in cross-user generalization accuracy, especially for difficult gestures in settings with a single multi-antenna receiver access point (AP).

</details>


### [96] [Anatomically Consistent Cross-Contrast Super-Resolution of Anisotropic Brain T2w MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08401)
*Mengqi Shen,Haicheng Wang,Meghna Trivedi,Tony J. Wang,Yuanguang Xu,Yingyan Zeng,Yading Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VIPP-SR是一种跨对比度引导的超分辨率框架，用于恢复各向异性T2加权脑MRI的层间分辨率，无需各向同性的真值T2w图像。该方法通过训练视角无关的patch生成器（VIP-GAN）学习T1c到T2w的解剖对应关系，并对三个正交视图进行零样本跨平面转移，最终通过基于投影的优化融合三个视角的体积，实现解剖一致性。实验表明该方法在BraTS-MET和BraTS-GLI（零样本）数据集上显著提升了下游分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: T2加权脑MRI在神经肿瘤学和放射治疗规划中至关重要，但其通常以各向异性体素采集，在冠状面和矢状面上呈现模糊或阶梯状伪影，掩盖了细小结构并削弱了3D分析性能。现有方法需要各向同性真值T2w训练，这在临床中难以获得，因此需要一种无需各向同性真值的跨平面超分辨率方案。

Method: VIPP-SR包含三个核心组件：(1) VIP-GAN，一个视角无关的patch生成器，从高分辨率轴向切片学习T1c到T2w的局部解剖对应；(2) 形状保持的patch化和最深跳过连接去除，以减少视角特定捷径并约束生成器学习局部表征，实现零样本跨平面迁移；(3) 基于投影的优化，通过在三个正交视图生成的T2w估计之间强制解剖一致性并进行融合，平衡层间自一致性与每个视图的数据保真度。

Result: 在BraTS-MET测试集上，VIPP-SR将平均标签Dice从0.330提升至0.465；在BraTS-GLI上零样本测试时，Dice从0.473提升至0.563。消融研究表明层间自一致性是性能提升的主要来源，验证了VIPP-SR无需重新训练即可跨队列泛化的能力。

Conclusion: VIPP-SR有效地解决了各向异性T2w MRI的超分辨率问题，通过跨对比度引导和投影优化，在无需各向同性真值T2w的情况下显著改善了下游分割性能，并展现出良好的跨队列泛化能力，为神经肿瘤学诊断和治疗规划提供了更可靠的图像质量。

Abstract: T2-weighted (T2w) brain MRI provides fluid-sensitive soft-tissue contrast that is important for neuro-oncology and radiotherapy planning. However, T2w scans are acquired with anisotropic voxels and appear blurred or stair-stepped on coronal and sagittal views, which obscures small structures and weakens any downstream 3D analysis. We propose VIPP-SR (View-Independent Patched Projection Super-Resolution), a cross-contrast guided super-resolution framework that restores the inter-plane resolution of an existing anisotropic T2w volume without an isotropic ground-truth T2w. VIPP-SR first trains a view-independent patched generator (VIP-GAN) to learn local T1c-to-T2w anatomical correspondence from high-resolution axial slices. The trained generator is then applied to axial, coronal, and sagittal views of the T1c volume to generate three orthogonal T2w estimates. Shape-preserving patching and deepest-skip removal reduce view-specific shortcuts, thereby constraining the generator to learn patch-local representations and enabling the zero-shot inter-plane transfer. Central to VIPP-SR, a projection-based optimization then enforces anatomical consistency across the three view-specific volumes, fusing them by balancing inter-plane self-consistency against per-view data fidelity. The generator is trained on BraTS-MET and evaluated on both the held-out BraTS-MET testing set and the BraTS-GLI cohort without retraining, assessing the cross-cohort generalizability. The results validate that VIPP-SR improves downstream segmentation over the real anisotropic T2w baseline, raising mean-label Dice from 0.330 to 0.465 on BraTS-MET and, zero-shot, from 0.473 to 0.563 on BraTS-GLI and ablation studies identify inter-plane self-consistency as the main source of the gain.

</details>


### [97] [Agentic AI-powered flexible fiber-bundle endoscopy for high-resolution NIR-II fluorescence imaging in vivo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08402)
*Yanzhao Shi,Yuanhua Liu,Sixin Xu,Wayne Jason Li,Yuyuan Chen,Danyang Xu,Zhisheng Wu,Hanze Yu,Ian Yu-Hong Wong,Simon Ying-Kit Law,Hongjie Dai,Liangqiong Qu,Feifei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种AI驱动的柔性内窥镜平台，通过光学-计算协同设计（优化超薄光纤束+Agent引导的混合专家模型）克服了光纤束内窥镜长期存在的低分辨率、蜂窝伪影和串扰问题，实现了从可见光到NIR-II波段的四倍分辨率提升，并在小鼠活体及人体样本成像中验证了其临床转化潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光纤束内窥镜自1950年代问世以来，一直受限于低空间分辨率、蜂窝伪影和光纤间串扰，尤其在近红外二区（NIR-II）波段串扰更为严重。而NIR-II波段对生物医学成像具有更好的对比度、分辨率和组织穿透深度。因此需要一个新平台来突破这些限制，充分发挥NIR-II光谱窗口的优势。

Method: 采用光学-计算协同设计：(1) 光学端：优化超薄光纤束以减少串扰引起的图像模糊，支持可见光至NIR-II全谱高通真图像传输；(2) 计算端：开发Agent-Guided Mixture-of-Experts (GAME)流水线，利用视觉语言模型动态路由输入到合适的恢复专家模块，实现蜂窝伪影去除和图像恢复，将分辨率提升四倍并超越奈奎斯特-香农采样极限。

Result: GAME流水线能在单一恢复入口处理细胞、小鼠和人体的多样生物医学图像；实现了超越奈奎斯特-香农采样极限的四倍分辨率提升；成功演示了小鼠活体NIR-II解剖结构成像，以及DMD投影的人体胃管和淋巴系统成像。

Conclusion: 该平台展示了AI与光学硬件协同设计的巨大潜力，有效克服了光纤束内窥镜的长期技术瓶颈，为未来NIR-II荧光内窥镜的临床转化铺平了道路。

Abstract: Fiber-bundle endoscopy offers a compact and flexible route for clinical fluorescence imaging through natural human orifices, but since its first report in the 1950s, it has remained limited by low spatial resolution, honeycomb artifacts, and inter-core crosstalk. The crosstalk becomes more pronounced at near-infrared-II wavelengths (NIR-II, 1000-3000 nm), a spectral window that offers superior contrast, resolution, and tissue penetration depth for biomedical imaging. Here, we present an AI-powered flexible endoscopy platform that overcomes these constraints through optical-computational co-design: optimizing ultrathin fiber bundles to mitigate crosstalk-induced image blur and enable high-fidelity image transmission across the visible-to-NIR-II spectral range, and developing an Agent-Guided Mixture-of-Experts (GAME) pipeline for honeycomb-artifact removal and image restoration. GAME provides a single restoration entry point for diverse biomedical images acquired with our endoscope, spanning cell, mouse and human samples. It dynamically routes each input to suitable restoration experts via a vision-language model, facilitating image reconstruction with a fourfold resolution improvement beyond the NyquistShannon sampling limit. The utility of our endoscope is demonstrated through in vivo NIR-II imaging of anatomical structures in mice, as well as imaging of the digital micromirror device (DMD)-projected human gastric tube and lymphatic system, paving the way for future clinical translation.

</details>


### [98] [Learning Deep Modality-Shared Self-Expressiveness for Image Clustering with Textual Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08418)
*Xianghan Meng,Wei He,Zhiyuan Huang,Chun-Guang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DeepMORSE，一种基于模态共享自表达模型的深度聚类方法。与现有方法直接强制跨模态对齐不同，DeepMORSE通过模态共享的自表达模型发现跨模态结构，同时学习符合模态专属子空间联合的结构化表示。在六个基准数据集上取得显著提升，并展现出良好的下游任务迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有利用视觉-语言模型进行图像聚类的方法通常直接强制跨模态一致性（如图像-文本相似度最大化），但这种策略未显式建模每个模态内部的内在结构，可能导致不可靠的对齐或扭曲对聚类至关重要的模态专属结构。

Method: 提出DeepMORSE框架，核心包括：(1) 模态共享自表达模型，用于发现跨模态结构；(2) 同时学习符合模态专属子空间联合的结构化表示；(3) 理论上证明模态共享自表达系数能抑制类间噪声，接近子空间保持解；(4) 揭示小批量优化过程对自表达模型引入隐式正则化。

Result: 在六个广泛使用的图像聚类基准上评估，在UCF-101、DTD-47和ImageNet-Dogs数据集上取得超过3%的性能提升。在图像检索和零样本分类等下游任务上达到最先进水平，且无需任务特定损失或后处理。

Conclusion: DeepMORSE通过显式建模模态内部结构并利用模态共享自表达系数，有效解决了跨模态对齐不可靠的问题，同时其学习到的表示具有强迁移能力，代码已开源。

Abstract: Leveraging textual information for image clustering has emerged as a promising direction, largely owing to the powerful representations learned by Vision-Language Models (VLMs). Existing approaches typically retrieve a textual counterpart for each image and then refine multimodal representations by directly enforcing cross-modal agreement, e.g., maximizing image-text similarity inherited from pretrained VLMs. However, such a strategy aligns heterogeneous representations across modalities without explicitly modeling the intrinsic structure within each modality and thus might yield unreliable alignment or distort modality-specific structures that are crucial for clustering. In this paper, we propose a simple but principled approach, termed deep modality-shared self-expressive model (DeepMORSE), which discovers cross-modal structures via a modality-shared self-expressive model and simultaneously learns structured representations that conform to a union of modality-specific subspaces. Moreover, we theoretically justify that the modality-shared self-expressive coefficients suppress inter-class noise towards a subspace-preserving solution, and show that mini-batch optimization procedure introduces an implicit regularization onto the self-expressive model. We evaluate our DeepMORSE on six widely used image clustering benchmarks and observe performance improvements exceeding 3% on the UCF-101, DTD-47, and ImageNet-Dogs datasets. In addition, we demonstrate the strong transferability of the learned representations by achieving state-of-the-art performance on downstream tasks such as image retrieval and zero-shot classification---without requiring any task-specific losses or post-processing. The code is available at: https://github.com/mengxianghan123/DeepMORSE.

</details>


### [99] [FreCast: Refining Radar Echo Intensity via Phase-Preserving Amplitude Residual Diffusion for Precipitation Nowcasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08436)
*Heping Fang,Zihuai Yin,Kaicheng Mao,Peiguang Zhang,Peng Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FreCast是一个两阶段雷达回波预测框架，第一阶段生成初始预测，第二阶段利用初始预测的空间结构作为约束来校正单个位置的强度偏差，在三个数据集上取得了持续改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度学习降水临近预报模型虽然能捕捉未来降水的整体空间分布，但在单个位置的雷达回波强度预测上仍存在显著偏差。作者认为与其无空间约束地重新生成整个雷达回波序列，不如利用预测的降水结构来指导单个位置回波强度的细化，从而直接针对强度偏差进行修正。

Method: 提出FreCast两阶段框架：第一阶段生成未来雷达回波的初始预测；第二阶段以初始预测的空间结构作为约束，进一步修正第一阶段预测中单个位置的强度偏差。

Result: 在三个数据集上的实验表明，FreCast在各项预报技能指标上均取得一致的改进。定性结果表明FreCast在较长预报时效下能更好地保留雨带连续性和强降水结构。

Conclusion: 结构引导的精细化修正策略能有效降低单点回波强度预测偏差，是提升降水临近预报精度的有效途径。

Abstract: Precipitation nowcasting predicts the spatiotemporal evolution of future radar echoes from historical radar echo sequences, thereby estimating the occurrence, development, and movement of precipitation over the near term. In recent years, deep learning has become an important approach to precipitation nowcasting. Although state-of-the-art models can generally capture the overall spatial distribution of future precipitation, their predictions still exhibit substantial biases in radar echo intensity at individual locations. This observation motivates a more targeted strategy for reducing forecast errors. Instead of regenerating an entire radar echo sequence without spatial constraints, the predicted precipitation structure can be used to guide the refinement of echo intensities at individual locations. This structure-guided refinement directly targets echo intensity biases. Accordingly, we propose FreCast, a two-stage framework for radar echo prediction. The first stage generates an initial forecast of future radar echoes. The second stage uses the spatial structure of the initial forecast as a constraint to further correct intensity biases at individual locations in the first-stage prediction. Experiments on three datasets demonstrate that FreCast achieves consistent improvements across forecast skill metrics. Qualitative results further show that FreCast better preserves rainband continuity and intense precipitation structures at longer lead times.

</details>


### [100] [InstructionCrafter: Generating Consistent and High-Fidelity Visual Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08460)
*Shun Okamoto,Satoshi Iizuka,Kazuhiro Fukui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出InstructionCrafter，一个基于扩散模型的框架，用于根据文本任务指令生成逐步的视觉指令图像序列。核心思想是通过空间冻结训练和指令感知适配器，将时间/指令对齐与每帧视觉质量分离优化，在保持生成先验的同时减少约50%的可训练参数，并在两个基准数据集上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从文本任务指令生成逐步视觉指令图像序列需要同时满足三个属性：步骤忠实度、跨图像一致性和每帧视觉质量。现有文本到图像生成方法难以同时满足这些要求，因为：独立采样破坏一致性、在低质量视频上微调降低每帧质量、冻结骨干网络缺乏多步理解能力。

Method: InstructionCrafter基于预训练视频扩散骨干网络，采用两个关键设计：(1)空间冻结训练——冻结控制每帧细节的空间层，仅更新时间层和文本条件路径，以保留每帧质量的生成先验并减少约50%的可训练参数；(2)两个轻量级适配器——Consistent Adapter聚合整个指令序列和相邻步骤的文本线索以保持跨帧的对象身份和属性一致性；Context-Aware Temporal Adapter将交叉注意力输出转换为时间自注意力的偏置，显式传播帧间关系。

Result: 在两个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在步骤忠实度、跨图像一致性和每帧视觉质量方面均达到最先进的整体性能，同时显著减少了噪声、模糊和虚假字幕。

Conclusion: 通过分离时间/指令对齐与每帧视觉质量的优化，InstructionCrafter有效解决了多步视觉指令生成中的关键挑战，代码和训练模型将公开发布。

Abstract: Given textual task instructions, generating step-by-step visual instructions as an image sequence requires the simultaneous satisfaction of multiple properties, specifically step faithfulness, cross-image consistency, and per-frame visual quality. Existing text-to-image generation approaches rarely meet all three properties, owing to independent sampling that breaks consistency, finetuning on low-quality video that degrades per-frame quality, and frozen backbones that lack multi-step understanding. In this work, we propose InstructionCrafter, a diffusion-based framework with the key idea of separating the optimization of temporal and instructional alignment from per-frame visual quality via (1) spatial-freeze training and (2) instruction-aware adapters. Built on a pretrained video diffusion backbone, InstructionCrafter freezes the spatial layers that control per-frame detail and updates only temporal and text-conditioning pathways to learn instruction semantics and inter-step relations, which preserves the generative prior for per-frame quality and reduces trainable parameters by about 50 percent compared with full finetuning. We also introduce two lightweight adapters that enhance the model's understanding of instructional context. The Consistent Adapter aggregates textual cues from the entire instruction sequence and from neighboring steps to keep object identity and attributes consistent across frames, and the Context-Aware Temporal Adapter converts cross-attention outputs into biases for temporal self-attention, explicitly propagating inter-frame relations. Extensive experiments on two benchmark datasets demonstrate state-of-the-art overall performance on step faithfulness, cross-image consistency, and per-frame visual quality while significantly reducing noise, blur, and spurious subtitles. Our code and trained models will be publicly available.

</details>


### [101] [RayLift: Lifting Complementary Ray-Wise Evidence with 3D Geometry Priors for Semantic Scene Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08476)
*Meng Wang,Hongxia Yu,Wenzhe He,Xingdong Song,Huilong Pi,Jiapeng Zhang,Ruihui Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RayLift是一个用于相机3D语义场景补全（SSC）的框架，利用立体几何作为度量参考，同时融合互补的射线证据来自适应恢复可靠的3D结构，在SemanticKITTI和SSCBench-KITTI-360上取得优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于相机的SSC方法通常将立体深度估计视为确定性的几何约束，导致深度不确定性和局部对应误差直接传播到体素表示中，影响场景理解的准确性。

Method: RayLift包含三个关键模块：1) Complementary Context Encoder利用冻结的3D视觉基础模型提取几何感知先验；2) Depth Ray Evidence Lifter联合建模几何不相似性、深度置信度和空间不确定性，沿每条相机射线自适应采样和加权候选表面位置；3) Semantic-Aware Voxel Integrator通过显式建模空间支持将射线证据注入体素特征。

Result: 在SemanticKITTI和SSCBench-KITTI-360数据集上的大量实验表明，RayLift取得了具有竞争力的性能，并且一致优于现有方法。

Conclusion: RayLift通过将立体几何作为度量参考并结合互补的射线证据，有效解决了深度不确定性传播问题，为相机3D语义场景补全提供了一种鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Camera-based 3D semantic scene completion (SSC) provides comprehensive scene understanding for autonomous driving and robotics. However, existing methods often treat stereo depth estimates as deterministic geometric constraints, causing depth uncertainty and local correspondence errors to propagate directly into voxel representations. To address this issue, we propose RayLift, a framework that uses stereo geometry as a metric reference while incorporating complementary ray evidence to recover reliable 3D structures adaptively. RayLift first employs a Complementary Context Encoder that extracts geometry-aware priors from a frozen 3D vision foundation model, thereby enriching the scene context. It then introduces a Depth Ray Evidence Lifter module that jointly models geometric dissimilarity, depth confidence, and spatial uncertainty to adaptively sample and weight candidate surface locations along each camera ray. Finally, a Semantic-Aware Voxel Integrator injects the resulting ray evidence into voxel features by explicitly modeling their spatial support. Extensive experiments on SemanticKITTI and SSCBench-KITTI-360 demonstrate that RayLift achieves competitive performance and consistently outperforms existing methods.

</details>


### [102] [RenderMatte: Exact-Alpha Rendering and Group-Relative Alignment for Image Matting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08487)
*Zecheng Ren,Yafei Hu,Jianing Zhao,Ruichen Cong,Qun Jin,Yiren Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RenderMatte，一个基于trimap引导的抠图框架，通过对FLUX.1 Kontext进行全参数微调，利用图像编辑先验实现保结构的alpha预测。引入alpha-edge目标函数和组相对alpha对齐策略，并构建大规模合成RenderMatte数据集（含精确到发丝级别的alpha标注），在多个基准上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放世界场景中精确alpha估计仍具挑战性：真实前景外观多样、透明度模式复杂，导致现有方法在语义模糊和细粒度透明度变化上表现不佳，尤其在难监督的稀疏边界区域容易失效。

Method: (1) 基于FLUX.1 Kontext全参数微调的trimap引导抠图框架，利用图像编辑先验进行保结构alpha预测；(2) alpha-edge目标函数：在监督适配阶段保留潜在流匹配信号并增强像素空间边界监督；(3) 组相对alpha对齐：在相同trimap条件下采样多个matte，用抠图特定奖励（alpha准确性、边界保真度、trimap符合度、合成一致性）进行对比优化；(4) 构建RenderMatte数据集：大规模合成数据，融合3D渲染RGBA前景与多样多源资产，具有精确到发丝级别的alpha标注。

Result: 在所有基准测试上均达到最先进的性能，验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文展示了一条迈向开放世界场景高保真抠图的可扩展路径，通过将大规模生成模型的编辑先验与精细化数据标注和训练策略相结合，有效解决了稀疏边界区域的监督难题。

Abstract: Image matting is an essential enabling technology for modern visual content production, where foreground extraction determines the realism and editability of downstream creation workflows. However, precise alpha estimation in open-world scenes remains challenging because real foregrounds exhibit highly diverse appearances and opacity patterns. This makes existing methods struggle with semantic ambiguity and fine-grained opacity variation, especially in sparse boundary regions that are fragile and difficult to supervise. To address this gap, we present RenderMatte, a trimap-guided matting framework that adapts FLUX.1 Kontext through full-parameter fine-tuning, leveraging image editing priors for structure-preserving alpha prediction. During supervised adaptation, an alpha-edge objective preserves the latent flow-matching signal while strengthening pixel-space boundary supervision. We further introduce group-relative alpha alignment for post-training. It compares multiple mattes sampled under the same trimap condition using matting-specific rewards for alpha accuracy, boundary fidelity, trimap compliance, and compositional consistency. To overcome the lack of precise edge annotations, we construct the RenderMatte dataset, a large-scale synthetic dataset combining 3D-rendered RGBA foregrounds with diverse multi-source assets. It features exact strand-level alpha annotations and diverse background composites. Experiments show state-of-the-art performance across all benchmarks, demonstrating a scalable path toward high-fidelity matting in open-world scenes.

</details>


### [103] [Linguistically-Aligned and Visually-Grounded Preference Optimization for Clinically-Augmented Medical Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08494)
*Qiang Hu,Yuxuan Luo,Yingjie Guo,Hao Wang,Qimei Wang,Qiang Li,Zhiwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DPO-Clin，一个面向医学报告生成的后期训练框架，通过实体级临床诊断（ECD）模块、检索增强的多模态DPO变体（M²DPO）以及反事实修改构建针对性偏好数据，将偏好优化聚焦于临床发现与跨模态对齐，在多个数据集上显著提升SFT基线并优于现有DPO方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的DPO-based MRG方法采用简单的偏好构建方式，直接将模型生成的报告与真实报告配对。这种策略会无意中将关键临床发现与临床无关的语言特征纠缠在一起，且缺乏显式的视觉-语言对齐，导致医学报告中的事实性错误仍然普遍存在。

Method: DPO-Clin框架包含三个核心组成部分：1）实体级临床诊断（ECD）模块，进行精确的实体级事实诊断，生成语言对齐的报告偏好对，分离临床差异与语言变异；2）M²DPO，一种检索增强的多模态DPO变体，通过视觉上下文切换触发文本偏好反转，实现细粒度的跨模态对齐；3）定位正确但高度不确定的预测实体，应用反事实修改构建针对性偏好数据以缓解潜在风险。

Result: 在两个公开胸部X光数据集（MIMIC-CXR和IU X-Ray）以及一个院内内窥镜数据集上的大量实验表明，DPO-Clin在临床感知指标上显著提升了SFT基线性能，并优于现有的DPO-based MRG方法，展现出在不同基线架构和多种医学成像模态上的良好泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过将偏好优化聚焦于临床发现和跨模态对齐，DPO-Clin有效解决了现有DPO策略中临床信息与语言特征纠缠的问题，显著提升了医学报告生成的可靠性与事实准确性，并具备跨架构、跨模态的鲁棒泛化性。

Abstract: Despite significant advances in Medical Report Generation (MRG), the reliability remains constrained by the prevalence of factual errors. While Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) has emerged as a promising post-training paradigm to enhance the performance of Supervised Fine-Tuned (SFT) MRG models, existing DPO-based MRG methods typically adopt a naive preference construction that directly pairs model-generated reports with ground truth reports. This strategy inadvertently entangles critical clinical findings with clinically irrelevant linguistic characteristics, and fundamentally lacks explicit vision-language alignment. To address these challenges, we propose DPO-Clin, a novel post-training framework that focuses preference optimization on clinical findings and cross-modal alignment. First, we introduce the Entity-level Clinical Diagnostic (ECD) module to perform a precise entity-level factual diagnosis. ECD guides the generation of linguistically-aligned report preference pairs, isolating clinical discrepancies from linguistic variations. Second, to achieve fine-grained cross-modal alignment, we develop M$^2$DPO, a retrieval-augmented multi-modal DPO variant that enforces textual preference inversion triggered by visual context switches. Third, we locate correct yet highly uncertain predicted entities and apply counterfactual modifications to construct targeted preference data for latent risk mitigation, thereby further enhancing the model reliability. Extensive experiments on two public chest X-ray datasets (MIMIC-CXR and IU X-Ray) and an in-house endoscopy dataset demonstrate that DPO-Clin significantly improves the SFT baselines on clinical-aware metrics. Furthermore, it achieves superior performance over existing DPO-based MRG methods, exhibiting robust generalizability across distinct baseline architectures and diverse medical imaging modalities.

</details>


### [104] [Towards Adaptive Super-Resolution and Quality Assessment via Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08508)
*Ajeet Kumar Verma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于测试时自适应（TTA）的博士研究，旨在解决真实世界条件下视频超分辨率（VSR）的泛化问题。研究包括三个核心贡献：基于TTA的无参考视频质量评估框架、用于屏幕内容超分辨率的Transformer架构、以及区域感知的TTA策略。实验表明这些方法在不依赖高质量监督的情况下，能显著提升感知质量和可读性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频超分辨率方法在异质设备、编码器和网络环境造成的未知退化条件下泛化能力差。传统方法依赖特定退化模型的训练，难以适应现实中多种未知退化组合，需要一种无需重训练或高质量监督的自适应方案来提升鲁棒性和感知质量。

Method: 本文提出统一的测试时自适应（TTA）范式，包含三部分：1）基于TTA的无参考视频质量评估框架，利用自适应质量预测为VSR提供感知引导；2）基于Transformer的屏幕内容超分辨率架构，保持文本清晰度与结构保真；3）区域感知的TTA策略，在不需高分辨率真值的情况下选择性细化文本与非文本区域。

Result: 在多个多样化基准上的实验结果表明，所提出的方法在感知质量和可读性方面均取得一致改进，验证了TTA范式在未知退化条件下的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了TTA作为统一范式在真实世界视频超分辨率中的巨大潜力，并为未来完全自适应的视频增强系统指明了方向，使其能够跨未知域泛化。

Abstract: This paper presents doctoral research on adaptive video super-resolution and perceptual quality modeling under real-world conditions. Existing video super-resolution (VSR) methods struggle to generalize under unknown degradations arising from heterogeneous devices, codecs, and network environments. We address this challenge through test-time adaptation (TTA), a unified paradigm that improves robustness and perceptual quality without retraining or high-quality supervision. Specifically, we: 1) propose a TTA-based framework for no-reference video quality assessment (VQA), where adapted quality predictions provide perceptual guidance for VSR under unseen distortions; 2) develop a transformer-based architecture for screen-content super-resolution that preserves text clarity and structural fidelity; and 3) introduce a region-aware TTA strategy that selectively refines text and non-text regions without requiring high-resolution ground truth. Experimental results across diverse benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements in perceptual quality and readability. We also outline ongoing work toward fully adaptive video enhancement systems capable of generalizing across unseen domains.

</details>


### [105] [eBIRD: Event-based Intensity Image Reconstruction Using Controllable Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08519)
*Ignacio Bugueno-Cordova,Fabian Valderrama,Rodrigo Verschae*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 这篇论文提出eBIRD框架，结合扩散概率模型（DDPM）和ControlNet条件机制，用于从事件流重建强度图像。实验在N-MNIST手写数字和RGBE-Gaze人脸数据集上进行，结果显示通用模型在N-MNIST上表现最佳（MSE 0.0052, SSIM 0.8982, PSNR 23.34dB），而专用模型在RGBE-Gaze上表现最佳（MSE 0.0161, SSIM 0.7605, PSNR 19.08dB）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机产生的数据具有二进制、稀疏和异步特性，使得从事件流重建强度图像极具挑战性。作者希望探索可控扩散模型是否能为这一难题提供更好的解决方案。

Method: 提出了eBIRD框架，将DDPM与基于ControlNet的条件机制相结合。比较了通用和专用两种扩散学习策略，使用33ms事件窗口进行重建任务。

Result: 在N-MNIST数据集上，通用学习策略表现最佳（MSE 0.0052, SSIM 0.8982, PSNR 23.34dB）；在RGBE-Gaze人脸数据集上，专用学习策略表现最佳（MSE 0.0161, SSIM 0.7605, PSNR 19.08dB）。

Conclusion: 可控扩散模型是事件引导强度图像重建的有前景方法，但最佳学习策略的选择依赖于具体的重建领域。

Abstract: Intensity-image reconstruction from event streams remains a challenging problem due to the binary, sparse, and asynchronous nature of event data. This work proposes eBIRD, an event-guided reconstruction framework that combines a DDPM with ControlNet-based conditioning. We analyze generic and specialized diffusion learning strategies for handwritten digit (N-MNIST) and face (RGBE-Gaze) reconstruction using 33ms event windows. On N-MNIST, the general model achieves the best reconstruction quality (MSE 0.0052, SSIM 0.8982, PSNR 23.34dB), whereas the specialized model performs best on RGBE-Gaze (MSE 0.0161, SSIM 0.7605, PSNR 19.08dB). These preliminary results suggest that controllable diffusion models are a promising approach for event-guided intensity-image reconstruction, while highlighting that the preferred learning strategy depends on the reconstruction domain.

</details>


### [106] [A Combined Feature-Based Framework for Disguise and Spoofing Detection in Face Recognition Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08521)
*Sangiya Pararajasingham*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出并比较了五种结合特征提取与分类的流水线（PM、LPM、HPM、SM、HM），用于在统一框架内同时解决人脸识别的两个失败模式：伪造攻击与伪装。实验表明HOG流水线（HPM）在各类条件下表现最稳定（伪装94.59%、姿态/光照81.5-93.2%、伪造91.67%），而LBP流水线（LPM）在伪造检测上精度最高（93.2%）但姿态鲁棒性较弱，揭示了经典特征表示在伪造敏感性与伪装鲁棒性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人脸识别系统面临两个通常被分开处理的失败模式：伪造（冒名者用照片或视频冒充授权用户）和伪装（合法用户因配件、胡须、光照或姿态等外观变化而被拒绝）。现有方法通常分别处理这两个问题，缺乏统一框架，且需要探索在单一系统中同时应对这两类挑战的方法。

Method: 提出并比较五种特征提取与分类流水线：PM（PCA+最小欧氏距离）、LPM（LBP+PCA+MED）、HPM（HOG+PCA+MED）、SM（SURF+MED）和HM（Harris角点+MED）。每种流水线遵循共同的两阶段流程：预处理、特征提取、特征过滤、分类。在115个来自FEI、Disguised Faces Database和NUAA数据库的对象上训练，并在六种测试条件下评估。

Result: HOG流水线（HPM）在各类条件下表现最稳定：混合外观伪装场景达94.59%准确率，姿态和光照变化下为81.5-93.2%，伪造检测为91.67%。LBP流水线（LPM）在伪造检测上达到最高准确率93.2%，但对姿态变化的鲁棒性较弱。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了经典特征表示在伪造检测敏感性与伪装鲁棒性之间存在可测量的权衡，这一发现为后续深度学习和跨数据库扩展研究提供了动机和方向。

Abstract: Face recognition systems face two distinct, commonly-separated failure modes: spoofing, where an impostor presents a photograph or video of an authorized user, and disguise, where a legitimate user is rejected because their appearance differs from their enrolled template due to accessories, facial hair, illumination, or pose. This paper proposes and compares five combined feature-extraction and classification pipelines that address both problems within a single framework: PM (PCA and Minimum Euclidean Distance, MED), LPM (Local Binary Patterns with PCA and MED), HPM (Histogram of Oriented Gradients with PCA and MED), SM (Speeded-Up Robust Features with MED), and HM (Harris corner features with MED). Each pipeline follows a common two-phase process comprising pre-processing, feature extraction, feature filtering, and classification. The methods were trained on 115 subjects drawn from the FEI, Disguised Faces Database, and NUAA databases and evaluated on six test conditions covering mixed appearances, frontal faces, dark illumination, left- and right-turned poses, and photo-spoofing attempts. The HOG-based pipeline (HPM) achieved the most consistent performance across conditions, with 94.59% accuracy on mixed-appearance disguise, 81.5-93.2% across pose and illumination variants, and 91.67% on spoofing, while the LBP-based pipeline (LPM) achieved the highest spoofing-detection accuracy (93.2%) but weaker robustness to pose change. These results reveal a measurable trade-off between spoof sensitivity and disguise robustness among classical feature representations, motivating the deep-learning and cross-database extensions discussed in the concluding sections.

</details>


### [107] [ERF-GS: Reconstructing Fast Motion from Disjoint Event-RGB Viewpoints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08531)
*Xiaoyang Bai,Zhenyang Li,Weiwei Xu,Edmund Y. Lam,Yifan Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ERF-GS提出了一种事件-RGB融合的高斯泼溅框架，将事件信息整合到高斯泼溅管道的优化和致密化阶段，利用事件传感器的高帧率优势来解决快速运动物体的动态3D场景重建难题。该方法在真实模拟设置下开发，实现事件基于学习与RGB输入的分离，可扩展到具有复杂布局、低帧率和严重运动模糊的自然视频场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于NeRF和3DGS的动态场景重建方法在处理快速运动物体（如体育赛事、动物摄影）时面临挑战，传统基于帧的视频在这些场景中往往效果不佳。需要一种能够利用事件传感器高帧率优势的方法来解决这一问题。

Method: ERF-GS框架将事件信息同时整合到高斯泼溅管道的优化阶段和致密化（densification）阶段。与以往的事件辅助场景重建方法不同，该方法在逼真的模拟设置下开发，实现了基于事件的学习脱离RGB输入，使其能适应自然视频中的复杂布局、低帧率和严重运动模糊场景。

Result: 实验表明，ERF-GS在Neu3D和Nvidia数据集的不同变体上（包含模糊RGB帧和不相交的RGB-事件视角）均优于4DGS基线和同时期的E-D3DGS方法。

Conclusion: ERF-GS通过将事件信息有效融入高斯泼溅流程，显著提升了快速运动场景下的动态3D重建精度，且具有超越合成数据、适用于自然视频的泛化能力。

Abstract: Deep learning-driven representations such as neural radiance fields (NeRFs) and 3D Gaussian splatting (3DGS) have revolutionized the field of dynamic 3D scene reconstruction with improved visual precision and scalability. However, the reconstruction of fast-moving objects remains a challenge; existing methods based on conventional frame-based videos often struggle in scenarios such as sports events and animal videography. We propose an event-RGB fusion Gaussian splatting (ERF-GS) framework that integrates event information into both optimization and densification stages of the Gaussian splatting pipeline, taking advantage of novel event sensors with high frame-rate. Unlike many other event-assisted scene reconstruction methods, ERF-GS was developed using realistic simulation settings and realizes event-based learning detached from RGB inputs. This design enables its application beyond straightforward synthetic data into the realm of natural video with complex layout, low frame rates and severe motion blur. Our experiments show that ERF-GS outperforms both the 4DGS baseline and the concurrent E-D3DGS on different variants of the Neu3D and Nvidia datasets which include blurry RGB frames and disjoint RGB-event viewpoints. Our code is available at https://github.com/andrewbxy/ERF-GS.

</details>


### [108] [Rethinking Attention Locality in Spiking Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08541)
*Zeqi Zheng,Zizheng Zhu,Yuping Yan,Wenxuan Pan,Zhaofei Yu,Yaochu Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对脉冲Transformer中Softmax-free脉冲自注意力（SSA）缺乏空间局部token交互的问题，通过MAD分析发现计算局部性并不等同于空间局部性，且统一应用局部增强会忽略架构相关的部署需求。为此提出SCLA-BCP方法：SCLA在空间相邻token的非重叠区域计算注意力，BCP通过轻量卷积路径促进跨边界信息交换，并开发了分层局部性部署策略以适应两大主流脉冲Transformer架构。在七个静态和神经形态数据集上（分类、检测、分割）取得了一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲Transformer虽提供高效视觉处理范式，但其SSA难以建立空间局部化的token交互。现有局部性增强方法虽提升精度，但不确定是否能跨层、跨架构一致地诱导空间局部性。MAD分析揭示计算局部性不一定转化为空间局部性，且统一应用局部增强会忽视架构依赖的部署需求。

Method: 提出SCLA-BCP框架：1）SCLA（空间连续局部注意力）在空间相邻token的非重叠区域内计算注意力；2）BCP（边界连续通路）通过轻量卷积路径促进跨边界信息交换；3）开发分层局部性部署策略，以有效适配两大主要脉冲Transformer架构。

Result: 在七个静态和神经形态数据集上覆盖分类、检测和分割任务，取得一致的性能提升且仅增加有限参数和能量开销。COCO 2017上mAP@50最高提升9.50%，ADE20K上mIoU最高提升3.42%。可视化、MAD分析和消融研究进一步验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: SCLA-BCP有效解决了脉冲Transformer中空间局部性缺失的问题，证明了架构感知的分层局部性部署策略的重要性，为脉冲Transformer在不同视觉任务中的高效应用提供了新思路。

Abstract: Spiking Transformers provide a promising paradigm for efficient visual processing with spike-driven computation, yet their Softmax-free Spiking Self-Attention (SSA) struggles to establish spatially localized token interactions. Although existing locality-enhanced SSA methods improve accuracy, it remains unclear whether they consistently induce spatial locality across layers and different Spiking Transformer architectures. Through Mean Attention Distance (MAD) analysis, we reveal that computational locality does not necessarily translate into spatial locality and show that uniformly applying the same locality enhancement overlooks architecture-dependent deployment requirements. Motivated by these observations, we propose Spatially Contiguous Local Attention with Boundary Continuity Pathway (SCLA-BCP). SCLA computes attention within non-overlapping regions of spatially adjacent tokens, while BCP facilitates cross-boundary information exchange through a lightweight convolutional pathway. Furthermore, we develop a hierarchical locality deployment strategy to effectively apply SCLA-BCP across the two major Spiking Transformer architectures. Extensive experiments on seven static and neuromorphic datasets covering classification, detection, and segmentation demonstrate consistent improvements with limited parameter and energy overhead. Notably, our approach improves mAP@50 by up to 9.50% on COCO 2017 and mIoU by up to 3.42% on ADE20K. Visualizations, MAD analysis, and ablation studies further validate its effectiveness.

</details>


### [109] [MotionCraft: Latent World Modeling with Sparse Attention for Visual Upscaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08553)
