*Rong Fu,Chunlei Meng,Yangchen Zeng,Xiaowen Ma,Yongtai Liu,Wangyu Wu,Shuo Yin,Zijian Zhang,Sicheng Li,Yingrui Ji,Chenhao Wang,Simon Fong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MotionCraft 是一个可控的视频超分辨率(VSR)框架，将恢复问题建模为运动感知的潜状态预测（受世界模型启发），结合自适应稀疏注意力与用户可访问的控制接口，在流式约束下实现时间一致且高质量的视频重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VSR方法存在权衡问题：卷积对齐方法在大运动或复杂退化下效果差；Transformer方法虽能捕捉长程依赖但计算量大；潜空间或扩散生成方法能合成丰富纹理但需专门的时序约束来维持一致性。需要一个能平衡局部细节保真、长程时空建模、感知真实感和效率的统一框架。

Method: MotionCraft包含三个核心组件：(1) 鲁棒运动融合；(2) 潜世界变换器(Latent World Transformer)，平衡局部性和目标化非局部交互；(3) 紧凑的条件解码器。框架将恢复任务形式化为运动感知的潜状态预测，并集成自适应稀疏注意力与显式用户可访问的控制接口。

Result: 实验表明MotionCraft在重建质量和感知性能上均表现优异，并能在时间平滑度和重建保真度之间实现可预测的权衡。

Conclusion: MotionCraft提供了一种新的可控VSR方案，通过世界模型视角的运动感知潜状态预测，在流式约束下同时兼顾了时间一致性、重建保真度和用户可控性。

Abstract: Video super-resolution (VSR) aims to recover high-fidelity high-resolution videos from low-resolution inputs and is central to applications ranging from mobile capture to streaming and archival restoration. Existing approaches trade off among local-detail fidelity, long-range spatio-temporal modeling, perceptual realism, and efficiency: convolutional alignment techniques preserve local structure but suffer when motion is large or degradations are complex; transformer-based methods capture long-range dependencies yet require architectural or algorithmic adaptations to remain computationally feasible; and recent latent or diffusion-based generators synthesize rich texture but require specialized temporal constraints to maintain coherence. We present MotionCraft, a controllable VSR framework that formulates restoration as motion-aware latent state prediction inspired by world models and integrates adaptive sparse attention with an explicit user-accessible control interface. MotionCraft combines robust motion fusion, a Latent World Transformer that balances locality and targeted non-local interactions, and a compact conditional decoder to deliver temporally consistent, high-quality reconstructions under streaming constraints. Empirical evaluations show that MotionCraft achieves strong reconstruction and perceptual performance while enabling predictable trade-offs between temporal smoothness and reconstruction fidelity.

</details>


### [110] [SC-Diff: Semantically Calibrated Diffusion for Visible-to-Infrared Image Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08555)
*Junyin Zhang,Siyu Huang,Jianxiong Ye,Haowei Gong,Ruicheng Zhang,Deyu Meng,Chenqiang Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了SC-Diff，一个语义校准的潜扩散框架，用于可见光到红外的图像翻译。通过SAM3提取语义掩码，并将其同时用于条件引导和去噪网络内部的自注意力校准（SGSC），以提升生成红外图像的语义一致性，并改善下游红外目标检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的可见光到红外图像翻译方法仅将语义先验作为外部条件，未显式调控去噪网络内部的token交互，难以保持目标的位置、形状和语义布局，限制了标注数据的可靠复用。

Method: 1) 使用SAM3模型配合预定义文本提示从可见光图像提取类别级语义掩码，合并为语义图并与可见光图像融合作为输入条件。2) 将语义图转换为token级语义标签，在去噪网络中校准自注意力。3) 提出语义引导的自注意力校准（SGSC），对同类别的query-key对施加自适应正向偏置，校准强度由注意力跨类别分散度和自身类别注意力决定，原始注意力分数进一步调制偏置。

Result: 大量实验表明SC-Diff在感知质量上优于现有方法，并且生成的合成红外数据对下游红外目标检测任务更有效。

Conclusion: SC-Diff证明将语义先验同时用于外部条件引导和内部自注意力校准，可以有效减少跨类别干扰同时保留全局上下文交互，从而提升生成红外图像的语义一致性和下游检测性能。

Abstract: Visible-to-infrared image translation provides a practical way to expand infrared training data using abundant visible images. Diffusion models are promising for this task because of their strong generative performance. However, existing diffusion-based methods typically use semantic priors only as external conditions, without explicitly regulating token interactions within the denoising network. Consequently, they struggle to preserve object locations, shapes, and semantic layouts required for reliable annotation reuse. We propose SC-Diff, a semantically calibrated latent diffusion framework that uses semantic priors for both conditional guidance and internal self-attention calibration. A pretrained SAM3 model with predefined text prompts first extracts category-specific semantic masks from visible images. These masks are merged into a semantic map and fused with the visible image as the input condition. The same map is converted into token-level semantic labels to calibrate self-attention in the denoising network. Based on these labels, we introduce Semantic-Guided Self-Attention Calibration (SGSC), which adaptively applies positive biases to query-key pairs of the same category. The query-wise calibration strength depends on the dispersion of attention across semantic categories and the attention assigned to the query's own category. The original attention scores further modulate the bias, giving greater calibration to same-category keys with stronger responses. This soft calibration reduces cross-category interference while retaining global contextual interactions, thereby improving semantic consistency in generated infrared images. Extensive experiments show that SC-Diff improves perceptual quality and produces more effective synthetic training data for downstream infrared object detection.

</details>


### [111] [On-Device Multi-Species Malaria Detection with Uncertainty-Calibrated Slide-Level Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08566)
*Idaya Seidu,Ahmed Tahiru Issah,Charles B. Delahunt,Carine Mukamakuza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向资源有限环境中疟疾自动诊断的完整临床部署流水线，在密切咨询国家卫生中心的基础上，解决了六项典型ML文献中被忽视的临床约束，包括停止准则、人在回路、多物种判别、厚血膜检测、不确定度计算和边缘设备部署。系统使用YOLOv13n在设备端推理，在2,739张标注图像上达到mAP@0.5为0.863，滑动级别寄生虫计数相关性r=0.951，完全离线运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 疟疾在资源有限地区仍是主要致死原因，但缺乏专家显微镜学家。虽然自动化诊断有巨大潜力，但算法要真正部署，必须满足一系列从ML视角看不明显的临床约束。现有ML疟疾文献通常忽略这些关键临床需求，因此需要开发一个真正满足医护中心实际要求的诊断系统。

Method: 与国家级卫生中心密切合作，开发了一套完整的疟疾诊断流水线，包含六项关键设计：(i)停止准则以减少图像采集和时间成本；(ii)人在回路功能以支持审查与问责；(iii)多物种判别（因治疗方案因物种而异）；(iv)厚血膜检测（显微镜标准方法）；(v)计算高效的不确定度计算以辅助临床审查；(vi)边缘设备平台（应对网络不稳定）。系统采用YOLOv13n通过TensorFlow Lite在设备端运行所有推理，从吉姆萨染色的厚血膜图像中检测四种疟原虫物种和白细胞，并将逐图像检测聚合成幻灯片级别寄生虫血症量化（符合WHO标准）。

Result: 在涵盖所有四种物种的2,739张标注图像上评估，系统实现mAP@0.5为0.863，逐图像寄生虫计数相关性r=0.812，幻灯片级别r=0.951（软计数，每幻灯片10张图像），完全离线运行，每张图像流水线处理时间为10.27±1.65秒。

Conclusion: 该工作强调了基于真实临床需求设计ML系统的价值，证明了在满足实际医疗部署约束（如离线运行、实时性、可审查性）的同时仍能达到较高诊断性能是可行的，为疟疾自动诊断在资源有限环境中的实际落地提供了可靠方案。

Abstract: Malaria remains a leading cause of mortality in resource-limited settings, where expert microscopists are scarce. Automated diagnosis based on microscopy images thus has strong potential to improve care delivery. But for an algorithm to deploy, a necessary requirement is that it meet a suite of non-obvious (from a machine learning (ML) perspective) clinical constraints. Therefore, in close consultation with a national health center we developed a malaria diagnosis pipeline which addresses key requirements listed by the health care center but typically ignored in the ML malaria literature. In particular, it includes: (i) stopping criteria (to reduce image acquisition and time-to-result); (ii) human-in-the-loop functionality (for review and accountability); (iii) multi-species discrimination (since treatment varies by species); (iv) thick film detection (standard for microscopy); (v) computationally-efficient uncertainty calculations (to aid clinician review); and (vi) an edge device platform (since internet can be spotty in this catchment area). The mobile system performs all inference on-device using YOLOv13n deployed via TensorFlow Lite. It detects four species and white blood cells from Giemsa-stained thick blood smear images, aggregating per-image detections into slide-level parasitemia with World Health Organization (WHO)-standard quantification. This paper highlights these various clinical constraints and offers methods to address them. Evaluated on 2,739 annotated images across all four species, the system achieves mAP@0.5 of 0.863, per-image parasite count correlation of r = 0.812, slide-level r = 0.951 (soft counting, 10 images/slide), and runs entirely offline with a pipeline time of 10.27 +- 1.65 s per image.

</details>


### [112] [CDGC-Net: 3D Medical Image Segmentation with Cooperative Dual-Scale Self-Attention and Grouped Channel Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08575)
*Zheyang Jing,Qin Lu,Jianwang Li,Yujie Yang,Chen Yi,Shaofeng Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CDGC-Net，一种3D医学图像分割网络，通过协作双尺度空间注意力（CDSA）和分组分层通道注意力（GHCA）在同一特征级别同时建模长距离解剖上下文与精细边界细节，在Synapse、ACDC、BraTS和LA数据集上均取得最优分割性能，同时相比UNETR++显著降低参数量和计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D医学图像分割方法将全局与局部特征分离建模，并在不同特征级别或独立模块中执行通道重校准，导致全局上下文与局部边界之间的语义不匹配、通道关系建模不足、空间-通道交互弱以及特征表示冗余等问题。

Method: CDGC-Net的核心是CDGC块，包含两个关键组件：(1) 协作双尺度自注意力（CDSA），将注意力头分配到并行局部窗口分支和全局稀疏分支，在同一特征级别同时捕获精细空间细节和长距离解剖上下文；(2) 分组分层通道注意力（GHCA），将通道组织为r个组，建模组内和组间依赖关系。CDSA和GHCA共享键投影以保持特征参考一致性，并通过残差特征对齐将精炼特征与原始表示融合。

Result: 在Synapse、ACDC、BraTS和LA数据集上，CDGC-Net的DSC分别为86.96%、92.91%、82.56%和93.52%，较次优方法分别提升0.39、0.47、0.17和0.32个百分点。模型参数量25.83M、FLOPs 28.62G（输入64×128×128），相比UNETR++分别减少39.87%和40.30%。

Conclusion: CDGC-Net在分割精度和计算复杂度之间取得了良好平衡，验证了在同一特征级别协同建模空间注意力与通道注意力、并通过共享键投影增强一致性的有效性。

Abstract: Accurate 3D medical image segmentation requires the integration of long-range anatomical context with fine boundary detail. Existing methods often model global and local features in separate modules or feature levels and perform channel recalibration independently. This may cause semantic mismatch between global context and local boundaries, insufficient channel relationship modeling, weak spatial-channel interaction, and redundant representations. We propose CDGC-Net, a 3D medical image segmentation network that combines cooperative dual-scale spatial attention with grouped hierarchical channel modeling. With-in each CDGC block, Cooperative Dual-Scale Self-Attention (CDSA) assigns attention heads to parallel local-window and global-sparse branches. The two branches capture fine spatial details and long-range anatomical context at the same feature level. Their outputs are concatenated into an $N\times C$ spatial representation and directly passed to Grouped Hierarchical Channel Attention (GHCA). GHCA organizes the channels into $r$ groups and models both within-group and cross-group dependencies. CDSA and GHCA reuse a shared key projection to maintain a consistent feature reference. Residual feature alignment subsequently integrates the refined features with the original representation. On the Synapse, ACDC, BraTS, and LA datasets, CDGC-Net achieved mean DSC values of 86.96\%, 92.91\%, 82.56\%, and 93.52\%, respectively, exceeding the next-highest reported values by 0.39, 0.47, 0.17, and 0.32 percentage points. CDGC-Net contains 25.83M parameters and 28.62G FLOPs for an input size of $64\times128\times128$, reducing these quantities by 39.87\% and 40.30\%, respectively, relative to UNETR++. These results indicate a favorable trade-off between segmentation accuracy and computational complexity.

</details>


### [113] [Where Is the Bee? Detecting Tiny Pollinators with a Single Collaborative-Head Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08580)
*Junsu Kim,Seungryul Baek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文针对CVPPA@ECCV 2026 BuzzSpot挑战赛，提出检测蜜蜂、熊蜂、食蚜蝇和蛾子的方法。通过比较10种检测器配置选定Swin-L骨干的Co-DINO，并采用crop-mosaic数据池微调提升罕见类标注比例（19.9%→55.1%）以及类加权单纯形等角紧框架（ETF）损失，最终以0.5062 mAP在FinalTest上排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战赛数据面临两个难点：目标框极小（中位数仅占帧面积0.16%），以及类别极度不均衡（蜜蜂占80%标签）。这两个问题分别影响小目标检测能力和罕见类别的学习效果。

Method: 1) 在留出关键帧上比较10种检测器配置，选择mAP最高的Co-DINO（Swin-L骨干）；2) 构建crop-mosaic池进行微调，将3个罕见类别的联合标注占比从19.9%提升到55.1%；3) 使用类加权单纯形等角紧框架（ETF）损失，将匹配的解码头查询投影状态拉向固定类方向；4) 训练分为12+3+2轮。

Result: 不使用推理时集成或测试时增强，在FinalTest上以0.5062 mAP@[.5:.95]排名第一。

Conclusion: 针对极小目标和类别不均衡的组合策略（选定合适检测器+数据重平衡+类加权ETF损失）在不依赖复杂推理增强的情况下，即可在该挑战赛中取得最优性能。

Abstract: The CVPPA@ECCV 2026 BuzzSpot Challenge asks us to detect bees, bumblebees, hoverflies, and moths in 1920x1080 field keyframes. Its annotations carry 2 difficulties: the median box occupies 0.16% of a frame, and bees account for 80% of the labels. To cope with the small boxes, we compare 10 recorded detector configurations on held-out keyframes; plain Co-DINO with a Swin-L backbone has the highest mAP in this comparison, so we select it. Training then addresses the bee dominance in 2 ways: fine-tuning on a crop-mosaic pool in which the combined annotation share of the 3 rare classes rises from 19.9% to 55.1%, and a class-weighted simplex equiangular tight frame (ETF) loss that pulls the projected states of matched decoder queries toward fixed class directions. The full schedule spans 12+3+2 epochs. Without inference-time ensembling or test-time augmentation, we rank first on FinalTest at 0.5062 mAP@[.5:.95].

</details>


### [114] [EvTrajGS: Accurate and Efficient 3D Gaussian Splatting from Unposed Event Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08585)
*Zixuan Chen,Jiakai Zhang,Junhao Dong,Guangcong Wang,Jianhuang Lai,Yew-Soon Ong,Xiaohua Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EvTrajGS，一个基于3D高斯泼溅的框架，用于无位姿事件流的稠密3D重建。通过将相机运动参数化为连续时间轨迹并对相邻轨迹状态进行时间耦合，实现了从粗位姿先验出发的高效联合位姿-场景优化，在保持计算效率的同时显著提升了重建质量和位姿精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于现成位姿估计的重建方法虽高效但重建保真度低（位姿初始化不准确导致累积误差），而SLAM式方法虽精度高但计算开销大。现有方法在效率和保真度之间存在难以调和的权衡，需要一种既能从粗位姿先验出发实现可靠联合优化，又无需昂贵SLAM流程的方法。

Method: 1) 将相机运动参数化为连续时间轨迹，从离散相机位姿初始化，为位姿精化提供统一表示；2) 聚合相邻轨迹状态为时间耦合位姿，促进联合优化中位姿更新的时间一致性；3) 引入损失重加权的事件采样策略，自适应地强调时间上欠重建的区间。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上，EvTrajGS在几何重建质量和位姿估计精度上均优于最先进方法：PSNR提升3.8 dB，SSIM提升0.1，ATE RMSE降低超过40%，同时保持高计算效率。

Conclusion: EvTrajGS成功解决了事件相机3D重建中效率与保真度之间的权衡问题，证明通过连续时间轨迹表示和时间耦合优化策略，可以从粗位姿先验实现高质量的联合位姿-场景优化，无需昂贵的SLAM式流程。

Abstract: Event cameras, with high temporal resolution, high dynamic range, and asynchronous sensing characteristics, have shown great potential for dense 3D reconstruction. Traditional reconstruction methods based on off-the-shelf pose estimates achieve high efficiency but produce low-fidelity results, as inaccurate pose initialization introduces cumulative reconstruction errors. In contrast, recent SLAM-style methods stabilize joint pose-scene optimization through incremental tracking and mapping, yielding higher reconstruction fidelity at the expense of considerable computational overhead. To address this trade-off, this paper presents EvTrajGS, an accurate and efficient 3D Gaussian Splatting framework for unposed event streams. Our method enables reliable joint pose-scene optimization initialized from coarse pose priors, eliminating the need for computationally expensive SLAM-style pipelines. EvTrajGS parameterizes camera motion as a continuous-time trajectory initialized from discrete camera poses, providing a unified representation for pose refinement. We then aggregate adjacent trajectory states into a temporally coupled pose, promoting temporally consistent pose updates during joint optimization. Additionally, we introduce a loss-reweighted event sampling strategy to adaptively emphasize temporally under-reconstructed intervals. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that EvTrajGS outperforms state-of-the-art methods in terms of both geometric reconstruction quality and pose estimation accuracy, achieving 3.8 dB higher PSNR, 0.1 higher SSIM, and over 40\% lower ATE RMSE while retaining high computational efficiency.

</details>


### [115] [RobustDefect-LLM: Explainable and Robustness-Aware Industrial Surface Defect Classification with Decision Support and AI-Assisted Reporting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08589)
*Nazlıcan Düşünmez,Halûk Gümüşkaya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RobustDefect-LLM，一个集成深度学习分类、可视化证据、置信度感知决策支持、受控AI辅助报告、可追溯存储和移动交互的工业表面缺陷检测框架。在NEU-DET六类数据集上评估四种迁移学习CNN，MobileNetV3-Large达到最高准确率99.26%，CPU推理速度16.66 FPS。模型在轻度合成退化下准确率降至87.78%，强退化下低于40%。置信度保守策略提供12.22%自动覆盖率，100%选择性准确率。报告生成全部通过一致性检查，平均延迟1.66秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业表面缺陷检测需要统一的质量控制工作流，现有方法缺乏将深度学习分类、可解释性证据、置信度感知决策支持和报告生成集成到一起的完整框架，且对模型在图像退化条件下的鲁棒性评估不足。

Method: 集成四部分：1)四种迁移学习CNN（ResNet50、EfficientNet-B0、DenseNet121、MobileNetV3-Large）在1799张NEU-DET图像上评估；2)Grad-CAM提供视觉证据；3)置信度低于0.90或top-2边际低于0.10的预测路由至人工审核；4)AI辅助报告生成。同时进行合成图像退化鲁棒性测试、bootstrap置信区间和McNemar统计检验。

Result: MobileNetV3-Large达到99.26%测试准确率和0.9926宏F1分数，bootstrap 95% CI为0.9815-1.0000，与DenseNet121无显著差异（p=1.000）。CPU前向传播平均0.060秒（16.66 FPS）。轻度合成退化下准确率降至87.78%，强退化下低于40%。置信度策略提供12.22%自动覆盖率，33个合格案例100%选择性准确率（95% CI: 89.43%-100.00%），两个观察到的分类误差均被路由至审核。100份报告全部通过确定性一致性检查，平均延迟1.66秒。

Conclusion: 结果表明集成工作流具备可行性，但需强调模型需要校准、重复评估和真实工业环境验证。作者将"鲁棒性感知"定义为在受控图像退化下的显式评估和置信度感知审核路由，而非内在鲁棒性保证。

Abstract: This paper presents RobustDefect-LLM, an industrial surface-defect inspection framework integrating deep-learning classification, operator-facing visual evidence, confidence-aware decision support, controlled AI-assisted reporting, traceable storage, and mobile interaction in a unified quality-control workflow. Here, robustness-aware denotes explicit evaluation under controlled image degradation and confidence-aware review routing, not an intrinsic robustness guarantee. Four transfer-learning-based convolutional neural networks, ResNet50, EfficientNet-B0, DenseNet121, and MobileNetV3-Large, were evaluated on 1,799 images from the six-class NEU-DET dataset using fixed training, validation, and held-out in-domain test partitions. MobileNetV3-Large achieved the highest numerical test accuracy (99.26%) and macro F1-score (0.9926), with a bootstrap 95% accuracy CI of 0.9815-1.0000. An exact paired McNemar test found no significant difference from DenseNet121 (p = 1.000). The selected model averaged 0.060 s per CPU forward pass (16.66 FPS). Under combined synthetic degradation, accuracy fell to 87.78% at mild intensity and below 40% at stronger intensities, revealing sensitivity to severe image-quality deterioration. Grad-CAM supplied visual evidence, while predictions with confidence below 0.90 or a top-2 margin below 0.10 were routed to HUMAN REVIEW. This conservative policy provided 12.22% automatic coverage and 100% observed selective accuracy among 33 eligible cases (95% CI: 89.43%-100.00%), while routing both observed classification errors to review. Under nominal controlled conditions, all 100 generated reports passed deterministic consistency checks, with a mean latency of 1.66 s. Results support the feasibility of the integrated workflow while emphasizing the need for calibration, repeated evaluation, and real-world industrial validation.

</details>


### [116] [Goal-oriented Navigation Instruction Generation with Tour Video Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08596)
*Fangdi Li,Juncheng Liao,Changxu Cheng,Jiazhi Wang,Senda Chen,Tao Wang,Wuyue Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VideoNIG，一个目标导向的视频驱动的导航指令生成任务，从第一视角游览视频、初始观察和文本/视觉目标生成导航指令，不依赖图或地图等中间表示。作者构建了包含60K游览视频和37K多模态提示的基准测试，并提出了结合文本相似度、空间一致性测试和下游导航执行的诊断评估协议。为解决该任务，他们提出两阶段课程学习框架（Action Warmup + Complexity Progression），实验表明现有MLLMs在此任务上表现困难，而该方法显著提升指令质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导航指令生成研究主要将其视为VLN的辅助任务，用于数据增强或多任务学习。但仅从紧凑的环境先验生成导航指令需要精细的空间推理，特别是当目标路线不简单地跟随演示游览时，这对当前多模态模型仍具有挑战性。作者旨在填补这一空白，提出一个更接近真实应用场景的独立导航指令生成任务。

Method: 1) 提出VideoNIG任务：从第一视角游览视频、初始观察和文本/视觉目标生成导航指令；2) 在受控模拟器基准中构建60K游览视频和37K多模态提示，并设置渐进难度级别；3) 提出两阶段课程学习框架：先进行Action Warmup（空间动作-视角对齐），再进行Complexity Progression（使用递增探索难度的轨迹学习）；4) 设计结合文本相似度、基于选择的空间一致性测试和下游导航执行的诊断评估协议。

Result: 实验表明现有多模态大语言模型在VideoNIG上表现不佳，而所提出的两阶段课程学习框架在互补的诊断指标上显著提升了指令质量。此外，将VideoNIG生成的指令与VLN代理集成，验证了该任务形式化对端到端导航的可执行性。

Conclusion: VideoNIG提出了一种更自然、更接近实际应用的导航指令生成范式，摆脱了对图、地图等中间表示的依赖。课程学习策略有效缓解了MLLMs在长距离空间推理上的不足，且生成的指令可实际执行于下游导航任务，为NIG和VLN领域提供了新的研究方向和基准。

Abstract: Navigation Instruction Generation (NIG) aims to produce step-by-step natural language instructions for navigation guidance. Existing studies primarily treat NIG as an auxiliary task for vision-andlanguage navigation (VLN), focusing on data augmentation or multi-task learning. However, generating navigation instructions from compact environmental priors requires meticulous spatial reasoning, especially when the target route does not simply follow the demonstrated tour, and remains challenging for current multimodal models. In this work, we introduce VideoNIG, a goal-oriented video-grounded NIG task that generates navigation instructions from ego-centric tour videos, an initial observation, and a textual or visual goal, without relying on intermediate representations such as graphs and maps. We instantiate VideoNIG in a controlled simulator benchmark with 60K tour videos across continuous indoor environments and 37K multimodal prompts with progressive difficulty levels. We further introduce a diagnostic evaluation protocol that combines text similarity, choice-based spatial consistency tests, and downstream navigation execution. To address this task, we propose a two-stage Curriculum Learning framework that decomposes the learning into foundational motion perception and long-horizon navigation reasoning. Specifically, we first employ Action Warmup for spatial action-view alignment, followed by Complexity Progression using trajectories with increasing exploratory difficulty. Extensive experiments show that existing MLLMs struggle with VideoNIG, while our approach significantly improves instruction quality across complementary diagnostic metrics. Finally, integrating VideoNIG-generated instructions with a VLN agent demonstrates the executability of this task formulation for end-to-end navigation.

</details>


### [117] [Population-Scalable Multi-Agent World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08600)
*Renjie Zhao,Yuxiang Wu,Mingyu Zhang,Jiaxin Li,Sisi Li,Yimin Sheng,Tianxi Tan,Zhenkai Zhang,Jianyi Zhu,Yong-Lu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Khora是一个可扩展的多智能体世界模型，通过将世界状态演化与视觉渲染解耦，实现了推理时向任意数量智能体的扩展，无需重新训练，并保持跨视角一致性和视觉质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型在多智能体环境中面临可扩展性挑战：传统方法在训练和推理时假设固定数量的智能体，限制了模型在推理时的可扩展性，无法泛化到未见过的智能体数量。

Method: 核心洞察是跨视角一致性应来源于共享世界状态（其演化不假设预定义数量的智能体），而智能体特定的观测应通过统一的渲染接口查询该状态生成。框架将世界状态演化与视觉渲染解耦，引入群体无关的渲染机制来整合其他智能体信息，通过共享世界状态而非观测流之间的密集交互来维持跨视角一致性。

Result: 该方法能够泛化到未见过的智能体数量，同时保持视觉质量和多智能体一致性，实际计算复杂度随查询视角数量近似线性扩展，并实现了实时交互系统以支持可扩展的开放世界模拟。

Conclusion: Khora通过解耦世界状态演化与视觉渲染的设计，有效解决了多智能体世界模型的可扩展性问题，为开放世界模拟和实时交互应用提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: World models have recently achieved impressive progress in visual prediction and interactive generation, but extending them to multi-agent environments introduces a fundamental scalability challenge. Existing methods generally assume a fixed number of agents during training and inference, which ties the model to a pre-determined agent population and limits inference-time scalability. Our key insight is that cross-view consistency should arise from a shared world state whose evolution does not assume a predefined number of agents, while agent-specific observations should be generated by querying this state through a unified rendering interface. Based on this insight, we propose Khora, a scalable multi-agent world model that supports inference-time expansion to arbitrary numbers of agents without retraining. Our framework decouples world-state evolution from visual rendering and introduces a population-agnostic rendering mechanism for incorporating other agent information. This design maintains cross-view consistency through the shared world state rather than through dense interactions among observation streams inside the expensive video generator, enabling approximately linear practical scaling with the number of queried views. Qualitative experiments demonstrate that our approach generalizes to unseen numbers of agents while maintaining visual quality and multi-agent consistency. We further implement a real-time interactive system to demonstrate scalable open-world simulation.

</details>


### [118] [REVEAL: A Rubric-Guided Agent for Explicit Evidence Sufficiency Verificationin Long-Video Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08612)
*Caijun Yan,Yang Zhou,Meixing Shi,Haoran Sun,Yichen Li,Yuxiang Cai,Yankai Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出REVEAL，一个基于规则引导的智能体框架，用于长视频问答。它通过自适应的视觉相似性预处理将相邻帧聚类为自然事件单元，构建离线-在线混合视频记忆，并利用自动构建的规则库显式验证检索证据的充分性，在证据不足时精准定位缺失线索并触发定向再检索。无需额外训练，REVEAL在广泛实验中持续优于闭源和开源的最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强和记忆增强方法在长视频问答中依赖固定长度的时态分块和静态离线记忆库，这会割裂连续事件且无法在实时推理中自适应；同时现有方法只追求检索相关性而忽视证据充分性，导致关键的时间、因果或细粒度动作证据缺失时仍然停止回答。

Method: REVEAL由两部分组成：(1) 自适应的视觉相似性预处理管道，将视觉上连贯的相邻帧聚合成自然事件单元，构建离线-在线混合视频记忆，其中离线部分捕获全局视频上下文，在线部分动态维护与问题相关的记忆；(2) 自动构建的规则库，用于显式验证检索到的证据是否满足充分性标准，在验证失败时定位缺失线索并定向进行补充检索。

Result: 在广泛的实验中，REVEAL无需任何额外训练，持续优于闭源和开源的现有最先进方法，证明显式验证证据充分性而非仅停留在语义相关性，能够检索到先前方法遗漏的关键线索并实现更可靠的长视频推理。

Conclusion: 显式验证证据充分性是长视频问答中提高推理可靠性的关键，REVEAL通过事件级记忆组织和规则引导的证据验证机制，成功克服了传统方法中证据不充分和事件碎片化的问题。

Abstract: Recently, retrieval-augmented and memory-augmented methods have emerged as two promising paradigms for long-video question answering. However, existing methods typically rely on rigid, fixed-length temporal chunking (e.g., 10s) and static offline memory banks, which not only fragment coherent continuous events but also fail to adapt during real-time reasoning. Moreover, whether using multi-scale summaries or multimodal knowledge graphs, current approaches prioritize retrieval relevance while overlooking evidence sufficiency, often stopping to answer once only semantically relevant clues are retrieved, even when key temporal, causal, or fine-grained action evidence is still missing. To tackle these challenges, we propose REVEAL, a rubric-guided agent framework. As a foundation, we introduce an adaptive visual-similarity-based preprocessing pipeline that groups visually coherent adjacent frames into natural event units to construct an offline-online video memory---capturing global video context offline while dynamically maintaining question-conditioned memory online. Built upon this structured memory, REVEAL uses an automatically constructed rubric library to explicitly verify whether retrieved evidence satisfies sufficiency criteria, pinpoints missing clues upon verification failure, and directs targeted re-retrieval for complementary information. Without any extra training, REVEAL consistently outperforms both closed-source and open-source state-of-the-art methods across extensive experiments. These results show that explicitly verifying evidence sufficiency, rather than stopping at semantic relevance, retrieves the decisive clues that prior methods miss and yields more reliable long-video reasoning.

</details>


### [119] [VADER: Adaptive Debiasing for Hallucination Mitigation in Video Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08622)
*Dong Xing,Jiaxin Chen,Hang Yang,Peixun Liu,Qiushi Yang,Yuqing Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VADER，一个无需训练的框架，通过视觉焦点重分配和选择性证据擦除两种互补模块来缓解大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）在视频理解中产生无证据支持的流畅错误回答的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无需训练的方法要么采用全局固定的视觉干预，无法适应视频依赖的融合路径；要么通过输入扰动构建对比分支，但容易被跨帧冗余所补偿。因此需要一种能够自适应视频内容且难以被跨帧信息弥补的去偏方法。

Method: 提出VADER框架，包含两个互补模块：（1）视觉焦点重分配（VFR）：为每个视频-问题输入自动实例化干预策略，诊断逐层的视觉到文本证据流，确定干预位置，并重新分配softmax前的注意力权重，从系统token转向视频token块；（2）选择性证据擦除（SEE）：独立地在每一帧中掩蔽高重要性视觉token，构建一个难以通过邻近帧补偿的先验偏置分支，然后通过对比解码降低在证据擦除后仍然保持高置信度的预测的权重。

Result: 在多个VideoLLM上，VADER在事件级定位和时间一致性方面取得了显著改进；在LLaVA-Video-7B上，事件幻觉基准（EventHallusion）达到了72.60%的准确率。

Conclusion: VADER通过视频自适应的干预策略和难以补偿的证据擦除机制，有效降低了LVLMs在视频理解中因先验偏差而产生的不忠实响应，提升了模型对视频证据的依赖和事实准确性。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) have demonstrated strong performance in open-ended video understanding, yet they remain prone to fluent responses unsupported by video evidence. Existing training-free methods typically apply a globally fixed visual intervention or construct a contrastive branch through input perturbation. The former cannot accommodate video-dependent fusion paths, while the latter can be compensated by cross-frame redundancy. We therefore propose Video-Adaptive Debiasing via Evidence Reweighting (VADER), a training-free framework with two complementary modules. Visual Focus Reallocation (VFR) automatically instantiates an intervention policy for each video-question input: it diagnoses layer-wise visual-to-text evidence flow, determines where to intervene, and derives how strongly to reallocate pre-softmax attention from system-token to video-token blocks. Selective Evidence Erasure (SEE) independently masks high-importance visual tokens in every frame, constructing a prior-biased branch that is difficult to compensate through neighboring frames. Contrastive decoding then down-weights predictions that remain confident after selective evidence erasure. Across multiple VideoLLMs, VADER yields substantial improvements on event-level grounding and temporal consistency; on LLaVA-Video-7B, it reaches 72.60% accuracy on EventHallusion.

</details>


### [120] [VLZip: Unified Visual and Textual Compression for Interleaved Long-Context Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08630)
*Yuqi Zhang,Cheng Chen,Yuyu Guo,Wenjie Yang,Lingchen Meng,Peng Di,Hang Yu,Zuxuan Wu,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VLZip是一个统一视觉与文本压缩的长上下文多模态框架，通过层级化将视觉和文本片段蒸馏为层特定的"软前缀"注入解码器各层，从而大幅缩短注意力序列并保留细粒度全局上下文。作者还提出LongVLBench基准，实验表明VLZip支持120K训练、280K+推理及最高2M token的记忆扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在处理超长交错图文序列时面临自注意力的二次复杂度问题。现有方案要么激进剪枝token导致不可逆信息损失，要么采用高效但不精确的架构，且普遍忽视了文本组件同样重要，缺乏对长上下文多模态推理的充分评估。

Method: VLZip在纯Transformer框架内统一视觉与文本压缩。核心机制是层级化地将视觉和文本片段蒸馏为紧凑的、层特定的"软前缀"，并将这些前缀注入每个解码器层的隐藏状态中，从而在显著缩短注意力序列的同时保留细粒度全局上下文。同时提出LongVLBench基准，基于视频叙事构建，要求整体叙事级推理。

Result: VLZip在长上下文多模态推理上达到领先性能；训练支持120K tokens（为基线的6倍）；推理可超过280K tokens且内存显著降低；记忆可扩展至2M tokens。在极端上下文长度下，现有方法崩溃而VLZip仍表现优异。

Conclusion: VLZip通过统一视觉与文本压缩，在保持高保真推理的同时实现了极高的上下文扩展能力，为长上下文多模态AI建立了高效且强大的新标准。

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) face significant challenges with ultra-long, interleaved image-text sequences due to the quadratic complexity of self-attention. Current solutions either resort to aggressive token pruning, risking irreversible information loss, or adopt efficient but less precise architectures, while largely ignoring the equally vital textual component. We introduce VLZip, a framework that unifies visual and textual compression for high-fidelity reasoning within a pure Transformer. At its core, VLZip hierarchically distills visual and textual segments into compact, layer-specific "soft prefixes" and injects them into each decoder layer's hidden states, drastically shortening the attention sequence while preserving fine-grained global context. To address deficient evaluations in the field, we also introduce LongVLBench, a new benchmark derived from video narratives that demands holistic, narrative-level reasoning. Extensive experiments show VLZip achieves leading performance on long-context multimodal reasoning, enabling training up to 120K tokens, a 6x increase over the baseline, and inference beyond 280K tokens with significantly reduced memory, while demonstrating the memory scalability to handle up to 2M tokens. By excelling at extreme context lengths where existing methods collapse, VLZip establishes an efficient and powerful new standard for long-context multimodal AI. Code is available at https://github.com/ShareLab-SII/VLZip.

</details>


### [121] [Agentic Visual Reasoning in Whole-Slide Pathology Images via Active Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08648)
*Jingyun Chen,Fengchun Liu,Linghan Cai,Songhan Jiang,Shenjin Huang,Hongpeng Wang,Lequan Yu,Yongbing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AdaptivePath是一个主动感知框架，将全玻片病理图像（WSI）的证据获取建模为序贯决策问题，通过学习病理学家标注指导的导航策略，在有限观察预算下从低到高倍率分层获取稀疏关键区域，结合形态学解释器、评议器和仲裁器完成问题条件化的视觉推理，在WSI和区域病理VQA基准上达到最优零样本性能，并在癌症亚型分类和临床诊断实用性研究中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WSI方法存在两方面问题：一是将密集采样的补丁压缩为全局表征，弱化了预测与形态学特征之间的联系；二是依赖预训练视觉-语言模型进行启发式区域选择，缺乏病理学训练的观察策略。因此需要一个能够主动、可解释地获取稀疏诊断证据的框架。

Method: AdaptivePath包含三个核心组件：(1) Navigator——学习与问题无关的异常驱动导航策略，通过交替表征学习和近端策略优化（PPO）训练，并结合几何与外观一致性目标微调以稳定焦点轨迹；(2) Morphology Interpreter——将采样的观察转换为问题条件化的证据；(3) Deliberator——跨倍率评估证据并修正中间答案；(4) Arbiter——整合审议历史生成最终答案。推理时导航器在有限ROI预算下从低到高倍率分层获取稀疏观察。

Result: AdaptivePath在WSI和区域病理VQA基准上达到最先进的零样本性能；在六个TCGA队列的癌症亚型分类中达到80.14%准确率；在盲法诊断实用性研究中，病理学家使用AdaptivePath选定的观察序列达到82.9%准确率。

Conclusion: 学习到的主动感知机制能够在千兆像素级病理玻片上实现有效且可追溯的视觉推理，验证了将证据获取建模为序贯决策的可行性，并展示了其在临床诊断辅助中的实用价值。

Abstract: Whole-slide visual reasoning requires identifying sparse diagnostic evidence in gigapixel pathology slides and integrating observations across spatial scales. Existing WSI methods either compress densely sampled patches into global representations or use pretrained vision-language models with heuristic region selection, weakening links between predictions and morphology or lacking pathology-trained observation policies. We present AdaptivePath, an active-perception framework that formulates WSI evidence acquisition as sequential decision making. The Navigator learns question-agnostic abnormality-driven navigation from pathologist-reviewed labels to select observation locations and spatial extents, avoiding costly question-specific trajectory annotations. We train this policy through alternating representation learning and proximal policy optimization, followed by fine-tuning with geometric and appearance consistency objectives to stabilize focus trajectories. During inference, the Navigator hierarchically acquires sparse observations from low to high magnification under a limited ROI budget. A Morphology Interpreter converts observations into question-conditioned evidence, while the Deliberator evaluates evidence and revises intermediate answers across magnifications. The Arbiter integrates deliberation history to produce final answers. AdaptivePath achieves state-of-the-art zero-shot performance on WSI and region pathology VQA benchmarks and reaches 80.14% accuracy for cancer subtype classification across six TCGA cohorts. In a blinded diagnostic-utility study, pathologists using AdaptivePath-selected observation sequences achieve 82.9% accuracy. These results demonstrate that learned active perception enables effective and traceable visual reasoning over gigapixel pathology slides.

</details>


### [122] [JSGS: JPEG State-Guided Supervision for 3D Gaussian Splatting from Mixed-Quality Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08659)
*Jinhua Cui,Anhong Wang,Kai Hu,Donghan Bu,Peihao Li,Tammam Tillo,Hao Jing,Shiao Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出JSGS方法，针对混合质量JPEG图像输入下3D高斯泼溅重建质量退化的问题。JSGS利用JPEG文件中的量化和色度量化表构建视角特定的JPEG观测算子，对渲染视图进行编解码以与解码后的输入图像进行域匹配比较。通过在低频段锚定粗结构、在中频段加权重分配监督，并引导高斯控制器正则化不一致区域的小型高不透明度基元，JSGS在7个场景和3种混合质量调度下取得最低LPIPS和最高SSIM，同时以约150 FPS渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准3DGS假设所有输入图像都能忠实采样场景辐射，但混合质量的JPEG图像因压缩导致的块效应和振铃伪影会破坏跨视图共享高斯的更新，从而降低重建质量。

Method: 1) 利用JPEG存储的亮度和色度量化表构建视角特定的JPEG观测算子，对渲染视图进行编码-解码以实现与输入图像的域匹配比较；2) 在低频段使用损失锚定粗结构；3) 在中频段使用亮度量化表提供的连续权重重新分配DCT坐标上的监督；4) 利用块不一致性指导高斯控制器正则化不一致区域中的小型高不透明度基元。

Result: 在七个场景和三种混合质量调度下，JSGS在每种调度下均达到最低的平均LPIPS和最高的平均SSIM，同时渲染速度约为150 FPS。

Conclusion: JSGS通过领域匹配的JPEG感知监督有效提升了混合质量视图下3DGS的重建质量，且保持了实时渲染性能。

Abstract: Standard 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) assumes that every input image faithfully samples scene radiance. However, mixed-quality JPEG images violate this assumption because compression-induced blocking and ringing artifacts can corrupt updates to Gaussians shared across views. To address this problem, we propose JPEG State-Guided Supervision for 3D Gaussian Splatting from Mixed-Quality Views (JSGS). JSGS uses luminance and chrominance quantization tables stored in each JPEG file to construct a view-specific JPEG observation operator. This operator encodes and decodes each rendered view for domain-matched comparison with the corresponding decoded input image. The luminance quantization table supplies continuous weights within a fixed middle frequency band. A loss in the low frequency band anchors coarse structure, while the weighted middle frequency loss redistributes supervision among the selected DCT coordinates. The resulting block disagreement also guides the Gaussian Controller to regularize small primitives with high opacity in disagreement regions. Across seven scenes and three mixed-quality schedules, JSGS achieves the lowest mean LPIPS and the highest mean SSIM under every schedule while rendering at approximately 150 FPS. Code: https://github.com/Jayden-Cui/JSGS.

</details>


### [123] [Degradation-Guided Underwater Image Restoration with Task-Oriented Latent Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08661)
*Xu Zhang,Xuhui Cao,Kangzhe Yuan,Laibin Chang,Yichu Xu,Shi Chen,Huan Zhang,Yong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PROTEUS，一种水下图像复原方法，通过耦合退化引导的特征适应与任务导向的潜在控制来解决退化信息在水下图像中的双重角色问题。PROTEUS从特征层面（引导动态特征调制模块）和表示层面（任务导向潜在控制器）两个互补视角出发，在五个配对和四个非参考水下基准上取得了极具竞争力的复原性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水下图像复原方法忽视了退化信息的双重作用：一方面退化信息的空间和光谱线索可用于指导自适应复原，另一方面退化纠缠的特征在解码过程中可能未经调控地传播。现有方法要么未充分利用退化线索，要么直接通过跳跃连接转发编码器特征，缺乏对退化特征的显式调控。

Method: PROTEUS从两个互补视角解决该问题：(1) 特征层面：引导动态特征调制模块（Guided Dynamic Feature Modulation Block）利用空间变化的退化线索来跨网络阶段自适应调整特征处理；(2) 表示层面：任务导向潜在控制器（task-oriented latent controller）在判别性正则化下学习结构化控制码，并用于跳跃连接特征的通道级调制，无需控制码形成度量上更干净的嵌入。

Result: 在五个配对基准和四个非参考水下基准上的大量实验表明，PROTEUS取得了极具竞争力的复原性能，在复原质量与计算成本之间保持了良好的平衡。

Conclusion: PROTEUS通过退化引导的特征适应与任务导向潜在控制的结合，有效解决了水下图像复原中退化信息的双重角色问题，在性能与效率之间取得了有利的权衡。

Abstract: Degradation information in underwater images plays a dual role: its spatial and spectral cues can guide adaptive restoration, while degradation-entangled features may be propagated without explicit regulation during decoding. Existing methods largely overlook this dual role, either underexploiting degradation cues or directly forwarding encoder features through skip connections. To address this issue, we propose PROTEUS, which couples degradation-guided feature adaptation with task?oriented latent control. PROTEUS tackles this problem from two complementary perspectives. At the feature level, the Guided Dynamic Feature Modulation Block exploits spatially varying degradation cues to adapt feature processing across network stages. At the representation level, the task-oriented latent controller learns a structured control code under discriminative regularisation and uses it for channel-wise modulation of skip features, without requiring the code to form a metrically cleaner embedding. Extensive experiments on five paired and four non-reference underwater benchmarks demonstrate that PROTEUS achieves highly competitive restoration performance, with a favourable balance between restoration quality and computational cost.

</details>


### [124] [FiRe: Fixed-Noise Refinement for Visual Counterfactual Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08664)
*Yan Zeng,Changlu Guo,Oskar Kristoffersen,Anders Nymark Christensen,Morten Rieger Hannemose,Anders Bjorholm Dahl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FiRe提出了一种固定噪声精炼框架，用于视觉反事实解释，通过将输入映射到固定噪声水平并迭代精炼，结合Pixel Mean Flow直接预测干净图像，配合动态双掩码、自适应引导和早停三种控制机制，实现比现有方法快3倍推理速度、少8倍FLOPs的同时保持或超越最先进的反事实编辑质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于DDPM的反事实解释方法沿长反向去噪轨迹进行分类器引导编辑，存在两个核心问题：1）变化的噪声水平使语义可编辑性和空间控制难以平衡；2）可编辑状态是带噪的，而目标分类器在干净图像上训练，导致需要昂贵的递归去噪或低质量的一步估计才能获得分类器可用的干净图像。

Method: FiRe框架包含三个关键组件：1）将输入映射到固定噪声水平并在此水平上迭代精炼噪声状态，而非沿去噪轨迹；2）将Pixel Mean Flow适配到视觉反事实解释任务，实现从噪声状态直接预测干净图像以供分类器引导；3）引入三种FiRe特有的控制机制：动态双掩码（决定编辑在哪里累积）、自适应引导（决定哪些变化可见）和早停（决定精炼何时停止），以产生最小化和局部化的反事实编辑。

Result: 在三个数据集的五个任务上，与最强近期基线相比，FiRe实现了约3倍更快的在线推理速度和8倍更少的FLOPs，同时获得相当或最优的反事实编辑质量。

Conclusion: FiRe通过固定噪声精炼范式有效解决了DDPM反事实编辑中噪声水平变化带来的编辑与分类器引导之间的不匹配问题，在显著提升计算效率的同时保持了高质量、局部化和最小化的反事实编辑效果，为视觉反事实解释提供了更实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Visual counterfactual explanations aim to change classifier decisions through realistic and localized edits while preserving decision-irrelevant content. Existing DDPM-based methods typically perform classifier-guided editing along a long reverse denoising trajectory. The changing noise levels make semantic editability and spatial control difficult to balance, and the editable state is noisy, whereas the target classifier is trained on clean images. As a result, these methods require either costly recursive denoising or low-quality one-step estimates to obtain classifier-facing clean images. We propose FiRe, a Fixed-noise Refinement framework for visual counterfactual explanations. Rather than following a reverse denoising trajectory, FiRe maps the input to a fixed noise level and iteratively refines the noisy state at that level. To provide clean images for classifier guidance, FiRe first adapts Pixel Mean Flow to visual counterfactual explanation, enabling direct clean-image prediction from noisy states. To make fixed-noise refinement produce minimal and localized counterfactual edits, FiRe introduces three FiRe-specific controls: a dynamic dual-mask strategy, adaptive guidance, and early stopping, which determine where edits accumulate, which changes become visible, and when refinement stops. Experiments on five tasks across three datasets show that, compared with the strongest recent baseline, FiRe achieves about 3$\times$ faster online inference and 8$\times$ fewer FLOPs while obtaining comparable or state-of-the-art counterfactual quality.

</details>


### [125] [UniSpace: Unified Visual Representation and Scalable Multimodal Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08676)
*Jinbo Yan,Limeng Qiao,Jie Qin,Junyan He,Feize Wu,Guanglu Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Patch Reparameterization方法，通过为预训练的语义ViT添加重建感知的patch嵌入，在不改变其语义通路的前提下保留细粒度视觉信息，从而在单一视觉表示空间中统一实现理解、生成和编辑任务，并进一步扩展为80亿参数的UniSpace模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义视觉编码器的最终token丢弃了细粒度视觉细节，导致像素级重建效果差，限制了其在图像生成和编辑等重建敏感任务中的应用。作者质疑是否可以在单一视觉表示空间中同时建模理解、生成和编辑。

Method: 作者发现语义ViT的冻结Transformer块本身并非无法保留视觉细节，问题出在原始的patch参数化方式驱动了语义抽象。基于此，提出Patch Reparameterization：保持原有语义通路不变，同时添加重建感知的patch嵌入，为相同的冻结ViT块提供细粒度视觉信息。进一步将该表示扩展为UniSpace——一个80亿参数的Mixture-of-Transformer-Experts模型。

Result: 该统一表示在保持多模态理解能力的同时，实现了高保真图像重建和良好的重建-生成权衡。UniSpace无需单独的VAE通路即可在同一视觉空间中执行理解、生成和编辑任务。系统级评估展示了实用的文本到图像生成和基于指令的图像编辑能力。

Conclusion: 重新参数化的预训练ViT可以作为可扩展多模态建模的统一视觉接口，证明理解、生成和编辑可以在单一视觉表示空间中共存，无需传统上分离的自编码器通路。

Abstract: Semantic vision encoders have become a central visual interface for multimodal understanding and semantic conditioning in image generation. However, their final tokens discard fine-grained visual details, leading to poor pixel reconstruction and limiting their use in reconstruction-sensitive tasks such as image generation and editing. In this work, we ask whether understanding, generation, and editing can be modeled in a single visual representation space built from a pretrained semantic ViT. We show that the frozen Transformer blocks of a semantic ViT are not intrinsically unable to preserve visual details. Instead, the original patch parameterization drives the representation toward semantic abstraction, making fine-grained information difficult to recover from the final tokens. Based on this observation, we introduce \emph{Patch Reparameterization}, which preserves the original semantic pathway while adding a reconstruction-aware patch embedding that provides fine-grained visual information to the same frozen ViT blocks. The resulting unified representation preserves multimodal understanding while enabling high-fidelity image reconstruction and a favorable reconstruction--generation trade-off. We further scale this representation into \emph{UniSpace}, an 8B Mixture-of-Transformer-Experts model that performs understanding, generation, and editing in the same visual space without a separate VAE pathway. System-level evaluations demonstrate practical text-to-image generation and instruction-based image editing, showing that a reparameterized pretrained ViT can serve as a unified visual interface for scalable multimodal modeling.

</details>


### [126] [Semi-Dense Matching Uncertainty Is Not Just Local Confidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08685)
*Khoa Hoang,Hoang-Tuan Nguyen,Huong Ninh,Hai Tran,Long Q. Tran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级、事后（post-hoc）的整体不确定性估计框架，仅需9个可学习参数即可构建双分量校准拉普拉斯混合模型，同时刻画局部精炼噪声与粗分配失败的尾部误差，并引入CoRe几何重拟合模块，显著提升下游几何估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有半稠密匹配方法基于粗到细范式，但在粗分配失败时无法提供良好量化的不确定性，导致截断误差分布和几何估计严重偏差。已有方法忽视了这类灾难性粗分配失败问题，需要一种能够显式捕获该尾部误差的不确定性估计方案。

Method: 提出一个双分量校准拉普拉斯混合模型（仅9个参数）作为轻量级事后不确定性估计框架；同时提出Coarse-success posterior Refit（CoRe）模块，利用粗分配成功的后验概率作为软对应权重进行几何重拟合。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在多种预训练匹配器和鲁棒估计器上，以极小的计算开销持续提升下游几何精度。

Conclusion: 所提框架能以极低参数代价有效量化和修正粗分配失败带来的不确定性，提升几何估计的可靠性，且具有通用性和即插即用性。

Abstract: Reliable semi-dense matching is essential for modern geometric vision systems. Designed under a coarse-to-fine paradigm, it achieves an optimal balance between performance and computational cost. However, existing methods often struggle to provide well-quantified uncertainties, where catastrophic coarse-assignment failures are ignored, leading to truncated error distributions and severely misjudged geometric estimations. In this paper, we propose a lightweight, post-hoc overall uncertainty estimation framework that introduces a two-component calibrated Laplace mixture model with only 9 learnable parameters. The objective is to explicitly capture both the sharp local refinement noise and the broader tail of coarse-assignment failures. We introduce the Coarse-success posterior Refit (CoRe) method, a geometric refitting module that utilizes the posterior probability of coarse-assignment success as soft correspondence weights. Extensive experiments show that our method consistently improves downstream geometric accuracy across various pretrained-only matchers and robust estimators with minimal computational overhead. Our code is available at https://github.com/khoavpt/Probabilistic-matching.

</details>


### [127] [CUPA-T2*: Covariance-Aware Uncertainty Propagation and Alignment for T2* Mapping in Accelerated MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08693)
*Gideon N. L. Rouwendaal,Natascha Niessen,Hannah Eichhorn,Dirk H. J. Poot,Christine Preibisch,Julia A. Schnabel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CUPA-T2*框架，通过随机蒙特卡洛dropout重建获得体素级跨回波不确定性，并利用协方差感知采样将其显式传播到下游T2*拟合中，实现不确定性感知的定量T2*映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定量T2*图在生物标志物发现中有巨大潜力，但扫描时间长限制了临床应用。虽然k空间欠采样结合学习重建可加速，但重建伪影和噪声会传播到下游T2*拟合中，降低准确性。

Method: CUPA-T2*框架：(1) 通过随机蒙特卡洛dropout重建获取体素级跨回波不确定性；(2) 利用协方差感知采样将不确定性显式传播到T2*拟合；(3) 使用异方差MLP进行T2*拟合，并引入基于相关性的正则化器，使预测方差与重建不确定性对齐。

Result: 在加速脑MRI数据上，CUPA-T2*在整体T2*拟合上表现具有竞争力，在高加速倍数下改善白质性能。与异方差基线相比，显著提高了重建不确定性与预测T2*方差之间的对齐度，但揭示了与校准(ECE)和选择性预测(AURC)性能之间的权衡。

Conclusion: CUPA-T2*实现了重建不确定性感知的T2*拟合，提供体素级不确定性图以支持定量T2*估计的解释，尽管存在不确定性对齐与校准/选择性预测之间的权衡。

Abstract: Quantitative T2* maps have strong potential for biomarker discovery but are limited by long scan times, rendering them impractical in clinical settings. Significant acceleration can be achieved through undersampling in k-space combined with learning-based reconstruction. However, reconstruction artifacts and noise can propagate into downstream T2* fitting, degrading its accuracy. We introduce CUPA-T2*, a framework that explicitly propagates voxel-wise inter-echo uncertainty from stochastic Monte Carlo dropout reconstructions to downstream T2* fitting via covariance-aware sampling. T2* fitting is performed with a heteroscedastic MLP and a correlation-based regularizer that encourages alignment between predicted variance and reconstruction uncertainty. Experiments on accelerated brain MRI data show tissue-dependent behavior: CUPA-T2* achieves competitive overall T2* fitting performance and improves white-matter performance at higher accelerations. Compared with a heteroscedastic baseline, the proposed framework substantially increases alignment between reconstruction uncertainty and predicted T2* variance, while also revealing a trade-off with calibration (ECE) and selective prediction performance (AURC). CUPA-T2* enables reconstruction uncertainty-aware T2* fitting and delivers voxel-wise uncertainty maps to support the interpretation of quantitative T2* estimates.

</details>


### [128] [OccAnyScene: Towards Unified Indoor-Outdoor 3D Occupancy Predictio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08696)
*Junjie Liu,Wanshui Gan,Zitong Dai,Guiping Cao,Yan Li,Ke Chen,Dongmei Jiang,Xiangyuan Lan,Jianguo Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了跨场景3D语义占用预测任务，并引入OccAnyScene框架，通过像素-截锥体中心的高斯表示，实现了单一模型在室内外异构场景下的统一预测，在Occ-ScanNet和SurroundOcc-nuScenes上均达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D语义占用方法通常局限于固定场景类型和占用协议，无法处理室内外异构场景中相机配置、空间范围、体素规格和语义类别的差异，缺乏能够跨场景泛化的统一模型。

Method: 提出OccAnyScene框架，基于预训练深度基础模型。包含两个核心模块：(1) 像素对齐截锥体特征聚合，为每个特征像素构建相机感知的截锥体查询；(2) 截锥体参数化高斯构建，将每个查询解码为多个高斯，其位置和大小由预测像素深度和对应截锥体几何约束。

Result: 在室内Occ-ScanNet上达到59.92% mIoU，在室外SurroundOcc-nuScenes上达到23.06% mIoU，均取得新的SOTA结果。

Conclusion: 通过像素-截锥体中心的高斯表示方法，OccAnyScene有效解决了跨场景3D语义占用预测中度量一致性与场景自适应的矛盾，证明了统一跨室内外场景的占用预测模型是可行的。

Abstract: 3D occupancy prediction is fundamental to scene understanding, yet existing 3D semantic occupancy methods are typically specialized to fixed scene types and occupancy protocols. We introduce Cross-Scene 3D Semantic Occupancy Prediction, a new task setting which requires a single model to handle heterogeneous indoor and outdoor scenes with varying cameras, spatial ranges, voxel specifications, and semantic taxonomies. This setting poses a fundamental challenge: achieving metric-consistent yet scene-adaptive image-to-3D lifting across varying camera configurations and scene scales. To address this challenge, we propose OccAnyScene, a pixel-frustum-centered Gaussian framework built upon a pretrained depth foundation model. Specifically, the framework employs Pixel-Aligned Frustum Feature Aggregation to construct a camera-aware frustum query for each feature pixel, and Frustum-Parameterized Gaussian Construction to decode each query into multiple Gaussians whose positions and sizes are constrained by the predicted pixel depth and corresponding frustum geometry. OccAnyScene sets new state-of-the-art results, achieving 59.92% mIoU on the indoor Occ-ScanNet and 23.06% mIoU on the outdoor SurroundOcc-nuScenes.

</details>


### [129] [SRE-FER: Regional residual evidence learning for mitigating local evidence dilution in fine-grained facial expression recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08702)
*Jiaye Song,Ruochen Zhang,Yuliang Wang,Jiaqi Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SRE-FER框架，通过读头级区域残差证据学习（RERA模块）解决细粒度面部表情识别中全局聚合导致局部证据稀释的问题，在三个基准上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度面部表情识别依赖捕捉区分相邻情绪的微妙肌肉线索，但现有方法存在两难困境：基于检测器的方法依赖脆弱的landmark管线，而直接迁移基础模型（如DINOv3）采用传统全局读头时，早期全局聚合会冲淡稀疏的肌肉信号，导致恐惧/惊讶、悲伤/中性等相似类别间持续混淆（即局部证据稀释问题）。

Method: 提出SRE-FER框架，核心模块为RERA（区域残差证据学习），通过添加零初始化的残差logits来细化类别边界同时保留骨干网络的全局预测；训练阶段利用基于FACS解剖学先验的动作单元（AU）引导，将区域特征导向表情相关区域，推理时无需外部面部管线；可选Full设置进一步路由样本特定的非冗余token。

Result: 在三个基准上，SRE-FER在RAF-DB上达到92.76%，在FERPlus上达到91.32%，在AffectNet-7上达到67.78%，展现出与现有FER方法相比极具竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: SRE-FER通过读头级别的区域残差证据学习有效解决了细粒度FER中的局部证据稀释问题，在无需推理时外部面部管线的前提下实现了高性能，验证了区域级残差学习和AU解剖先验引导的有效性。

Abstract: Fine-grained facial expression recognition (FER) hinges on capturing subtle muscular cues that distinguish adjacent emotions. Yet capturing these cues presents a dilemma. Detector-based methods depend on fragile landmark pipelines, whereas we find that directly transferring foundation models such as DINOv3 under conventional global readouts can cause local evidence dilution: early global aggregation washes out sparse muscular signals and leaves persistent confusion between categories such as fear/surprise and sad/neutral. To recover this evidence, we propose SRE-FER, a readout-level regional residual evidence learning framework. Its core module, RERA, adds zero-initialized residual logits that refine class boundaries while preserving the backbone's global prediction. Training-time action unit (AU) guidance steers regional features toward expression-relevant areas using Facial Action Coding System (FACS)-based anatomical priors, without requiring an external facial pipeline at inference. An optional Full setting further routes sample-specific non-redundant tokens. On three benchmarks, SRE-FER attains 92.76% on RAF-DB, 91.32% on FERPlus, and 67.78% on AffectNet-7, demonstrating highly competitive performance compared to existing FER methods.

</details>


### [130] [Resolution Meets Reduction: Efficient Visual Context for 3D Radiology Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08713)
*Jonathan Suprijadi,Raphael Stock,Moritz Langenberg,David Zimmerer,Kim-Celine Kahl,Stefan Denner,Yannick Kirchhoff,Karol Gotkowski,Maximilian Rokuss,Jeremias Traub,Tassilo Wald,Constantin Ulrich,Klaus Maier-Hein*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了CT报告生成中视觉token预算的分配策略，评估了4种视觉编码器、5种token压缩投影器和5种指令微调LLM在两大CT数据集上的表现，发现解剖引导的感兴趣区域裁剪是最一致的策略，而PerceiverResampler配合高分辨率特征可获得最佳配置，在CT-RATE和Merlin上分别达到49.5和49.0的临床宏F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型应用于3D CT体数据时，视觉编码器产生的数万个vision token成为计算瓶颈。虽然投影器可以压缩序列，但可能丢失临床细节；而有效压缩可用固定token预算容纳更高分辨率输入。因此如何分配视觉token预算（视野、空间分辨率、视觉到语言投影）是一个未解决的设计问题。

Method: 系统评估四个异构视觉编码器（CNN和ViT）、五种token压缩投影器（最高64倍压缩）及非压缩MLP投影器基线、五种指令微调LLM（1.7B-4B），在CT-RATE和Merlin两个大规模CT报告数据集上，对比分析解剖引导的感兴趣区域裁剪、输入分辨率提升等策略在不同编码器和投影器组合下的表现。

Result: 在匹配的LLM token预算下，解剖引导的ROI裁剪是最一致的策略，在20个设置中的19个提升临床宏F1，对3D ViT Primus编码器平均提升+3.7分，对2D ViT Curia编码器提升+1.1分。增加输入分辨率的收益高度依赖投影器类型：PerceiverResampler配高分辨率Curia特征在两个数据集上均为最强配置。最佳配置在CT-RATE上达49.5、Merlin上达49.0的临床宏F1，达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: 视觉token预算的分配策略对CT报告生成至关重要：ROI裁剪提供最稳定且一致的优势，而分辨率提升的效果取决于所选的投影器架构。这项系统研究为3D医学影像报告生成的token预算分配提供了实证指导。

Abstract: Vision-language models offer a promising path toward automating radiology report generation, but applying them to full 3D CT volumes poses substantial computational challenges. Modern foundation vision encoders (VEs) can produce tens of thousands of vision tokens per scan, making the visual sequence passed to the large language model (LLM) a primary computational bottleneck. Vision-to-language projectors can compress this sequence to reduce computation, but may discard clinically relevant detail; conversely, effective compression can accommodate higher-resolution inputs while keeping the downstream token count fixed. How this vision-token budget should be allocated across input field of view, spatial resolution, and vision-to-language projection therefore remains an open design question. We systematically evaluate four heterogeneous VEs (CNN- and ViT-based), five token-reducing projectors at up to 64x compression alongside a non-reducing MLP projector baseline, and five instruction-tuned LLMs (1.7B--4B) on two large-scale CT report datasets (CT-RATE and Merlin). At matched LLM token budgets, anatomy-guided region of interest cropping is the most consistent strategy, improving clinical macro F1 in 19 of 20 settings by +3.7 points on average for the 3D ViT Primus encoder and +1.1 for the slice-based 2D ViT Curia encoder. Increasing input resolution further is strongly projector-dependent: the PerceiverResampler, paired with higher-resolution Curia features, yields the strongest configuration in the resolution study on both datasets. Our best configurations achieve state-of-the-art clinical macro F1 on the test sets, reaching 49.5 on CT-RATE and 49.0 on Merlin. Code and models will be published upon publication.

</details>


### [131] [High-Quality Exposure Correction with Diffusion-Based Image Generation Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08720)
*Ziwen Li,Meng Cao,Jinpu Zhang,Chunyang Li,Long Bao,Heng Sun,Yuehuan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DPEC（基于扩散先验的曝光校正）框架，利用预训练大规模扩散模型中的图像生成先验来解决曝光校正问题。通过高效微调策略在单步去噪过程中生成增强图像，并设计联合交叉注意力模块集成多尺度扩散先验特征，融合扩散模型与回归模型的优势，在多个数据集上超越现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有曝光校正方法过度关注整体像素级精度，难以有效建模极端曝光区域，导致感知质量欠佳。扩散模型在图像生成领域表现出色，但如何将其成功应用于曝光校正仍是挑战性难题，核心难点在于在随机扩散过程中生成准确图像结构并保持高保真度。

Method: 1) 提出高效微调策略，从预训练扩散模型中推导出曝光校正器，实现单步去噪的增强图像生成；2) 设计联合交叉注意力模块，融合扩散模型与回归模型的优势，集成多尺度扩散先验特征；3) 扩散模型专注于低频内容，而非全部复杂纹理细节，从而有效保留高频细节并减少随机伪影。

Result: 在多个曝光校正数据集上，DPEC方法在保真度、感知质量和视觉效果方面均一致优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: DPEC成功将扩散模型先验应用于曝光校正任务，通过扩散模型与回归模型的协同设计，有效解决了极端曝光区域建模难题，实现了保真度与感知质量的平衡提升。

Abstract: Although most existing exposure correction methods achieve high fidelity, they often place excessive focus on overall pixel-wise accuracy, making it challenging to effectively model extreme exposure regions, which results in suboptimal perceptual quality. Recently, diffusion models have received significant attention due to their remarkable performance in the realm of image generation. However, their successful application to exposure correction remains a challenging and open question. The key challenge lies in generating accurate image structures and maintaining high image fidelity during stochastic diffusion processes. In this paper, we propose DPEC (Diffusion Prior-based Exposure Correction), a novel framework for image exposure correction that utilizes diffusion-based image generation priors encapsulated in pre-trained large-scale diffusion models. Specifically, we first propose an efficient fine-tuning strategy to derive an exposure corrector from pre-trained models, enabling the generation of enhanced images in a single-step denoising process. Moreover, we seamlessly combine the strengths of diffusion models and regression models, and design a joint cross-attention module to integrate multi-scale diffusion prior features, thereby effectively preserving high-frequency details and minimizing random artifacts. The diffusion model focuses on dealing with low-frequency content rather than all the intricate texture details. The experimental results demonstrate that the proposed DPEC method consistently outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods on multiple exposure correction datasets, whether in terms of fidelity, perceptual quality, or visual effects.

</details>


### [132] [TomaMMU: A Comprehensive Multimodal Understanding Benchmark for Tomato Leaf Diseases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08727)
*Gia-Han Truong,Khang Nguyen Quoc,Luyl-Da Quach*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TomaMMU大规模番茄叶病多模态理解数据集和TomaBench基准，包含28,808张高质量图像、15个类别和213,119个人工标注的视觉问答对。TomaBench将七个农业任务按层次分为基础感知、病理理解和专家诊断三个层级，系统评估VLM从低级视觉识别到高级诊断推理的能力。实验发现14个SOTA VLM在细粒度识别和事实推理上存在明显差距，但简单微调可将挑战性MCQ准确率提升至96.09%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型(VLM)在农业领域特别是植物病害理解上缺乏大规模多模态数据集和系统化评估基准，导致无法全面衡量模型对植物病理学的掌握程度。

Method: 采用三阶段流水线（数据收集、人工标注、问答生成）构建TomaMMU数据集；设计TomaBench基准，将七个农业任务组织为三层级分类体系（基础感知、病理理解、专家诊断），并用14个SOTA VLM进行系统评估。

Result: 14个SOTA VLM在细粒度识别和事实推理任务上表现不佳，尤其在挑战性MCQ和开放式问题上普遍欠佳；简单微调后MCQ准确率提升至96.09%，超越近期VLM。

Conclusion: 当前VLM难以将视觉感知转化为可靠的诊断知识，需要针对性的领域适配；TomaMMU数据集微调能显著缩小差距，为农业AI诊断提供有前景的方向。

Abstract: To address this gap, we introduce TomaMMU, a large-scale Tomato leaf disease MultiModal Understanding dataset, alongside TomaBench, a benchmark for evaluating VLMs on tomato disease understanding. TomaMMU comprises 28,808 high-quality images spanning 15 categories and 213,119 human-annotated visual question-answer pairs, generated through a three-stage pipeline comprising Data Collection, Human Annotation, and Question-Answer Generation. Building on this foundation, TomaBench organizes seven agricultural tasks into a hierarchical three-level taxonomy spanning Basic Perception, Pathology Understanding, and Expert Diagnosis, which together enable systematic evaluation from low-level visual recognition to high-level diagnostic reasoning. The tasks assess visual symptom recognition, taxonomic relationships, and diagnostic reasoning, offering a comprehensive view of how well models grasp plant pathology. Our results pronounced gaps in fine-grained recognition and factually grounded reasoning with 14 state-of-the-art VLMs, consistently underperforming on both challenging MCQs and open-ended questions. These results suggest that current VLMs struggle to translate visual perception into reliable diagnostic knowledge, motivating the need for targeted domain adaptation. Simple fine-tuning on TomaMMU substantially narrows this gap, boosting accuracy on challenging MCQs to 96.09%, outperforming recent VLMs, and pointing toward promising directions for future work. All data and code is available in https://huggingface.co/datasets/enalis/TomaMMU.

</details>


### [133] [AnchorFold: A Focus-Then-Fold Framework via Recursive Attention Propagation for Efficient Multi-Vector Visual Document Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08732)
*Haoyu Zuo,Yibo Yan,Xin Zou,Shuliang Liu,Yi Cao,Mingdong Ou,Xuming Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AnchorFold是一种无需训练的文档侧索引压缩框架，通过"先聚焦后折叠"策略，利用递归注意力传播选择高中心性token作为锚点，并将其余token按相似度分配到最接近的锚点组，通过中心性加权聚合实现索引压缩，在5倍压缩下保持98.3%的NDCG@5，在20倍压缩下保持92.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多向量视觉语言检索模型通过晚期交互实现细粒度视觉文档检索，但存储和计算每页数百个视觉patch嵌入带来巨大开销。现有无训练方法要么通过剪枝（在激进压缩下性能严重下降），要么通过合并（但合并时未明确优先考虑重要区域来形成代表性token）。

Method: AnchorFold采用"先聚焦后折叠"的两阶段框架：（1）聚焦阶段：在视觉自注意力图上应用递归注意力传播，在每头内进行多步传播并跨头和跨层整合得分，选择最高中心性token作为锚点；（2）折叠阶段：在归一化检索空间中将剩余token分配到最相似的锚点，并通过中心性加权聚合来汇总每个锚点为中心的组，保留非锚点贡献的同时集中容量于结构上重要的token。

Result: 在ViDoRe v1/v2和REAL-MM-RAG数据集上，结合三种不同检索骨干模型，AnchorFold在压缩率γ≤0.20时一致优于所有评估的无训练基线。在ViDoRe v1/v2上，5倍压缩时平均保留98.3%的全索引NDCG@5，实现近乎无损压缩；20倍压缩时保留92.4%。

Conclusion: AnchorFold通过融合注意力传播的中心性分析和锚定分组压缩方法，在无需训练的前提下实现了对多向量视觉文档检索索引的高效压缩，取得了比现有剪枝和合并方法更优的性能，尤其在高压缩率下仍能保持较高的检索质量。

Abstract: Multi-vector vision-language retrievers enable fine-grained Visual Document Retrieval (VDR) through late interaction, but storing and scoring hundreds of visual patch embeddings per page incurs substantial overhead. Existing training-free methods rely on pruning or merging: pruning degrades sharply under aggressive compression, whereas merging does not explicitly prioritize important regions when forming representatives. We introduce AnchorFold, a training-free focus-then-fold framework for document-side index compression. AnchorFold applies Recursive Attention Propagation over visual self-attention graphs, performing multi-step propagation within each attention head and integrating scores across heads and layers. The focus stage selects the highest-centrality tokens as anchors. The fold stage assigns remaining tokens to their most similar anchors in the normalized retrieval space and summarizes each anchor-centered group through centrality-weighted aggregation. This preserves non-anchor contributions while concentrating capacity on structurally important tokens. Across ViDoRe v1/v2 and REAL-MM-RAG with three diverse retrieval backbones, AnchorFold consistently outperforms all evaluated training-free baselines at $γ\leq 0.20$. On ViDoRe v1/v2, it retains 98.3% of full-index NDCG@5 on average at $5\times$ compression, achieving near-lossless compression, and 92.4% at $20\times$ compression.

</details>


### [134] [IDATA: Scalable Invertible Diffusion for Unrestricted Adversarial Transfer Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08734)
*Yi Pan,Jun-Jie Huang,Tianrui Liu,Zihan Chen,Lin Liu,Zhao Wentao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出IDATA，一种用于无限制对抗迁移攻击的存高效扩散框架。它通过可逆扩散模块实现恒内存的反向传播，并利用低频约束模块基于离散小波变换限制扰动到低频子空间，在多个基准和模型架构上取得了优于现有方法的攻击成功率、存效率和视觉不可感知性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的对抗迁移攻击方法面临两大挑战：(1)多步反向传播需要大量内存来存储完整的去噪链；(2)对中间潜变量的扰动缺乏频率感知，导致迁移性受限。

Method: 提出IDATA框架，包含两个关键组件：(1)可逆扩散模块(IDM)，将扩散轨迹上的对抗优化重构为可逆过程，通过按需重建中间状态实现恒内存反向传播；(2)低频约束模块(LFCM)，利用离散小波变换(DWT)将潜变量分解为低高频分量，将扰动限制在语义稳定的低频子空间，提升迁移性同时保持视觉不可感知性。

Result: 在多个基准测试和多样模型架构上的大量实验表明，IDATA在攻击成功率、存效率和视觉不可感知性方面持续优于最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: IDATA是评估深度视觉模型黑盒鲁棒性的有力工具，代码已开源。

Abstract: Unrestricted adversarial transfer attacks are important for evaluating the black-box robustness of deep visual models. Diffusion-based attacks have shown promising transferability and visual imperceptibility by optimizing adversarial perturbations along denoising trajectories in latent space. However, existing methods are limited by two challenges: memory-intensive multistep backpropagation and frequency-agnostic perturbation over intermediate latents. To address these issues, we propose IDATA, a memory-efficient diffusion framework for unrestricted adversarial transfer attack. IDATA consists of two key components: an Invertible Diffusion Module (IDM) and a Low-Frequency Constraint Module (LFCM). Specifically, IDM reformulates adversarial optimization over diffusion trajectories as an invertible process, enabling constant-memory backpropagation through on-demand reconstruction of intermediate states instead of storing the full denoising chain. Moreover, LFCM leverages Discrete Wavelet Transform (DWT) to decompose latent variables into low- and high-frequency components, restricting perturbations to semantically stable low-frequency subspaces, thereby improving transferability while preserving visual imperceptibility. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks and diverse model architectures demonstrate that IDATA consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in attack success rate, memory efficiency, and visual imperceptibility. These results suggest that IDATA is a promising tool for black-box robustness evaluation of deep visual models. Code is available at https://github.com/colourful-pan/IDATA.

</details>


### [135] [Parcel2Progression: An Anatomy-aware Longitudinal Framework for Alzheimer's Disease Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08753)
*Madhumitha Venkatesh,Shanawaj S Madarkar,Konda Reddy Mopuri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Parcel2Progression (P2P)，一种纵向Transformer框架，利用图谱引导的分区编码器将3D sMRI扫描tokenize为解剖学表征，并通过纵向Transformer整合不规则、可变长度的纵向访视数据，实现在阿尔茨海默病进展预测中的高效计算和分区级可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AD进展是纵向过程，早期有细微病理线索，但计算约束限制了神经影像模型要么牺牲空间信息、要么限制纵向扫描数量。现有方法难以充分利用高分辨率、可变长度的4D sMRI扫描序列。

Method: P2P框架包含两个核心组件：(1) Atlas-guided Parcel Encoder，将3D扫描tokenize为解剖学分区表征；(2) Longitudinal Transformer，整合不规则、任意长度的纵向访视数据与患者年龄。该设计实现了线性计算复杂度（相较于朴素4D ViT的二次方复杂度）以及分区级可解释性。

Result: P2P在ADNI、AIBL和MIRIAD数据集上的MCI到AD转化预测和AD vs CN分类任务中均优于先前工作和基线。利用纵向扫描相比单次扫描基线，在AD分类和MCI转化预测的平衡准确率上分别提升最高5%和7%。可解释性分析揭示了临床一致的萎缩模式，并在合成数据集上验证了异常检测可靠性。

Conclusion: P2P成功克服了高分辨率纵向sMRI分析的计算瓶颈，在性能、可解释性和计算效率方面均展现出显著优势，并验证了其对其他神经退行性疾病（如额颞叶痴呆）的泛化能力。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease (AD) progression is a longitudinal process with subtle pathological cues in the early stages. Yet, computational constraints have limited most neuroimaging models to either compromise spatial information or limit the number of longitudinal scans. We aim to overcome this bottleneck and fully leverage high-resolution, variable-length T1w structural MRI (4D sMRI) scan sequences. We introduce Parcel2Progression (P2P), a Longitudinal Transformer Framework which tackles this challenge using an Atlas-guided Parcel Encoder that tokenizes 3D scans into a set of richer anatomically grounded representations. A Longitudinal Transformer then integrates irregular, arbitrary-length longitudinal visits with patient age. This synergy delivers two key advantages: (1) parcel-specific interpretability, and (2) computational tractability for long-term analysis, which scales linearly with the number of scans compared to a naive quadratic 4D ViT cost. P2P outperforms prior works and baselines in both MCI (Mild Cognitive Impairment) to AD conversion prediction and AD vs. CN (Cognitively Normal) classification tasks across ADNI, AIBL, and MIRIAD datasets. Leveraging longitudinal scans boosts performance over single-scan baselines by up to 5% and 7% in balanced accuracy for AD classification and MCI conversion prediction tasks, respectively. Interpretability analysis using parcel saliencies and attention rollouts reveals clinically consistent atrophy patterns in AD and MCI subjects. We also demonstrate the frameworks' reliability in anomaly detection using a synthetic dataset, and test the model's generalizability for other neurodegenerative diseases like Frontotemporal Dementia.

</details>


### [136] [UPolarSQ: Polar Representation Learning for Optic Disc and Peripapillary Atrophy Segmentation and Quantification in Fundus Photographs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08771)
*Mengxian He,Yunyun sun,Ziyue Gao,Wengkei Lam,Shunyi Zhang,Wu Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UPolarSQ框架，将眼底图像映射到极坐标系中，实现高度近视眼底图像中视盘（OD）和视盘周围萎缩（PPA）的统一分割与生物标志物定量分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近视引起的后极部重塑常伴随视盘变形和视盘周围萎缩（PPA），这些是临床相关的结构生物标志物。然而在笛卡尔坐标系眼底图像中，PPA常呈不规则且部分可见的新月形，导致分割碎片化且量化结果依赖后处理。

Method: UPolarSQ首先将OD中心区域映射至极坐标系，使OD和PPA边界可表示为径向轮廓；然后采用UPolarSeg（基于U-Net的分割网络，增强径向-角度解耦模块和边界感知辅助监督）建模各向异性极坐标特征和径向边界过渡；最后从预测的极坐标掩膜中确定性提取临床生物标志物（如视盘形状和PPA宽度相关测量）。

Result: 在内部和外部队列上的实验表明，UPolarSQ改善了OD/PPA分割性能，并支持可靠的极坐标原生生物标志物估计。

Conclusion: UPolarSQ通过统一的极坐标系表示，将分割与定量分析对齐，为近视眼底分析提供了更鲁棒和可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Myopia-induced posterior-pole remodeling is frequently accompanied by Optic Disc (OD) deformation and Peripapillary Atrophy (PPA), both of which provide clinically relevant structural biomarkers. In Cartesian fundus images, however, PPA often appears as an irregular and partially visible crescent adjacent to the OD, leading to fragmented segmentation and post-processing-dependent quantification. We propose UPolarSQ, a unified polar-domain framework for OD/PPA segmentation and biomarker quantification in myopic fundus images. UPolarSQ first maps an OD-centered region of interest into polar coordinates, where OD and PPA boundaries can be represented as radial profiles. It then employs UPolarSeg, a U-Net-based segmentation network enhanced with a Radial-Angular-Decoupled Module and boundary-aware auxiliary supervision to model anisotropic polar features and radial boundary transitions. Clinical biomarkers, including disc shape and PPA-width-related measurements, are deterministically extracted from the predicted polar masks, aligning segmentation and quantification within a shared geometric representation. Experiments on internal and external cohorts demonstrate that UPolarSQ improves OD/PPA segmentation and supports reliable polar-native biomarker estimation for myopic analysis.

</details>


### [137] [LASA: Language-and-Source-Anchored Alignment for Domain Generalized Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08805)
*Jinhong Zhu,Weiqi Yan,Shengchuan Zhang,Liujuan Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LASA框架用于域泛化语义分割，通过文本-源锚定的风格迁移、域感知查询适配和域感知解码器优化三个组件，解决传统风格随机化和特征归一化方法破坏特征完整性的问题，在多个基准上超越SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统DGSS方法存在两大缺陷：风格随机化因其粗粒度特性会扭曲底层特征流形；特征归一化因其僵化设计会抑制具有判别性的、域敏感的语义细节，这两者都损害了特征完整性。

Method: 提出LASA框架，包含三个协同组件：1) TSGST模块利用源特征作为结构锚点、VLM先验作为细粒度指导来缓解流形扭曲；2) DAQA模块通过类别指导和域感知签名重新校准对象查询以恢复被抑制的判别性细节；3) DADO模块将查询分布与共享分类器对齐，确保跨域一致的类别响应。

Result: 在多个具有挑战性的基准数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 通过语言-源锚定对齐框架，有效解决了风格随机化和特征归一化对特征完整性的破坏问题，实现了更优的域泛化语义分割性能。

Abstract: Domain Generalization Semantic Segmentation (DGSS) focuses on generalizing knowledge from labeled source domains to unseen target domains where data is unavailable during the training phase. While conventional methods utilize style randomization or feature normalization to mitigate domain shifts, they often impair feature integrity. Specifically, style randomization distorts the underlying feature manifold due to its coarse-grained nature, while feature normalization suppresses discriminative, domain-sensitive semantic details owing to its rigid design. To address these limitations, we propose the Language-and-Source-Anchored Alignment (LASA) framework, which comprises three synergistic components: Text-and-Source-Guided Style Transfer (TSGST), Domain-Aware Query Adapter (DAQA), and Domain-Aware Decoder Optimizer (DADO). Concretely, the TSGST module addresses manifold distortion by utilizing source features as structural anchors and vision-language model (VLM) priors as fine-grained guidance. To restore suppressed discriminative and domain-sensitive details, the DAQA module recalibrates object queries via categorical guidance and domain-aware signatures, while the DADO module aligns the resulting query distributions with a shared classifier to ensure consistent categorical responses across domains. Extensive experiments on challenging benchmarks demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [138] [AdapterMoE: A Two-Stage Hard-Routing Mixture-of-Experts Architecture for Multi-Crop Disease Recognition with Calibrated Rejection and Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08808)
*Pin-Hsun Huang,Shaou-Gang Miaou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AdapterMoE，一种基于确定性硬路由的作物病害识别系统，将冻结的EfficientNet-B0骨干网络与五个逐作物的适配器结合，通过RouterHead进行作物分类和基于Maximum Softmax Probability的阈值拒识，并配备双门控Energy+KNN的分布外检测模块。该系统避免了专家坍缩，且支持局部、逐作物的增量更新（add_crop接口），在PlantVillage数据集上实现了与最佳基线统计上无显著差异的精度（Macro-F1差异在0.24点以内），而训练成本仅为全网络基线的约9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统软路由MoE在作物病害识别中存在专家坍缩（少数专家主导）、专家与作物无语义对应、重训练成本高、拒识不稳定以及精度天花板饱和等问题。作者希望将目标从单纯追求精度转向部署成本、扩展灵活性与拒识稳定性之间的权衡。

Method: 采用确定性硬路由替代软路由。RouterHead负责作物分类并对非目标作物通过Maximum Softmax Probability阈值进行拒识；双门控Energy+KNN模块捕捉分布偏移输入；五个逐作物适配器置于冻结的EfficientNet-B0骨干之上进行病害判别，每个适配器通过Temperature Scaling校准。由于专家在数据层面硬隔离，可避免专家坍缩，并提供add_crop接口支持局部、逐作物更新而非全量重训练。

Result: 在PlantVillage数据集（5种作物，26个类别）上，与五个系统的公平对比中，AdapterMoE取得了与最佳基线在统计上无显著差异的精度（Macro-F1差距在0.24点以内），同时将训练成本降至全网络基线的约9%，并能高效扩展到新作物。

Conclusion: AdapterMoE验证了确定性硬路由MoE在作物病害识别中的可行性，在保持精度的同时显著降低了训练成本、增强了拒识稳定性，并提供了灵活的逐作物扩展能力，为资源受限的农业场景提供了更实用的部署方案。

Abstract: Timely crop-disease identification is critical to food security. Multi-crop recognition suits Mixture-of-Experts (MoE), but conventional soft-routing MoE learns crop assignment freely end-to-end, letting a few experts dominate (expert collapse) with no semantic correspondence to crops, and facing high retraining costs, unstable rejection of non-target inputs, and a saturated accuracy ceiling. We shift the objective from accuracy toward a trade-off among deployment cost, scaling flexibility, and rejection stability, using deterministic hard routing. We propose AdapterMoE: a RouterHead classifies the crop and rejects non-target crops via a Maximum Softmax Probability threshold, with a dual-gate Energy+KNN out-of-distribution module catching distribution-shifted inputs; five per-crop Adapters atop a frozen EfficientNet-B0 backbone discriminate diseases, each calibrated via Temperature Scaling. Because experts are hard-isolated at the data level, the design avoids expert collapse and exposes an add_crop interface for local, per-crop updates instead of full retraining. On PlantVillage (5 crops, 26 classes), across a fair five-system comparison, AdapterMoE attains accuracy statistically indistinguishable from the best baselines (Macro-F1 within a 0.24-point band) while cutting training cost to about 9% of full-network baselines, expanding to a new crop in

</details>


### [139] [360CityArena: A Realistic Virtual Urban Navigation Benchmark for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08814)
*Kenta Watanabe,Atsuyuki Miyai,Mizuki Takenawa,Kiyoharu Aizawa,Toshihiko Yamasaki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了360CityArena，一个基于360度视频构建的真实感城市场景中评估具身智能体城市探索能力的基准。它利用东京秋叶原地区的602段360度视频重建了85条街道，包含175个精心设计的任务，涵盖环境理解、路径推理和空间推理三个类别。实验表明最强模型Gemini 2.5 Flash（17.1%）远低于人类水平（77.3%），揭示了城市级具身导航与推理仍面临巨大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有户外基准要么缺乏照片级真实感，要么复杂度不足，与真实城市环境存在较大差距，无法充分评估具身智能体在真实城市中的探索能力。

Method: 基于东京秋叶原地区602段360度视频（覆盖85条街道）构建照片级真实环境，设计175个手工任务，分为环境理解、路径推理和空间推理三类，使用最先进的LMM-based智能体进行评测。

Result: 最强模型Gemini 2.5 Flash仅达到17.1%的准确率，而人类水平为77.3%，两者差距显著，表明现有模型在城市规模具身导航和推理任务上远未达到人类能力。

Conclusion: 360CityArena为照片级真实城市区域导航和空间推理提供了一个必要且具有挑战性的测试平台，揭示了当前具身智能体在城市探索方面的重大局限，为该领域设立了新的评估标准。

Abstract: We present 360CityArena, a benchmark for evaluating the urban exploration capabilities of embodied agents within a photorealistic environment constructed from 360-degree videos. Existing outdoor benchmarks either lack sufficient photorealism or complexity, resulting in a considerable gap from real-world urban environments. 360CityArena is built on a realistic reconstruction of the Akihabara district in Tokyo, Japan, using 602 360-degree video segments covering 85 streets, and consists of 175 meticulously human-crafted tasks. It encompasses three task categories: Environment Understanding, Path Reasoning, and Spatial Reasoning, covering fundamental abilities required for urban exploration, such as localization, landmark search, path planning, and relational spatial reasoning, thereby enabling comprehensive evaluation in realistic urban scenes. Our evaluation using state-of-the-art LMM-based agents shows that even the strongest model, Gemini 2.5 Flash, performs far below human level (human: 77.3% vs. Gemini 2.5 Flash: 17.1%), revealing substantial challenges that remain in city-scale embodied navigation and reasoning. 360CityArena provides a necessary and challenging testbed for photorealistic urban-district navigation and spatial reasoning.

</details>


### [140] [MRI super-resolution in ten sampling steps using a diffusion bridge model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08819)
*Mojtaba Safari,Hang Yu,Zach Eidex,Mingzhe Hu,Ryan J. Sanford,Alexandru Florea,Shansong Wang,Chih-Wei Chang,Erik H Middlebrooks,Aditya Juloori,Stanley L. Liauw,Ralph Weichselbaum,Xiaofeng Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SR-DBM，一种基于扩散桥模型的MRI超分辨率重建框架，通过Doob's h-变换将LR到HR图像的重建建模为随机输运过程，仅需10个采样步骤即可直接从LR数据重建HR图像，在7T脑部和前列腺MRI数据集上超越九种对比方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MRI采集时间长导致患者不适和运动伪影，需要在空间分辨率和扫描时间之间权衡。现有扩散SR方法需要大量采样步骤且从高斯先验初始化，不适合图像恢复任务。

Method: 提出SR-DBM，将超分辨率建模为LR和HR图像分布之间的随机输运。通过Doob's h-变换的均值回归随机微分方程，将过程端点固定到配对的HR和LR图像，从实测解剖结构而非高斯噪声初始化重建。使用确定性反向轨迹，网络仅需预测十个采样步骤中的每个干净图像。

Result: 在7T脑部T1 MP2RAGE和盆腔T2加权前列腺MRI上，SR-DBM在PSNR、SSIM上最高，GMSD最低（脑部：27.66±1.52 dB, 0.96±0.02, 7.96±1.86；前列腺：27.87±2.29 dB, 0.80±0.05, 8.38±1.44），所有指标均显著优于对比方法（Wilcoxon符号秩检验，Holm校正，p<0.05）。最强基线SR-EMamba排名第二。定性上残差最小，最好地保留细结构和病变。

Conclusion: SR-DBM通过扩散桥模型有效地解决了MRI超分辨率重建问题，仅需少量采样步骤且从数据驱动先验而非高斯噪声初始化，在定量和定性指标上均表现最优，具有临床应用的潜力。

Abstract: Objective. MRI provides excellent soft-tissue contrast, but long acquisition times can cause patient discomfort and lead to motion artifacts, forcing a trade-off between spatial resolution and scan time. Diffusion-based super-resolution (SR) reconstructs high-resolution (HR) images from low-resolution (LR) inputs, but typically needs many sampling steps and initializes from a Gaussian prior ill-suited to image restoration. We developed an efficient diffusion framework that reconstructs HR MRI directly from LR data. Approach. We propose super-resolution diffusion bridge model (SR-DBM), a super-resolution diffusion bridge model that casts SR as a stochastic transport between the LR and HR image distributions. Through a Doob's h-transform of a mean-reverting stochastic differential equation, SR-DBM pins the process to the paired HR and LR images at its endpoints, initializing reconstruction from the measured anatomy rather than from Gaussian noise. The HR image is recovered by a deterministic reverse trajectory in which a network predicts the clean image at each of only ten sampling steps. We evaluated SR-DBM on ultra-high-field 7T brain T1 MP2RAGE maps and pelvic T2-weighted prostate images against nine comparison methods using PSNR, SSIM, GMSD, and LPIPS. Main results. SR-DBM attained the highest PSNR and SSIM and the lowest GMSD on both datasets (brain: 27.66+-1.52 dB, 0.96+-0.02, 7.96+-1.86$; prostate: 27.87+-2.29 dB, 0.80+-0.05, 8.38+- 1.44), with statistically significant gains over every comparison method (two-sided Wilcoxon signed-rank test with Holm correction, p<0.05). The strongest baseline, SR-EMamba, ranked second. Qualitatively, SR-DBM produced the smallest residual errors and best preserved fine structures and lesions.

</details>


### [141] [LogiShot: Logically Coherent Cross-Shot Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08820)
*Shuai Guo,Yuhang Yang,Zeyu Zhang,Pengfei Yu,Wei Zhai,Yang Cao,Zheng-Jun Zha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LogiShot，一种跨镜头视频生成框架，通过联合编码上下文视频与条件信号（提供多模态线索）以及维护视觉记忆（保持视觉一致性）两条互补路径，实现跨镜头的逻辑连贯生成。同时构建了110K样本的数据集和专用基准，实验证明其在逻辑连贯性上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨镜头视频生成工作流（如短剧制作）依赖孤立的文本脚本或显式参考图像，当用户指令不明确或模糊时，生成的片段可能在视觉上看似合理，但无法与整体叙事对齐，导致内容脱节。缺乏跨镜头逻辑连贯性的生成能力。

Method: LogiShot通过两条互补路径整合信息：1) 联合编码上下文视频和其他条件信号，生成密集的多模态线索，为跨镜头生成提供视觉语义证据；2) 在生成过程中维护上下文视频的视觉记忆，以保持跨镜头的视觉一致性。此外，构建了包含110K样本的数据集和专用基准用于评估跨镜头逻辑连贯性。

Result: 实验表明LogiShot在跨多个镜头的逻辑连贯性上持续优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: LogiShot证明了通过结合上下文视频的多模态编码与视觉记忆机制，可以有效解决跨镜头视频生成中的逻辑连贯问题。模型和数据将公开提供。

Abstract: Generating cross-shot videos that are logically connected is essential for content creation.
