These findings suggest that future trustworthy MLLMs for infrared scene understanding should be evaluated and developed to produce thermally grounded explanations rather than merely accurate answers.

</details>


### [176] [Multi-Submap Implicit Neural SLAM with Local-to-Global Loop Closure for Large-Scale Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09146)
*Tianchen Deng,Chongdi Wang,Nailin Wang,Lei Zhao,Ziqi Ma,Tianjun Zhang,Zhe Liu,Danwei Wang,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多子图架构和双层回环闭合机制的鲁棒大规模神经SLAM系统（MSN-SLAM），通过渐进式映射策略、光流跟踪和基于基础模型的全局描述子回环检测，解决了NeRF-SLAM在复杂大场景中的灾难性遗忘和轨迹漂移问题，并在公共基准及自建大规模室内外数据集上验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的NeRF-based SLAM方法在小规模场景重建中表现出色，但将其扩展到大规模、复杂环境时面临两大核心挑战：灾难性遗忘（catastrophic forgetting）和累积轨迹漂移（accumulated trajectory drift），亟需一种可扩展的神经SLAM解决方案。

Method: 核心方法包括四个方面：（1）多子图架构与渐进式映射策略，动态分配神经子图，避免大规模场景中的内存爆炸；（2）基于光流的跟踪模块，增强对剧烈运动的位姿估计鲁棒性；（3）局部到全局的回环闭合框架，利用基础模型提取高表现力的全局描述子，提升跨视角的重定位精度；（4）子图间在线蒸馏算法，在后端优化中强制执行重叠子图边界处的几何与外观一致性。此外，论文还开发了定制的手持机电平台用于系统验证。

Result: 在公共基准和自建的大规模室内外数据集上进行了广泛评估，并在板载计算单元上直接部署验证。实验结果表明，该方法在重建质量和定位鲁棒性方面优于最先进的神经SLAM方法，为真实机器人感知和数字孪生提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Conclusion: 该工作通过多子图架构与双层回环机制的有机结合，成功将NeRF-SLAM拓展至大规模复杂环境，同时解决了记忆容量和全局一致性问题，代表了神经SLAM在实际大规模应用中的可行演进方向。代码将公开发布。

Abstract: Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF)-based SLAM has demonstrated impressive results in small-scale scene reconstruction, yet scaling these methods to extensive, complex environments remains challenging due to catastrophic forgetting and accumulated trajectory drift. This paper presents a robust, large-scale neural SLAM system featuring a multi-submap architecture and a dual-tier loop closure mechanism. Specifically, we propose a progressive mapping strategy that dynamically allocates neural submaps to maintain high-fidelity representations without memory explosion. For robust pose estimation, an optical-flow-based tracking module is integrated to handle aggressive motions. To address global consistency, we introduce a local-to-global loop closure framework leveraging the foundation model for high-performance global descriptor extraction, significantly enhancing relocalization accuracy under varying viewpoints. Furthermore, an inter-submap online distillation algorithm is designed during back-end optimization to enforce geometric and appearance consistency across overlapping submap boundaries. To validate the system, we developed a customized handheld mechatronic platform and conducted extensive evaluations on both public benchmarks and our large-scale indoor-outdoor datasets. Experimental results, including direct deployment on an onboard computing unit, demonstrate that our approach outperforms state-of-the-art neural SLAM methods in reconstruction quality and localization robustness, providing a scalable solution for real-world robotic perception and digital twinning. We will release the code publicly on \href{https://github.com/dtc111111/MSN-SLAM}{https://github.com/dtc111111/MSN-SLAM} .

</details>


### [177] [RefineAny3D: Depth Refinement as Semantic Alignment for Monocular 3D Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09147)
*Zhihao Zhang,Gengwei Zhang,Tianlong Chen,Xiaoming Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RefineAny3D，一个将单目3D目标检测中物体级深度修正视为独立任务的视觉语言模型。核心洞察是深度误差在图像空间有直接视觉特征（正确放置的3D框投影后紧密包围物体），因此将深度修正转化为视觉对齐问题而非数值回归问题。通过扩展VLM词汇表使用动作token进行类别决策，并在大规模思维链数据上训练，该模型作为后处理步骤可一致提升闭集检测器、开放词汇检测器和3D自动标注工具的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前深度基础模型虽有强零样本泛化能力，但缺乏3D检测所需的物体级精度——将SOTA深度基础模型替换强检测器的预测深度反而导致精度下降，甚至不如检测器自身预测。作者无意端到端改进检测器或深度模型，而是将物体级深度修正独立出来处理。

Method: 1) 关键洞察：深度误差在图像空间有直接视觉签名——投影到图像后，正确框紧密包围物体，过远框投影过小，过近框投影过大。2) 将深度修正转化为视觉对齐问题而非度量回归问题。3) 扩展VLM词汇表加入动作token（替换数值深度输出为类别决策）。4) 在大规模思维链数据集上训练，每步决策基于明确视觉证据。

Result: 作为单一后处理步骤，RefineAny3D在闭集检测器、开放词汇检测器和3D自动标注工具上均带来一致增益，并能泛化到新类别、新场景和新相机，无需重训练。

Conclusion: 物体级深度修正可以作为独立任务，通过视觉语言模型用类别决策（而非数值回归）来解决，方法有效且泛化性强。

Abstract: Monocular 3D object detection spans two regimes: closed-set detectors operating within a fixed category vocabulary, and open-vocabulary detectors that localize arbitrary categories by leveraging depth foundation models for 3D geometry. We find that current depth foundation models, despite their strong zero-shot generalization, lack the object-level precision 3D detection demands: substituting a state-of-the-art depth foundation model for a strong detector's predicted depth degrades accuracy, even falling below the detector's own prediction. Rather than pushing detectors or depth models to be more accurate end-to-end, we treat object-level depth refinement as a stand-alone task and present RefineAny3D, a vision-language model that corrects depth without ever predicting a numerical value. Our key insight is that depth error has a direct visual signature in image space: when projected onto the image, a correctly placed box tightly encloses the object, while a too-far box projects too small and a too-close box projects too large. Depth refinement thus reduces to a visual alignment problem rather than a metric regression problem, which we instantiate by extending the VLM's vocabulary with action tokens that replace numerical depth output with categorical decisions, and by supervising the model on a large-scale chain-of-thought dataset that grounds each decision in explicit visual evidence. Applied as a single post-hoc step, RefineAny3D delivers consistent gains across closed-set detectors, open-vocabulary detectors, and 3D auto-labeling tools, and generalizes to novel categories, scenes, and cameras without retraining.

</details>


### [178] [OGG-FR: Orthogonal Gradient Gaming and Frequency Rectification for Unmanned Aerial Vehicle Infrared Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09150)
*Yongsong Huang,Qingzhong Wang,Xiaofeng Liu,Tomo Miyazaki,Yaohou Fan,Shinichiro Omachi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OGG-FR，一种即插即用的优化框架，通过将频率梯度分解为与像素梯度平行的冗余分量和正交的创新分量，解决了无人机红外图像超分辨率中多损失训练梯度冲突/不对齐的问题，在BI和BD退化条件下×4和×8尺度均取得广泛性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机红外图像超分辨率需要在资源受限平台上部署轻量模型，多损失训练（像素域+频域目标）虽常用但容易不稳定。由于红外图像存在低对比度、有限高频内容和传感器噪声，像素梯度与频率梯度的对齐性差甚至相互冲突，导致优化歧义问题。

Method: 提出OGG-FR框架：(1) 将频率梯度分解为相对于像素梯度的冗余平行分量和正交创新分量；(2) 在冲突区域，使用MGDA计算安全基梯度并添加方差校正的正交创新；(3) 在兼容区域，丢弃冗余平行信息，并根据高频残差估计的置信度分数注入正交创新。

Result: 在无人机热成像基准上，在BI和BD退化条件下、×4和×8缩放尺度均获得广泛性能提升；梯度分析验证了所提出的冲突感知更新规则的有效性。

Conclusion: OGG-FR通过冲突感知的梯度分解与正交创新注入策略，有效缓解了像素与频率目标间的优化冲突，为多损失深度学习训练提供了一种鲁棒且即插即用的解决方案。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) infrared image super-resolution aims to recover weak thermal structures for deployment on resource-constrained platforms; lightweight models are therefore preferred, but multi-loss training can be unstable. A common strategy combines pixel-domain and frequency-domain objectives; however, low contrast, limited high-frequency content, and sensor-specific noise often make their gradients weakly aligned or conflicting. To address this optimization ambiguity, we propose Orthogonal Gradient Gaming and Frequency Rectification (OGG-FR), a plug-and-play optimization framework that decomposes the frequency gradient into a redundant parallel component and an orthogonal innovation component relative to the pixel gradient. In the conflict regime, OGG-FR computes a safe base gradient using the Multiple Gradient Descent Algorithm (MGDA) and adds a variance-rectified orthogonal innovation; in the compatible regime, it discards redundant parallel information and injects the orthogonal innovation according to a confidence score estimated from the high-frequency residual. Experimental results on the UAV thermal benchmark show broad gains under BI and BD degradations at $\times 4$ and $\times 8$ scales, while gradient analyses support the effectiveness of the proposed conflict-aware update rule.

</details>


### [179] [LightAIR: Lightweight Action Inversion and Riemannian Rectification for Text-based Person Anomaly Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09152)
*Yulun Zhang,Zixu Li,Zhiwei Chen,Zhiheng Fu,Wenbo Wang,Zihang Qiu,Zhilin Wang,Ruxin Wang,Yupeng Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LightAIR网络，解决文本行人异常搜索（TPAS）中外观与动作特征耦合、硬负样本引导的捷径学习等问题，通过轻量动作反演算子提取纯动作特征、正交零空间投影实现前向解耦、黎曼梯度整流模块限制反向传播轨迹，在TPAS和TIPR数据集上超越现有SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统文本行人搜索仅匹配静态外观属性，忽略动态动作信息。TPAS任务虽弥补这一差距，但现有方法面临三重挑战：（1）外部显式姿态估计器在非约束监控场景下脆弱不可靠；（2）隐式学习在像素级纠缠下遭遇视觉解耦失败，外观信息吞没并污染细微动作特征；（3）在传统欧氏空间中对硬负样本（同外观不同动作）进行对比优化会引发严重的捷径学习。

Method: LightAIR包含三个核心模块：（1）轻量动作反演算子：以文本语义先验为锚点提取纯动作特征，克服视觉固有耦合；（2）正交零空间投影：将外观特征约束在动作特征的正交补空间中，保证严格前向解耦；（3）黎曼梯度整流模块：计算黎曼梯度约束反向传播轨迹，强制梯度流沿保持解耦性质的切空间更新，切断有害捷径。

Result: 在广泛使用的TPAS和TIPR数据集上进行了大量实验，LightAIR显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: LightAIR通过前向特征解耦与反向梯度整流相结合的双向解耦策略，系统性地解决了不同模态信息相互干扰和硬负样本捷径学习问题，为文本行人异常搜索提供了更鲁棒和高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Traditional Text-based Person Search (TPS) is typically limited to matching static appearance attributes, severely neglecting dynamic action information. The Text-based Person Anomaly Search (TPAS) task bridges this gap, requiring models to locate micro-level specific abnormal behaviors while matching macro-level appearance of pedestrians. However, current TPAS methods face fundamental limitations: external explicit pose estimators are fragile in unconstrained surveillance scenarios, and implicit learning encounters visual decoupling failure under pixel-level entanglement, causing dominant appearance information to easily swallow and contaminate subtle action features. Furthermore, performing contrastive optimization on hard negative samples (``same appearance, different actions'') in conventional Euclidean spaces induces severe shortcut learning. To address these, we propose the Lightweight Action Inversion and Riemannian rectification network (LightAIR). First, it introduces textual semantic priors as anchors via a lightweight action inversion operator to extract pure action features, thereby overcoming visual-inherent coupling. Subsequently, it employs orthogonal null-space projection to constrain appearance features within the orthogonal complement space of action features, guaranteeing strict forward decoupling. Finally, we designed a gradient rectification module that computes the Riemannian gradient to constrain the backpropagation trajectory, forcing the gradient flow to update strictly along the tangent space that preserves decoupling properties, thereby cutting off harmful shortcuts. Extensive experiments on the widely used TPAS and TIPR datasets demonstrate that LightAIR significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods. Codes are available at https://github.com/rainy-london/LightAIR

</details>


### [180] [Not All Visual Tokens Are Equally Safe to Remove:Consequence-Sensitive Visual Token Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09176)
*Jingbo Wen,Liang He,Mingyu Cao,Haoyu Wang,Minxuan Hu,Kangning Cui,Xilu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种后果敏感（consequence-sensitive）的视觉token压缩方法，根据请求的潜在错误成本差异来分配视觉计算资源，而非假设所有错误代价相等。通过先校准后分配（calibrate-then-allocate）的流程，该方法能显著降低高后果错误率，并在多个基准和不同预算机制上验证了其泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉token压缩方法都基于注意、冗余、不确定性等标准来最大化平均准确率，隐含假设所有错误成本相同。然而在实际下游任务中，错误后果往往不对称（如读错发票金额比误分类背景颜色代价大得多），因此需要一种能感知后果差异的分配策略。

Method: 采用"校准-分配"（calibrate-then-allocate）两阶段方法：离线阶段估计不同后果特性下的错误-预算曲线；在线阶段利用来自问题或任务信息的后果信号，按校准后的token预算进行分配。

Result: 在受控基准上，高后果错误从0.300降至0.133（相同token预算下），而内容驱动的分配器不优于均匀分配。该分配原则在3个密集视觉语言基准、2种预算实现机制（token删除和分辨率重分配）、2种VLM架构及多种token选择策略上均表现良好。在实际混合工作负载下，后果敏感分配将成本加权错误降低38%，同时比全分辨率推理降低约21%的延迟。

Conclusion: 均匀分配在错误代价相等时是最优的；随着代价差距拉大，将token向高后果问题转移的收益越来越大。后果敏感的分配原则在不同设置下具有很强的泛化能力，为视觉token压缩提供了更实际、更符合下游需求的新视角。

Abstract: Visual token compression for vision--language models (VLMs) has largely relied on criteria such as attention, redundancy, and uncertainty to maximize average accuracy under a fixed compute budget, implicitly assuming that all errors carry equal cost. However, the consequence of an incorrect prediction on downstream tasks is rarely symmetric: misreading an invoice amount can be far more costly than misclassifying a background color. Motivated by this, we introduce consequence-sensitive visual token compression, which allocates visual computation across requests according to their potential error costs. Our method follows a calibrate-then-allocate procedure, estimating consequence-specific error-budget curves offline and applying the calibrated token budgets online using consequence signals available from question or task information. On a controlled within-task benchmark, high- and low-consequence questions are drawn from the same document images, so content alone cannot reveal which questions are costly to get wrong. In this setting, our method reduces high-stakes errors from 0.300 to 0.133 under the same total token budget, whereas a content-driven allocator performs no better than uniform allocation. Measuring how error rates change with token budget across different cost ratios, we derive an allocation frontier: uniform allocation is optimal when errors are equally costly, and token transfer toward high-consequence questions becomes increasingly beneficial as the cost gap grows. This allocation principle generalizes well across three dense vision-language benchmarks, two budget realization mechanisms (token deletion and resolution reallocation), two VLM architectures, and multiple token selection strategies. On a realistic mixed workload, consequence-sensitive allocation reduces cost-weighted error by 38% while achieving approximately 21% lower latency than full-resolution inference.

</details>


### [181] [Rethinking Medical Landmark Localization with Prototype Learning-based Progressive Offset Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09182)
*Jingxian Xu,Yuhao Huang,Rusi Chen,Yanfeng Zhou,Dong Ni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PPOC-LL，一种参数经济的医学图像关键点定位模型，通过原型学习驱动的渐进式偏移校正实现粗到细的优化，在保证精度的同时显著降低计算成本，并在X光和超声三种数据集上验证了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学图像关键点定位方法中，多阶段细化策略虽然能缓解单阶段全局预测的解剖结构模糊性问题，但计算成本过高，限制了实际临床应用。因此需要一种既保持细化策略优势又降低计算开销的方案。

Method: PPOC-LL包含三个核心贡献：(1)多尺度动态感知策略，用于patch级特征金字塔建模，驱动粗到细的关键点优化；(2)相似性驱动的原型学习机制，捕捉信息丰富的局部语义以进行鲁棒的偏移预测，应对解剖结构相似的模式；(3)基于容差平衡的误差感知可靠性正则化，稳定模型学习并提升整体性能。

Result: 在涵盖X光和超声模态的三个数据集（两个公开、一个私有）上进行了大规模验证，覆盖头影测量、骨盆胎头和胎儿心脏关键点，实验表明PPOC-LL在精度和模型复杂度之间取得了良好的平衡。

Conclusion: PPOC-LL通过原型学习与渐进偏移校正的结合，实现了参数高效且精确的医学图像关键点定位，证明了该方法在准确性与计算效率之间的优越权衡。

Abstract: Accurate landmark localization in medical images is a fundamental step for quantitative clinical measurement and downstream analysis. Existing localization methods have advanced, among which multi-stage refinement is a superior solution. Although this strategy mitigates the anatomical ambiguity inherent in single-stage global predictions, its high computational cost limits practical applicability. In this work, we propose a parameter-economic model, PPOC-LL, which leverages Prototype learning-based Progressive Offset Correction for Landmark Localization. Our contribution is three-fold. First, to drive coarse-to-fine landmark optimization, we introduce a multi-scale dynamic perception strategy for patch-level feature pyramid modeling. Second, to effectively handle anatomically similar patterns, we design a similarity-driven prototype learning mechanism that captures informative local semantics for robust offset prediction. Last, to stabilize the model learning and improve the overall performance, we incorporate a novel error-aware reliability regularization via tolerance-based balancing. We collected a large validation cohort, including two public and one private datasets spanning X-ray and ultrasound modalities, covering cephalometric, symphysis-fetal head, and fetal heart landmarks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PPOC-LL achieves satisfactory performance with a favorable trade-off between accuracy and model complexity.

</details>


### [182] [RAGMesh with FaME-G2E: Long-Form Text-Driven 3D Face Generation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09186)
*Hao Li,Ju Dai,Feng Zhou,Mengting Shi,Haofei Wang,Zhen Song,Wei Zhou,Lei Li,Junjun Pan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RAGMesh，一个基于检索增强的细粒度文生3D人脸框架。作者首先构建了FaME-G2E多模态数据集（包含细粒度文本-网格标注和文本-blendshape配对），并提出多尺度检索融合模块（MSRF）和自适应RAG引导监督（AdaRAGS），以解决现有方法难以捕捉微妙局部形变（如眉毛张力、脸颊收缩、不对称嘴部运动）的问题，在局部几何精度和编辑可控性上超越现有SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本驱动3D人脸生成方法主要对齐全局文本语义与面部结构，难以捕捉精细的局部形变（如眉毛张力、脸颊收缩、非对称嘴部运动），导致几何保真度和编辑精度有限。此外，缺乏包含细粒度文本-网格/文本-blendshape标注的大规模数据集，限制了细粒度文本驱动人脸建模的发展。

Method: 1) 构建FaME-G2E大规模多模态数据集，包含细粒度文本-网格标注及文本-blendshape配对；2) 提出RAGMesh检索增强框架，包含两个核心模块：MSRF（多尺度检索融合）在blendshape空间中检索并融合语义一致的全局与区域面部先验，抑制冲突的局部形变同时保持连贯的形变模式；AdaRAGS（自适应RAG引导监督）作为区域感知约束，显式对齐文本语义与对应面部区域，增强区域可控性和编辑精度。

Result: 在FaME-G2E数据集上的大量实验表明，RAGMesh在局部几何精度、文本引导可控性、区域编辑精度和推理效率方面均优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 通过利用文本相关的几何先验与检索增强机制，RAGMesh有效解决了细粒度文本驱动3D人脸生成中局部形变难以捕捉的问题，为高质量面部合成与编辑提供了新范式。代码和数据集将公开发布。

Abstract: Text-driven 3D face generation and editing remains challenging due to the difficulty of translating long-form descriptions into fine-grained facial geometry. Existing methods primarily align global textual semantics with facial structures but often struggle to capture subtle local deformations, such as eyebrow tension, cheek contraction, and asymmetric mouth motions, resulting in limited geometric fidelity and editing precision. To facilitate fine-grained text-driven facial modeling, we first construct FaME-G2E, a large-scale multimodal dataset containing detailed text--mesh annotations and paired text--blendshape samples for unified 3D facial generation and editing. Based on this dataset, we propose RAGMesh, a retrieval-augmented framework that leverages text-correlated geometric priors to improve high-fidelity facial synthesis and editing. Specifically, the Multi-Scale Retrieval Fusion (MSRF) module retrieves semantically consistent global and regional facial priors and fuses them in the blendshape space, suppressing conflicting local deformations while preserving coherent deformation patterns. Furthermore, we introduce Adaptive RAG-guided Supervision (AdaRAGS), a region-aware constraint that explicitly aligns textual semantics with corresponding facial regions, enhancing regional controllability and editing accuracy. Extensive experiments on FaME-G2E demonstrate that RAGMesh achieves superior performance over state-of-the-art methods in local geometric accuracy, text-guided controllability, regional editing precision, and inference efficiency. Video demo is available at https://youtu.be/Yr0_XkpWcNk, and the source code and dataset will be released upon paper acceptance.

</details>


### [183] [NBA_Streaming: A Large-Scale Benchmark for Fine-Grained Basketball Commentary Generation in Continuous Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09200)
*Lifang Wu,Yuyang Wu,Yangdong Gao,Fengyu Liu,Ya Jing,Liang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出NBA_Streaming基准，包含307小时篮球直播和约3.5万个时序对齐事件，用于在线细粒度篮球解说生成。同时提出因果两阶段框架（先定位完成事件，再以球为中心的语义锚定），在在线时序、事实锚定和细粒度描述上显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解说生成方法主要针对预分割片段或完整视频，不适用于连续直播流；现有数据集对球员身份、细粒度动作、事件属性和连贯事件链的标注有限，限制了生成解说的事实丰富度。

Method: 构建NBA_Streaming大规模基准（307小时、约3.5万事件，含事件边界、球员身份、细粒度动作、事件链及自然语言解说标注），并提出因果两阶段框架：先进行完成优先的事件定位（从观测流中识别完整事件），再进行以球为中心的语义锚定（组织场景、事件、身份和动作线索以生成解说）。

Result: 实验表明NBA_Streaming难度较大，现有基线在在线时序、事实锚定和细粒度描述方面表现不佳；所提框架在所有强基线上均取得一致提升，但剩余差距表明该基准仍有很大探索空间。

Conclusion: NBA_Streaming作为流式体育视频理解与生成的有价值基准，推动了从孤立片段到连续流的解说生成研究，其因果两阶段框架验证了有效性，但现有差距凸显了未来工作方向。

Abstract: Live basketball commentary generation requires determining when an event is sufficiently observable and describing it before subsequent events unfold. However, existing methods are primarily designed for pre-segmented clips or complete videos, making them unsuitable for continuous streams. Existing datasets also provide limited supervision for player identities, fine-grained actions, event attributes, and coherent event chains, restricting the factual richness of generated commentary. To address these limitations, we introduce NBA_Streaming, a large-scale benchmark for online fine-grained basketball commentary generation. It contains 307 hours of basketball broadcasts and approximately 35K temporally aligned events, with annotations of event boundaries, player identities, fine-grained actions, event chains, and natural-language commentary. By moving from isolated clips to continuous streams, NBA_Streaming enables unified evaluation of event localization, response reliability, factual grounding, and commentary quality under causal constraints. We further propose a causal two-stage framework that combines completion-first localization with ball-centric semantic grounding, enabling the system to identify complete events from observed streams and organize scene, event, identity, and action cues for commentary generation. Extensive experiments reveal the difficulty of NBA_Streaming, where existing baselines struggle with online timing, factual grounding, and fine-grained description. Our framework consistently improves over strong alternatives, while the remaining gap highlights NBA_Streaming as a valuable benchmark for streaming sports video understanding and generation.

</details>


### [184] [PatchHead: Learning Spatial Patch Evidence for Generalizable AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09223)
*Shengbo Qi,Hongyi Fang,Benjia Zhou,Rui Mao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PatchHead，一种轻量级空间聚合头，通过保留DINO patch token的二维空间结构而非使用全局CLS token聚合，来提升AI生成图像检测器的跨数据集泛化能力，平均准确率提升3.0个百分点，最差情况准确率提升6.9个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成图像检测器在训练和测试图像来自不同生成器或数据集时泛化能力差。尽管DINO等视觉基础模型能产生丰富的空间表征，但现有检测器通常只用全局聚合的CLS token进行分类，这可能会掩盖在空间中分布广泛的生成痕迹。作者假设全局聚合DINO特征到单一CLS token会丢失空间分布信息。

Method: 引入PatchHead，一个轻量级空间聚合头，保留DINO patch token的二维组织结构，并整合相邻区域的证据。训练时冻结预训练的DINO骨干网络，仅优化插入的LoRA适配器、PatchHead和辅助投影头。

Result: 在九个跨数据集基准上，PatchHead在七个数据集上排名第一，两个上排名第二。相比最强先前方法，平均平衡准确率从91.6%提升到94.6%（+3.0点），最差情况准确率从82.4%提升到89.4%（+6.9点），仅增加8.6%可训练参数和0.08%额外FLOPs。

Conclusion: 定性分析表明PatchHead（i）减少类条件域差异，（ii）将表征从内容主导的显著性转向空间分布的认证证据。这些观察提供了表征层面的解释，说明为何空间patch聚合比单一CLS全局表征在跨生成器和数据集间转移更可靠。

Abstract: AI-generated image detectors generalize poorly when their training and test images originate from different generators or datasets. Despite the rich spatial representations produced by vision foundation models like DINO, existing detectors typically classify images using only the globally aggregated CLS token. We hypothesize that globally aggregating DINO features into a single CLS token obscures spatially distributed generation traces. To test this hypothesis, we introduce PatchHead, a lightweight spatial aggregation head that preserves the two-dimensional organization of DINO patch tokens and integrates evidence across neighboring regions. During training, we freeze the pretrained DINO backbone and optimize only the inserted LoRA adapters, PatchHead, and auxiliary projection head. Across nine cross-dataset benchmarks spanning manually curated and in-the-wild settings, PatchHead ranks first on seven datasets and second on the remaining two. It improves the strongest prior method from 91.6% to 94.6% in average balanced accuracy (+3.0 points) and raises the worst-case accuracy from 82.4% to 89.4% (+6.9 points), while introducing only 8.6% more trainable parameters and 0.08% additional FLOPs. Further qualitative analysis suggests that PatchHead (i) reduces class-conditional domain discrepancy, and (ii) redirects the representation from content-dominated saliency toward spatially distributed authenticity evidence. Together, these observations provide a representation-level account of why spatial patch aggregation transfers more reliably across generators and datasets than a single CLS-based global representation. Our code and models will be made available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [185] [RL-Native Distillation: Exploiting Scored Trajectories for Few-Step Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09226)
*Yuhan Li,Fangao Zeng,Sicong Kang,Mengfei Xu,Hao Zhou,Wei Li,Pipei Huang,Bingbing Ni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: REST是一个单阶段的RL-蒸馏协同训练框架，利用扩散RL生成的有奖励打分的轨迹作为蒸馏监督源，通过优势调制蒸馏(AMD)让少步学生模型匹配甚至超越40步RL教师模型，训练成本仅为纯RL的25%以下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的文本到图像生成中，基于RL的奖励对齐和少步蒸馏通常是顺序执行的，这增加了训练成本，并且存在压缩过程中奖励收益损失的风险。作者希望将两者统一为一个单阶段流程，避免累积误差并提高效率。

Method: 提出REST框架：将解耦的学生模型附加到任意RL教师模型上，学生从教师不断演化的rollout轨迹进行分段学习，同时保持教师优化不变。核心创新是AMD(优势调制蒸馏)，将rollout优势转换为基础蒸馏损失上的有符号权重，增强高奖励轨迹的监督并轻微排斥低奖励轨迹。该框架无需额外图像rollout、无需单独蒸馏数据集、无需对抗训练。

Result: 实验覆盖组合生成、视觉文本渲染和人类偏好对齐，REST实现的少步CFG-free推理匹配或超越其40步RL教师，额外训练成本低于纯RL的25%。在DrawBench PickScore上比RTDMD提升0.82，且仅需其五分之一的训练迭代。

Conclusion: REST证明了从RL-native视角出发，将rollout轨迹的中间状态视为蒸馏监督来源而非副产品，可以高效统一奖励对齐与少步蒸馏，实现轻量级、即插即用的单一阶段训练框架。

Abstract: Efficient text-to-image generation requires both reinforcement-learning (RL)-based reward alignment and few-step distillation, yet these procedures are typically performed sequentially, increasing training cost and risking the loss of reward gains during compression. We instead take an RL-native perspective: diffusion RL already generates reward-scored finite-step trajectories, whose intermediate states provide a natural source of distillation supervision rather than a disposable byproduct of sampling. Based on this insight, we propose REST (Reward-Enhanced Scored-Trajectory Distillation), a single-stage RL-distillation co-training framework that attaches a decoupled student to an arbitrary RL teacher. The student learns segment-wise from the teacher's evolving rollout trajectories while leaving the original teacher optimization unchanged. To prevent uniform imitation from preserving undesirable low-reward behaviors, we further introduce Advantage-Modulated Distillation (AMD), which transforms rollout advantages into signed weights over a base distillation loss. AMD strengthens supervision from preferred trajectories and mildly repels the student from low-reward ones. The resulting framework is lightweight and plug-and-play, requires no extra image rollouts, no separate distillation dataset, and no adversarial training. Experiments on compositional generation, visual text rendering, and human-preference alignment show that REST enables few-step CFG-free inference that matches or surpasses its 40-step RL teacher, with an overall additional training cost below 25% over pure RL. REST improves DrawBench PickScore over RTDMD by 0.82 while requiring only one-fifth of the training iterations.

</details>


### [186] [BAG: Budget-Aware Gating for Diffusion Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09231)
*Tong Zhao,Mingkun Lei,Yucheng Han,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BAG（Budget-Aware Gating）是一种新的扩散缓存策略，通过轻量级门控网络动态决定每一步是完整计算还是复用缓存特征，结合全局预算节奏与实例自适应特征复用，并利用离线到在线的调度蒸馏训练策略，在FLUX.1-dev和Wan2.1上的多种加速档位中优于SOTA缓存方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散缓存方法存在根本性权衡：在线启发式缺乏全局预算意识，而静态调度缺乏实例自适应性，无法灵活适应变化的运行时预算约束。

Method: 提出BAG缓存策略，采用轻量级门控网络，在每个去噪步骤中联合基于预算状态和局部轨迹反馈动态决定执行完整计算或复用缓存特征。通过离线到在线的调度蒸馏训练策略，将离线搜索得到的调度决策转移为紧凑的在线门控。

Result: 在FLUX.1-dev和Wan2.1上的大量实验表明，BAG在各种加速档位中一致优于最先进的缓存方法，并且在不同分辨率、种子和引导尺度下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: BAG成功弥合了在线启发式与静态调度之间的差距，统一了全局预算节奏与动态实例自适应特征复用，为DiT加速提供了更灵活高效的缓存方案。

Abstract: Diffusion caching is a lightweight strategy that accelerates Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) by reusing intermediate features across denoising steps, but existing paradigms face a fundamental trade-off: online heuristics lack global budget awareness, whereas static schedules lack instance adaptivity and fail to flexibly adapt to varying runtime budget constraints. To bridge this gap, we present BAG (Budget-Aware Gating), a novel caching policy that unifies global budget pacing with dynamic, instance-adaptive feature reuse. Rather than relying on hand-crafted rules, BAG employs a lightweight gating network that dynamically decides whether to execute a full computation or reuse cached features at each step by jointly conditioning on the budget state and local trajectory feedback. We train this policy via offline-to-online schedule distillation, transferring the decision-making of offline-searched schedules into a compact online gate. Extensive experiments on FLUX.1-dev and Wan2.1 demonstrate that BAG consistently outperforms state-of-the-art caching methods across various speedup tiers while remaining robust across different resolutions, seeds, and guidance scales. Code will be released.

</details>


### [187] [RealDenseFace: Real-time Monocular 3D Face Reconstruction from Dense UV-space Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09238)
*Linzhou Li,Tianjia Shao,Kun Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RealDenseFace，一种基于优化的实时单目3D人脸重建方法。通过将3DMM拟合公式化为非线性最小二乘问题，并使用定制的高斯-牛顿求解器在几次迭代内收敛，配合网络预测的稠密UV空间对应图和相对深度图，实现了80+ FPS的在线跟踪速度和比此前优化方法快20倍以上的离线重建速度，在NeRSemble SVFR基准上达到最先进精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于优化的单目3D人脸重建方法虽然通过将3DMM拟合到网络预测的稠密先验获得高保真度，但优化阶段计算开销巨大，每张图像需要数十秒，无法满足实时应用需求。

Method: 方法分为两个阶段：(1) 网络从单张RGB图像预测两个稠密UV空间图——用于UV到图像对齐的对应图，以及用于沿视角方向几何约束的相对深度图；(2) 使用定制的Gauss-Newton求解器，将3DMM拟合建模为非线性最小二乘问题，对从这些图中在顶点UV坐标处采样的逐顶点目标进行拟合，仅需几次迭代即可收敛。该方法支持单图拟合、离线序列重建和在线跟踪三种设置。

Result: 在NeRSemble SVFR基准上达到最先进精度；在线跟踪器运行速度达80+ FPS；离线序列重建比此前基于优化的方法快20倍以上。

Conclusion: 通过精心设计的Gauss-Newton求解器与稠密UV空间预测相结合，成功弥合了基于优化方法的高保真度与实时性能之间的鸿沟，证明了优化式3D人脸重建也可以达到实时速度。

Abstract: Recent monocular 3D face reconstruction methods achieve high fidelity by fitting a 3D Morphable Model (3DMM) to dense priors predicted by networks, but the optimization stage is computationally expensive, often taking tens of seconds per image. We present RealDenseFace, a real-time optimization-based 3D face reconstruction method with dense UV-space network predictions. Our key idea is to formulate 3DMM fitting as a nonlinear least-squares problem and solve it with a tailored Gauss-Newton solver that converges in only a few iterations. The reconstruction is conducted in two stages. In the first stage, the network predicts two dense UV-space maps from a single RGB image: a correspondence map for UV-to-image alignment, and a relative-depth map for geometric constraints along the viewing direction. In the second stage, the solver fits per-vertex targets sampled from these maps at the vertex UV coordinates. The solver supports all three reconstruction settings: single-image fitting, offline sequence reconstruction, and online tracking. Our method achieves state-of-the-art accuracy on the NeRSemble SVFR benchmark. The online tracker runs at 80+ FPS, and the offline sequence reconstruction is over 20 times faster than previous optimization-based baselines.

</details>


### [188] [In-Loop Model Adaptation with Coupled Latent-Noise Guidance for High-Fidelity Subject-Driven Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09244)
*Yushun Tang,Weiming Chen,Siyi Liu,Yi Zhang,Feng Wu,Zhihai He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为In-Loop Model Adaptation (IMA)的新方法，用于基于主体的文本到图像生成。该方法在图像生成的每一步实时适配核心扩散模型，无需事先用参考图像训练模型，通过DDIM反演链和文本生成链之间的掩码潜在一致性损失与噪声正则化损失来指导模型适配，从而在保持主体身份的同时与文本提示对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主体驱动的文本到图像生成面临核心挑战：当参考图像变化时，扩散模型无法高效适应不同视觉上下文同时维持主体身份一致性。现有方法要么依赖大规模领域特定数据集训练，要么需要数百次迭代微调后才生成，效率低下且成本高昂。

Method: IMA方法通过三条核心机制实现：(1) 建立DDIM反演链将参考图像映射为潜在序列，以及仅由文本提示驱动的生成链；(2) 引入掩码潜在一致性损失和噪声正则化损失，刻画每一步生成中扩散模型与两条链之间的潜在-噪声差异；(3) 利用耦合的潜在-噪声损失在生成过程中实时指导模型适配，实现主体身份保持与文本对齐的平衡，无需生成前的任何模型训练。

Result: 大量实验表明，IMA方法显著提升了基于主体的文本到图像生成的性能，在保持主体身份一致性和文本提示对齐方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 本文验证了在生成过程中实时适配扩散模型这一新范式的有效性，无需生成前的训练或微调，即可实现高效的主体驱动文本到图像生成。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models have achieved remarkable success in generating high-quality images from a given text prompt. Subject-driven generation aims to synthesize customized images to mimic the appearance of subjects in given reference images within different visual contexts specified by the text prompts. The central challenge here is that, when the reference image changes, the diffusion model cannot efficiently adapt to different visual contexts while consistently maintaining the subject identity. Existing methods either train the model with a large domain-specific dataset or fine-tune the model using the reference image for hundreds of iterations before actual image generation. In this work, we explore a new approach, called \textit{In-Loop Model Adaptation} (IMA), which adapts the core diffusion model at each generation step during the actual process of image generation, without being trained on the reference image before the generation process. To this end, we establish a DDIM inversion chain that maps the reference image to a sequence of latent, as well as a text-to-image generation chain which generates the image from the text prompt only. We then introduce a masked latent consistency loss and a noise regularization loss to characterize the latent-noise difference between the diffusion model and these two chains at each generation step. This coupled latent-noise loss is used to guide the in-loop model adaptation to preserve the subject identity specified by the reference image while maintaining accurate alignment with the text prompt, resulting in high-fidelity text-to-image generation. Our extensive experiments demonstrate that our proposed IMA method significantly improves the performance of subject-driven text-to-image generation.

</details>


### [189] [Task-Adaptive 3D Cross-Field MRI Translation via Field-Conditioned Content-Style Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09264)
*Haowen Pang,Yingqi Hao,Pengli Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于场条件内容-风格预训练的3D无配对跨场MRI翻译框架，用于解决MRIxFields 2026挑战中的跨磁场强度图像翻译问题。该框架通过解耦解剖内容与场依赖对比特征，结合对抗学习、循环一致性等约束，实现了从0.1T/1.5T/3T到5T/7T的目标场图像合成，同时保持三维解剖结构完整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁场强度是MRI中领域偏移的主要来源，影响信噪比、组织对比度、空间细节和解剖边界可见性。跨场MRI翻译的核心挑战在于同一受试者在多个场强下的配对采集数据极少，难以通过监督学习训练。

Method: 提出3D无配对跨场MRI翻译框架，包含5个核心组件：3D内容编码器、3D风格编码器、场条件风格生成器、AdaIN调制解码器和多场判别器。采用两步策略：先在所有可用场强上通过解耦解剖内容与场依赖对比特征学习可控的场间翻译预训练，再针对具体任务目标域进行微调适配。训练采用对抗学习、循环一致性、身份、内容、风格和多样性约束。

Result: 在涵盖五个场强和三种MRI模态的MRIxFields数据上评估，在配对测试数据上展示了框架能适应三种挑战设置（Any-to-7T、0.1T-to-High、Any-to-Any），同时保持合成体积中的三维解剖结构。

Conclusion: 该框架通过场条件内容-风格预训练有效解决了无配对跨场MRI翻译问题，代码已公开，为MRI多场强翻译提供了实用且可复现的方案。

Abstract: Magnetic field strength is a major source of domain shift in magnetic resonance imaging (MRI), affecting signal-to-noise ratio, tissue contrast, spatial detail, and the visibility of anatomical boundaries. The MRIxFields 2026 challenge investigates this problem through cross-field MRI translation across acquisitions at 0.1T, 1.5T, 3T, 5T, and 7T. Its three tasks, Any-to-7T, 0.1T-to-High, and Any-to-Any synthesis, require the generation of target-field image characteristics while preserving subject-specific anatomy. This problem is particularly challenging because paired acquisitions of the same subject across multiple field strengths are rarely available for training. We propose a 3D unpaired cross-field MRI translation framework based on field-conditioned content-style pretraining. The proposed framework first learns controllable field-to-field translation across all available field strengths by disentangling anatomical content from field-dependent contrast characteristics. The pretrained backbone is then adapted to task-specific target domains. Our model comprises a 3D content encoder, a 3D style encoder, a field-conditioned style generator, an AdaIN-modulated decoder, and a multi-field discriminator. Adversarial learning encourages realistic target-field appearance, while cycle-consistency, identity, content, style, and diversity constraints promote anatomical fidelity and controllable translation. We evaluate the proposed method on MRIxFields data spanning five field strengths and three MRI modalities. Experiments on paired test data demonstrate that the framework can adapt to the three challenge settings while preserving three-dimensional anatomical structure in the synthesized volumes. The implementation code is publicly available at https://github.com/Idea89560041/3D-MRI-Field-Translation.

</details>


### [190] [Did the Grid Erase the Event? EndoClock for Auditing Medical World-Model Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09266)
*Yarin Udi,Tom Sharon-Shahak,Roee Masad,Dan Pri-Tal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出医学世界模型在多模态同步预处理中可能丢失任务相关证据的问题，引入四机制分类法（采样值、网格单元更新模式、原生时序、外部采集通道）来表征证据存活位置，并提出EndoClock作为保守的预训练审计工具，通过超声心动图的案例说明同步可能抹除关键信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学世界模型通常将多模态记录同步到固定频率的网格上进行预处理，但每个数据流有自身的观察时钟。当该时钟依赖于潜在或采集状态时（内源性时钟），同步并非中性操作，可能在模型看到数据之前就已抹除任务相关的关键证据。现有方法未对这种同步带来的信息丢失进行系统分析和预警。

Method: 本文提出一个四机制分类法（taxonomy），将区分目标事件或状态所需的证据可能存活的四个位置进行分类：采样值中、网格单元更新模式中、原生时序中、或仅存在于外部采集通道中。基于该分类法，构建EndoClock作为保守的预训练审计工具，它报告当前证据支持下最低级别的"见证承载表示"（witness-bearing representation），或者在无法确定任何机制时标记为"未解决"（unresolved）。

Result: 在超声心动图中验证了该失败模式：当脉冲波多普勒采集期间B模式视频写入停止，对应的测量事件仅记录在外部采集日志中。这展示了同步预处理可能完全抹除任务所需证据的实际案例。

Conclusion: 本文是初步的失败预警和可执行的审计工具。其核心实践建议是：保留原生观察过程足够长的时间，以确定同步是否已抹除目标任务所需的信息。

Abstract: Medical world models commonly learn from multimodal recordings synchronized onto a fixed-rate grid. This preprocessing resamples each native stream onto a shared time axis. Each stream has an observation clock that governs when observations are emitted or updated. When this clock depends on the latent or acquisition state, it is endogenous. In such settings, synchronization may not be neutral and can erase task-relevant evidence before the model sees the data. We introduce a four-regime taxonomy that characterizes where the evidence needed to distinguish a target event or state survives. The relevant witness may remain in the sampled values, in grid-cell update patterns, in native timing, or only in an external acquisition channel. EndoClock operationalizes this taxonomy as a conservative pretraining audit. It reports the lowest witness-bearing representation supported by the available evidence, or unresolved when no regime can be established. We illustrate this failure in echocardiography, where B-mode video write-outs cease during pulsed-wave Doppler acquisition while the corresponding measurement events remain recorded only in an external acquisition log. This work is a preliminary failure alert and executable audit. Its practical message is to preserve the native observation process long enough to determine whether synchronization has erased information required by the intended task.

</details>


### [191] [GRASP: Granularity-Aware Region Alignment and Semantic Prototype Learning for Fine-Grained Cross-Modal Understanding in Drone Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09270)
*Jiahui Cui,Yan Zhao,Kan Wei,Enze Zhu,Peirong Zhang,Lei Wang,Yiru Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GRASP学习框架，通过区域聚焦对齐(RFA)和语义扰动增强匹配(SPEM)两种协同策略，解决无人机视角下细粒度跨模态理解中的宏观跨模态焦点错位和微观视觉同构两大挑战，在GeoText-1652基准和ERA数据集上取得有竞争力的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机场景的广视角和俯视角度带来双重挑战：宏观层面，视觉表示中过多的背景杂波导致跨模态焦点错位，模型倾向于关注全局环境相似性而非具体物体细节；微观层面，视觉同构造成歧义，候选目标具有相似几何结构但仅在细微属性上有差异。

Method: 提出GRASP框架，包含两个协同策略：1) 区域聚焦对齐(RFA)，促进以物体为中心的跨模态对齐并抑制背景干扰；2) 语义扰动增强匹配(SPEM)，利用前景纯化的语义原型码本(SPC)构建语义扰动负样本以实现细粒度语义判别。

Result: 在GeoText-1652基准测试和未见过的ERA数据集上，GRASP在无人机视角细粒度图文检索任务中取得了有竞争力的性能，验证了其在航空场景跨模态理解中的有效性。

Conclusion: GRASP通过区域级对齐和语义原型扰动两种互补策略，有效解决了无人机视角下跨模态理解的宏观焦点错位和微观视觉同构问题，为航空场景的细粒度跨模态理解提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Fine-grained cross-modal understanding in drone views is essential for aerial vision-language navigation. However, the inherent wide field of view and overhead perspective of drone scenarios impose dual challenges on vision-language understanding. At the macro level, overwhelming background clutter in visual representations leads to Cross-Modal Focus Misalignment, where the model prioritizes global environmental similarities over specific object details. At the micro level, Visual Isomorphism creates ambiguity, where candidates share similar geometric structures yet differ only in subtle attributes. To address these challenges, we propose the Granularity-Aware Region Alignment and Semantic Prototype (GRASP) learning framework, enhancing discriminative capability through two synergistic strategies. Specifically, we introduce Region-Focused Alignment (RFA) to promote object-centric cross-modal alignment while suppressing background interference. Concurrently, to tackle visual isomorphism, we propose Semantic Perturbation Enhanced Matching (SPEM), which leverages a foreground-purified Semantic Prototype Codebook (SPC) to construct semantically perturbed negatives for fine-grained semantic discrimination. Extensive experiments on the GeoText-1652 benchmark and the unseen ERA dataset demonstrate that GRASP achieves competitive performance in drone-view fine-grained image-text retrieval, validating its effectiveness for cross-modal understanding in aerial scenarios. Our code implementation is available at https://github.com/UCAS-JC/GRASP.

</details>


### [192] [UniDFKD: A Unified Semantic Prior Framework for Architecture-Agnostic Data-Free Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09287)
*Xuewan He,Tong Chu,Zihan Cheng,Yuchen Su,Qianxin Xia,Guoming Lu,Jielei Wang,Wen Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UniDFKD，一个统一的无数据知识蒸馏框架，用显式、与架构无关的语义先验替代依赖架构特定统计量（如Batch Normalization）的方法，通过三个维度（类别语义条件、空间语义锚定、空间语义蒸馏）控制合成与蒸馏全过程，在CNN和ViT上均取得超过20%的平均绝对性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DFKD方法严重依赖架构特定的统计先验（如BN统计量）来引导数据合成，但这类先验在ViT等现代架构中往往不存在，导致合成数据语义质量下降、性能灾难性退化。因此需要一种与架构无关的统一框架。

Method: 提出UniDFKD框架，包含三个维度的设计：(1) 类别语义条件（CSC）用语言派生嵌入持续调制生成器，明确"合成什么"以捕捉语义多样性；(2) 空间语义锚定（SSA）将教师的空间归因锚定到高斯先验，明确"证据属于哪里"；(3) 空间语义蒸馏（SSD）显式对齐师生空间证据与预测，控制"知识如何迁移"。

Result: 在CNNs和ViTs上的广泛实验表明，UniDFKD在同构和异构设置下均取得了新的最先进结果，平均绝对性能提升超过20%。

Conclusion: UniDFKD通过将架构相关的统计先验替换为架构无关的语义先验，成功解决了DFKD在ViT等现代架构上的退化问题，验证了语义级先验比统计级先验更具普适性和有效性。

Abstract: Data-Free Knowledge Distillation (DFKD) transfers knowledge from a pretrained teacher model to a compact student model by synthesizing semantically informative data, eliminating the need for access to the original training dataset. Existing DFKD methods rely heavily on architecture-specific statistical priors (e.g., Batch Normalization statistics) to guide data synthesis, however, such architecture-dependent priors are often absent in modern architectures such as Vision Transformers (ViTs), resulting in degraded semantic quality of the synthesized data and consequently catastrophic performance degradation. In this paper, we propose \emph{UniDFKD}, a unified data-free knowledge distillation framework that replaces architecture-specific statistics with explicit, architecture-agnostic semantic priors. \emph{UniDFKD} governs the entire synthesis-distillation pipeline along three dimensions: (1) Categorical Semantic Conditioning (CSC) defines \emph{what} to synthesize by persistently modulating the generator with language-derived embeddings to capture semantic diversity; (2) Spatial Semantic Anchoring (SSA) dictates \emph{where} evidence belongs by anchoring the teacher's spatial attributions to a Gaussian prior; and (3) Spatial Semantic Distillation (SSD) controls \emph{how} knowledge is transferred by explicitly aligning teacher-student spatial evidence alongside predictions. Extensive experiments across CNNs and ViTs demonstrate that UniDFKD establishes a new state-of-the-art, outperforming existing methods by an average absolute margin of over 20\% in both homogeneous and heterogeneous settings.

</details>


### [193] [Bootstrapping Vision-Language Model for Hysteroscopic Surgical Scene Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09302)
*Jun Huang,Meiyi Chen,Zijie Yue,Yuhang Xiao,Fang Li,Hanli Wang,Xiaowen Tong,Yi Guo,Miaojing Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的宫腔镜手术场景分割方法VLM-hyster，能够对宫腔镜手术场景中的15类目标进行像素级定位，在大型多中心数据集上显著优于现有AI模型，并经妇科医生评估及多中心前瞻性验证展现出良好的鲁棒性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宫腔镜手术场景分割对理解术中环境和计算机辅助干预至关重要，但该任务面临两大挑战：不同病变之间形态高度相似，以及手术视频中存在的镜面反射、运动模糊和液体遮挡等伪影，现有方法难以有效解决这些问题。

Method: 提出VLM-hyster框架，包含三个关键组件：(1) 利用预训练图像编码器进行鲁棒视觉特征提取的分割骨干网络，配合基于Transformer的解码器进行密集预测；(2) 为15个类别设计类别特定的文本提示；(3) 引入掩码蒸馏分支，过滤与文本提示相关性低的视觉特征，使模型更聚焦于类别特定的图像区域，从而提升分割性能。同时构建了包含4,020张高分辨率图像及详细掩码标注的大型多中心宫腔镜手术场景数据集。

Result: VLM-hyster在宫腔镜手术场景分割任务上大幅超越现有最先进AI模型；通过妇科医生的广泛评估以及多中心、前瞻性验证，证实了该方法的鲁棒性和泛化性，展现了在宫腔镜手术中实现手术器械和病变AI辅助定位的巨大潜力。

Conclusion: VLM-hyster作为首个基于VLM的宫腔镜手术场景分割方法，通过视觉-语言对齐和掩码蒸馏机制有效解决了病变形态相似和手术伪影干扰问题，展示了在宫腔镜手术中辅助器械与病变定位的临床应用前景，代码已开源。

Abstract: Hysteroscopic surgical scene segmentation plays a pivotal role in understanding the hysteroscopic intraoperative environment as well as computer-assisted intervention. However, this task presents unique challenges due to the high morphological similarity among different lesions and the presence of artifacts such as specular reflections, motion blur, and fluid occlusions in surgical videos. In this work, we propose the first vision-language model (VLM)-based hysteroscopic surgical scene segmentation method, which performs pixel-wise localization for fifteen representative categories in hysteroscopic surgical scenes. Our VLM-hyster has a segmentation backbone that utilizes the pretrained image encoder for robust visual feature extraction, coupled with a transformer-based decoder for dense prediction. Moreover, we design category-specific text prompts and incorporate a masked distillation branch to filter out visual features with low correlation to the text prompts, enabling the model to focus more effectively on category-specific image regions and thereby enhancing segmentation performance. We collect a large multicentric hysteroscopic surgical scene dataset, containing 4,020 high-resolution images with detailed mask annotations, for model training and evaluation. Experimental results demonstrate that VLM-hyster substantially outperforms state-of-the-art AI models. Furthermore, extensive assessments by gynecologists, as well as multicentre and prospective validations, demonstrate VLM-hyster's robustness and generalizability. The results suggest that VLM-hyster earns considerable potential in enabling AI-assisted localization of surgical instruments and lesions in hysteroscopic surgeries. Code is available at https://github.com/viscom-tongji/VLM-hyster.

</details>


### [194] [Degraded Infrared Small Object Detection via Degradation-Adapted Physics-Guided Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09311)
*Xinkai Lu,Wenjun Chen,Yi Li,Yi Chang,Luxin Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DAISOD框架，一种自适应退化的红外小目标检测方法，能够识别退化类型和严重程度，通过专属分支处理并用物理模型指导恢复，避免过度恢复抹除小目标，在多样化退化数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红外小目标检测在雾、非均匀性等退化条件下，目标-背景对比度被抑制，检测难度大幅增加。现有方法主要依赖图像恢复作为预处理，但通常针对特定退化类型设计，无法泛化到不同退化场景。

Method: DAISOD框架包含三个步骤：1) 识别退化的类型和严重程度；2) 通过专属分支自适应处理退化；3) 融合各分支结果用于后续检测。此外引入物理引导的恢复机制，显式估计退化参数并通过物理模型去除退化影响，避免过度恢复导致小目标被抹除。

Result: 构建了覆盖多种退化类型和程度的退化红外小目标检测数据集，大量实验表明DAISOD在各种退化条件下均优于最先进的方法。

Conclusion: DAISOD通过退化自适应和物理引导恢复的结合，有效提升了红外小目标检测在多种退化条件下的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Infrared small object detection has made significant progress in recent years. However, degradations such as fog and nonuniformity can suppress target-background contrast, substantially increasing detection difficulty. Existing methods mainly rely on image restoration as preprocessing, but they are typically designed for specific degradation types and fail to generalize to varying degradations. To alleviate this, we propose DAISOD, a degradation-adapted infrared small object detection framework for robust detection under different degradations. DAISOD first identifies the type and severity of degradations, then adapts the processing via dedicated branches, and finally fuses the results for subsequent detection. Moreover, a physics-guided restoration mechanism is incorporated to explicitly estimate degradation parameters and remove degradation effects through physical models, avoiding excessive restoration that may erase small targets. Moreover, we construct a degraded infrared small object detection dataset covering diverse degradation types and levels. Extensive experiments show that DAISOD outperforms state-of-the-art methods under various degradation conditions.

</details>


### [195] [MemeMind: Reference-Guided Trace Construction for Offline Context Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09316)
*Run Yang,Weihang Wang,Boheng Sheng,Yuchen He,Jielei Zhang,Pengyu Chen,Zhiyu Wu,Qiang Sun,Huyang Sun,Longwen Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MemeMind是一个离线上下文优化框架，通过利用离线参考答案来恢复失败rollout中的缺失经验。它包含TraceBuilder（构建工具追踪）和ToolGuide（生成工具指南）两个组件，在动漫、漫画和游戏模因解释任务上显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线上下文优化通过修正指令和示例来改进agent，但在某些查询中所有rollout都失败时，优化器看不到成功使用工具到达正确答案的示例，导致经验缺失。现有方法无法处理这种"原生rollout组全部失败"的情况。

Method: MemeMind包含两个核心组件：(1) TraceBuilder：利用离线参考答案，识别所需证据，执行文本搜索、图像检索和视觉定位，验证工具追踪后将其加入适应缓冲区；(2) ToolGuide：将收集的追踪总结为共享指南和各工具的独立指令。参考答案和构建的追踪仅用于适应阶段，推理时使用学习到的指南并保持模型冻结。

Result: 在MemeX基准（1000个模因）上，MemeMind在Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B上比最强上下文优化基线提升22.0%和21.1%，在Qwen3-VL-235B-A22B上提升8.1%和8.0%。消融实验和保留追踪显示，为失败组构建成功的工具使用提供了最大组件增益，并在推理时产生更有效的证据获取。

Conclusion: 为失败的rollout组构建成功的工具追踪是离线上下文优化中最关键的改进来源，MemeMind通过引入参考答案驱动的追踪构建有效解决了这一问题，并在具有挑战性的模因解释任务上验证了方法有效性。

Abstract: Offline context optimization improves an agent by revising its instructions and examples while keeping the model frozen. This approach learns from rollouts on an adaptation set, but some queries produce only failed rollouts. In these cases, the optimizer sees no successful example of how the available tools can reach the correct answer. We introduce MemeMind, which uses an offline reference answer to recover this missing experience. TraceBuilder identifies the evidence required by the reference, executes text search, image retrieval, and visual grounding, and verifies the resulting tool trace before adding it to the adaptation buffer. ToolGuide then summarizes the collected traces into a shared guide and separate instructions for each tool. The reference answers and constructed traces are used only during adaptation, while inference uses the learned guides with a frozen model. We study this problem through Anime, Comic, and Game meme interpretation. These memes combine edited and ambiguous visual content, overlaid text, long tail franchise knowledge, and culture specific references. Their interpretation can require coordinated visual grounding, image retrieval, and text search, making them a demanding setting in which native rollout groups may fail together. We evaluate MemeMind on MemeX, a benchmark of 1,000 such memes annotated by experts. Across two Qwen3-VL models, two language partitions, and two independent judges, MemeMind improves over the strongest context optimization baseline by 22.0% and 21.1% on Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B, and by 8.1% and 8.0% on Qwen3-VL-235B-A22B under GPT-5 judging. Ablations and held out traces show that constructing successful tool use for failed groups provides the largest component gain and produces more effective evidence acquisition at inference time.

</details>


### [196] [Warp-free Cross-view Geo-localization via Feature-space Consensus Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09321)
*Zhuo Song,Lian Xu,Runqing Jiang,Yongjian Zhang,Kunhong Li,Ye Zhang,Yulan Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种联合视角共识引导的学习框架，用于跨视角地理定位。该方法完全绕开显式几何变换，而是在特征空间中动态挖掘和强化语义共识，通过辅助联合视角路径、全局模式探针和共识介导的对比目标，将共识挖掘能力蒸馏到单视角编码器中，在四个标准基准上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨视角地理定位方法通常依赖几何变换（如透视变形）来暴露共视线索，但这类方法依赖于严格的空间假设，且在视角依赖的可见性下会引入严重视觉畸变，导致噪声监督和脆弱对应关系。因此需要一种无需显式几何对齐的新方法。

Method: 1) 训练阶段引入辅助联合视角路径实现跨视角直接交互，使各视角选择性聚合佐证证据形成统一共识表示；2) 提出全局模式探针作为语义字典，将单视角和联合视角的异质特征投影到严格对齐的度量空间；3) 通过共识介导的对比目标，将单视角嵌入朝联合视角锚点拉近，将共识挖掘能力蒸馏到单视角编码器中用于推理。

Result: 在四个标准基准数据集上达到最先进的性能，验证了发现跨视角语义共识对可靠地理定位的重要性。

Conclusion: 通过完全绕开显式几何变换、在特征空间中挖掘语义共识，可以更鲁棒地解决跨视角地理定位中的视角变化和外观差异问题，为地理定位提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Cross-view geo-localization is challenging due to drastic viewpoint changes and large appearance discrepancies between street-level and satellite imagery. Although existing methods often use geometric warping to expose co-visible cues, such transformations rely on restrictive spatial assumptions and inevitably introduce severe visual distortions under view-dependent visibility, yielding noisy supervision and fragile correspondences. To overcome this, we propose a novel joint-view consensus-guided learning framework that entirely bypasses explicit geometric warping. Instead of forcing rigid spatial alignment, we dynamically mine and adaptively strengthen a semantic consensus directly within the feature space. Specifically, an auxiliary joint-view pathway during training enables direct cross-view interaction, allowing each view to selectively aggregate corroborative evidence into a unified consensus representation. To resolve feature heterogeneity among the single- and joint-view streams, we introduce global pattern probes acting as a semantic dictionary to project divergent modalities into a strictly aligned metric space. Guided by a consensus-mediated contrastive objective, single-view embeddings are explicitly pulled toward the joint-view anchor during training, distilling this consensus-mining capability into the single-view encoders for robust retrieval at inference. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance across four standard benchmarks, underscoring the importance of discovering cross-view semantic consensus for reliable geo-localization.

</details>


### [197] [Diffusion Image Editing via Asynchronous Token Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09322)
*Yang Shi,Liangsi Lu,Minzhe Guo,Yifeng Xie,Yanhui Chen,Jingchao Wang,Xuhang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ATDEdit 是一种推理时框架，通过异步令牌解码实现文本引导的图像编辑。它将每个采样步骤视为全局耦合令牌矩阵的并行更新，利用令牌级条件惊异度估计可编辑位置，仅对选中令牌应用目标条件校正，同时对保留令牌使用源键/值记忆并投影回源值，从而在无需外部掩码或微调的情况下实现局部编辑与背景保持的平衡。在 PIE-Bench 上取得了最强的保真度指标（PSNR 27.44 dB，LPIPS 0.055），同时保持竞争力十足的对齐效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本引导扩散图像编辑中，简单切换文本条件会导致全局漂移，因为去噪动力学将变化传播到所有令牌，破坏未编辑区域。因此需要一种无需外部掩码或微调、既能局部编辑又能保持背景的方法。

Method: 提出异步令牌解码编辑（ATDEdit），将采样步骤建模为全局耦合令牌矩阵的并行更新，支持令牌索引的条件切换与差异化更新策略。具体包括：1) 用令牌级条件惊异度估计可编辑位置；2) 对选中令牌应用目标条件校正；3) 在保留令牌位置提供源键/值记忆，并将选中的保留令牌潜行投影回源值以促进背景保持。

Result: 在 PIE-Bench 基准上，ATDEdit 取得了最强的保真度指标：PSNR 27.44 dB、LPIPS 0.055，同时保持有竞争力度的语义对齐。

Conclusion: 异步令牌解码策略能够在不依赖外部掩码或模型微调的前提下，有效结合局部编辑与背景保持，为文本引导图像编辑提供了一种新的推理时方案。

Abstract: Text-guided diffusion image editing aims to modify semantic attributes of an image while preserving its identity, layout, and background. However, naïvely switching the text condition during sampling often causes global drift, as denoising dynamics propagate changes across tokens and can disrupt unedited regions. To address this issue, we propose \textbf{A}synchronous \textbf{T}oken \textbf{D}ecoding \textbf{Edit} (ATDEdit), an inference-time framework that views each sampler step as a parallel update of a globally coupled token matrix and enables token-indexed condition switching with differentiated update policies. Instead of applying synchronous target-conditioned updates to all tokens, ATDEdit estimates editable locations using token-wise conditional surprisal and applies target-conditioned corrections to the selected token set. It supplies source key/value memory at keep-token positions and projects selected keep-token latent rows back to their source values; these operations promote background preservation but do not constitute a pixel-level invariance guarantee. This approach combines local editing and background preservation without external or user-provided spatial masks and without model fine-tuning. On PIE-Bench, ATDEdit achieves the strongest reported preservation metrics, including 27.44~dB PSNR and 0.055 LPIPS, while retaining competitive semantic alignment.

</details>


### [198] [Revisiting the Current Frame: Physical-Trace-Guided Network Output Correction for Video Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09342)
*Yifeng Lin,Liuxiang Qiu,Guangming Ren,Tiesong Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ANCHOR是一个模型无关的视频修复修正框架，通过将低质量当前帧作为时间对齐的锚点，估计空间信任域来平衡修复结果与原始观测，在HDR视频重建和视频去雨任务上提升了多种SOTA模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频修复方法主要关注改进修复网络本身，但忽略了生成结果在不同空间位置上的可靠性问题。参考帧可能引入不一致退化、内容差异或重建误差，导致修复输出在某些区域不可靠。

Method: 提出ANCHOR框架，将低质量当前帧重新审视为时间对齐的锚点；从异构物理轨迹证据中估计空间信任域（spatial trust field），自适应地在修复提案和原始观测之间进行平衡，实现可靠性感知的输出修正。

Result: 在HDR视频重建和视频去雨两项任务上，ANCHOR在多种SOTA修复模型上均取得一致的性能提升，验证了可靠性感知输出修正的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模输出空间位置的可靠性并进行自适应修正，能够有效提升视频修复模型的整体性能，且该框架与具体修复模型无关，具有较好的泛化性和实用价值。

Abstract: Video restoration methods exploit temporal information to recover information missing from degraded observations. However, reference frames within the sequence may introduce inconsistent degradation, content discrepancy, or reconstruction errors due to physical image-formation variations, occlusion, and imperfect temporal aggregation. Existing approaches mainly focus on improving restoration networks, while the reliability of the generated outputs at different spatial locations remains largely unexplored. In this work, we propose ANCHOR, a model-agnostic framework that revisits the low-quality current frame as a temporally aligned anchor for video restoration correction. Specifically, ANCHOR estimates a spatial trust field from heterogeneous physical-trace evidence and adaptively balances the restoration proposal with the original observation. Experiments on High Dynamic Range video reconstruction and video deraining demonstrate consistent improvements across various state-of-the-art restoration models, validating the effectiveness of reliability-aware output correction for video restoration.

</details>


### [199] [Beyond Global Editing: Per-Instance Disentangled Subspaces for Training-Free Hallucination Mitigation in LVLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09344)
*Ali Cheraghian,Hamidreza Dastmalchi,Hamed Barzamini,Morteza Saberi,Mojtaba Golzan,Shafin Rahman,Hossein Rahmani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种训练免费的幻觉抑制框架，通过构建解耦的幻觉子空间并在测试时动态计算每个输入与各子空间的关系权重，自适应组合投影以按实例抑制幻觉，同时保持图像语义，在多个视觉语言基准和LVLM上取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型（LVLM）在多模态推理中表现强大，但经常产生幻觉（生成的文本不准确描述视觉输入）。虽然微调可以缓解，但计算成本高且需要大量精选数据集。现有的模型编辑方法依赖单一全局子空间矫正幻觉，将所有测试样本同等对待，无法捕捉不同输入间多样的幻觉模式。

Method: 提出一个训练免费的幻觉抑制框架。首先构建一组解耦的幻觉子空间（Disentangled Hallucination Subspaces），每个子空间隔离一种不同的幻觉模式。在推理时，模型自适应计算每个输入与这些子空间的权重关系，引导动态组合的投影，选择性抑制最可能的幻觉方向，同时保留基于图像语义的信息。

Result: 在多个视觉语言基准和多种LVLM家族上进行了大量实验，展示了该方法在鲁棒性、泛化性和效率方面的一致改进。

Conclusion: 该方法作为一种训练免费的幻觉缓解方案，通过动态、逐实例的幻觉抑制，相比依赖单一全局子空间的现有模型编辑技术能更好地适应多样化幻觉模式，具有更好的鲁棒性、泛化性和效率。

Abstract: Recent advances in large vision-language models (LVLMs) have enabled powerful multimodal reasoning by integrating visual encoders with large language models (LLMs). However, their reliability is frequently undermined by hallucinations, where generated text inaccurately describes the visual input. Although fine-tuning can mitigate this problem, it is computationally expensive and requires large, curated datasets, making training-free alternatives attractive. Among these, model editing is more promising than decoding-based approaches: decoding methods adapt outputs per input but introduce computational overhead and instability, whereas model editing modifies internal representations offline, providing a more efficient and stable solution. However, existing model-editing techniques typically rely on a single global subspace to correct hallucinations, treating all test samples identically and failing to capture diverse hallucination modes across inputs. To address this limitation, we propose a training-free hallucination mitigation framework for dynamic, per-instance suppression at test time. Our method first constructs a set of Disentangled Hallucination Subspaces, each isolating a distinct hallucination mode. During inference, the model adaptively calculates weights reflecting each input's relationship to these subspaces, guiding a dynamically combined projection that selectively suppresses the most probable hallucination directions while preserving image-grounded semantics. Extensive experiments across multiple vision-language benchmarks and LVLM families demonstrate consistent improvements, highlighting the robustness, generalizability, and efficiency of our approach.

</details>


### [200] [One-Time Training for All Grains: Open-Set Grain Recognition and Quantitative Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09345)
*Qihe Su,Mengyu Sun,Yuxi Ke,Zhuoyan Jiang,Wanneng Yang,Chenglong Huang,Ziyuan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GROW框架，实现无需重新训练的开放集谷物识别与定量分析。通过类不可知谷物定位、可扩展的GrainBank特征库和基于排名相似度的top-k检索，新谷物品种只需添加描述符即可识别，无需模型重训。实验表明其将品种注册时间从4153秒降至39秒，同时保持竞争力的识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统谷物识别方法在固定品种集上训练，引入新品种需要额外数据采集和模型重训练，成本高昂且难以维护。随着作物育种产生越来越多谷物品种，亟需一种可扩展、无需重训练的识别方案。

Method: GROW包含三个核心组件：(1)类不可知谷物定位，将混合谷物图像分割为单个实例以实现计数和表型测量；(2)融合视觉嵌入与形态学描述符，构建可扩展的GrainBank特征库；(3)基于排名相似度加权的top-k检索进行查询谷物识别，新品种通过追加描述符即可融入。

Result: 在渐进品种扩展、不同谷物密度和背景域偏移等场景下验证了GROW的可扩展性、鲁棒性和适应性。与联合重训练相比，品种注册时间从4153秒降至39秒（约100倍加速），同时保持具有竞争力的识别性能。

Conclusion: GROW为可扩展的谷物识别、计数和表型分析提供了一种高效且易维护的解决方案，消除了重复模型重训练的需求，适用于不断引入新品种的实际育种场景。

Abstract: Advances in crop breeding have introduced an increasing number of grain varieties, creating a growing demand for efficient variety recognition and quantitative analysis. However, existing methods are typically trained on a fixed variety set, and incorporating newly introduced varieties requires additional data collection and model retraining. To address this limitation, we propose GROW, a framework for Grain Recognition and quantitative analysis in Open sets Without retraining. GROW first performs class-agnostic grain localization, converting mixed-grain images into individual instances for variety-wise counting and phenotypic measurement. It then combines visual embeddings and morphological descriptors into fused grain descriptors stored in an extensible GrainBank. Query grains are recognized through rank-similarity weighted top-k retrieval, and newly introduced varieties are incorporated by appending their descriptors without updating the deployed models. Extensive experiments under progressive variety expansion, varying grain densities, and background domain shifts demonstrate the scalability, robustness, and adaptability of GROW. Compared with joint retraining, GROW reduced the average category-registration time from 4153 s to only 39 s while maintaining competitive recognition performance. These results demonstrate that GROW provides an efficient and maintainable solution for extensible grain recognition, counting, and phenotypic analysis without repeated model retraining.

</details>


### [201] [Alpha as an Efficiency Signal: Visibility-Routed RGBA Image-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09355)
*Zhe Li,Honghao Qiao,Zhixin Xu,Qijie Wang,Bo Peng,Dawei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GameAlpha-2.4K数据集（2400个游戏风格RGBA视频）和基于参考条件的RGBA视频生成模型，通过可见性路由器跳过透明token的DiT更新，实现单遍联合生成RGB和alpha通道，相比两阶段管线获得更低FVD，并实现1.2倍推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 游戏行业需要RGBA视频资产，但现有RGBA视频数据集以照片写实内容为主、缺乏游戏资产覆盖；传统先生成后抠像（generate-then-matte）管线因半透明区域被背景模糊导致抠像不稳定；现有联合建模方法多为文本条件且存在效率和质量问题。

Method: 1) 构建GameAlpha-2.4K数据集，采用matte友好的合成、多假设alpha恢复和基于合成的质量门控；2) 训练参考条件RGBA视频生成器，单遍联合生成RGB帧和alpha遮罩；3) 提出可见性路由器，早期识别透明token并跳过其后续DiT更新；4) 使用x_0-lock引导透明token沿原始流匹配调度向自预测终点。

Result: 相比传统两阶段管线获得更低FVD；可见性路由器在最后两步DiT去噪中跳过35%的token评估，骨干网络获得1.2倍加速，质量几乎无损失。

Conclusion: 通过数据集的改进和高效的联合生成架构，有效解决了游戏RGBA视频生成中的数据覆盖不足、半透明区域抠像不稳定和生成效率低的问题，为游戏资产生产提供了更好的方案。

Abstract: RGBA videos combine RGB appearance with an alpha channel, enabling animated assets to be applied across arbitrary backgrounds, which are heavily used in gaming industry. However, generating high-quality RGBA animations for games remains challenging for two reasons. First, most existing RGBA video datasets are dominated by photorealistic content, with limited coverage of game assets. Second, the traditional generate-then-matte pipelines estimate alpha only after RGB synthesis, so semi-transparent regions are often blurred by background, resulting in unstable matting outputs. More recently, many methods have begun to model RGB and alpha jointly, but existing approaches are mostly text-conditioned, and still have unresolved issues in efficiency and quality. To address these challenges, we introduce GameAlpha-2.4K, a 2.4K-clip game-style RGBA video dataset built with matte-friendly synthesis, multi-hypothesis alpha recovery, and compositing-based quality gates. Using this dataset, we train a reference-conditioned RGBA video generator that jointly produces RGB frames and alpha mattes in a single pass. To improve efficiency, we propose a visibility router that identifies transparent tokens in an early stage and bypasses their later DiT updates, while x_0-lock guides them along the original flow-matching schedule toward self-predicted endpoints. Our model obtains lower FVD than traditional two-stage pipelines, and the visibility router skips 35% of token evaluations in the final two DiT denoising steps, providing a 1.2x backbone speedup with negligible quality degradation compared to dense inference.

</details>


### [202] [ControlRadio: Prompt-Driven Controllable Diffusion for Cross-Modal Radio Map Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09357)
*Kangjun Liu,Xiying Pan,Shuhang Zhang,Xiang Xiang,Ke Chen,Yaowei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ControlRadio，一个可控生成框架，能从自然语言描述和环境布局生成无线电地图，相比传统仿真方法计算时间减少四个数量级以上，并达到最先进的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的无线电地图构建依赖密集测量或高计算成本的物理仿真，难以扩展和实时部署。现有生成式AI方法缺乏细粒度控制和物理一致性，无法很好适用于真实无线环境。

Method: 提出了ControlRadio可控生成框架，通过联合语义和空间条件实现可解释且传播合理的生成；利用受控潜在先验和布局感知条件提升稳定性和结构一致性。输入为自然语言描述和环境布局（包括建筑结构和发射器位置），输出为无线电地图。

Result: 在多种城市场景下达到最先进的精度和强泛化能力，计算时间相比传统基于仿真的方法减少超过四个数量级。

Conclusion: ControlRadio为可扩展且可控的无线环境建模提供了新范式，对下一代通信系统和数据驱动的无线电感知具有广泛意义。

Abstract: Radio maps describe how wireless signals propagate across space and are essential for wireless communication, sensing, and network planning. However, constructing accurate radio maps traditionally requires either dense measurements or computationally expensive physical simulations, which limits scalability and real-time deployment. Recent advances in generative artificial intelligence offer a promising alternative, but existing approaches lack fine-grained control and physical consistency when applied to real-world wireless environments. Here we present \textbf{ControlRadio}, a controllable generative framework that produces radio maps from natural-language descriptions and environmental layouts, including building structures and transmitter locations. Joint semantic and spatial conditioning enables interpretable, propagation-plausible generation, while a controlled latent prior and layout-aware conditioning improve stability and structural consistency. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ControlRadio achieves state-of-the-art accuracy and strong generalization across diverse urban scenarios, while reducing computation time by more than four orders of magnitude compared with conventional simulation-based methods. Such results suggest a new paradigm for scalable and controllable wireless environment modeling, with broad implications for next-generation communication systems and data-driven radio sensing.

</details>


### [203] [Deep Learning based Detection of Fishing Vessels and Fishing Monitoring using Nightlight Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09360)
*Shantakar Mohanty,Prasun Kumar Gupta,Raian Vargas Maretto*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究利用SDGSAT-1卫星夜间灯光影像结合双分支YOLO11深度学习架构，检测印度西海岸的小型渔船，识别出77.3%的潜在"暗船"，性能卓越（F1-score 0.96），并揭示了2022-23年间的渔业活动时空模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海上监视需求迫切，特别是监测不发射AIS信号的"暗船"活动。传统方法难以检测小型渔船，需要开发结合夜间灯光卫星影像与深度学习的创新方案，以增强印度西海岸的渔业监控能力。

Method: 采用SDGSAT-1卫星的10米全色和40米RGB夜间灯光影像，设计双分支YOLO11架构：并行卷积分支分别处理两种模态数据，特征图拼接后用于增强特征提取，专门优化小目标检测性能。

Result: 双分支YOLO11模型性能最优：精确率0.99、召回率0.93、F1-score 0.96、mAP@50 0.96，显著优于单分支YOLOv5s/YOLOv8s/YOLO11s。2022-23年间检测到31,525艘船，其中77.3%为潜在暗船。捕捞活动在1-4月达到高峰，主要活动走廊位于离岸50-100公里的大陆架区域。

Conclusion: 夜间灯光卫星影像结合深度学习可有效检测小型渔船，为海上监视提供了新的技术手段。研究成果揭示了印度水域的捕捞模式及潜在的监管合规问题，对渔业管理和海上安全具有重要价值。

Abstract: The demand for maritime surveillance has given rise to the need for monitoring fishing vessel activities, particularly in addressing the challenge of "dark vessels" that operate without Automatic Identification System (AIS) transmission. This study presents a novel approach for detecting small-scale fishing vessels using nighttime light (NTL) imagery from the SDGSAT-1 satellite, combined with deep learning techniques to enhance fishing monitoring awareness along the western coast of India. A dual-branch YOLO11 architecture was developed to exploit both the 10-meter panchromatic and 40-meter RGB imagery from SDGSAT-1. The custom model architecture was specifically optimized for small object detection in NTL imagery, featuring parallel convolutional backbones that process both modalities before concatenation for enhanced feature extraction. The dual-branch YOLO11 model demonstrated optimal performance with a precision of 0.99, recall of 0.93, F1-score of 0.96, and mAP@50 of 0.96, significantly outperforming single-branch implementations of YOLOv5s, YOLOv8s, and standard YOLO11s architectures. When applied to the western coast of India, the model detected 31525 vessel instances across the temporal dataset spanning 2022-23. Cross-matching analysis with AIS data revealed that only 7146 (22.7%) of detected vessels had corresponding AIS transmissions, while 24379 (77.3%) were identified as potential dark vessels. Spatio-temporal analysis showed peak fishing activity during January-April, with a primary activity corridor parallel to the coastline within 50-100 km, corresponding to productive continental shelf areas. This research contributes to maritime surveillance capabilities by highlighting the effectiveness of nighttime lights satellite imagery for fishing vessel detection and provides valuable insights into fishing patterns and potential regulatory compliance issues in Indian waters.

</details>


### [204] [FeedbackTrack: Visual-Cortex-Inspired Cross-Frame Feedback for Transformer Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09369)
*Yueyang Cang,Xiaoteng Zhang,Zhiyuan Ning,Yuchen He,Li Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FeedbackTrack，一种受视觉皮层启发的框架，通过稀疏的、组级层对齐的跨帧反馈机制增强预训练Transformer跟踪器的时序信息整合能力。该方法无需改变原有跟踪流程，仅需固定大小的一帧缓存，在SPMTrack和ARTrackV2上均取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer跟踪器大多依赖前馈式特征提取，时序机制通常只更新模板、提示、查询或预测状态，很少有工作将中间表示复用来调制相应处理阶段，导致时间整合不够有效。

Method: FeedbackTrack引入两条轻量级反馈通路：Query Feedback用于token级查询调制，Gate Feedback用于上下文相关的特征调制。前一帧的中间状态被分离、缓存并返回到当前帧对应的Transformer组中。反馈是稀疏的、组级层对齐的，且仅需固定的单帧缓存。

Result: 在SPMTrack和ARTrackV2上，FeedbackTrack在LaSOT和GOT-10k数据集上一致提升了五种骨干网络配置，SPMTrack-G达到83.4 AO和79.1 AUC，而参数量增加不到1%。跨帧反馈比同帧调制高出1.8-3.2 AO点。

Conclusion: 实验证明性能提升主要来自循环的历史信息而非同帧调制。进一步分析显示学习到的反馈强度呈现非均匀的深度相关组织，验证了循环反馈对Transformer跟踪的有效性。

Abstract: Visual object tracking requires effective temporal integration, yet most Transformer trackers still rely on predominantly feed-forward feature extraction. Existing temporal mechanisms typically update templates, prompts, queries, or prediction states, while intermediate representations are rarely reused to modulate corresponding processing stages. We propose \textbf{FeedbackTrack}, a visual-cortex-inspired framework that introduces sparse, group-level layer-aligned cross-frame feedback into pretrained Transformer trackers. Previous-frame intermediate states are detached, cached, and returned to corresponding Transformer groups in the current frame through two lightweight pathways: Query Feedback for token-level query modulation and Gate Feedback for context-dependent feature modulation. FeedbackTrack preserves the original tracking pipeline with only a fixed-size one-frame cache. Across SPMTrack and ARTrackV2, FeedbackTrack consistently improves five backbone configurations on LaSOT and GOT-10k, achieving 83.4 AO and 79.1 AUC with SPMTrack-G while adding less than 1\% parameters. Controlled comparisons show that cross-frame feedback outperforms same-frame modulation by 1.8--3.2 AO points, demonstrating that the gains mainly come from recurrent historical information. Further analysis reveals a non-uniform depth-dependent organization of learned feedback strengths, highlighting the effectiveness of recurrent feedback for Transformer tracking.

</details>


### [205] [Preserve More Details: Mitigating Content Drift in Real-World Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09373)
*Chunxiao Liu,Wei Liu,Anbin Xiong,Erli Meng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FSP-Diff 是一种新型一步式扩散模型，通过双路径架构（细节条件路径和细节调制语义路径）解决真实图像超分中低质量输入导致的内容漂移问题（视觉细节退化与文本语义偏移），在标准基准上超越现有一步式扩散方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的真实图像超分方法虽然利用低质量输入和Stable Diffusion的先验取得了不错效果，但往往忽略了低质量输入清晰度不足会不可避免地导致生成高分辨率图像时出现内容漂移，表现为视觉细节退化和文本语义偏移，严重损害保真度和感知质量。

Method: 提出FSP-Diff，一种一步式扩散模型，包含双路径架构：1）细节条件路径（Detail-Conditioned Pathway），用于注入结构化细节以恢复精细结构；2）细节调制语义路径（Detail-Modulated Semantic Pathway），利用结构化细节细化语义引导，以缓解语义偏差。

Result: 在标准Real-ISR基准上的大量实验表明，FSP-Diff在定量和定性指标上均超越了现有的一步式扩散方法。

Conclusion: 通过双路径架构同时处理细节恢复和语义保真问题，FSP-Diff有效解决了真实超分中的内容漂移难题，显著提升了生成图像的质量。

Abstract: Real-world image super-resolution (Real-ISR) aims to reconstruct high-quality (HQ) images from low-quality (LQ) inputs subject to diverse real-world degradations. Recent advances have leveraged the LQ inputs and natural image priors learned by Stable Diffusion models to achieve impressive results. However, existing methods often overlook insufficient clarity of LQ inputs inevitably induce content drift in the generated HQ images. This manifests primarily as visual detail degradation and textual semantic shift, severely compromising both fidelity and perceptual quality. To address this challenge, we propose FSP-Diff, a novel one-step diffusion model featuring a dual-pathway architecture. This architecture comprises a Detail-Conditioned Pathway for injecting structured details to recover fine structures, and a Detail-Modulated Semantic Pathway that refines semantic guidance using structured details to mitigate semantic deviations. Extensive experiments on standard Real-ISR benchmarks demonstrate that FSP-Diff surpasses existing one-step diffusion methods in both quantitative and qualitative metrics.

</details>


### [206] [Efficient Human-Contact Representation for Human-Scene Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09388)
*Nghia Vu,Tuong Do,Binh X. Nguyen,Erman Tjiputra,Anh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效的人-场景接触表示方法——稀疏接触掩码（sparse contact masks），通过选择性地提取关键接触信息来减少高维输入中的冗余数据，并设计了一套稀疏算子替代传统深度网络层中的稠密算子，从而在提升计算速度的同时提高人-场景交互模型的精度。实验表明，该方法在三个公开基准数据集上优于现有最先进模型，重建精度更高且计算速度提升至少12倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人-场景交互是虚拟现实、游戏、机器人和监控等领域的重要研究方向。尽管现有工作在网络架构和模型参数优化方面取得了显著进展，但人体与环境之间接触的高效表示仍然是一个开放挑战，现有稠密表示包含大量冗余数据，影响计算效率和模型精度。

Method: 1) 提出稀疏接触掩码（sparse contact masks），策略性地选择关键接触信息，显著减少高维输入中的冗余数据；2) 基于该高效接触表示，设计一套稀疏算子以替代深度网络层中的传统稠密算子，实现更快的计算；3) 稀疏化不仅加速计算，还过滤非必要的接触数据，提升交互模型精度。

Result: 在三个公开基准数据集上针对接触预测和场景合成两个关键任务进行实验，结果表明所提方法在重建精度上优于最先进模型，且计算速度相比近期基线提升至少12倍。

Conclusion: 稀疏接触掩码作为一种高效的人-场景接触表示，既能减少冗余数据、加速计算，又能提升交互模型的精度，验证了该方法在人-场景交互任务中的有效性和实用价值。

Abstract: Human-scene interaction is an active research topic with several industrial applications in virtual reality, gaming, robotics, and surveillance. Despite significant progress in network architectures to improve the results or optimize models' parameters for fast inference speed, the efficient representation of contact between humans and their environments remains an open challenge. In this paper, we propose a new efficient human-contact representation for human-scene interaction. Our primary contribution is the introduction of sparse contact masks that strategically select essential contact information, significantly reducing redundant data in high-dimensional inputs. Leveraging this efficient contact representation, we propose a suite of sparse operators to replace traditional dense operators within deep network layers for faster computation. Our approach not only enhances computational speed but also filters out non-essential contact data, thereby improving the precision of human-scene interaction models. To validate the effectiveness of our method, we conduct intensive experiments across three public benchmark datasets, focusing on two critical tasks for human-scene interaction: contact prediction and scene synthesis. The experimental results show that our approach outperforms state-of-the-art models in reconstruction accuracy and achieves a computation speed-up of at least 12 times over recent baselines.

</details>


### [207] [CoInS-Net: A Continuous Position-Aware Network for Joint Medical Image Interpolation and Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09391)
*Yujia Sun,Ningfeng Que,Peiting Shi,Rongrong Fu,Yingying Yang,Xinhang Li,Yin Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CoInS-Net，一个连续位置感知交互网络，用于医学图像帧插值和病灶分割的联合任务，通过共享Swin编码器、连续坐标查询和原型互交互模块实现双向信息交换，在四个公开医学数据集上优于传统单任务方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 各向异性医学体数据在稀疏层面的采样常导致结构不连续和边界模糊，而现有方法将插值和分割独立执行，既产生冗余计算，又未能充分利用连续切片间互补的结构信息。

Method: 提出CoInS-Net：1）共享Swin编码器配合连续空间坐标查询实现双向交互；2）空间连续位置插值模块从相对坐标和物理间距生成目标位置特征；3）原型驱动的任务互交互模块通过共享原型集交换全局结构；4）多尺度任务协作解码器将每尺度分离为共享和任务特定组件。

Result: 在四个涵盖多种模态和解剖区域的公开医学数据集上，联合优化框架优于传统单任务方案，有效实现了插值与分割任务的相互促进。

Conclusion: 联合优化框架能有效实现插值和分割的互促，为智能临床医学图像分析提供了可靠且通用的技术方案。

Abstract: Accurate medical image interpolation and anatomical structure segmentation are fundamental for computer-aided diagnosis and treatment planning. Anisotropic medical volumes with sparse through-plane sampling often suffer from structural discontinuity and boundary blur, hindering reliable clinical image analysis. Most existing methods implement interpolation and segmentation independently, which introduces redundant computation and fails to fully exploit complementary cross-slice structural information between sequential slices. To address these issues, we propose a continuous position-aware interaction network, termed CoInS-Net, for joint frame interpolation and lesion segmentation. Unlike conventional cascaded interpolation-then-segmentation paradigms, the framework enables bidirectional interaction under a shared Swin encoder with continuous spatial coordinate queries. A spatially continuous position interpolation module generates target-position features at every scale from the relative coordinate and physical spacing, and a prototype-based task mutual interaction module lets the segmentation and interpolation branches exchange global structure through a small set of shared prototypes rather than dense feature mixing. A multi-scale task-cooperative decoder further separates each scale into shared and task-specific components, so the two tasks reinforce common anatomy while preserving their distinct requirements down to the boundary level, without extra annotations. Experiments on four public medical imaging datasets with diverse modalities and anatomical regions demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms conventional single-task schemes. The joint optimization framework effectively realizes mutual promotion between interpolation and segmentation tasks, providing a reliable and universal technical scheme for intelligent clinical medical image analysis.

</details>


### [208] [CableDex: Cable Length Estimation on Industrial Reels Using a Handheld Device](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09392)
*Francisco Guillén,Ricardo Almeida,Bruno Silva,João C. Neves*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CableDex是一个计算机视觉系统，通过手机拍摄的单张照片自动估算工业电缆卷筒上的电缆长度，替代耗时且不准确的人工测量。系统结合相机标定、实例分割、姿态估计和体积计算，在五种卷筒类型上达到4.90%的平均绝对百分比误差，且推理时间仅5.66毫秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业电缆卷筒上的电缆长度测量目前依赖人工，既耗时又不准确，需要一种自动化、便捷且精度足够的测量方案。

Method: 系统结合相机标定、实例分割（基于1000张人工标注图像训练的模型）、姿态估计和体积计算，通过移动应用实现从标签扫描、图像采集到分割和长度估算的端到端流程。

Result: 实例分割模型达到99.5%的mAP50，推理时间5.66毫秒/图；在75个卷筒、五种类型上的评估中达到4.90%的平均绝对百分比误差，低于工业界常见的10%误差容限。

Conclusion: CableDex验证了基于单张手机照片的视觉测量方法在工业电缆卷筒长度估算中的可行性，精度满足工业标准，且推理速度快，具有实际部署价值。
