Abstract: CableDex is a computer vision system that addresses the time-consuming and inaccurate manual measurement of cable length on industrial reels from a single photograph captured with a mobile phone. The system combines camera calibration, instance segmentation, pose estimation, and volumetric calculation to estimate the cable length across five different reel types and various cable sizes. This system is based on an instance segmentation model trained on 1,000 manually annotated images, achieving 99.5\% mAP50 with an inference time of 5.66 ms per image. Evaluated on 75 reels across five reel types, the system achieves a MAPE of 4.90\%, within the 10\% error tolerance commonly accepted in industrial cable-reel measurement. The demonstration presents the end-to-end pipeline, from reel label scanning and image capture to segmentation and length estimation, through the mobile application.

</details>


### [209] [Sign Language Recognition Using Original and Synthetic Depth Image Based Point Cloud Data Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09400)
*Rustem Ozakar,Eyup Gedikli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出利用Depth Anything V2网络从RGB图像生成合成深度图像，进而转化为点云数据用于手语识别。在三个手语数据集（Real-time ASL Fingerspelling、KArSL、AUTSL）上，对比了原始深度图像和合成深度图像生成的点云数据在不同PointNet架构中的分类性能。结果表明原始深度点云整体表现优于合成点云，但在部分模型中合成深度点云表现更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手语识别研究主要依赖RGB图像，而提供深度图像的数据集有限。深度图像生成的点云可结合PointNet等神经网络用于手语识别，因此探索从RGB图像合成深度图像以扩充深度数据是一种可行途径。

Method: 使用Depth Anything V2网络从RGB图像生成合成深度图像，将原始和合成的深度图像分别转换为点云数据，采用多种PointNet架构，并结合基于帧、Point Gesture Map和LSTM三种数据模型进行手语识别分类，对比性能。

Result: 原始和合成深度点云在大多数模型中都取得了可接受的性能。总体而言，基于原始深度的点云模型表现优于合成深度模型，但在某些模型上合成深度模型的性能优于原始深度模型。

Conclusion: 合成深度图像可作为深度数据不足时的有效补充手段用于手语识别，虽然整体性能略逊于原始深度数据，但在特定模型下仍具有竞争力。

Abstract: Research regarding the sign language recognition mostly relies on RGB images, whileas sign language datasets that provide depth images are limited. Point clouds obtained from depth images can be used for sign language recognition with neural networks like PointNet. In recent years, various neural networks are used for generating realistic depth images from monocular RGB images. In this work, synthetic depth images were created from RGB images using Depth Anything V2 network. For this purpose, three sign language datasets (Real-time ASL Fingerspelling, KArSL, AUTSL) which contain both RGB and depth images were used. Classification accuracies of the point cloud data created from both original and synthetic depth images using various PointNet architectures were measured for sign language recognition. From the original and synthetic point clouds, frame based, Point Gesture Map and Long Short Term Memory data models were used for classification and their performances were compared. In the results, both original and synthetic based data achieved acceptable performance in most models. In general, original depth based point cloud models performed better than synthetic ones, however in some models synthetic depth based models performed better than the originals.

</details>


### [210] [One Model to Magnify Them All: Efficient Scale-Invariant Histopathology via Conditional Normalization and Continuous Magnification Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09403)
*Agnieszka Florkowska,Henning Müller,Marek Wodzinski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出条件层归一化（CLN）轻量机制，通过小MLP从输入像素尺寸生成仿射归一化参数，使标准CNN能处理任意放大倍率的全切片图像（WSI），无需多模型集成即可泛化到未见的缩放级别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WSI在数字病理学中以离散倍数获取，编码从全局结构到细胞形态的互补诊断信息，但深度学习模型对尺度变化敏感。现有放大不变方法依赖预定离散分辨率的多样架构，而临床获取的放大倍数连续变化，很少与训练分辨率对齐，中间尺度常见，全面覆盖需昂贵的多模型集成。

Method: 提出条件层归一化（CLN）：通过小型MLP根据输入像素尺寸生成仿射归一化参数，集成到标准CNN架构中用于WSI分类和分割。模型在连续像素尺寸范围内采样补丁训练，使推理与扫描仪相关的放大倍数解耦，测试时可泛化到任意未见过的尺度。

Result: 在PANDA前列腺癌数据集上，该方法平均匹配或超过独立训练的单放大模型，在包括训练时未见过的每个评估放大倍数中均排名前三。将五模型集成压缩为单一网络，训练和推理成本降低约4-5倍，同时乘加运算量不变。

Conclusion: 提出的CLN方法成功实现了单一网络对不同放大倍率的优雅泛化，消除了多模型集成的需要，显著降低了计算成本，为临床中多变扫描条件下的WSI分析提供了实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Whole slide images (WSIs) in digital histopathology are acquired at discrete magnification levels encoding complementary diagnostic information from global tissue architecture to fine-grained cellular morphology. Yet, deep learning models remain sensitive to scale variation. Existing magnification-invariant methods rely on multi-scale architectures at predefined discrete resolutions, while in clinical deployment the acquisition magnification varies continuously, rarely aligns with a model's fixed training resolution, and intermediate scales are common, so robust coverage otherwise demands a costly ensemble of magnification-specific models. We propose Conditional Layer Normalization (CLN), a lightweight mechanism that generates affine normalization parameters from input pixel size via a small MLP, integrated into standard CNN architectures for both WSI classification and segmentation. Trained on patches sampled continuously across a range of pixel sizes, the model decouples inference from scanner-dependent magnification and generalizes to arbitrary, previously unseen scales at test time. On the PANDA prostate cancer dataset, our approach on average matches or exceeds independently trained single-magnification models and ranks among the top three performers at every evaluated magnification, including those unseen during training. This collapses a five-model ensemble into a single network and reduces training, and inference cost roughly 4-5 times, while leaving the multiply-accumulate count unchanged. The code is available at: https://github.com/aflorkowska/OneModelToMagnifyThemAll.

</details>


### [211] [MeanSR: Restoration Trajectory Learning for One-Step Perceptual Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09405)
*Axi Niu,Jiawei Kou,Kang Zhang,Qingsen Yan,Jinqiu Sun,Yanning Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MeanSR是一种单步感知超分辨率方法，通过学习LR条件平均速度场直接捕捉从低分辨率到高分辨率图像的有限时间转换，无需蒸馏或显式ODE一致性训练，在多个感知质量指标上超越CTMSR并大幅降低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散超分方法虽感知质量好但迭代去噪成本高；单步蒸馏方法依赖昂贵的预训练教师模型；CTMSR虽通过PF-ODE一致性训练避免蒸馏，但未显式建模LR到HR的重建动力学过程。

Method: 1) 提出MeanSR，学习LR条件下的平均速度场，直接捕捉从退化/噪声输入到合理HR输出的有限时间转换；2) 重新公式化分布轨迹匹配用于平均速度生成；3) 引入Stage-Aware Temporal Sampling策略以改善轨迹学习。

Result: 在合成和真实世界基准上，MeanSR在CLIPIQA、MUSIQ和MANIQA指标上均优于CTMSR，同时显著降低FLOPs和推理延迟；重建出更锐利的结构和更真实的纹理，感知伪影更少。

Conclusion: MeanSR通过平均速度场学习和阶段感知时间采样策略，实现了高质量、高效率的单步感知超分辨率，为无需蒸馏的快速扩散式SR提供了新范式。

Abstract: Diffusion-based super-resolution (SR) achieves strong perceptual quality but requires costly iterative denoising. Existing one-step distillation methods reduce inference time but depend on expensive pretrained teachers, whereas CTMSR avoids distillation through PF-ODE consistency training yet does not explicitly model the restoration dynamics from low-resolution (LR) inputs to high-resolution (HR) images. We propose MeanSR, a one-step perceptual SR method that learns an LR-conditioned average velocity field to directly capture the finite-time transition from degraded or noisy inputs to plausible HR outputs. We further reformulate distribution trajectory matching for average-velocity generation and introduce a Stage-Aware Temporal Sampling strategy to improve trajectory learning. Experiments on synthetic and real-world benchmarks show that MeanSR outperforms CTMSR on CLIPIQA, MUSIQ, and MANIQA while substantially reducing FLOPs and inference latency. MeanSR also reconstructs sharper structures and more realistic textures with fewer perceptual artifacts.

</details>


### [212] [Foundation Models are Implicit Deepfake Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09427)
*Stefan Smeu,Dragos-Alexandru Boldisor,Elisabeta Oneata,Dan Oneata*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现预训练自监督表征中真实与伪造媒体存在系统性的特征幅度差异（伪造样本特征幅度更低），并据此将深度伪造检测视为异常检测问题，仅用特征幅度的简单统计量即可达到与复杂方法竞争的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前深度伪造检测方法虽广泛使用预训练自监督表征，但缺乏对其可区分性来源的深入理解，作者希望揭示真实与伪造媒体在表征层面可分的根本原因。

Method: 跨多个预训练模型、数据集及图像/视频域观察到伪造样本特征幅度系统性偏低的现象；将检测建模为异常检测问题，使用特征幅度的简单统计量进行判别；并分析该效应源于伪造内容引入的语义偏移而非底层生成指纹。

Result: 简单特征幅度统计即可达到与更复杂深度伪造检测方法相当的性能；特征幅度降低主要由语义偏移驱动；基座模型规模越大，该判别信号越强。

Conclusion: 表征学习模型的进步可自然转化为更强的零样本深度伪造检测器，为构建更简单、可扩展的深度伪造检测方案提供了新思路。

Abstract: Pretrained self-supervised representations have emerged as a core component of current deepfake detection methods, yet it remains unclear which of their properties make real and fake media distinguishable. In this work, we uncover a surprisingly consistent phenomenon: across multiple pretrained models, datasets, and both image and video domains, fake samples systematically produce lower-magnitude representations than their real counterparts. Motivated by this finding, we formulate deepfake detection as an anomaly detection problem and show that simple statistics of feature magnitude achieve competitive performance with far more sophisticated deepfake detection methods. We further investigate the origin of this effect and demonstrate that reduced feature magnitude is primarily associated with semantic shifts introduced by fake content, while low-level generative fingerprints play a comparatively smaller role. Finally, we show that this discriminative signal strengthens as the size of the underlying foundation model grows, suggesting that advances in representation learning naturally translate into stronger zero-shot deepfake detectors.

</details>


### [213] [Unveiling the Secret of AdaLN-Zero in Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09438)
*Jie Zhu,Mingyu Ding,Boqiang Duan,Leye Wang,Jingdong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文深入探讨了扩散变压器（DiT）中的关键条件机制adaLN-Zero，发现其中零初始化的作用最为关键，并基于此提出了两种改进方法：adaLN-Gaussian初始化策略和SE-adaLN-Zero条件机制，实验验证了它们的有效性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DiT作为新兴图像生成架构备受关注，但对其内部机制的理解仍停留在表面。尤其是adaLN-Zero相比adaLN表现更优，但其背后的驱动因素尚未被充分研究。

Method: 1) 系统分析adaLN-Zero中三个潜在关键因素：SE-like结构、零初始化、渐变更新顺序；2) 基于分析提出adaLN-Gaussian初始化策略；3) 受SE-like结构启发，提出改进条件机制SE-adaLN-Zero。

Result: 证明零初始化是最具影响力的因素；adaLN-Gaussian和SE-adaLN-Zero在四个数据集（特别是ImageNet1K）上均表现出色，且在文本到图像生成任务中也验证了泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅加深了对DiT中adaLN-Zero机制原理的理解，还提出了实用的改进方法，为扩散Transformer在图像生成中的优化和设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Diffusion transformer (DiT), a rapidly emerging architecture for image generation, has gained much attention. However, despite ongoing efforts to improve its performance, the understanding of DiT remains superficial. In this work, we delve into and investigate a critical conditioning mechanism within DiT, adaLN-Zero, which achieves superior performance compared to adaLN. Our work studies three potential elements driving this performance, including an SE-like structure, zero-initialization, and a "gradual" update order, among which zero-initialization is proved to be the most influential. Building on this understanding, we propose an analysis-guided initialization strategy, termed adaLN-Gaussian, which serves both as an empirical validation of our analysis and as a practical initialization method that consistently improves optimization efficiency. On the other hand, inspired by the SE-like structure, we introduce an improved conditioning mechanism called SE-adaLN-Zero. Extensive experiments following DiT on four datasets, especially on ImageNet1K demonstrate the effectiveness and generalization of adaLN-Gaussian and SE-adaLN-Zero. Beyond class-to-image generation, we also evaluate the generalization of the two improved methods on text-to-image generation.

</details>


### [214] [Sekai2: From World Exploration to Interactive World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09449)
*Kang He,Wenshuo Peng,Zihui Gao,Jiaming Tan,Kaipeng Zhang,Yongtao Ge*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Sekai2是一个多源真实世界视频数据集，包含128,892个视频片段（总计2,826小时，来自10,428个源视频，覆盖113个国家/地区），每个片段都配有相机轨迹和分层时间标注。该数据集特别强调长时观测（43,594个片段达到完整的120秒），并引入了982个沿非线性轨迹拍摄的全景序列，其"重访"特性为学习持久场景表征、长期空间记忆和几何一致的视频世界模型提供了关键监督信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视频数据集难以同时提供长视频、相机轨迹和时间对齐的语义标注：大规模网络视频虽有视觉多样性但缺乏轨迹和时序文本，而姿态标注的数据集通常范围短或面向重建。训练长时程生成和相机控制的视频世界模型需要三者兼具的数据资源。

Method: 从Sekai世界探索素材中构建多源真实视频数据集，采用统一的120秒分解方案，为每个片段发布相机轨迹和层次化标注（区分主体运动、环境动态、静态场景内容和相机行为），并专门引入了982个沿非线性轨迹（含环路和重访）拍摄的全景序列。

Result: 数据集中51.4%的素材为完整的2分钟长片段，共649,597个时间对齐的标注片段。语料库级别的分析证实了：完整的姿态与字幕覆盖、广泛的地理和语义多样性、多样的相机轨迹以及高度非冗余的时间描述。

Conclusion: Sekai2成为一个可扩展的资源，适用于长时程视频生成、相机可控合成和交互式世界模型预训练。其核心创新在于通过全景序列中的"重访"机制，为学习跨时间和视角的持久场景表征提供了独特的监督信号。

Abstract: Video world models must capture how scenes evolve over time and across viewpoints. Training them for long-horizon generation and camera control therefore benefits from long videos paired with camera trajectories and temporally grounded semantics. Existing corpora rarely offer the three together: large-scale web video provides broad visual diversity but no trajectories or time-aligned text, while pose-annotated datasets are typically short-range or reconstruction-oriented. We introduce Sekai2, a multi-source real-world video dataset that carries the world-exploration footage of Sekai toward interactive world modeling. The release contains 128,892 clips totaling 2,826 hours from 10,428 source videos across 113 countries or regions, and is deliberately weighted toward sustained observation: under a common 120-second decomposition, 43,594 segments reach the full two minutes and account for 51.4% of all footage. Every clip includes a released camera trajectory and hierarchical annotations disentangling subject motion, environment dynamics, static scene content, and camera behavior, resulting in 649,597 temporally grounded segments. Crucially, we further introduce 982 panoramic sequences captured along non-linear trajectories with loops and revisits. These revisits provide repeated observations of the same locations across time and viewpoints, offering essential supervision for learning persistent scene representations, long-term spatial memory, and geometrically consistent world models. Corpus-scale analyses demonstrate complete pose-and-caption coverage, broad geographic and semantic diversity, varied camera trajectories, and highly non-redundant temporal descriptions. Together, these properties make Sekai2 a scalable resource for long-horizon video generation, camera-controllable synthesis, and interactive world-model pre-training.

</details>


### [215] [A Content-Aware Pure Permutation with Intrinsic Avalanche Effect: Breaking the Diffusion-Permutation Dichotomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09452)
*Zahra Ghoraeian,Mohammad-Reza Sadeghi,Samaneh Mashhadi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出三角内容感知置换（TCA）算法，通过Canny边缘检测结合Delaunay三角剖分，使得纯像素置换即可产生差分敏感性（NPCR=97.10%，UACI=20.06%），挑战了传统文献中"置换无法产生雪崩效应"的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统文献普遍认为纯像素置换无法产生差分敏感性，因为置换只是改变像素位置而不改变其值。这一假设限制了置换在加密和脆弱水印等需要差分敏感性的场景中的应用，本文旨在挑战这一假设，证明纯置换也能产生显著的雪崩效应。

Method: TCA算法首先使用Canny算子提取边缘点，然后结合角点对边缘点和角点进行Delaunay三角剖分，创建独特的分区。由于三角剖分对图像几何高度敏感，单个像素的改变会改变边缘图，导致完全不同的三角剖分结果和全局置换模式。通过内容感知的迭代置换来放大这种敏感性。

Result: 在50张图像上的实验表明，TCA平均14.81次迭代即可达到NPCR=97.10%和UACI=20.06%，而传统方法保持近零NPCR。迭代阈值根据内容复杂度在6.4到30.7之间变化。低PSNR（11.93 dB）和近零相关性（约10^-3）验证了优越的统计性能。尽管因三角剖分而比经典方法慢，但这是为更强安全性所做的有意权衡。

Conclusion: TCA证明纯置换可以产生显著的差分敏感性，其非解析、内容依赖的置换模式非常适合基于参考的加密、脆弱水印和非盲隐写等安全应用。

Abstract: Pixel permutation is a fundamental tool in image processing, image encryption, and data hiding (including watermarking and steganography) that rearranges pixels without changing their values. A common assumption in the literature is that permutation alone cannot create differential sensitivity; changing one pixel merely relocates that pixel in the output, producing no avalanche effect. This paper challenges this by introducing the Triangular Content-Aware Permutation (TCA) algorithm.
  The method extracts edge points using Canny and applies Delaunay Triangulation to edges and corners, creating a unique partition. Since triangulation is highly sensitive to image geometry, changing a single pixel alters the edge map, resulting in a completely different triangulation and global permutation pattern. Unlike classical dimension-based permutations and advanced content-aware methods (2025-2026), which lack differential sensitivity, TCA increases NPCR from near-zero to 97.10% solely through pixel relocation.
  Experiments on 50 images show that TCA, with an average of 14.81 iterations, achieves NPCR = 97.10% and UACI = 20.06%, proving pure permutation can create significant differential sensitivity. Conventional methods maintain near-zero NPCR. The iteration threshold varies from 6.4 to 30.7 based on content complexity. Low PSNR (11.93 dB) and near-zero correlation (~10^-3) confirm superior statistical performance. Although slower than classical methods due to triangulation, this is a deliberate trade-off for stronger security. Given the non-analytic, content-dependent nature of the pattern, TCA is ideal for reference-based encryption, fragile watermarking, and non-blind steganography.

</details>


### [216] [RecoverFly: A Failure-Aware Reinforcement Learning Post-Training Framework for Aerial Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09467)
*Boxiong Wang,Hui Kang,Geng Sun,Jiahui Li,Chao Yu,Daxin Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RecoverFly是一个面向端到端无人机视觉-语言-动作（UAV-VLA）策略的失败感知强化学习后训练框架，通过token级RL、失败案例重访和两阶段长尾场景课程来解决行为克隆监督不足、样本效率低、长尾分布和策略分布偏移等问题，在TravelUAV基准上取得了最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的端到端UAV-VLA策略虽然减少了对独立感知、规划和控制模块的依赖，但其行为克隆目标对交互式闭环执行提供的有益监督有限。强化学习虽然是个有前景的方案，但受限于样本利用率低、长尾场景分布和优化过程中的策略分布偏移等问题。

Method: RecoverFly包含三个核心组件：1）采用token级RL实现语法约束自回归无人机动作的稳定优化；2）重访未解决的失败案例以加强修正学习和样本利用率；3）结合两阶段长尾场景课程与参考策略正则化，提升场景适应能力同时保留已习得能力。

Result: 在TravelUAV基准上，RecoverFly在seen、unseen-map和unseen-object三个数据划分上均取得最佳性能。相较于AerialVLA初始化，在约30%训练集大小的总rollout预算下，成功率提升了3.12到8.37个百分点。

Conclusion: RecoverFly通过失败感知的强化学习后训练框架有效提升了端到端UAV-VLA策略的鲁棒性和泛化能力，实验结果验证了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicle vision-language navigation (UAV-VLN) requires agents to translate visual observations and language instructions into reliable flight actions in complex environments. Although recent end-to-end UAV vision-language-action (UAV-VLA) policies reduce reliance on separately designed perception, planning, and control modules, their behavior-cloning objectives provide limited corrective supervision for interactive closed-loop execution. Reinforcement learning (RL) offers a promising solution, while its effectiveness is constrained by inefficient use of samples, long-tailed scene distributions, and policy distribution shift during optimization. To this end, we propose RecoverFly, a failure-aware RL post-training framework for end-to-end UAV-VLA policies. Specifically, RecoverFly adapts token-level RL for stable optimization of grammar-constrained autoregressive UAV actions, revisits unresolved failure cases to strengthen corrective learning and sample utilization, and combines a two-stage long-tail scene curriculum with reference-policy regularization to improve scene adaptation while preserving acquired capabilities. Experiments on the TravelUAV benchmark demonstrate that RecoverFly achieves the best performance on the seen, unseen-map, and unseen-object splits. Moreover, compared to the AerialVLA initialization, RecoverFly improves success rate by 3.12 to 8.37 percentage points under a total rollout budget of about 30\% of the training-set size, validating its effectiveness, robustness, and generalization capabilities.

</details>


### [217] [FaLCon: Facet-Anchored Retrieval with Late Consensus for Sim2Real Text-Based Person Anomaly Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09474)
*Hieu Dinh Trung Pham,Phuong Huu Vu Tran,Thuan Duc Mai,Son Nguyen Minh Le,Khang Le Minh,Hoang Vo,Minh-Chi Phung,Huy Minh Nhat Nguyen,Cuong Tuan Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于锚点约束的由粗到精检索框架，用于文本驱动的行人异常搜索（Sim2Real场景）。框架先通过异构视觉-语言检索器进行全局语义匹配（含软声明感知融合），再通过Qwen3重排序和两个互补的语义验证模块（异常感知完形填空与多智能体证据推理）进行细粒度验证，最后通过不确定性门控共识模块自适应加权。在PAB基准上达到95.41% mAP@10、94.44% R@1和99.09% R@5，显著优于单一检索骨干网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本驱动的行人异常搜索面临两大挑战：一是视觉相似的候选对象往往仅在细微动作、物体交互或外观属性上有差异，需要细粒度判别；二是直接对整个图库应用多模态大语言模型在计算上不可行。现有模型主要基于合成数据训练，Sim2Real场景下检索难度进一步加大。

Method: 采用锚点约束的粗到精两阶段检索框架：1) 粗检索阶段：将查询表示为原始标题、结构化拼接和多个语义面（外观、动作、物体），集成异构视觉-语言检索器，通过逐查询分数校准和软声明感知融合保留候选召回，其中完整和拼接标题作为锚点，各语义面提供有界的矫正证据；2) 精排阶段：使用判别式Qwen3重排序器以及两个互补的语义验证模块（异常感知完形填空和多智能体证据推理）；3) 不确定性门控共识模块自适应重加权三个专家。

Result: 在PAB基准上，软声明感知检索达到86.44% mAP@10，优于单个检索骨干；完整框架进一步提升至95.41% mAP@10、94.44% R@1和99.09% R@5。

Conclusion: 保留强全局检索能力的同时，将昂贵的语义推理限制在小型候选中是有效的，能够应对细粒度Sim2Real行人异常搜索任务。

Abstract: Text-based person anomaly search requires retrieving real-world pedestrian images from detailed natural-language descriptions using models trained primarily on synthetic data. This Sim2Real setting is particularly challenging because visually similar candidates may differ only in subtle actions, object interactions, or appearance attributes, while applying multimodal large language models to the entire gallery is computationally expensive. We propose an anchor-constrained coarse-to-fine retrieval framework that combines global semantic matching with fine-grained verification. First, each query is represented by its original caption, a structured concatenation, and several semantic facets. Heterogeneous vision-language retrievers are then integrated through robust per-query score calibration and soft claim-aware fusion. Full and concatenated captions serve as anchors to preserve candidate recall, whereas appearance, action, and object facets provide bounded corrective evidence. The resulting candidate pool is further refined by a discriminative Qwen3 reranker and two complementary semantic verification modules based on anomaly-aware cloze completion and multi-agent evidence reasoning. Finally, an uncertainty-gated consensus module adaptively reweights the three experts on ambiguous queries. Experiments on the PAB benchmark show that the proposed soft claim-aware retrieval achieves 86.44% mAP@10, substantially outperforming individual retrieval backbones. The complete framework further improves performance to 95.41% mAP@10, 94.44% R@1, and 99.09% R@5. These results demonstrate that preserving strong global retrieval while restricting expensive semantic reasoning to a small candidate pool is effective for fine-grained Sim2Real person anomaly search. Our code will be available on Github.

</details>


### [218] [Agreement-Based Audio-Visual Segmentation:Champion Report for the MeViS-Audio Track in the 8th LSVOS Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09475)
*Yiwen Ren,Jianing Liu,Yingxin Wang,Kexin Zhang,Licheng Jiao,Lingling Li,Xu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MeViS-Audio赛道第一名的解决方案：采用Qwen3-ASR将语音转为文本，用多个互补的定位与分割模型生成候选掩码轨迹，通过平均掩码一致性选择最优轨迹，辅以方向、计数和复数规则修正，最后由视频级分类器判断目标是否存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MeViS-Audio任务要求系统分割视频中由口语运动表达描述的对象，并在目标缺失时返回空掩码。任务的挑战在于口语表达的复杂性（方向、计数、复数等）以及目标可能不存在的情况，单一路径预测难以可靠处理。

Method: 采用分阶段流水线：(1) Qwen3-ASR将语音转录为文本；(2) 多个互补的grounding和分割模型生成多个视频掩码轨迹；(3) 选择与其他候选平均掩码一致性最高的轨迹，而非信任单一预测；(4) 用显式的方向、计数和复数规则修正需要超越普通单目标跟踪的查询；(5) 视频级分类器融合视觉、音视频和视频内查询分数以判定目标是否存在。

Result: 系统在MeViS-Audio赛道获得J&F 0.5952，无目标准确率0.7931，目标准确率0.9205，最终得分0.769589，排名第一。

Conclusion: 多候选轨迹一致性投票策略结合显式语言规则修正和视频级目标存在性分类器，能够有效解决口语运动表达的视听分割任务并取得冠军成绩。

Abstract: The MeViS-Audio track asks a system to segment the objects described by a spoken motion expression throughout a video and to return empty masks when the described target is absent. We present a simple staged solution. Qwen3-ASR first converts speech into text. Several video mask tracks are then produced with complementary grounding and segmentation models. Instead of trusting a single prediction, we select the track that has the highest average mask agreement with the other candidates. A small set of explicit direction, count, and plural rules corrects queries that require more than ordinary single-object tracking. Finally, a video-level classifier combines visual, audio-visual, and within-video query scores to decide whether any target is present. The submitted system obtains 0.5952 J &F, 0.7931 no-target accuracy, 0.9205 target accuracy, and a final score of 0.769589. The challenge organizers notified our team that this result ranked first in the track.

</details>


### [219] [Beyond Uniform Restoration: Empowering All-in-One Restoration with Pixel-Level Multimodal Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09482)
*Chunxiao Liu,Wei Liu,Anbin Xiong,Erli Meng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MGN-AIR，一种像素级全合一图像复原框架，通过估计像素级视觉提示并结合文本与视觉提示提供全局和局部退化线索，实现更细粒度的复原控制，在去噪、去雨、去模糊、去雾、去雪和低光照增强等多个基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全合一图像复原方法虽然通过学习退化自适应提示或网络架构取得了显著进展，但它们通常对整幅图像采用统一的复原策略，忽略了不同区域可能遭受不同类型和不同程度退化的问题，缺乏像素级别的精细控制能力。

Method: 提出MGN-AIR像素级复原框架：首先学习估计像素级视觉提示，然后结合文本提示和视觉提示分别提供全局和局部退化线索，引导模型在每个像素位置知道"看哪里"以及"如何复原"。

Result: 在多个全合一图像复原基准上进行广泛实验，涵盖去噪、去雨、去模糊、去雾、去雪和低光照增强等任务，实验结果表明所提方法一致且显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 像素级的复原策略能够更精细地处理图像中不同区域不同类型的退化，MGN-AIR通过文本与视觉双提示机制实现了更准确的全局与局部退化感知，为全合一图像复原提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: All-in-one image restoration is a unified low-level vision task that aims to effectively recover high-quality images from inputs degraded by various types and levels of corruption using a single model. Recent works have achieved remarkable progress by learning degradation-adaptive prompts or network architectures. However, these methods typically apply a uniform restoration strategy across the entire image, neglecting the fact that different regions may suffer from distinct degradation types and varying degrees of severity. In contrast, we propose to perform restoration at the pixel level, thereby enabling more fine-grained and precise control over the restoration process. Specifically, we present MGN-AIR, a novel pixel-level restoration framework for all-in-one image restoration. Our approach first learns to estimate a pixel-level visual prompt. Then, it leverages both textual and visual prompts to provide global and local degradation cues, guiding the model on where to look and how to restore at each pixel. We conduct extensive experiments on multiple all-in-one image restoration benchmarks, covering a wide range of tasks including denoising, deraining, deblurring, dehazing, desnowing, and low-light enhancement. Experimental results demonstrate that our proposed method consistently and significantly outperforms existing approaches.

</details>


### [220] [GeoRoute: Geometry-Aware Hybrid Inference for Traffic Future-Frame Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09493)
*Khang Minh Le,Hieu Dinh Trung Pham,Luu Thanh Danh,Nam-Tien Le,Hieu Anh Ngo,Phuong Huu Vu Tran,Son Nguyen Minh Le,Nguyen Trong Nghia,Tu Tran Thi Cam,Huy Minh Nhat Nguyen,Cuong Tuan Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个无需训练的推理框架，通过多帧时间上下文和视角条件路由来稳定预训练视频扩散模型在长时程交通场景未来帧预测中的静态几何结构，在AI City Challenge Track 5基准上取得顶级排名竞争性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程未来帧预测对自动驾驶、交通监控等至关重要，但存在时间鬼影、几何漂移和目标运动不一致等问题。现有的潜在视频扩散模型虽能生成高质量视觉内容，但直接应用于结构化交通场景会导致几何不稳定和时间连贯性退化。

Method: 提出训练自由的推理框架：(1) 对前视摄像头视频，使用多帧深度分层渲染器，从观测历史帧中投影静态几何，同时保留生成基础模型中的动态区域；(2) 对异构交通视角，使用冻结的视觉-语言模型从观测片段推断粗略相机组，选择专门的基于运动的预测器；(3) 无需重新训练或微调底层视频模型，可直接应用于预训练生成器。

Result: 在AI City Challenge Track 5基准上验证，最终系统在顶级团队中取得具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 几何感知的推理时细化和视角条件混合推理能改善静态几何稳定性和低层结构保真度，而无需改变原始模型架构。

Abstract: Long-horizon future-frame prediction is important for autonomous driving, traffic surveillance, and intelligent transportation systems, yet remains challenging due to temporal ghosting, geometry drift, and inconsistent object motion. Recent latent video diffusion models have achieved impressive visual quality, but directly applying them to structured traffic scenes often leads to unstable geometry and degraded temporal coherence over extended horizons. We present a training-free inference framework that stabilizes reliable static structure in pretrained video predictions through multi-frame temporal context and view-conditioned routing. For front-camera videos, our method refines generated futures with a multi-frame depth-layered renderer that projects static geometry from observed history frames while preserving dynamic regions from the generative base model. For heterogeneous traffic views, a frozen vision-language model infers a coarse camera group from the observed clip and selects a specialized motion-based predictor. The framework requires neither retraining nor fine-tuning of the underlying video model and can be applied directly to pretrained generators. We validate the proposed framework on the AI City Challenge Track 5 benchmark, where our final system achieves competitive performance among the top-ranked teams. These results demonstrate that geometry-aware inference-time refinement and view-conditioned hybrid inference can improve static-geometry stability and low-level structural fidelity without changing the original model architecture.

</details>


### [221] [SwissCrop25: A National Multi-Year Benchmark for Operational Crop Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09497)
*Thomas Lauber,Mehmet Ozgur Turkoglu,Sélène Ledain,Helge Aasen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了SwissCrop25，一个覆盖瑞士七个生长季（2019-2025）的国家级作物测绘基准数据集，包含Sentinel-2时间序列、每日温度观测、73类细粒度作物分类（含草地管理类型）和5类非作物土地覆盖类别。通过留一年验证协议和联合耕地划分与作物分类任务，对U-TAE、TSViT和Galileo等代表性架构进行基准评估，发现领域特定模型优于基础模型，TSViT取得最佳整体性能，比U-TAE高出12个百分点宏mIoU优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有作物测绘数据集只能孤立地评估泛化能力、细粒度分类和耕地区分这三个关键需求，缺乏能够同时评估这三个方面挑战的综合基准。作者希望构建一个更贴近实际运营条件的基准数据集。

Method: 构建SwissCrop25数据集，整合Sentinel-2时间序列和每日温度观测，采用73类细粒度作物分类体系（含草地管理类型）和5类非作物土地覆盖类别；设计留一年验证（leave-one-year-out）协议，定义联合耕地划分与作物分类任务，用于评估三种代表性模型架构（卷积时序注意力模型U-TAE、Transformer时空模型TSViT、EO基础模型Galileo）。

Result: 领域特定模型在现实部署条件下优于基础模型Galileo；TSViT取得最佳整体性能，相对U-TAE有12个百分点宏mIoU优势；数据集暴露了显著的跨年分布偏移；融合温度衍生的物候信息可提升鲁棒性；季节内评估显示模型间存在权衡——U-TAE在生长季早期表现更好，而TSViT后期通过改善稀有类别判别获得优势。

Conclusion: SwissCrop25为在现实运营条件下评估作物测绘系统提供了具有挑战性的测试平台，揭示了常规基准无法显现的架构间差异，并公开数据集供社区使用。

Abstract: Operational crop mapping requires models that generalise across years, resolve fine-grained crop taxonomies, and distinguish cropland from surrounding landscapes. However, existing crop mapping datasets enable evaluation of these requirements only in isolation. We therefore introduce SwissCrop25, a national-scale crop mapping benchmark dataset spanning seven growing seasons (2019-2025). SwissCrop25 combines Sentinel-2 time series, daily temperature observations, a fine-grained 73 crop taxonomy including grassland management types, and 5 explicit non-crop land cover classes. To evaluate realistic deployment conditions, we define a leave-one-year-out protocol with joint cropland delineation and crop classification for benchmarking representative crop mapping architectures. Evaluating U-TAE (convolutional temporal-attention model), TSViT (transformer-based spatio-temporal model), and Galileo (EO foundation model) reveals differences between architectures hidden by conventional benchmarks. In this setting, domain-specific models outperform Galileo, with TSViT achieving the best overall performance and a 12 pp macro-mIoU advantage over U-TAE. SwissCrop25 also exposes substantial interannual distribution shifts and shows that incorporating temperature-derived phenological information improves robustness. Finally, in-season evaluation reveals a trade-off between models, with U-TAE performing better early in the season and TSViT gaining an advantage later through improved rare-class discrimination. SwissCrop25 provides a challenging testbed for evaluating crop mapping systems under realistic operational conditions and is publicly released at https://huggingface.co/datasets/EOA-team/SwissCrop25 .

</details>


### [222] [XFeat Revisited: Reproducibility and Evaluation of a Lightweight Image Matcher](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09519)
*Lazar Đoković,Aimee Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对轻量级局部特征提取与匹配器XFeat进行了可复现性研究，重新实现了其架构，重新评估了作者发布的checkpoint，并通过架构消融实验和下游任务评估揭示了许多原始论文中未充分说明的实现细节和设计选择问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XFeat论文、补充材料和公开代码在骨干网络布局、融合模块和训练损失等多个实现细节上存在差异，且原始论文中部分架构设计决策缺乏充分论证。因此需要系统性的可复现性研究来验证其核心主张，并评估其在更广泛场景下的泛化能力。

Method: 基于论文和补充材料重新实现XFeat架构；对比重新实现模型与作者官方checkpoint在MegaDepth-1500和ScanNet-1500上的性能；进行额外的架构消融实验（特别是并行关键点分支和跳跃连接的放置）；复现下游任务评估（单应矩阵估计和Aachen视觉定位）；并扩展到零样本跨域及跨模态匹配场景（视网膜、热红外-可见光、多模态遥感图像）。

Result: 重新实现的模型在MegaDepth-1500和ScanNet-1500上与原始checkpoint表现接近甚至更优；消融实验表明并行关键点分支对半稠密匹配重要但收益不如原始论文所述，跳跃连接放置的证据尚不确定；单应估计复现结果接近，但Aachen视觉定位结果低于原论文报告，即使是官方checkpoint也如此；在严重模态差异下XFeat性能急剧下降。

Conclusion: XFeat在标准图像匹配基准上准确率-效率权衡的核心主张得到支持，但原始论文对部分架构设计的论证过于乐观，且跨模态极端场景下的泛化能力有限，同时下游任务评估细节的不完整对结果重现造成显著影响。

Abstract: We present a reproducibility study of XFeat, a lightweight local feature extractor and matcher designed to identify corresponding points across images efficiently on resource-constrained hardware. We re-implement the architecture based on the paper and supplementary material, re-evaluate the authors' released checkpoint alongside our re-implementation, and conduct additional architectural ablations to examine design choices that were not fully justified in the original work. This distinction between re-evaluation and reproduction is important, as the paper, supplement, and public code differ in several implementation details, including the backbone layout, fusion block, and training losses. Empirically, our reproduced models closely match and, in some cases, outperform the re-evaluated original checkpoint on MegaDepth-1500 and ScanNet-1500, supporting the main claim that XFeat provides a strong accuracy-efficiency trade-off for standard image-matching benchmarks. Our ablations provide a more nuanced view of two architectural arguments from the original paper. In particular, the parallel keypoint branch is important for semi-dense matching, but its benefit is less pronounced than originally claimed, while the evidence for the specific placement of the single skip-connection remains inconclusive. Finally, we reproduce the original downstream evaluations and find close agreement for homography estimation, while Aachen visual localization remains below the reported results, even for the released checkpoint, suggesting sensitivity to underspecified evaluation details. We then extend the analysis to zero-shot out-of-distribution and cross-modal matching across retinal, thermal-visible, and multimodal remote-sensing imagery, where XFeat remains effective in some settings but degrades sharply under severe modality shifts.

</details>


### [223] [A Height-Constrained 2-Point Minimal Solver for Pose Estimation from Active LED Markers with Event Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09520)
*Runze Yuan,Alexander Kappler,Jun Zhang,Kuangyi Chen,Fabio Morbidi,Pascal Vasseur,Cédric Demonceaux,Friedrich Fraundorfer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种仅使用两个LED标记物即可估计相机位姿的最小求解器，通过融合IMU/高度计提供的倾斜角与相机高度信息，在保持精度的同时降低了对标记物数量的需求，适用于空间受限的自主定位场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于事件相机和主动LED标记的实时定位方法通常依赖PnP求解器（如P3P），需要至少三个标记物。但在空间受限的场景中部署结构化标记布局较为困难，而机载传感器（IMU、高度计）已能提供部分自运动信息（重力方向和高度），这些信息未被充分利用。

Method: 推导了一个最小求解器，利用两个LED标记并结合已知的倾斜角（来自IMU）和相机高度（来自高度计）来估计相机位姿。该公式提供闭式解和线性最小二乘解两种求解方式，并分析了退化配置，刻画了高度信息对旋转估计无贡献的条件。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据（自主构建的事件相机主动标记系统配合运动捕捉系统提供真值）上进行了实验，结果表明所提方法在精度上优于最先进的P2P求解器，并与P3P性能相当。

Conclusion: 通过利用机载传感器提供的倾斜角度和高度信息，仅需两个LED标记即可实现与P3P相媲美的定位精度，在空间受限场景中具有明显优势，为事件相机主动标记定位提供了一种更灵活的替代方案。

Abstract: In many autonomous applications requiring real-time localization, active marker-based systems are preferred due to their low latency and ease of deployment compared to computationally demanding feature-based methods. Event~\mbox{cameras} offer high temporal resolution and minimal delay and are commonly used with active LED markers for robust real-time localization. Existing methods typically rely on Perspective-n-Point (PnP) solvers for pose estimation. However, structured marker layouts can be challenging to deploy in space-constrained scenarios, while partial self-motion information (e.g., gravity direction and altitude) is readily available from onboard sensors. We derive a robust and accurate minimal solver that estimates camera pose from only two LED markers by incorporating known tilt angle and camera height measured by an onboard sensor, such as an IMU or an altimeter. The proposed formulation uniquely determines the camera pose through both a closed-form and a linear least-squares solution. We further analyze degenerate configurations and characterize the conditions under which height information does not contribute to rotation estimation. For evaluation, we developed an event-based active marker system to collect real-world data with ground truth from a motion capture system. Experiments on both synthetic and real data demonstrate improved accuracy over the state-of-the-art P2P solver and competitive performance relative to P3P.

</details>


### [224] [TriView-YOLO: Early Multi-View Fusion for Ground Penetrating Radar Cavity Detection in Soft, High-Water-Content Soils](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09522)
*Suphawut Thawinutchokaudom,Sompote Youwai,Warat Kongkitkul,Mitsumasa Yamashina,Jose M. D. S. Rodrigues Neto,Jun Shinohara*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TriView-YOLO，一种面向高含水率软土路面下探地雷达（GPR）空洞检测的多视角YOLOv12检测器。通过将纵向B-scan、水平C-scan和横截面B-scan三个共配准视图融合为9通道输入，替换YOLOv12的stem层，在曼谷软海相黏土（含水量80-140%）实地数据集上实现mAP50达0.558±0.028，且消融实验表明辅助视图有效，而公共/合成训练数据、更大模型规模和COCO预训练均无增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高含水率的软弱地基中，GPR信号被导电的饱和土壤强烈衰减，导致空洞反射退化，检测极为困难。然而这正是空洞最易形成的工况条件。目前缺乏针对此类地质条件下专用的深度学习空洞检测评估研究，因此需要开发专门的检测框架。

Method: 构建TriView-YOLO：将三个共配准视图（纵向B-scan、水平C-scan、横截面B-scan）拼接为9通道输入，通过TripleInputConv层替换YOLOv12的stem，网络其余部分保持不变，仅在纵向视图上设置边界框监督。训练使用1,600个专家验证的实地样本（主要来自曼谷道路普查，辅以日本较坚硬路基数据），测试集完全来自曼谷软黏土地区。

Result: 在未经数据增强的纯实地测试集上（按测线内随机划分），TriView-YOLO在三个随机种子下达到mAP50为0.558±0.028，计算量为23.6 GFLOPs，单张图像推理耗时3.1ms。消融实验表明移除辅助视图会降低mAP50和召回率，而公共/合成训练图像、DINOv3特征、更大模型规模及COCO预训练均无性能增益。

Conclusion: 多视角信息融合对于衰减严重的GPR空洞检测至关重要，但仅在真实现场数据上的训练才是有效的；外部数据、更大模型和特征预训练在目标地质条件下均无益。该方法为软土地基中的道路空洞筛查提供了首个专用深度学习基准评估。

Abstract: Automated detection of subsurface cavities from Ground Penetrating Radar (GPR) is most difficult in soft, high-water-content ground, where conductive, water-saturated soil attenuates the signal and degrades cavity reflections, yet this is also the condition under which cavities most readily form. This paper proposes TriView-YOLO, a multi-view YOLOv12 detector for road cavity screening in such ground. Three co-registered views (longitudinal B-scan, horizontal C-scan, and cross-section B-scan) form a 9-channel input fused by a TripleInputConv layer that replaces the YOLOv12 stem; the rest of the network is unchanged, and bounding boxes are required on the longitudinal view only. Training used 1,600 expert-verified field samples, principally metropolitan road surveys of Bangkok, Thailand, acquired with a vehicle-mounted multichannel three-dimensional GPR mobile mapping system, with surveys over the firmer subgrades of Japan added to training and validation only. The test set comes exclusively from the Bangkok surveys, over soft marine clay with 80-140% water content and a water table at 1-2 m depth, a ground condition for which no dedicated deep learning cavity-detection evaluation has been reported. On this unaugmented, field-only test set, split randomly within surveys, the proposed model attains mAP50 of 0.558 +/- 0.028 over three seeds at 23.6 GFLOPs and 3.1 ms per image. Ablations show that removing the auxiliary views lowers mAP50 and recall, whereas public and synthetic training images, DINOv3 features, larger model scale, and COCO pretraining bring no gain.

</details>


### [225] [Towards Expressive and Faithful Audio-to-Image Generation: A Unified Multimodal Dataset and Synthesis Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09529)
*Dongxu Ge,Shansong Liu,Cheng Gong,Xiao-Lei Zhang,Chi Zhang,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对音频到图像生成（A2I）领域数据质量不足的问题，提出了A2I-Set三模态数据集（包含323K配对音频、图像和文本描述），并基于此微调出AudioCanvas模型，在视觉表现力和跨模态对齐上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有A2I生成性能受限于传统数据集缺乏高保真图像和精确跨模态对齐，即使借助强大的T2I模型进行微调仍难以实现高质量音频到图像生成，制约了实际应用。

Method: 1) 构建A2I-Set：包含323K配对音频、图像和详细文本描述的统一高质量三模态数据集；2) 开发经人工监督的混合来源测试集；3) 提出AudioCanvas模型，在A2I-Set上对强T2I模型进行微调。

Result: AudioCanvas在视觉表现力和跨模态对齐方面均取得更优效果，整体性能超越现有方法。

Conclusion: 高质量三模态数据集是提升A2I生成的关键，AudioCanvas验证了通过微调强T2I模型可以获得更佳的音频条件图像生成效果，数据集和代码已开源。

Abstract: As an important subfield of cross-modal generation, synthesizing static visual content in the form of images from audio, namely audio-to-image (A2I) generation, has attracted increasing research attention in recent years. Nevertheless, despite the remarkable visual quality of modern text-to-image (T2I) models, the performance of A2I remains fundamentally limited by traditional datasets, which often lack both high-fidelity images and precise cross-modal alignment. As a result, existing methods still struggle to achieve high-quality audio-to-image generation through finetuning strong T2I models, thereby constraining practical applications in this area. Motivated by this gap, we introduce A2I-Set, a unified, high-quality tri-modal dataset consisting of 323K paired audio, images, and detailed text captions, specifically designed for audio-visual research, including audio-conditioned image generation. Besides, we developed a new mixed-source test set for the A2I task through human supervision. We further propose an A2I model, AudioCanvas, fine-tuned on our A2I-Set. Experiments show that AudioCanvas achieves more visually expressive as well as cross-modal alignment results that generally outperforming existing approaches. Our dataset and source code are available at https://github.com/gdx012/A2I-Generation.

</details>


### [226] [DocPure: Prompt-Free Unified Document Restoration via Degradation-Aware Structure-Guided Wavelet Modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09536)
*Lingming Su,Wanglong Lu,Tao Wang,Kaihao Zhang,Nan Zhang,Liyan An,Hanli Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DocPure是一个无需提示（prompt-free）的统一文档图像恢复框架，通过退化感知结构自编码器和结构引导的小波交互机制，在推理阶段无需退化标签即可同时处理去模糊、去噪、压缩伪影去除和去阴影等多种退化任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量文档图像对信息存档和下游自动处理至关重要，但在采集和传输过程中常受到多种退化影响。现有的统一文档恢复方法存在三个主要问题：需要训练多个退化专用模型、依赖人工任务特定提示、或需要跨任务数据配对。作者希望解决这些局限性，提出一个无需提示的统一恢复框架。

Method: DocPure包含两个核心设计：1）退化感知结构自编码器：通过退化信息路由正则化从退化输入中预测干净的结构先验，推理时无需提示，退化标签仅作为训练阶段的辅助监督信号；2）结构引导的小波交互机制：桥接频域特征和空间语义，其中跨频率自适应调制利用低频子带调制高频恢复，确保结构一致性。

Result: 在去模糊、去噪、压缩伪影去除和去阴影等多个任务上，DocPure相比现有最先进方法取得了更强的性能。

Conclusion: DocPure成功实现了无需提示的统一文档图像恢复框架，克服了多退化模型训练、人工提示依赖和跨任务数据配对等限制，通过退化感知结构和频域-空间交互机制在多种退化任务上达到先进水平。

Abstract: High-quality document images are pivotal for information archiving and downstream automatic processing. However, they are frequently compromised by diverse degradations during uncontrolled acquisition and transmission. While unified document restoration techniques have been proposed to restore images from multiple degradations, they often struggle with training multiple degradation-specific models, reliance on manual task-specific prompts, or cross-task data pairing. To address these limitations, we propose DocPure, a prompt-free unified framework that achieves degradation-aware document restoration.
  We design a degradation-aware structure auto-encoder with degradation-informed routing regularization to predict clean structural priors from degraded inputs. The model is prompt-free at inference, and degradation labels are only used as auxiliary supervision for the routing regularization during training.
  Furthermore, we introduce a structure-guided wavelet interaction mechanism to bridge frequency-domain features and spatial semantics. Within the structure-guided wavelet interaction mechanism, a cross-frequency adaptive modulation utilizes low-frequency sub-bands to modulate high-frequency recovery, ensuring structural consistency. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DocPure achieves strong performance compared with state-of-the-art methods across various tasks, including deblurring, denoising, compression artifact reduction, and deshadowing.

</details>


### [227] [Towards Collaborative Joint Perception and Prediction: Framework, Baseline Evaluation, and Deployment Perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09541)
*Lei Wan,Hannan Ejaz Keen,Alexey Vinel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种协作联合感知与预测（Co-P\&P）框架，统一协同感知与运动预测，以缓解感知误差累积和视觉遮挡两大挑战。实验比较了不同融合策略，发现预测级融合性能下降，而检测级或跟踪级融合更优。端到端原型通过RENO神经编解码器共享点云并联合FutureDet进行检测-预测，在约34倍低通信带宽下仍保持协作带来的预测精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协同感知（CP）虽然通过V2X通信充分利用多源传感器信息，但仍面临感知误差累积和视觉遮挡两大持续挑战。现有研究大多局限于感知层面，未将感知与运动预测统一考虑，因此需要一种将两者结合的新范式来提升复杂动态交通环境中的态势感知能力。

Method: 提出Co-P\&P概念框架，统一协同感知与运动预测；对比预测级、检测级、跟踪级三种融合策略的性能；实现最小化的端到端原型，将RENO神经编解码器用于协作点云共享，并与FutureDet的联合检测-预测模块耦合。

Result: 实验表明预测级融合相对检测级或跟踪级融合会导致整体系统性能下降；端到端Co-P\&P原型显示协作能提升预测精度，且神经压缩在约34倍更低通信带宽下仍能保持这一效益。

Conclusion: Co-P\&P作为一种联合感知与预测的统一范式，能够有效提升复杂交通环境中的态势感知能力。检测级或跟踪级融合优于预测级融合，且神经压缩技术可在大幅降低通信开销的同时保留协作预测的收益，为未来CAV协同系统的实际部署提供了可行方向。

Abstract: Connected Autonomous Vehicles (CAVs) increasingly exploit Vehicle-to-Everything (V2X) communication to exchange multi-source sensor information, enabling advanced Collaborative Perception (CP) capabilities. Extending beyond these capabilities, this work focuses on Collaborative Joint Perception and Prediction (Co-P&P), a paradigm that unifies CP with motion prediction to mitigate two persistent challenges: the accumulation of perception errors and visual occlusions. We present a conceptual framework for Collaborative Joint Perception and Prediction (Co-P&P) that improves motion prediction of surrounding road users, thereby enhancing situational awareness in complex and dynamic traffic environments. Building upon our preliminary study, this extended version compares the performance of different fusion strategies and establishes baseline performance for a modular design of perception and prediction. Experimental results show that prediction-level fusion leads to a decline in overall system performance compared to detection-level or tracking-level fusion. We further implement a minimal end-to-end Co-P&P prototype that couples collaborative point-cloud sharing via the RENO neural codec with joint detection-forecasting via FutureDet, showing that collaboration improves forecasting accuracy while neural compression preserves this benefit at roughly 34x lower communication bandwidth.

</details>


### [228] [PressureMesh: 3D Human Mesh Estimation from Multi-Device Pressure Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09550)
*Changhai Ma,Ziyu Wu,Yunkang Zhang,Fangting Xie,Mengting Niu,Heyu Ding,Quan Wan,Jiayue Yuan,Boyan Liu,Yi Ke,Xiaohui Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MDP-Net，一种端到端网络，能够从多设备的时间压力数据中直接估计人体网格，解决了现有压力感知方法局限于单设备、监测范围受限的问题。同时构建了高质量的MDP多设备时间压力数据集，实验表明该方法在人体姿态监测上具有良好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人体姿态监测在康复评估和人机交互等领域至关重要，压力感知因其隐私保护特性成为非侵入式传感的主要方式。然而现有方法受限于单一设备，监测范围有限，亟需一种能够融合多设备压力信息的方法来扩大有效监测范围。

Method: 提出MDP-Net端到端网络，直接从多设备时间压力数据估计人体网格；采用基于Mixture of Experts (MoE)框架启发的多模态融合机制，实现跨设备压力信息的有效互补与增强；构建MDP多设备时间压力数据集，包含2D/3D关节点和人体网格等多种姿态标签。

Result: 在MDP数据集上，MDP-Net实现了12.6cm的关节点位置误差，验证了多设备压力信息融合的有效性。

Conclusion: 融合多设备压力信息是日常人体姿态监测的一种有效且有前景的新解决方案，MDP-Net的成功证明了该方向的可行性。

Abstract: Human pose monitoring is crucial in fields such as rehabilitation assessment and human-computer interaction. Due to its privacy-preserving nature, pressure-based human pose monitoring has become a primary approach for unobtrusive sensing. However, existing methods are generally limited to a single device, which restricts the effective monitoring range. To address this limitation, we propose MDP-Net, an end-to-end network capable of directly estimating human meshes from temporal pressure data across multiple devices. We introduce a multimodal fusion mechanism inspired by the Mixture of Experts (MoE) framework to achieve effective complementarity and enhancement of cross-device pressure information. To support the training and evaluation of MDP-Net, we constructed MDP, a high-quality multi-device temporal pressure dataset that includes various pose labels such as 2D/3D joints and human meshes. Experimental results demonstrate that MDP-Net achieves a joint position error of 12.6 cm on the MDP dataset. These results prove that fusing multi-device pressure information is an effective and promising new solution for daily human pose monitoring.

</details>


### [229] [From Semantic Grounding to Decision Optimization: A Unified Framework for Long-Horizon UAV Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09564)
*Zeyuan Ma,Jiaxin Chen,Di Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的自语义到决策的框架来解决无人机视觉-语言导航（UAV-VLN）中的三个耦合问题：指令相关地标在视觉中的弱定位、长时程历史利用不足、以及局部陷阱或重复探索下的决策不稳定。方法包括指令接地语义增强模块、相关性感知动态时序聚合策略和拓扑感知决策方法，在AerialVLN和OpenFly基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UAV-VLN方法存在三个相互耦合的问题：视觉观测中指令相关地标的接地能力弱、长时程历史信息利用不充分、以及在局部陷阱或重复探索情境下决策不稳定，这限制了无人机智能体在开放3D环境中遵循自然语言指令导航的能力。

Method: 提出统一的语义到决策框架，包含三个核心组件：1) 指令接地语义增强模块，将物体级语义和相对空间线索注入当前观测状态；2) 相关性感知动态时序聚合策略，对完整历史缓冲区进行重加权，并将少数高相关帧转化为结构化地标提示供解码器使用；3) 拓扑感知决策方法，结合局部最优认知与基于进度、目标、语义和路径合规奖励的组相对策略优化。

Result: 在广泛使用的AerialVLN和OpenFly基准测试上，该方法达到了最先进的性能（SOTA）。

Conclusion: 通过统一的语义到决策框架，同时解决了指令接地、历史利用和决策稳定性三个耦合问题，实证验证了该方法的有效性与先进性。

Abstract: UAV vision-language navigation (UAV-VLN) focuses on enabling an aerial agent to follow natural-language instructions in open 3D environments from egocentric visual observations. Current approaches suffer from three coupled issues: weak grounding of instruction-relevant landmarks in visual observations, insufficient exploitation of long-horizon history, and unstable decisions under local traps or repeated exploration. To address these issues, we propose a unified semantic-to-decision framework. First, we present an instruction-grounded semantic enhancement module that injects object-level semantics and relative spatial cues into the current observation state. Subsequently, we develop a relevance-aware dynamic temporal aggregation strategy that reweights the full history buffer while converting a few high-relevance frames into structured landmark prompts for the decoder. Finally, we devise a topology-aware decision method that combines local-optimum cognition with group-relative policy optimization under progress, goal, semantic, and path-compliance rewards. Experiments on the widely used AerialVLN and OpenFly benchmarks clearly demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [230] [VideoVIBE: A Video-Grounded Diagnostic Benchmark for One-Shot Interactive Website Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09573)
*Jiajun Xu,Yanghao Zhou,Jingyun Liao,Yu Bai,Jinxing Zhou,Chengliang Liu,Changsen Yuan,Bo Wang,Qian Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了VideoVIBE基准和V2Lens系统，将自然语言驱动的"vibe coding"生成的网页应用质量评估从孤立人工制品评分转向基于视频的可诊断评估。VideoVIBE包含约1.7K个诊断性视频QA实例，覆盖6,338个已验证的失败案例；V2Lens是一个无需训练、基于证据的多智能体系统，通过视觉和源代码验证来挑战和精炼初始视频诊断。实验显示Gemini-2.5-Flash为最强单模型（64.54分），V2Lens达到71.72分，提升7.18分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于自然语言的"vibe coding"生成的网页应用质量评估方法滞后，通常仅对孤立人工制品或最终任务结果打分，无法提供关于哪些失败发生及原因的细粒度证据。需要一种行为上忠实且诊断信息丰富的评估方式。

Method: 引入VideoVIBE基准：将人工操作的网页录制视频转化为细粒度诊断任务，包含约1.7K个诊断视频QA实例，源自6,338个已验证的失败案例，覆盖语义逻辑、视觉运动、结构时序和功能四类失败。提出V2Lens：一个免训练、基于证据的多智能体系统，通过针对性的视觉和源代码验证来挑战并选择性精炼初始视频诊断。

Result: 在13个闭源和开源视频多模态大语言模型上评测，Gemini-2.5-Flash是最强单模型（64.54分），V2Lens达到71.72分，比最强单模型提升7.18分。

Conclusion: 视频导向的评估可以超越孤立人工制品和聚合结果，走向对生成应用质量的行为忠实且诊断信息丰富的评估框架。

Abstract: Natural-language-driven "vibe coding" enables the one-shot generation of visually rich and interactive web applications, yet reliable assessment of their quality has not kept pace. Existing evaluations often score isolated artifacts or final task outcomes, offering limited evidence about which failures occur and why. We introduce VideoVIBE, a video-grounded benchmark that transforms human-operated webpage recordings into fine-grained diagnostic tasks. It contains approximately 1.7K diagnostic Video QA instances derived from 6,338 verified failures across generated webpages, spanning semantic-logical, visual-motion, structural-temporal, and functional failures. Diagnoses are grounded primarily in recorded presentation and behavior, with webpage source code used as complementary context. We further propose V2Lens, a training-free, evidence-grounded multi-agent system that challenges and selectively refines initial video-based diagnoses through targeted visual and source-code verification. Across thirteen closed-source and open-weight Video MLLMs, Gemini-2.5-Flash is the strongest standalone model with a score of 64.54, while V2Lens reaches 71.72, an improvement of 7.18 points. Together, our results show that video-grounded evaluation can move beyond isolated artifacts and aggregate outcomes toward a behaviorally faithful and diagnostically informative account of generated application quality.

</details>


### [231] [MSP-Net: Manifold-Guided Spectral Prompt Network for Hyperspectral Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09575)
*Juliu Li,Hanlin Qin,Shuowen Yang,Jingjing Li,Yuedong Tan,Shuai Yuan,Huixin Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MSP-Net（流形引导的谱提示网络），用于高光谱目标跟踪。该网络通过图驱动的流形路由重建波段关系并形成自适应光谱分组，联合分组光谱统计与模板外观构建动态条件提示，并随跟踪过程实时演化，以增强目标特征并抑制背景干扰。在HOT2020和HOT2023基准上分别取得超过0.80的AUC和0.96的Precision。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高光谱目标跟踪方法将高光谱图像视为RGB的多通道扩展，在固定波段顺序下进行特征融合，导致模型依赖特定传感器配置、忽略波段间流形关系，难以泛化到异构传感器。此外，波段的判别贡献随目标属性和场景变化而动态改变，静态融合策略表达力有限。

Method: MSP-Net包含三个关键模块：1) 图驱动的流形路由重构波段关系并形成自适应光谱分组；2) 将分组光谱统计与模板外观联合，构建目标相关的动态条件提示，增强目标特征并抑制背景；3) 随跟踪进程基于中间目标表征持续演化谱条件，并利用可靠历史状态约束目标定位与尺度波动，提升跨传感器时间稳定性。

Result: 在HOT2020和HOT2023数据集上，MSP-Net的AUC超过0.80，Precision超过0.96，在异构传感器、目标形变和复杂背景条件下表现出优异的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MSP-Net通过流形引导的自适应光谱分组和动态条件提示机制，有效解决了高光谱跟踪中传感器泛化性差和静态融合表达力不足的问题，为跨传感器高光谱目标跟踪提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Hyperspectral object tracking leverages abundant spectral information to provide unique advantages for target discrimination in complex scenes. However, existing methods typically treat hyperspectral images as multi-channel extensions of RGB images, performing feature fusion in fixed band order. This approach leads to models dependent on specific sensor configurations while neglecting manifold relationships between bands, making generalization to heterogeneous sensors difficult. Moreover, the discriminative contribution of bands dynamically changes with target attributes and scene variations, further limiting the representational capacity of static fusion strategies. To address this, we propose the Manifold-Guided Spectral Prompt Network (MSP-Net). This network first reconstructs band relationships and forms adaptive spectral grouping through graph-driven manifold routing, then jointly integrates grouped spectral statistics with template appearance to construct target-related dynamic conditional prompts, enhancing target features while suppressing background interference. Furthermore, as tracking progresses, spectral conditions continuously evolve based on intermediate target representations, enabling target prompts to adapt in real-time to appearance and scene changes. Meanwhile, reliable historical states are used to constrain target localization and scale fluctuations, significantly improving temporal stability in cross-sensor tracking. Experiments on HOT2020 and HOT2023 demonstrate that MSP-Net achieves AUC and Precision exceeding 0.80 and 0.96, respectively, exhibiting exceptional robustness under heterogeneous sensors, target deformation, and complex background conditions. The code will be released at https://github.com/GGML668897/MSP-Net.

</details>


### [232] [You Only Flow Once: Calibrated and Real-Time Radar Pose Estimation with Multi-Hypothesis Normalizing Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09579)
*Jonas Leo Mueller,Sebastian Hoefler,Dario Zanca,Naga Venkata Sai Jitin Jami,Thomas Altstidl,Bjoern M. Eskofier*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了MH-NFPG，一种基于条件归一化流的雷达人体姿态估计方法，通过单次前向传播并行生成多假设姿态分布，在三个雷达基准上优于扩散模型，推理速度快20倍以上，校准误差降低达85%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏且有噪声的毫米波雷达点云观测通常对应多个合理的人体姿态，导致确定性姿态估计本质上是病态的。现有雷达方法仍是确定性的，无法建模多假设分布；扩散模型虽能建模多假设但需要昂贵的顺序去噪采样且缺乏校准的不确定性。

Method: 将时空Transformer骨干网络与条件归一化流相结合，将Laplace基分布通过单次前向传播并行变换为具有表现力的后验分布，实现高效的多假设姿态生成。

Result: 在三个雷达基准（MM-Fi、mmRadPose、mRI）上，校准性能优于扩散模型，姿态精度在两个数据集上提升、一个持平，推理速度提升超过20倍，校准误差降低最高达85%。扩散模型校准严重退化，而流方法保持可靠的覆盖率，且在跨环境设置中同样有效。

Conclusion: 归一化流可作为扩散模型的实际替代方案，用于实时、不确定性感知的雷达姿态估计，且代码将公开。

Abstract: Sparse and noisy millimeter-wave radar point cloud observations often correspond to multiple plausible human poses, making deterministic pose estimation fundamentally ill-posed. Yet existing radar methods remain deterministic, collapsing this ambiguity into a single estimate. Diffusion-based alternatives can model multi-hypothesis distributions but require costly sequential denoising for each distribution sample and lack calibrated uncertainty. We propose Multi-Hypothesis Normalizing Flow Pose Generator (MH-NFPG), which models pose distributions from radar point clouds using a conditional normalizing flow. Specifically, we combine a spatiotemporal transformer backbone with a normalizing flow that transforms a Laplace base distribution into an expressive posterior, generated in parallel through a single forward pass. Leveraging this efficiency, we outperform diffusion-based alternatives in calibration across three radar benchmarks (MM-Fi, mmRadPose, mRI), improve pose accuracy on two, and match it on the third, while achieving over 20x faster inference for applications and reducing calibration error by up to 85%. We find that calibration degrades substantially for diffusion models, whereas our flow-based approach maintains reliable coverage, also in cross-environment settings. These results demonstrate normalizing flows as a practical alternative to diffusion models for real-time, uncertainty-aware radar pose estimation. Our code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [233] [GenTrack3: Hybrid Stochastic-Deterministic Online Multi-Object Tracking with Cluster-Aware Association](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09581)
*Toan Van Nguyen,Rasmus G. K. Christiansen,Dirk Kraft,Leon Bodenhagen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在线多目标跟踪（MOT）框架，该框架融合了确定性方法（如检测后跟踪的数据关联）和随机方法（如粒子滤波）的优势，以在不确定性条件下实现稳健跟踪。同时引入了一种新颖的轨迹-检测匹配方法，提升目标数量增加时的可扩展性，并支持群体跟踪，参考实现已在GitHub上开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定性MOT方法（如基于检测的跟踪与数据关联）结果可复现、计算高效，但过度依赖运动模型且对噪声检测敏感，容易产生关联错误；而随机方法虽能显式建模不确定性、处理复杂非线性动态，但计算复杂度高且随机采样导致结果波动。单独使用任一范式均存在局限，亟需一种融合两者优势的框架。

Method: 提出一个在线MOT框架，将确定性原理与随机原理（粒子集）相结合。每个目标关联一个随机粒子集用于计算与检测的匹配代价。引入新颖的轨迹-检测匹配方法以提高目标数增加时的可扩展性，并支持群体跟踪。跟踪推理机制采用轨迹块（tracklet），包含标识符、状态、速度、轨迹惩罚和轨迹年龄等属性，支撑系统化跟踪流程。

Result: 论文提供了所提方法及基线跟踪器的参考实现，代码已发布在GitHub上（https://github.com/SDU-VelKoTek/GenTrack3），用于验证方法的有效性和可复现性。

Conclusion: 通过融合确定性与随机跟踪原理，该框架能够在不确定性条件下实现稳健的多目标跟踪，同时凭借可扩展的匹配方法支持规模较大的目标和群体跟踪场景。

Abstract: Multi-object tracking (MOT) involves maintaining consistent target identities as objects dynamically enter and leave a scene. Deterministic approaches, such as tracking-by-detection with data association, produce reproducible results and are computationally efficient, but they rely heavily on motion models and are sensitive to noisy detections that can lead to association errors. In contrast, stochastic methods explicitly model uncertainty and can better handle complex non-linear dynamics, albeit at the cost of increased computational complexity and variability arising from random sampling. This paper presents an online MOT framework that integrates deterministic and stochastic principles to achieve robust tracking under uncertainty. Furthermore, a novel track-to-detection matching approach is introduced to enhance scalability with increasing target numbers while supporting group tracking. The tracking inference mechanism employs a tracklet that includes identifiers, states, velocities, track penalties and track ages of targets, supporting a systematic tracking pipeline. Each target is associated with a stochastic particle set to compute the matching cost to detections. Reference implementations of the proposed approach and baseline trackers can be found on GitHub: https://github.com/SDU-VelKoTek/GenTrack3.

</details>


### [234] [TeaMatch: Teachable Cross-Modal Representation Learning for 2D-3D Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09590)
*Chongjian Wang,Junjie Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TeaMatch是一个用于2D-3D匹配的框架，引入了"可教性"（teachability）作为跨模态表征学习的标准，通过任务特定的弱学生模型模拟常见失败模式，并优化教师模型以提升学生在退化输入下恢复可靠对应关系的能力，在不增加推理成本的情况下显著提升了匹配鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的2D-3D检测无关匹配方法主要在同一模型内优化匹配，在噪声输入、低重叠率和模糊结构等挑战性条件下难以保持可靠的对应关系。作者希望引入一种新的表征学习标准，使学习到的表征在退化条件下仍具备结构一致性和鲁棒性。

Method: 提出TeaMatch框架：1) 定义"可教性"为表征在退化输入下能被弱学习者有效恢复的能力，反映结构一致性和鲁棒性；2) 构建一组任务特定的弱学生模型模拟常见失败模式，在训练集上模仿教师模型；3) 在独立的元分割集上评估学生的可恢复性；4) 通过对应级和几何感知约束优化教师模型，提升学生恢复可靠对应的能力；5) 可无缝集成到现有coarse-to-fine匹配管道中。

Result: 大量实验表明TeaMatch提升了匹配鲁棒性，在具有挑战性的2D-3D匹配基准上达到了最先进的性能，且不增加推理成本。

Conclusion: TeaMatch通过将"可教性"作为跨模态表征学习的训练准则，有效提升了2D-3D匹配在困难条件下的鲁棒性，验证了利用弱学习者反馈来指导教师模型优化的有效性。

Abstract: Learning reliable correspondences between images and point clouds is fundamental for 2D-3D matching. Despite recent progress in detection-free methods, existing approaches primarily optimize matching within a single model and often struggle to maintain reliable correspondences under challenging conditions such as noisy inputs, low overlap, and ambiguous structures. In this work, we propose TeaMatch, a novel framework that introduces teachability as a criterion for cross-modal representation learning. We define teachability as the ability of a representation to be effectively recovered by weak learners under degraded inputs, reflecting its structural consistency and robustness. To this end, we construct a set of task-specific weak students that simulate common failure modes and train them to imitate the teacher on a training split while evaluating their recoverability on a disjoint meta split. The teacher is then optimized to improve the students' ability to recover reliable correspondences, guided by correspondence-level and geometry-aware constraints. Our framework can be seamlessly integrated into existing coarse-to-fine matching pipelines without additional inference cost. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TeaMatch improves matching robustness and achieves state-of-the-art performance on challenging 2D-3D matching benchmarks.

</details>


### [235] [Illusion or Integrity? Geometrical Consistency Metric for AIGC Video Quality Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09594)
*Yifei Xue,Yuanchen Fei,Hao Zhang,Chenzhi Nie,Tie ji,Yizhen Lao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了GeoCon-Bench，一个基于几何一致性（物理规律遵从度）来评估AI生成视频质量的基准。它通过翻译估计、单应性或基础矩阵拟合以及内点比例和几何误差等指标，定量衡量生成视频跨帧的几何一致性，并发布了包含20个场景、6种运动类别的数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成视频质量评估方法主要关注视觉和谐性、视频-文本一致性和领域特定对齐，但缺乏对物理定律遵从度的定量度量指标，无法可靠评估生成内容是否符合真实世界物理规则。

Method: GeoCon-Bench通过四个步骤评估视频质量：1) 通过翻译估计捕获全局运动；2) 使用背景对应点拟合单应性或基础矩阵模型；3) 计算内点比例和几何误差作为互补指标；4) 基于这些指标定量衡量跨帧几何一致性，作为物理规律遵从度的代理。

Result: 在最新的AIGC模型上进行的实验表明，GeoCon-Bench作为视频质量评估指标具有可靠性。

Conclusion: GeoCon-Bench为AIGC视频质量评估提供了一个全新的物理一致性维度，补充了现有方法在物理规律定量度量方面的空白，能够有效评估生成视频对真实世界物理规则的遵从程度。

Abstract: Recently, AI-driven video generation has attracted considerable attention. This surge increases the demand for reliable video quality assessment (VQA) metrics to evaluate AI-generated content (AIGC) videos and guide model optimization. Existing studies assess video quality through visual harmony, video-text consistency, and domain-specific alignment, yet lack quantitative metrics for measuring fidelity to physical laws. To address this limitation, we present a novel benchmark that evaluates the quality of AIGC videos based on their compliance with physical principles by quantitatively measuring geometric consistency across frames extracted from generated sequences. This serves as a proxy for estimating the extent to which generated videos conform to real-world physical rules. Specifically, GeoCon-Bench captures global motion through translation estimation, fits homography or fundamental matrix models using background correspondences, and reports complementary metrics, including inlier ratio and geometric error. We also release a dataset containing 20 scenes across six motion categories. Experiments on state-of-the-art AIGC models demonstrate the reliability of GeoCon-Bench as a video quality assessment metric.

</details>


### [236] [ResemBrick: Brick Reconstruction from Photographs with Perceptual Fidelity and Buildability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09597)
*Xilun Chen,Hanwen Wan,Yusong Zhao,Zexin Lin,Ruixiang Liao,Xiaoqiang Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ResemBrick提出一种从少量照片生成可手工搭建的彩色砖块模型的管线，将体素化与组装视为耦合的优化问题：通过预算受限的占用补全网络在单个前馈过程中决定填充哪些表面体素（一套权重支持13种分辨率），再结合前瞻感知的贪心放置与确定性修复保证可搭建性，在匹配预算下达到最优感知保真度且零漂浮、零不稳定砖块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有砖块管线只处理视觉相似性或结构稳定性中的单一方面，且把体素化当作固定的预处理步骤而非可优化变量；在粗晶格下视觉相似性与结构稳定性相互冲突，缺乏同时优化两者的方法。

Method: 1) 预算受限占用补全：将离散化重构为分配问题，给定目标占用体素数，单个分辨率条件网络一次前馈决定填充哪些表面体素，一套权重覆盖13种分辨率；2) 可搭建性保证：结合支持感知与前瞻感知的贪心放置算法，配合确定性、可证明终止的修复机制来接地所有漂浮组件。

Result: 在匹配预算下，ResemBrick在感知保真度上超越现有体素选择器，并唯一地在未过滤的留出对象上实现零漂浮和零不稳定砖块；作为完整管线，其感知保真度优于所有先前的砖块构建系统。

Conclusion: 结果表明应将离散化和组装视为紧密耦合的两个阶段而非相互独立的步骤，这为在硬装配约束下的3D内容生成提供了新思路。

Abstract: Producing a hand-buildable, colored brick model of a 3D object from a few casual photographs is a clean testbed for a broader challenge: generating 3D content that meets hard physical-assembly constraints under a discrete, budget-limited voxel grid. On a coarse lattice, visual resemblance and structural stability pull against each other, yet prior brick pipelines address only one side and treat voxelization as fixed preprocessing rather than a variable to optimize. We present ResemBrick, which couples the two. Budgeted occupancy completion reframes discretization as allocation: given a target occupied-voxel count, a single resolution-conditioned network decides in one feed-forward pass which surface voxels to fill for best appearance, one weight set spanning 13 resolutions. Buildability by construction then combines support- and look-ahead-aware greedy placement with a deterministic, provably terminating repair that grounds every floating component. Under a matched budget, ResemBrick surpasses existing voxel selectors in perceptual fidelity while uniquely reaching zero floating and zero unstable bricks on unfiltered held-out objects; as a complete pipeline, it attains the best perceptual fidelity among prior brick-construction systems. Our results point to treating discretization and assembly as tightly coupled stages rather than independent ones.

</details>


### [237] [Structure-Enhanced Features and Quality-Aware Dynamic Anchor Scoring for Robust Lane Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09610)
*Weize Cai,Yongqi Dong,Zhida Shao,Yichen Liu,Zixin Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个结构增强与质量感知的车道检测框架，在保持ADNet推理流程不变的前提下，通过GHVT模块增强骨干网络特征连续性，并通过LQAS模块校准锚点分类置信度与线级定位质量的相关性，在VIL-100数据集上F1@50从89.97提升至91.28。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锚点式车道检测器受两个耦合问题制约：1) 骨干网络特征在车道部分遮挡时缺乏结构连续性；2) 分类置信度与线级定位质量脱钩，导致不准确的锚点在NMS前得以保留。

Method: 1) GHVT（Gated Horizontal-Vertical Token）模块：通过轻量级方向性token交互和可学习残差门控增强中高层骨干特征；2) LQAS（Line-Quality-Aware Dynamic Anchor Scoring）模块：利用质量监督、难负样本抑制和成对排序来校准分类logits，不增加推理分支。

Result: 在VIL-100数据集上，ADNet-R34的F1@50从89.97提升至91.28，同时减少假阳性和假阴性。在CULane和TuSimple上的额外实验、广泛消融、分数分布诊断和运行时分析均确认了互补的结构与排序改进，且计算开销极小。

Conclusion: 通过结构特征增强与质量感知锚点评分的结合，可以在不改变推理流水线和最小计算开销的前提下，显著提升锚点式车道检测的性能。

Abstract: Lane detection requires recovering thin, elongated, and frequently occluded lane structures under challenging driving conditions. While anchor-based detectors provide efficient candidate generation, their performance is limited by two coupled issues: backbone features often lose structural continuity along partially visible lanes, and classification confidence may decouple from line-level localization quality, allowing inaccurate anchors to persist before non-maximum suppression (NMS). We propose a structure-enhanced and quality-aware framework that improves lane representation and dynamic-anchor scoring while preserving the inference pipeline of the Anchor Decomposition Network (ADNet). Specifically, a Gated Horizontal-Vertical Token (GHVT) module enhances mid- and high-level backbone features via lightweight directional token interactions with a learnable residual gate. In parallel, Line-Quality-Aware Dynamic Anchor Scoring (LQAS) calibrates existing classification logits using quality supervision, hard-negative suppression, and pairwise ranking without adding inference branches. On the VIL-100 dataset, our method improves ADNet-R34 from 89.97 to 91.28 in F1 score at the 0.5 intersection-over-union threshold (F1@50), reducing both false positives and false negatives. Additional experiments on CULane and TuSimple datasets, extensive ablations, score-distribution diagnostics, and runtime analysis confirm complementary structural and ranking improvements with minimal computational overhead.

</details>


### [238] [Marrying Optimal Transport and ODEs for Unified Continuous-Time 4D Reconstruction and Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09613)
*Liying Yang,Hao Mo,Jialun Liu,Chen Liu,Xinxing Yu,Chenhao Guan,Hui Ma,Xiao Cao,Ajian Liu,Yanyan Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Uni4R框架，通过结合最优传输（OT）和常微分方程（ODE）学习连续速度场，统一了4D重建与点追踪任务。该连续速度场作为运动学先验，同时惠及两个子任务。提出的流匹配引导解码器（FMGD）利用FM理论在锚点特征流形上构建概率路径，实现任意时间戳的速度预测，并采用积分一致性训练策略解决分数帧缺乏真值速度的监督问题。实验在4D重建、点追踪和新的运动学感知基准上均取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的统一4D重建与点追踪方法通常依赖启发式插值或仅在整数时间戳上预测，缺乏运动学一致性，无法在任意时间戳建模动态过程，限制了连续时间下的动力学建模能力。

Method: 1) 提出流匹配引导解码器（FMGD），包含全局速度分支提取锚点特征捕捉全局动态状态；2) 利用流匹配（Flow Matching）理论在锚点特征流形上构建由OT定义的概率路径，实例化为FM引导的速度特征；3) 局部速度预测模块融合几何特征、速度特征和时间嵌入，解码任意时间戳的速度；4) 提出积分一致性训练策略，用ODE求解器积分速度恢复目标点图，实现端到端监督。

Result: Uni4R在4D重建和点追踪任务上均达到SOTA性能，并在新构建的连续时间运动学感知基准上取得最优结果。

Conclusion: 通过将OT与ODE结合学习连续速度场，并以该速度场作为运动学先验，Uni4R成功实现了4D重建与点追踪的统一，证明利用连续动力学建模可以显著提升两个任务的性能，并在任意时间戳上保持运动学一致性。

Abstract: Existing unified 4D reconstruction and point tracking approaches typically rely on heuristic interpolations or just predict at integer timestamps, lacking kinematic coherence and failing to model dynamics at any arbitrary timestamp. In this paper, we propose Uni4R, a framework that unifies these tasks by learning continuous velocity fields through the synergy of Optimal Transport (OT) and Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE). Importantly, this continuous velocity field acts as a kinematic prior that mutually benefits both 4D reconstruction and point tracking. Specifically, we propose the Flow Matching Guided Decoder (FMGD). A global velocity branch first extracts anchor features that capture the global dynamic state of the sequence. Then, FMGD leverages Flow Matching (FM) theory to formulate a probability path defined by OT on the anchor feature manifold, instantiating it as FM-guided velocity features for velocity prediction. This establishes a robust kinematic inductive bias. Meanwhile, a point reconstruction branch provides geometric features. The local velocity prediction module then joint above features and time embeddings, to decode velocities at arbitrary timestamps. To overcome the absence of high-quality ground-truth velocities in fractional frames, we propose an integral-consistency training strategy. This strategy uses an ODE solver to integrate velocities to recover target pointmaps, enabling the model to be supervised end-to-end directly from integer timestamps. Experimental results demonstrate that Uni4R achieves SOTA performance in both 4D reconstruction and point tracking, and achieves SOTA in our new kinematics-aware benchmark at continuous time.

</details>


### [239] [LoRA-based Adaptation Alone Is Not Enough: Understanding the Limits of Foundation Models for Face Presentation Attack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09633)
*Peter Lorenz,Anjith George,Marcel Sébastien*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究系统评估了32种基础模型（FMs）在人脸呈现攻击检测（PAD）中的表现，发现零样本提示在跨模型族和规模上均接近随机水平；而低秩适配（LoRA）在仅调整不到1%参数的情况下，多数模型的域内ACER低于2%，但跨数据集ACER显著更高，表明LoRA主要优化域内决策边界，对跨域泛化贡献有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PAD方法在跨数据集评估时性能严重退化（从近乎完美降至接近随机）。基础模型因预训练数据规模远大于PAD数据集而成为有前景的替代方案，但现有研究过度聚焦于CLIP模型，缺乏对其他架构和训练方式的基础模型的系统评估。

Method: 系统评估32种不同架构和训练程序的基础模型，比较两种适配策略：零样本提示（zero-shot prompting）和低秩适配（LoRA视觉编码器），并在MCIO基准（MSU-MFSD、CASIA-FASD、Replay-Attack、OULU-NPU）上进行域内和跨数据集测试。

Result: 1) 零样本提示在所有模型族和规模上性能接近随机水平；2) LoRA适配（<1%可训练参数）在大多数情况下实现域内ACER低于2%；3) 跨数据集ACER显著高于域内，表明LoRA主要优化域内决策边界；4) 预训练表示和适配数据集对跨域泛化的影响大于轻量适配策略本身。

Conclusion: 轻量适配（LoRA）不足以解决PAD的跨数据集泛化挑战，跨域性能差距的根源在于预训练表示和适配数据集的特性，而非适配策略本身，未来研究应关注如何更好地利用预训练表示或改进适配数据集。

Abstract: Face presentation attack detection (PAD) aims to reliably detect a wide range of presentation attacks. While PAD methods achieve strong performance within individual datasets, their performance degrades under cross-dataset evaluation. Variations in sensors or lighting conditions can reduce the effectiveness of detectors from near-perfect to nearly random. Foundation models (FMs) have emerged as a promising alternative because typical PAD datasets, such as the MCIO benchmarks (MSU-MFSD, CASIA-FASD, Replay-Attack, and OULU-NPU), are small relative to the scale used for web-based pretraining. However, existing PAD systems primarily focus on CLIP-based foundation models, while overlooking other FMs with different architectures and training procedures. This study addresses this question by systematically evaluating 32 FMs. Zero-shot prompting achieves performance near chance across model families and scales. The vision encoders, when low-rankadapted (LoRA) with fewer than 1% trainable weights, achieve below 2% intra-dataset ACER in most cases, while cross-dataset ACER is substantially higher. LoRA primarily refines the decision boundary within a dataset, suggesting that pretrained representations and the adaptation dataset play a larger role in cross-dataset generalization than the evaluated lightweight adaptation strategy.

</details>


### [240] [NeuroRefiner: Morphology-Aware Multi-Agent Refinement for 3D Fluorescence Microscopy Neuron Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09636)
*Haiyang Yan,Jinyue Guo,Yanchao Zhang,Bingqing Wang,Zhenchen Li,Jing Liu,Jiazheng Liu,Linlin Li,Hua Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出NeuroRefiner，一个多智能体系统，用于荧光显微镜中3D神经元分割。它通过三个协作智能体（诊断拓扑错误、生成修正指令、验证精炼质量）模拟人类专家工作流，结合TopoRefineNet（基于3D U-Net的跨模态特征融合工具）进行多轮推理和体素级编辑，以在保留局部细节的同时保持全局拓扑，在三个数据集上超越SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经元的稀疏和细长形态使得现有分割方法难以同时保留局部细节和全局拓扑，导致分割结果碎片化，3D神经元分割的准确性受到挑战。

Method: 提出NeuroRefiner多智能体系统，包含三个协作智能体（诊断拓扑错误、生成修正指令、验证精炼质量），将人类专家迭代式全局观察与局部编辑的工作流形式化。配套提出TopoRefineNet，一种基于3D U-Net的工具，利用跨模态特征融合生成精炼掩膜，通过多轮智能体推理和体素级编辑实现分割精炼。

Result: 在BigNeuron、CWMBS和ZBFWB三个数据集上，NeuroRefiner优于现有最先进方法，尤其在具有挑战性的ZBFWB数据集上F1分数提升了3.02%。

Conclusion: NeuroRefiner通过多智能体协作和指令引导的分割精炼，能够产生拓扑更准确且可解释性更强的神经元分割结果，为3D神经科学图像分析提供了新的有效方案。

Abstract: Accurate 3D neuron segmentation in fluorescence microscopy is critical for neuroscience. However, the sparse and elongated morphology of neurons poses significant challenges to existing segmentation methods. These methods struggle to preserve both local details and global topology, leading to fragmented results. To address this, we propose NeuroRefiner, a multi-agent system that formalizes the human expert workflow involving iterative global observation and local editing. Specifically, NeuroRefiner comprises three collaborative agents dedicated to diagnosing topological errors, generating correction instructions, and validating refinement quality. To facilitate agent instruction-guided segmentation refinement, we propose TopoRefineNet, a dedicated 3D U-Net-based tool that leverages cross-modality feature fusion to generate refined masks. Through multi-round agent reasoning and voxel-level editing, NeuroRefiner produces topologically more accurate segmentations with enhanced interpretability. Experiments on the BigNeuron, CWMBS, and ZBFWB datasets demonstrate that NeuroRefiner outperforms state-of-the-art methods, notably achieving a 3.02% improvement in F1 score on the challenging ZBFWB dataset.

</details>


### [241] [DUET: A Diversity-Quality Duet of Distillation Experts for Two-Step Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09637)
*Zian Li,Litong Gong,Borui Liao,Pengfei Liu,Xinyu Wang,Xinyuan Wei,Yifan Gao,Tiezheng Ge,Muhan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DUET/DUET+提出一种噪声层级专家分工范式，将两步视频生成中的高噪声步交由sCM专家（保多样性）处理、低噪声步交由DMD专家（保质量）处理，通过独立训练与强化学习引导的专家适配，同时在生成质量与结构多样性上取得优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型视频生成质量高但采样成本昂贵，少步蒸馏虽降低成本，却在质量与多样性之间陷入两难：轨迹级蒸馏（sCM）重多样性，分布级蒸馏（DMD）重质量。如何在极端的两步视频生成中同时兼顾两者是核心挑战。

Method: 提出噪声层级专家分工（duet of experts）：将两步生成按噪声高低拆分，高噪声步由sCM专家负责（确保结构多样性），低噪声步由DMD专家负责（细化外观质量）。两专家独立训练，避免损失级组合的优化困难；进一步识别中继接口与高噪声阶段为瓶颈，采用RL引导的专家适配得到DUET+。

Result: 基于Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B骨干，DUET将两步sCM质量提升至接近DMD水平，同时保留约两倍于DMD的结构多样性；DUET+在保持多样性优势的基础上进一步提升了整体质量。

Conclusion: 噪声层级的专家特化成为两步视频生成中调和多样性与质量的一种简单有效的新范式，解决了质量-多样性权衡问题。

Abstract: Diffusion models have enabled high-quality video generation in recent years, but the high cost of iterative sampling hinders their practical deployment. Few-step distillation alleviates this cost, yet exposes a quality--diversity trade-off between its two dominant paradigms: trajectory-level distillation (e.g., sCM) favors diversity, whereas distribution-level distillation (e.g., DMD) favors quality. Targeting extreme two-step video generation, we introduce DUET, which reconciles the two paradigms through a noise-level duet of experts: an sCM expert takes the high-noise step to lay out diverse structure, and a DMD expert takes the low-noise step to refine appearance detail. Since the two experts are trained independently with their native objectives, DUET sidesteps the optimization difficulties of loss-level combinations and delivers quality and diversity jointly rather than trading one for the other. We further identify the relay interface and the high-noise stage as the remaining bottlenecks, and address them with RL-guided expert adaptation, yielding DUET+. With the Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B backbone, DUET lifts the two-step quality of sCM close to the level of DMD while retaining nearly all of its structural diversity---about twice that of DMD---and DUET+ further improves overall quality while preserving this diversity advantage. Together, these results establish noise-level expert specialization as a simple, effective paradigm for reconciling diversity and quality in two-step video generation.

</details>


### [242] [EgoHieraLoc: A Cortically Inspired Hierarchical Segmentation-Guided Framework for Egocentric Visual Query Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09656)
*Yifei Cao,Guolong Wang,Mingliang Hou,Xiya Bu,Daming Liu,Yu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EgoHieraLoc，一个统一框架同时解决VQL-2D和VQL-3D任务，通过模仿人类分层视觉处理机制（前景筛选、选择性注意、全局-局部反馈、跨视角可信度整合）实现鲁棒的视觉查询定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉查询定位在物体边界模糊时具有挑战性，全局上下文无法有效引导细粒度定位，现有方法缺乏类似人类视觉的分层处理机制来处理歧义和干扰。

Method: 包含三个模块：(1)判别性解析模块利用分割先验提取前景感知的查询表示；(2)查询感知模块通过判别性相关滤波和可变形建模进行鲁棒目标定位；(3)区域自适应模块将多尺度上下文反馈到局部区域以恢复精确物体边界。针对3D定位提出几何-语义联合置信度(GSJC)，将分割置信度与局部深度一致性、多视角反投影一致性和三角化基线质量相乘耦合。

Result: 在VQL-2D和VQL-3D基准测试上均达到最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 通过模仿人类分层视觉处理机制，EgoHieraLoc有效解决了VQL中物体边界模糊和全局上下文引导不足的问题，并在2D和3D定位任务中均取得最优结果。

Abstract: Visual query localization (VQL) aims to retrieve and re-localize a queried object in egocentric videos, yet remains challenging when object boundaries are ambiguous and global context cannot effectively guide fine-grained localization. Human vision handles such ambiguity through a hierarchical process: it rapidly screens foreground candidates, selectively attends to the target despite distractors, refines perception via feedback between global context and local detail, and, when a single view is unreliable, integrates evidence across viewpoints according to its credibility. Inspired by these competencies, we propose \textbf{EgoHieraLoc}, a unified framework for VQL-2D and VQL-3D.
