A Discriminative Parsing Module first extracts foreground-aware query representations using segmentation priors; a Query-Aware Module then performs robust target localization through discriminative correlation filtering with deformable modeling; and a Regional Adaptation Module feeds multi-scale context back into local regions to recover precise object boundaries. To extend this perceptual hierarchy to 3D localization, we introduce Geometric-Semantic Joint Confidence (GSJC), which multiplicatively couples segmentation confidence with local depth consistency, multi-view back-projection consistency, and triangulation-baseline quality, so that a viewpoint contributes to the 3D estimate only when it is credible both semantically and geometrically. Extensive experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art performance on both VQL-2D and -3D benchmarks.

</details>


### [243] [CIFA: Contextual-Intersectional Fairness Auditing for Hidden Subgroup Discovery in Face Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09669)
*Nazia Aslam,Khalid Adnan Alsayed,Thomas B. Moeslund,Kamal Nasrollahi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出上下文-交叉公平性审计框架（CIFA），用于识别计算机视觉模型中由人口统计属性与上下文属性（如光照、模糊、图像质量等）交互作用产生的隐藏子群体性能退化问题。实验表明，传统聚合准确率和仅按人口统计分组的评估会掩盖显著的上下文-交叉差异，且现有缓解策略无法一致消除这些差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算机视觉公平性评估仅关注聚合准确率和人口统计子群体分析，而忽视了视觉模型对光照、模糊、图像质量、面部配饰等上下文因素的敏感性。这些上下文因素与人口统计特征交互可能产生隐藏子群体，导致性能严重退化，即便整体准确率和按人口统计维度的公平性表现良好也无法发现。

Method: 提出CIFA框架，包含三个层次的审计：人口统计审计、上下文审计和上下文-交叉审计，随后通过最差群体发现（worst-group discovery）来识别和排序最脆弱的属性组合。在FairFace、CelebA和UTKFace数据集上，使用ResNet-50和ViT-B/16模型进行性别分类评估，并通过"审计-缓解-再审计"协议测试多种缓解策略。

Result: 实验结果证实聚合准确率和仅按人口统计维度的评估会掩盖显著的上下文-交叉差异。现有缓解策略虽然能减少部分最差群体差异，但没有任何单一策略能在所有数据集和架构上一致地消除这些差异。

Conclusion: 上下文-交叉审计应当成为公平性评估的重要组成部分。CIFA框架为面部分析系统提供了可复现的框架，用于发现、优先排序和重新评估隐藏子群体风险。

Abstract: Fairness evaluation in computer vision commonly relies on aggregate accuracy and demographic subgroup analysis. However, visual models are also sensitive to contextual factors such as illumination, blur, image quality, facial accessories, and appearance attributes. These factors may interact with demographic characteristics, producing hidden subgroups in which performance degrades substantially despite strong aggregate accuracy and apparently acceptable demographic fairness. To address this, we propose the Contextual-Intersectional Fairness Auditing Framework (CIFA), a structured framework for identifying subgroup vulnerabilities arising from interactions between demographic and contextual attributes. CIFA performs demographic, contextual, and contextual-intersectional auditing, followed by worst-group discovery to identify and rank the most vulnerable attribute combinations. We evaluate CIFA on gender classification using ResNet-50 \cite{he2016deep} and ViT-B/16 \cite{dosovitskiy2020image} across FairFace \cite{Karkkainen2021}, CelebA \cite{Liu2015}, and UTKFace \cite{Zhang2017}. Our results show that aggregate accuracy and demographic-only evaluation can mask substantial contextual-intersectional disparities. We further assess several established mitigation strategies through an audit--mitigate--reaudit protocol and find that, although some worst-group disparities are reduced, no single strategy consistently eliminates them across datasets and architectures. These findings establish contextual-intersectional auditing as an important component of fairness evaluation and provide a reproducible framework for discovering, prioritizing, and reassessing hidden subgroup risks in face analysis systems.

</details>


### [244] [MPISuperRes-PnP: A Super-Resolution Zero-Shot Plug-and-Play Reconstruction Algorithm for Magnetic Particle Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09672)
*Vladyslav Gapyak,Thomas März,Andreas Weinmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于能量最小化的磁粒子成像（MPI）超分辨率重建方法。该方法采用plug-and-play策略，将SR问题分解为重建和去噪两个子问题，并使用预训练的高斯去噪器以零样本方式处理去噪任务，从而无需训练数据即可利用深度学习优势。在合成数据和真实数据上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPI是一种新兴的医学成像模态，但即使使用最先进的基于测量的重建方法，其空间网格仍然非常粗糙，因此超分辨率（SR）技术至关重要。现有SR方法要么对系统矩阵进行上采样，要么对重建结果进行插值，尚未有将SR整合到重建任务中的统一框架。

Method: 通过能量最小化公式将SR融入MPI重建任务。采用plug-and-play能量最小化方法推导分裂方案，将一个高斯去噪子问题用预训练的深度学习高斯去噪器以零样本（zero-shot）方式处理，从而无需训练数据即可利用深度学习优势。通过扩展参数搜索选择超参数。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据（MPIData和2D-OpenMPI数据）上验证了方法的适用性。该方法无需训练数据，去噪器行为保守，未观察到幻觉伪影。定量和定性评估均支持方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的SR方法具有通用性，可应用于未来涉及不同正则化器或不同成像任务的MPI场景。通过零样本深度学习去噪器，避免了当前MPI训练数据稀缺的问题。

Abstract: Magnetic Particle Imaging (MPI) is an emerging medical imaging modality. MPI is based on the non-linear response of magnetic nanoparticles to an applied magnetic field and avoids ionizing radiation. The measured signal is the voltage induced in receive coils by the particles' response. Reconstructing the particle concentration from the signal constitutes the imaging task. Even using state-of-the-art measurement-based reconstruction, the associated spatial grid is very coarse, hence super-resolution (SR) techniques are important. In this work, we propose an approach for SR in MPI inspired by energy minimization. Different methods have been proposed for SR in MPI, ranging from upscaling of the associated system matrix to interpolation of the reconstruction. Here we incorporate SR into the reconstruction task via an energy minimization formulation. Following the plug-and-play approach to energy minimization we derive a splitting scheme and a SR method for MPI where the arising Gaussian denoising task is treated with a pre-trained learned Gaussian denoiser in a zero-shot fashion. This way, we incorporate benefits of deep learning without training and avoid the need of training data. Further, we provide a quantitative and qualitative evaluation of the proposed method. Hyper-parameter are selected via an extended parameter search. The found parameters are applied for reconstruction on real data. We show the applicability of our method on synthetic and on real data (MPIData: EquilibriumModelWithAnisotropy and 2D-OpenMPI Data). The proposed method employs a deep-learning denoiser without training -- thus it does not require presently scarcely available MPI training data. The denoiser behaves conservatively, i.e., no hallucination artifacts were observed. The SR approach is generic such that it can be applied in future MPI contexts involving different regularizers or different imaging tasks.

</details>


### [245] [Thinking With Tools, Not With Pixels: Tool Calls as Text Scaffolds for Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09682)
*Jiahao Shao,Yuanbo Yang,Yiyi Liao,Yujun Shen,Ceyuan Yang,Yinghao Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Tool-Call Scaffold假说：在"用图像思考"的视觉语言模型中，真正起作用的信号是工具调用时产生的结构化文本（工具名、坐标、目标描述、意图），而非返回的图像像素。作者引入TextCall方法（保留文本脚手架但用占位符替换返回图像）进行验证，三组实验支持该假说，并可将延迟降低29-46%且消除工具执行API调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究通过盲测、增益分解和注意力分析发现，工具增强视觉语言模型中返回的图像像素对性能贡献甚微，这引发了疑问：如果像素不承载增益，那么什么才是真正有效的信号？作者假设是工具调用时产生的结构化文本脚手架在起关键作用。

Method: 提出TextCall（call-but-no-return）方法：保留工具调用前的文本脚手架（工具名、坐标、目标描述、意图），但将返回的图像替换为文本占位符[Image output skipped]。通过三项研究验证：(i)非必要性——在LoRA、全微调和强化学习下对比TextCall与完整"图像思考"；(ii)充分性——匹配训练查询下脚手架单独输入与返回图像输入的准确率对比；(iii)组件特异性——将脚手架分解为推理文本和空间代码，分析各自贡献。

Result: (i) TextCall在LoRA、全微调、RL下均匹配或超过完整"图像思考"；在RL下能保留工具使用，避免看到返回图像导致模型停止调用工具直接回答的失败模式。(ii) 脚手架单独输入在匹配训练查询上达到与返回图像输入相当的准确率。(iii) 推理文本和空间代码均有贡献，主导组件因任务而异。TextCall在保持准确率的同时减少29-46%延迟并消除工具执行API调用。

Conclusion: 支持Tool-Call Scaffold假说：当前"图像思考"分布中，活跃信号是工具调用时产生的结构化文本，返回的图像只是冗余载体。该发现对当前基准成立，但构建像素真正承载信息的任务仍是开放性方向。

Abstract: Tool-augmented vision-language models increasingly "think with images": they call crop, zoom, or code tools and reason over the returned pixels. However, recent work using blind tests, gain decompositions, and attention analyses has shown that returned images contribute little, raising the question: if pixels do not carry the gain, what does? We hypothesize that the load-bearing signal is the structured text emitted before any returned pixel arrives: tool name, coordinates, target description, and intent. This textual scaffold encodes where to look and what to find. We introduce TextCall (call-but-no-return) to test this: it keeps the scaffold but replaces returned images with the text placeholder [Image output skipped]. Three studies support the hypothesis. (i) Non-necessity of returned pixels: across LoRA, full fine-tuning, and RL, TextCall matches or exceeds full thinking-with-images; under RL it preserves tool use at the reported checkpoint, avoiding the failure mode where, under matched settings, seeing the returned image causes the model to stop calling tools and answer directly. (ii) Sufficiency of the scaffold: on matched training queries, scaffold-only input yields equivalent accuracy to returned-image input. (iii) Component specificity: decomposing the scaffold into reasoning text and spatial code shows both components contribute, with the dominant one varying by task. Together these results support the Tool-Call Scaffold Hypothesis: in current thinking-with-images distributions, the active signal is the structured text emitted at tool-call time; the returned image is a redundant carrier. TextCall preserves accuracy while reducing latency by 29-46% and eliminating tool-execution API calls. Our claims hold for current thinking-with-images benchmarks; constructing tasks where pixels are genuinely load-bearing remains an open direction.

</details>


### [246] [Diffuse the object, keep its label: curating detector training data from a few unlabeled photographs via VLM-built 3D vegetation scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09691)
*Mario Malizia,Marnix Enting,Rob Haelterman,Ken Hasselmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无监督站点适应方法：利用vision-language模型从少量部署站点照片生成粗粒度3D植被场景，放置3D物体网格合成带标注的训练图像，并通过diffusion重纹理化来提高合成图像的真实度。实验表明该方法在标准检测器上优于跨站点标签复用，且mask-lock的扩散程度是最关键的调节因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 植被中隐藏的小物体（如地雷）标注图像稀缺，且跨站点训练检测器泛化能力差。传统做法是复用其他站点的标签，但效果不理想。需要一种无需人工标注就能生成站点特定训练数据的方法。

Method: 1) 从少量未标注现场照片出发，用vision-language模型生成粗粒度3D植被场景；2) 在场景中放置3D物体网格，直接从场景几何中获取边界框、分割掩码和逐实例遮挡，无需人工标注；3) 用轻量adapter在照片上微调，条件化diffusion pass对渲染结果进行重纹理化；4) 引入分级mask-lock控制diffusion对物体本体的影响程度。

Result: 在一个人道主义排雷基准上，用合成图像训练的检测器在多个随机种子下一致地匹配或超越了用更大规模真实跨站点数据集训练的检测器。消融实验显示：轻微扩散物体区域可提升少数类召回率，完全保护物体像素效果次之，无限制扩散会使物体消失；增益对照片和裁剪预算不敏感；域内精度无法预测跨站点性能。

Conclusion: 证明通过合成站点特定训练数据可以无需人工标注地实现有效的无监督站点适应。扩散程度（mask-lock分级）是最具影响力的数据生成选择，为未来合成训练数据生成策略提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Labeled images of small objects hidden in vegetation are scarce, and detectors trained on them generalize poorly across sites. Rather than reusing labels collected at another site, we synthesize labeled training images from a handful of unlabeled photographs of the deployment site itself. A vision--language model generates a coarse 3D vegetation scene from one photograph; placing 3D object meshes in the scene yields bounding boxes, segmentation masks, and per-instance occlusion directly from the scene geometry, without manual annotation. A lightweight adapter fine-tuned on the photographs conditions a diffusion pass that re-textures the renders, and a graded mask-lock sets how much diffusion may touch the object itself. In our runs this grade was the most influential curation choice: lightly diffusing the object improves minority-class recall over fully protecting its pixels, while unrestricted diffusion dissolves it. Trained on these images, a standard detector matched or exceeded its counterpart trained on a larger labeled dataset of real images from a different site, consistently across seeds on a humanitarian-demining benchmark; the comparison is thus unsupervised site adaptation from a handful of photographs against conventional cross-site label reuse. In our ablations the gains were largely insensitive to the photograph and crop budgets, and in-domain accuracy did not predict cross-site performance.

</details>


### [247] [LookAgain: Closed-Loop GUI Grounding with Visually Grounded Reflection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09723)
*Renshan Zhang,Haoyang Meng,Yixiao He,Rui Shao,April Hua Liu,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LookAgain，一个闭环GUI grounding框架，通过"定位-再看-修正"的多轮预测后视觉反思机制，将坐标预测视为可被检验和修正的假设，显著提升了小目标、密集控件和分布外界面上的grounding性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI grounding方法在标准基准上表现优异，但在小目标、密集控件和分布外界面上性能急剧下降。作者认为根本原因在于现有方法存在范式性局限：没有将生成的坐标视为需要反思和修正的假设，导致缺乏事后反思、视觉证据与预测脱节、以及只在视图层面而非预测层面进行细化这三个耦合问题。

Method: LookAgain将GUI grounding重新定义为多轮"预测-再看-修正"的闭环过程，包含两个核心原语："locate"用于提出坐标假设、在图像上渲染标记并附加预测区域的局部补丁，将前一轮预测作为空间先验锚定下一轮推理；"confirm"用于接受或拒绝该假设并终止流程。训练采用两阶段策略：先用SFT在构造的反思轨迹上进行冷启动，再用GRPO以最终grounding正确性作为唯一奖励进行强化学习。

Result: 实验表明LookAgain在拒绝感知和通用GUI grounding基准上均一致地提升了性能，达到最先进水平。全面的消融实验进一步验证了所提出框架各组成部分的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将坐标预测视为可迭代修正的假设而非最终结论，LookAgain证明了事后视觉反思机制能有效弥补现有GUI grounding方法在小目标、密集控件和分布外场景下的性能短板，为GUI grounding提供了新的范式方向。

Abstract: Recent graphical user interface (GUI) grounders have significantly advanced single-shot accuracy on standard benchmarks, yet their performance degrades sharply on small targets, densely packed controls and out-of-distribution interfaces. We attribute this gap to a paradigmatic limitation shared by existing approaches: none of them treats a produced coordinate as a hypothesis to be reflected upon and revised under new visual evidence. This manifests as three coupled issues: 1) Lack of post-hoc reflection. The prediction is frozen at the moment of emission, leaving no internal mechanism to challenge or refine it. 2) Visual evidence decoupled from the prediction. The auxiliary visual evidence is gathered to support the upcoming coordinate rather than to scrutinise the one already committed to. 3) Refinement over views, not over predictions. The iterative zoom-in refines the inspected region instead of inheriting a previous coordinate as a spatial prior to be corrected. In this paper, we propose LookAgain, a closed-loop GUI grounder driven by post-prediction visual reflection. LookAgain reformulates grounding as a multi-turn predict-look-again-refine process with two primitives: "locate" posts a coordinate hypothesis, renders a marker on the image and appends a local patch of the predicted region. It anchors the next reasoning step to the previous prediction as a spatial prior; "confirm" accepts or reject the hypothesis and terminates the procedure. We train the LookAgain grounder with SFT on constructed reflective trajectories as a cold start, followed by GRPO with terminal grounding correctness as the sole reward. Extensive experiments show that LookAgain consistently improves performance on both refusal-aware and general GUI grounding benchmarks, achieving state-of-the-art results. Comprehensive ablations further verify the effectiveness of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [248] [World Tokens: Enhancing Embodied Policies with Training-Time World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09730)
*Qu Tang,Benhui Zhuang,Bo Yuan,Xue Yu,Longteng Guo,Junlan Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出World Tokens，一种基于World Adapter的具身策略架构，通过训练时将世界模型用于增强动作策略，推理时移除视频世界模型分支以实现高效部署。该方法在LIBERO、SIMPLER和真实R1 Pro机器人上均取得具有竞争力的表现，且推理延迟与VLA模型相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在闭环控制方面高效但缺乏对物理场景演化的显式建模；而新兴的世界动作模型（WAMs）虽能捕捉时空演化，但推理时保留未来生成或大型视频骨干网络会大幅增加推理成本。作者希望兼顾世界动力学建模带来的性能提升与高效部署。

Method: 核心是World Adapter模块：1) 将VLM特征转换为一组固定的world tokens；2) 这些tokens同时条件化联合微调的未来视频去噪器和动作专家；3) 共享条件化使未来视频去噪的梯度直接塑造用于动作预测的表示，而排他性路由防止策略绕过该表示；4) 部署时移除世界模型分支，仅保留VLM、World Adapter和动作专家，无在线视频模型推理。

Result: 使用2B骨干且无具身动作预训练的情况下，World Tokens在LIBERO上具有高度竞争力，在SIMPLER上取得最佳平均结果，在真实R1 Pro机器人上显著超越匹配的动作-only基线，且每个动作块的生成延迟达到VLA级水平。

Conclusion: World Tokens证明了无需在推理时保留视频生成模型，仅通过训练阶段的世界建模蒸馏即可增强动作策略，实现了世界动力学建模优势与高效部署的兼顾。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models are a widely adopted paradigm for embodied policies. They excel at efficient closed-loop control but do not explicitly model how physical scenes evolve as a task unfolds. Recently emerging world-action models (WAMs) leverage pretrained video world models to capture spatiotemporal evolution, yet retaining future generation or a large video backbone in the control loop substantially increases inference cost. We introduce World Tokens, an embodied policy architecture built around a World Adapter that bridges visual-language understanding, world-dynamics modeling, and action generation. It uses world modeling during training to enhance the action policy while preserving efficient deployment. Specifically, the World Adapter transforms VLM features into a fixed set of world tokens, which condition a jointly fine-tuned future-video denoiser and simultaneously serve as the action expert's sole visual-language context. This shared conditioning allows gradients from future-video denoising to directly shape the representation used for action prediction, while exclusive routing prevents the policy from bypassing that representation. At deployment, the world-model branch is removed, leaving only the VLM, World Adapter, and action expert, with no online video-model inference. With a 2B backbone and no embodied action pretraining, World Tokens is highly competitive on LIBERO, attains the best reported averages on SIMPLER, substantially improves real-world R1 Pro success over a matched action-only baseline, and generates each action chunk at VLA-level latency.

</details>


### [249] [HandSplatter: Automated Digital Goniometry from Neural Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09735)
*Emmett Chen,Neal Chen,Xiang Li,Quanzheng Li,Siyeop Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于神经渲染的3D手部关节定位与姿态估计新流程，用于客观量化手指关节活动度（ROM），克服了传统量角器人工测量耗时、可靠性差以及现有软件精度不足的缺陷，并引入离散密度爬山算法实现3D空间投影地标的有意义校正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手部和手指疾病是肌肉骨骼残疾的主要成因，精确量化关节运动具有临床必要性。当前标准工具量角器依赖人工操作，耗时且因检查者技术差异导致观察者间信度不一致；现有软件方法又缺乏临床所需的精度，亟需一种既准确又实用的替代方案。

Method: 提出一种新颖的3D手部关节定位与姿态估计流程，其核心是神经渲染技术。具体包括：(1) 将2D特征提取与视图合成相结合（区别于先前仅依赖单一模态的方法），显著提升精度和临床可行性；(2) 引入离散密度爬山算法，在3D空间中对投影地标进行有意义的校正。

Result: 该系统的精度和临床可行性显著优于先前方法，克服了人工测量的低效和现有软件的不准确性，提供了稳健的客观功能评估工具。

Conclusion: 该系统为临床关节活动度评估提供了一种稳健、客观、精确的自动化工具，有望替代传统量角器和现有不精确的软件方案，满足临床对精确关节运动量化的需求。

Abstract: Hand and finger disorders are leading contributors to musculoskeletal disability, creating a clinical need for precise methods to quantify joint motion. Range of motion (ROM) serves as the metric for diagnosis, rehabilitation monitoring, and evaluating surgical outcomes. Currently, the goniometer is the standard tool for assessing finger flexion and extension. However, manual goniometry is labor-intensive and suffers from inconsistent inter-rater reliability due to variations in examiner technique. While digital alternatives exist, current software-based approaches often lack the necessary accuracy for clinical usage. To address these limitations, we present a novel pipeline for 3-D hand joint location and pose estimation using neural rendering. Unlike previous methods, our approach combines 2-D feature extraction with view synthesis to significantly improve accuracy and clinical viability. Furthermore, we introduce a discrete density hill climbing algorithm that facilitates the meaningful correction of projected landmarks in 3-D space. This system overcomes the inefficiencies of manual measurement and the inaccuracies of existing software, providing a robust tool for objective functional assessment.

</details>


### [250] [Disentangling Co-Occurring Retinal Pathologies with Saliency-Guided Sparse Expert Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09752)
*Nagur Shareef Shaik,Jeongwoo Park,Yeong-Jin Kim,Jaeuk Jung,Hyunjung Oh,Dong Hye Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合引导上下文门控（GCG）空间注意力和稀疏路由混合专家（MoE）模块的新型架构，用于视网膜眼底图像多病种共现诊断。该方法通过专家分配实现可解释的数据驱动分解，在五类患者分离交叉验证中达到0.912宏AUC和0.653宏F1，并通过Grad-CAM++和t-SNE验证了专家路由与病灶定位的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视网膜眼底图像常存在多种病理共现情况，但标准深度学习分类器对所有图像采用静态、相同的计算方式，未考虑不同疾病分布的差异，缺乏针对性和可解释性。

Method: 提出稀疏条件计算架构：前端采用引导上下文门控（GCG）空间注意力机制，后端采用在特征token上运行的稀疏路由混合专家（MoE）模块，实现动态、数据驱动的计算分配。

Result: 在五类、患者分离的5折交叉验证基准上，模型达到0.912±0.008的宏AUC和0.653±0.014的宏F1。专家分配显著依赖疾病类别（p<0.001），正常状态和形态独特的病理（如ERM、AMD）隔离到专属专家。Grad-CAM++和t-SNE可视化证实专家路由与局部病灶对齐，并将共现病例几何映射到其组成簇之间。

Conclusion: 稀疏MoE可作为多病种视网膜筛查的可解释方法，专家路由不仅能提升诊断性能，还能提供与病理形态学一致的、可解释的数据分解。

Abstract: Retinal fundus images frequently exhibit multiple co-occurring pathologies, yet standard deep learning classifiers apply static, identical computation to every image regardless of the underlying disease distribution. We propose a novel architecture that resolves this via sparse conditional computation, pairing a Guided Context Gating (GCG) spatial attention front-end with a sparsely-routed Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) block operating over feature tokens. Crucially, this routing yields an interpretable, data-driven decomposition. Expert allocation is significantly disease-dependent (p < 0.001), with the healthy Normal state and morphologically distinct pathologies (e.g., ERM, AMD) isolating to dedicated experts. On a five-class, patient-disjoint 5-fold cross-validation benchmark, our model achieves 0.912 +/- 0.008 macro AUC and 0.653 +/- 0.014 macro F1. Furthermore, Grad-CAM++ and post-MoE t-SNE visualizations confirm that expert routing aligns with localized lesions and geometrically maps co-occurring cases between their constituent clusters, positioning sparse MoE as an interpretable approach to multi-disease retinal screening.

</details>


### [251] [C$^2$A: Coupling Spatial Evidence with Clinical Priors via Co-occurrence Aware Class Attention for Multi-Label Chest X-Ray Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09774)
*Akash Gogineni,Nagur Shareef Shaik,Aasrith Mandava,Adnan Masood,Dong Hye Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出C²A（共现感知类别注意力）分类头，通过将池化视为每类空间注意力图上的期望，显式耦合空间证据与临床先验，并用从经验标签共现初始化的可学习图来耦合类别描述符。在CheXpert上达到0.895 macro-mean AUROC，超越先进的上下文门控基线，且在高度共现、空间证据模糊的类别上收益显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胸部病理很少孤立出现，但标准多标签分类器依赖共享的全局描述符，忽略了病灶的位置和共现方式，限制了分类性能。

Method: 1) 将池化建模为学习到的每类空间注意力图上的期望，得到每个疾病的局部化描述符；2) 通过从经验标签共现初始化的可学习图来耦合这些描述符，使用单次残差消息传递共享相关发现间的证据，其中共现通过显式双线性交互进入每个logit。

Result: 在CheXpert上达到0.895 macro-mean AUROC，优于高级上下文门控基线；收益集中在高度共现且空间证据模糊的类别上（如Atelectasis较GCG提升+1.5），仅需一个线性投影和C×C边矩阵，开销可忽略。

Conclusion: C²A证明将临床共现先验作为有界扰动身份映射纳入分类头，能有效正则化并提升多标签胸部X光分类准确率，尤其在空间证据模糊的高度共现类别上，且计算开销极低。

Abstract: Thoracic pathologies rarely occur in isolation, yet standard multi-label classifiers rely on shared global descriptors, discarding \emph{where} findings lie and \emph{how} they co-occur. We propose \textbf{C$\mathbf{^2}$A} (Co-occurrence Aware Class Attention), a classification head that explicitly couples spatial evidence with clinical priors. First, C$^2$A casts pooling as an expectation over learned per-class spatial attention maps, yielding localized descriptors for each disease. Second, it couples these descriptors via a learnable graph warm-started from empirical label co-occurrence. A single residual message-passing step shares evidence among related findings, proving to be a bounded perturbation of the identity where co-occurrence enters each logit through an explicit bilinear interaction. On CheXpert, C$^2$A achieves a superior $0.895$ macro-mean AUROC, outperforming advanced context-gating baselines. Crucially, gains concentrate on highly co-occurrent classes with ambiguous spatial evidence (rescuing Atelectasis by $+1.5$ over GCG), demonstrating the prior's regularizing effect with a negligible overhead of one linear projection and a $C\!\times\!C$ edge matrix.

</details>


### [252] [NTIRE 2026 Low-light Enhancement: Twilight Cowboy Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09782)
*Aleksei Khalin,Egor Ershov,Artyom Panshin,Sergey Korchagin,Georgiy Lobarev,Arseniy Terekhin,Sofiia Dorogova,Amir Shamsutdinov,Yasin Mamedov,Bakhtiyar Khalfin,Bogdan Sheludko,Emil Zilyaev,Nikola Banić,Georgy Perevozchikov,Radu Timofte,Shuai Liu,Yuqian Zhang,Lize Zhang,Yibin Huang,Chaoyu Feng,Luyang Wang,Xiaotao Wang,Dongqing Zou,Lei Lei,Tianli Liu,Dejun Hao,Chunxia Lei,Furkan Kınlı,Andrei Mironov,Alexander Dikov,Aleksei Sadokhin,Vladimir Zvorygin,Constantine Habarlak,Shuwei Yue,Egor Mirantsov,Daniil Okunev,Dmitry Arkhipov,Aleksandr Yugay,Anas M. Ali,Bilel Benjdira,Wadii Boulila,Wei Zhou,Linfeng Li,Lingdong Kong,Jiachen Tu,Guoyi Xu,Yaoxin Jiang,Jiajia Liu,Yaokun Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了NTIRE 2026低光增强挑战赛（Twilight Cowboy），该竞赛旨在将低光条件下拍摄的多张未对齐智能手机原始图像合并为一张干净图像，同时解决视觉退化（噪声、混合光源）和手抖导致的几何不一致问题。竞赛收集了585个真实场景数据集，采用三阶段评估协议，最终10支团队超过基线，最佳提升+6.49 dB PSNR和+0.0101 SSIM，建立了突发式低光图像增强的新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低光摄影面临两个核心挑战：一是高噪声和混合场景光照等视觉退化问题，二是多帧拍摄时手抖导致的几何不一致问题。现有方法往往将这两个问题分开处理，缺乏统一的解决方案和基准数据集来推动该领域研究。

Method: 竞赛在原始(raw)域中处理一组未对齐的智能手机低光图像，通过burst合并将它们合成为一张干净图像。数据集包含585个真实场景（室内低光和室外夜间）。评估采用三阶段协议：前两阶段通过CodaBench平台自动验证，最终排名通过私有测试集的盲评完成。

Result: 10支参赛团队超越了现有基线，实现了最高+6.49 dB的PSNR提升和+0.0101的SSIM提升，刷新了突发式低光图像增强的最新技术水平。结果表明在真实世界的噪声、运动和光照变化处理方面取得了显著进展。

Conclusion: 该挑战赛证实了在原始域进行多帧合并同时处理噪声和几何失真的可行性，为低光计算摄影设立了新的基准和SOTA，相关结果和排行榜已公开供研究社区参考。

Abstract: This paper presents a review of the NTIRE 2026 Low-light Enhancement: Twilight Cowboy Challenge. The objective of the competition was to merge a set of misaligned smartphone images in the raw domain, captured in low-light conditions, into a single, clean image. Introduced setup simultaneously addresses two problems of low-light photography: visual degradations such as high noise and mixed scene illuminants, and the geometric inconsistencies caused by hand movement during multi-frame capture. To advance research in low-light and nighttime computational photography, a challenging dataset was collected comprising 585 real-world scenes, spanning indoor low-light and outdoor nighttime conditions, for training and benchmarking participant solutions. The competition employed a three-stage evaluation protocol: automatic validation via the CodaBench platform in stages one and two, followed by blind assessment on a private test set for the final ranking. Ten teams surpassed the established baseline, achieving improvements of up to +6.49 dB in PSNR and +0.0101 in SSIM, thereby establishing new state-of-the-art performance for burst-based low-light image enhancement. These results demonstrate significant progress in handling real-world noise, motion, and illumination variability in the low-light setting. Comprehensive results, leaderboards, and additional information are publicly available at https://nightimaging.org.

</details>


### [253] [ADOPD: Reference-Privileged On-Policy Distillation for MLLM-Based Industrial Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09789)
*Jingtai He,Shiyuan Meng,Wenchao Meng,Qinmin Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ADOPD提出了一种参考特权在线策略蒸馏框架，将多模态大语言模型在推理时依赖参考图像比较的能力内化到模型参数中，实现零样本工业异常检测。在MMAD基准上达到77.31%的平均准确率，比Qwen3-VL-4B骨干提升6.14个百分点，甚至超越其一shot设置2.64个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在工业异常检测中通过推理时比较查询图像与参考图像来提升识别精度，但这依赖额外的检索和处理开销。作者希望探索是否可以将参考比较带来的收益直接内化到模型参数中，使查询-only的学生模型在零样本推理时也能获得类似的性能，从而消除推理时的检索依赖。

Method: 提出ADOPD（reference-privileged on-policy distillation）框架。核心思路是：训练阶段让参考感知的教师模型监督查询-only的学生模型。采用在线策略蒸馏，教师评估学生在匹配与不匹配参考下生成的rollouts。匹配参考下教师到学生的log-ratio定义了token级学习方向；两种参考视角间的似然差距用于估计参考特定支持度并校准序列级权重。

Result: 在MMAD基准上零样本推理达到77.31%的平均准确率，相比Qwen3-VL-4B骨干提升6.14个百分点，并超越其one-shot设置2.64个百分点。实验表明ADOPD确实学到了从参考比较中提炼出的细粒度异常检查策略。

Conclusion: ADOPD证明了参考比较的能力可以成功蒸馏到模型参数中，消除了推理时对参考检索的依赖，同时获得了超越one-shot设置的性能。这为工业异常检测中高效、自足的MLLM推理提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Industrial anomaly detection (IAD) requires identifying fine-grained deviations from normal visual patterns. Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can improve recognition accuracy by comparing query images with references at inference time, but these benefits rely on additional retrieval and processing. We investigate whether the benefits of reference comparison can instead be internalized in the model parameters. Access to references during training allows a reference-aware teacher to supervise a query-only student. However, the teacher may favor plausible responses based on query cues or language priors rather than valid visual information. We propose ADOPD, a reference-privileged on-policy distillation framework. The teacher evaluates student-generated rollouts under matched and mismatched references. The matched-reference teacher-to-student log-ratio defines the token-level learning direction, specifying what the student should learn. The likelihood gap between the two reference views estimates reference-specific support and calibrates the sequence-level weight. ADOPD achieves 77.31% average accuracy on the MMAD benchmark under zero-shot inference, improving the Qwen3-VL-4B backbone by 6.14 points and outperforming its one-shot setting by 2.64 points. Experiments show that ADOPD learns a fine-grained anomaly inspection strategy from reference comparison. The project will be available at https://github.com/withTai/ADOPD.

</details>


### [254] [Modern Backbones Improve Multi-task DETR for Mammography Classification and Lesion Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09801)
*Dinh Tan Nguyen,Quang-Hien Kha,Le-Hoang Nguyen,Minh-Toan Dinh,Xuan-Huy Nguyen,Dac Phu Ho,Cao Truong Tran,Sai Ho Ling,Lan T Ho-Pham,Liem Pham,Nguyen Quoc Khanh Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于多任务DETR框架的乳房X光检查AI辅助系统，同时进行图像级恶性预测和病灶定位。通过在OPTIMAM和活检确认的SGM1k队列上评估不同骨干网络，发现ConvNeXtV2和DINOv3表现最佳，而MambaVision竞争力较弱，表明骨干网络质量是多任务乳房X光检查效果的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合考试级预测和候选区域定位有望提高AI在乳房X光检查中的实用性，但需要研究共享表征框架下不同骨干网络的性能表现。

Method: 采用多任务DETR框架，通过共享表征同时支持图像级恶性预测和病灶定位，在OPTIMAM和SGM1k两个数据集上对比了多代骨干网络（包括ResNet风格、ConvNeXtV2、DINOv3和MambaVision）。

Result: OPTIMAM上ConvNeXtV2最佳（97.96% AUC，99.89%敏感性，25.08% mAP@.5，74.38% recall@.25）；SGM1k上DINOv3最佳（90.97% AUC，86.28%敏感性，82.00%特异性，27.04% mAP@.5，77.32% recall@.25）。现代骨干网络整体优于旧ResNet风格。

Conclusion: 骨干网络质量是有效多任务乳房X光检查的关键因素，ConvNeXtV2被确认为在该框架下与乳房X光检查高度匹配的CNN骨干网络。

Abstract: Joint exam-level prediction and candidate-region localization may improve the usefulness of AI support in mammography. We study this setting using a multi-task DETR framework, where shared representations support both image-level malignancy prediction and lesion localization, and evaluate its performance on OPTIMAM and a biopsy-confirmed SGM1k cohort. Across both datasets, modern backbones consistently outperformed older ResNet-style features, with ConvNeXtV2 and DINOv3 giving the strongest overall results, whereas MambaVision was less competitive. On OPTIMAM, ConvNeXtV2 achieved the best overall performance, reaching 97.96% AUC, 99.89% sensitivity, 25.08% mAP@.5, and 74.38% recall@.25. On SGM1k, DINOv3 gave the strongest overall results, with 90.97% AUC, 86.28% sensitivity, 82.00% specificity, 27.04% mAP@.5, and 77.32% recall@.25. These findings suggest that backbone quality is a critical factor in effective multi-task mammography, with ConvNeXtV2 emerging as a particularly strong and well-matched CNN backbone for mammography in this framework.

</details>


### [255] [MedPixel: A Unified Pixel-Language Model for Medical Reasoning and Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09818)
*Haoyu Yang,Meixing Shi,Zengjie Chen,Haoran Sun,Haitao Leng,Xiaoming Shi,Yuxiang Cai,Yankai Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MedPixel，一个统一的医学像素-语言模型，通过共享的语言-掩码接口弥合医学视觉语言模型与分割模型之间的鸿沟。作者构建了MedPLG-440K数据集（约44万个像素-语言任务样本）以及Pixel-Level Preference Optimization训练策略，使模型能同时支持显式定位、隐式推理、空间交互、接地解释和医学VQA等多种任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学视觉语言模型缺乏精确的像素级定位能力，而医学分割模型又依赖显式的目标类别或精确的空间提示。这种分裂源于监督信号的不匹配：分割数据集提供精确掩码但缺乏语言监督，而医学视觉语言数据很少将语言与密集空间标注配对。作者旨在解决这一gap，构建统一的医学像素-语言理解模型。

Method: 1) 提出MedPixel统一模型，围绕共享的语言-掩码接口设计；2) 构建MedPLG-440K数据集，通过临床动机驱动的合成过程生成约44万像素-语言任务样本（无需外部LLM标注）；3) 采用联合多任务监督微调配合Pixel-Level Preference Optimization训练，利用ground-truth掩码作为离线验证器，从掩码质量推导响应偏好。

Result: MedPixel在显式接地、隐式推理、空间交互、接地解释和医学VQA等广泛任务谱系上均取得强性能，在像素级预测和响应生成两方面表现优异，并展现出对外部接地基准的有效零样本迁移能力，以及对不完美空间提示的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过统一的语言-掩码接口、大规模像素-语言数据集和偏好优化策略，MedPixel成功弥合了医学视觉语言理解与像素级分割之间的鸿沟，为医学影像的可靠理解提供了一个多任务统一的解决方案，代码和模型将开源。

Abstract: Reliable medical image understanding requires models to connect clinical language and visual reasoning with pixel-level grounding. Yet medical vision-language models often lack precise localization, whereas medical segmenters typically rely on explicit target categories or precise spatial prompts. This divide is reinforced by a supervision mismatch: segmentation datasets provide precise masks but little language supervision, whereas medical vision-language data rarely pair language with dense spatial annotations. To address this gap, we present MedPixel, a unified medical pixel-language model built around a shared language--mask interface. To provide scalable supervision, we introduce MedPLG-440K, comprising approximately 440K pixel-language task samples constructed through a clinically motivated synthesis process without external LLM annotation. MedPixel is trained with joint multi-task supervised fine-tuning followed by Pixel-Level Preference Optimization, which uses ground-truth masks as offline verifiers to derive response preferences from mask quality. MedPixel supports a broad spectrum of tasks spanning explicit grounding, implicit reasoning, spatial interaction, grounded explanation, and medical VQA. Across this task spectrum, MedPixel achieves strong performance in both pixel-level prediction and response generation, together with effective zero-shot transfer to external grounding benchmarks and robustness to imperfect spatial prompts. Code and model checkpoints will be released at https://github.com/yhy-whu/Medpixel.

</details>


### [256] [From Diagnosis to Correction: Benchmarking and Improving Real-World Table Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09842)
*Jutao Xiao,Yuan Qu,Dongsheng Ma,Fan Wu,Tianyao He,Weihong Li,Jie Yang,Yu Qiao,Bin Wang,Conghui He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TableParseMap诊断基准（916张真实世界复杂表格，涵盖5个挑战场景和9种失败类型），揭示现有文档解析器在复杂表格上的真实性能远低于聚合基准得分（最强解析器仅85.03 TEDS）。作者据此提出DEC框架（Decompose-Enhance-Correct），利用通用视觉语言模型作为控制器，在不重训练的情况下改善冻结表格解析器，通过视觉一致性门控和排序器实现干预与回滚，在三个冻结解析器上平均提升1.57 TEDS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档解析器在OmniDocBench等基准上TEDS得分超过93，但社区反馈和审计显示其在真实世界复杂表格上仍持续失败。聚合基准得分掩盖了模型在复杂场景下的实质缺陷，缺乏一个能够量化这一差距的诊断性基准。

Method: 1) 构建TableParseMap诊断基准：916张真实表格，5个挑战场景，9种失败类型。2) 提出DEC框架：Decompose（沿结构感知边界分割大表格）、Enhance（暴露弱视觉证据并重新解析变换视图）、Correct（诊断并修复残余错误）。3) VC-Gate选择性触发干预，VC-Ranker在无标注HTML的情况下验证候选更新并支持回滚。4) 从4556个候选中通过离线指标和跨模型共识构建1977张表的Consensus-Hard Set。

Result: 最强评估解析器在TableParseMap上仅达85.03 TEDS。DEC在三个冻结解析器上平均提升1.57 TEDS；在TableParseMap上整体提升1.89，结构错误上提升2.62，大表格上提升5.66 TEDS。

Conclusion: 聚合基准分数掩盖了复杂真实世界表格的解析缺陷；DEC框架通过视觉一致性引导的agentic方法，无需重新训练即可有效改善冻结解析器的性能，验证了分解-增强-纠正范式在表格解析中的有效性。

Abstract: Recent document parsers achieve table TEDS scores above 93 on OmniDocBench v1.6, yet community feedback and our audit reveal persistent failures on complex real-world tables. To quantify this gap, we introduce TableParseMap, a diagnostic benchmark of 916 real-world tables organized into five challenging scenarios and nine failure types. The strongest evaluated parser achieves only 85.03 TEDS, showing that aggregate benchmark scores conceal substantial weaknesses. Our analysis attributes these failures to three complementary limitations: large tables exceed the reliable processing scale of a single pass, weak or ambiguous visual cues hinder structure perception, and the reconstructed table may remain visually inconsistent with the image. We therefore propose DEC (Decompose--Enhance--Correct), a visual-consistency-guided agentic framework that improves frozen table parsers without retraining. DEC uses a general VLM as the controller: Decompose partitions large tables along structure-aware boundaries, Enhance exposes weak visual evidence and reparses transformed views, and Correct diagnoses and repairs residual errors. A Visual Consistency Gate (VC-Gate) selectively triggers intervention, while a Visual Consistency Ranker (VC-Ranker) verifies candidate updates and supports rollback without ground-truth HTML at inference time. We further derive a 1,977-table Consensus-Hard Set from 4,556 candidates through offline metrics and cross-model consensus. Across three frozen parsers, DEC improves TEDS by 1.57 points on average; on TableParseMap, gains reach 1.89 points overall, 2.62 on structural errors, and 5.66 on large tables.

</details>


### [257] [Sci-VBench: Evaluating Knowledge- and Reasoning-Intensive Video Generation in Science Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09873)
*Diandian Zhang,Tingyu Song,Lin Fu,Zheyuan Yang,Yilun Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了Sci-VBench，一个跨四个学科领域、包含1,253个专家标注样例的科学视频生成基准，并建立了基于评分细则的评估协议，通过16个模型的评测发现视觉真实感与科学因果正确性之间存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成基准主要评估表面视觉合理性，缺乏对科学知识和推理能力的深度评测，无法反映模型在科学领域知识密集型和推理密集型视频生成中的真实表现。

Method: 构建覆盖自然科学、医疗健康、人文社科和工程四个核心学科、60个主题的1,253个专家标注样例；设计基于评分细则的评估协议，验证非专家人类评估者和MLLM-as-Judge系统与专家判断的一致性；对16个前沿专有和开源模型进行系统基准测试。

Result: 非专家评估者和MLLM评估系统能与专家判断达到较高一致性；自动感知质量分数在各模型间紧密聚集，但在提示接地性和科学因果正确性上差异显著，专有与开源模型之间存在明显差距。

Conclusion: 当前视频生成的视觉真实感进步尚未转化为对科学和因果动力学的可靠建模能力，指出了未来视频生成在科学推理方向上的关键挑战。

Abstract: We introduce Sci-VBench, a comprehensive benchmark for evaluating knowledge- and reasoning-intensive video generation across scientific domains. It contains 1,253 expert-annotated examples spanning 60 subjects across four core disciplines: Natural Science, Healthcare, Humanities & Social Sciences, and Engineering. Each example requires models to generate temporally rich videos that demand scientific reasoning and knowledge-grounded synthesis, going beyond surface-level visual plausibility. We further establish a rubric-based evaluation protocol. Our analysis shows that, under this protocol, both non-expert human evaluators and MLLM-as-Judge systems can achieve relatively high agreement with expert judgments, supporting reproducible evaluation at scale. We benchmark 16 frontier proprietary and open-source models and find that, while automatic perceptual-quality scores cluster tightly across systems, performance on Prompt Grounding and Scientific and Causal Correctness varies substantially, with a pronounced proprietary-open-source gap. These findings show that advances in visual realism have not yet translated into reliable modeling of scientific and causal dynamics.

</details>


### [258] [Financial Numerical Prediction and Allocation as Token Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09880)
*Xu Ouyang,Moontae Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FinATOM提出了一种统一的、无头（head-free）的因果语言模型框架，通过受限token生成直接进行金融预测和决策。该模型用于三步股票收益预测和五ETF动态资产配置，通过序数/排名监督、策略微调和DAPO增强的GRPO优化，在2023-2025年ETF测试中提升了Sharpe比率，验证了语言模型直接生成数值预测与决策的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统金融预测方法依赖任务特定的回归、排序或策略头，将语言模型与实际评估的数值目标分离。作者希望探索因果语言模型能否直接通过受限token生成来表示预测和决策，从而统一模型架构并更直接地优化最终目标。

Method: FinATOM采用无头统一的token生成范式：1）预测模型自回归生成波动率标准化的收益token，通过序数和排名监督训练，并辅以单epoch的token级策略阶段；2）资产配置模型生成归一化的多头权重，监督微调模仿因果均值-方差锚点，并通过DAPO增强的GRPO优化实现21天Sharpe比率，同时保持锚点一致性约束。

Result: 在2023-2025年ETF测试中：配置策略将池化总Sharpe从1.428提升至1.529，5bp交易成本下的净Sharpe从1.394提升至1.494；多模态配置输入达到最高三周期平均Sharpe 1.540，2025年优势最明显。在FinTexTS上，SFT和策略策略分别达到73.52%/2.68和73.72%/2.69的累计收益/Sharpe。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持直接语言模型token生成用于金融数值预测和决策的可行性，同时提示需要跨资产、跨市场环境和随机种子进行更广泛的测试以验证泛化性。

Abstract: Financial prediction typically relies on task-specific regression, ranking, or policy heads, separating the language model from the numerical object ultimately evaluated. We investigate whether a causal language model can instead represent forecasts and decisions directly through constrained token generation. FinATOM introduces a unified, head-free interface for three-step stock-return forecasting and dynamic five-ETF allocation. The forecasting model autoregressively emits volatility-standardized return tokens and is trained with ordinal and ranking supervision followed by a one-epoch token-level policy stage. The allocation model generates normalized long-only weights; supervised fine-tuning imitates a causal mean--variance anchor, and DAPO-augmented GRPO optimizes realized 21-day Sharpe subject to anchor consistency. In 2023--2025 ETF tests, the allocation policy improves pooled gross Sharpe from 1.428 to 1.529 and net Sharpe under a 5-bp transaction-cost model from 1.394 to 1.494. The multimodal allocation input attains the highest three-period mean Sharpe of 1.540, with its clearest advantage in 2025. On FinTexTS, the SFT and policy strategies achieve 73.52\%/2.68 and 73.72\%/2.69 cumulative-return/Sharpe, respectively. These results support the feasibility of direct language-model token generation for financial numerical prediction and decision-making, while motivating broader tests across assets, regimes, and random seeds.

</details>


### [259] [Space-Creating versus Dead Possession: An Off-Ball Possession-Quality Index for Broadcast Football](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09887)
*Seongjin Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出双层次框架来区分足球中"无效控球"与"创造空间的控球"：第一层基于事件数据的junk-possession指数，在2026世界杯103场比赛中验证其与积分和xG差显著负相关；第二层通过广播视频投影到球场坐标，用Space-Creation Index(SCI)判断控球窗口是否真正创造空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有控球率是最常被引用也最易误导的指标——60%的控球可能是无效回传而非压制对手。已有的事件型控球价值框架（expected threat、VAEP、on-ball value）只对持球动作定价，忽略了无球问题：控球是否创造了空间，还是死循环的传导？

Method: 第一层：事件侧junk-possession指数，基于expected-threat网格对每个控球序列按其峰值威胁增益定价，并重建实时比分以排除保护领先的传导，在平局或落后状态下标记低威胁序列。第二层：将广播视频投影到球场坐标，测量净控球变化（Space-Creation Index，SCI），判断标记的控球窗口是空间死亡还是创造了空间。

Result: 在2026世界杯103场比赛（206个队次）中，junk标记与积分负相关(r=-0.37)、与xG差负相关(r=-0.51)。控制进攻VAEP和field tilt后，junk标记仍与积分显著负相关(p<0.0001)，而VAEP不显著。在31个标记窗口中，74%为非空间创造型、19%弱推进、6%空间创造型。

Conclusion: 两层框架能区分"创造空间但未转化"与"无效控球"这两种情况，这是纯事件型on-ball价值无法区分的。junk指数提供了超越现有on-ball价值模型的信息。

Abstract: Ball possession is the most-cited and most-misleading number in football: 60% recycled in one's own half is not 60% spent pinning the opponent back. Existing event-based possession-value frameworks (expected threat, VAEP, on-ball value) price on-ball actions but ignore the off-ball question a sterile possession poses: did holding the ball create space, or was the circulation dead? We answer this in two layers. First, an event-side junk-possession index prices each possession sequence by its peak threat gain under an expected-threat grid and -- after reconstructing the live scoreline to exclude lead-protecting circulation -- flags low-threat sequences in tied-or-losing states. On the 2026 FIFA World Cup (103 matches, 206 team-matches) the flag correlates negatively with points (r=-0.37) and xG difference (r=-0.51, partly index-coupled). It is not a repackaging of on-ball value: with team offensive VAEP and field tilt held fixed, the junk flag stays strongly negatively associated with points (p<0.0001, also match-clustered) while VAEP is not significant -- in this same-match (descriptive) regression it adds information beyond this on-ball action-value model. Second, for a flagged window we resolve whether it was spatially dead or space-creating by projecting broadcast video to pitch coordinates and measuring a Space-Creation Index (SCI): a net pitch-control change capturing whether the possession seized space or pushed the opponent's block back. Across 31 of 35 flagged windows from nine World Cup matches (a purposive sample), 74% are spatially non-space-creating, 19% weak progression, and 6% space-creating windows the event flag alone would score as failure -- including a side with 73% of the ball that exited on penalties (two non-creating windows). The two layers separate space-creating-but-unconverted from sterile possession, a distinction event-only on-ball value cannot make.

</details>


### [260] [DistMoE: Private-data Rehearsal-free Routing in Mixture-of-Experts for Distributed Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09907)
*Mainak Singha,Niccolò Biondi,Elisa Ricci,Subhankar Roy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DistMoE，一种基于混合专家（MoE）的分布式视觉指令微调方法。它在语言解码器的每一层，用客户端特定的私有FFN专家增强公共FFN，以获取领域特定知识；并提出公共锚定的专家组合阶段（仅更新路由器和轻量级私有投影适配器，配合各向同性正则化损失），以减少客户端漂移并支持免重放的跨客户端组合。推理时通过模块化路由实现无领域标签的token级组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）虽具备强大的多模态指令跟随能力，但将其适配到不同视觉-语言领域通常假设数据集中访问且需要昂贵的联合训练。当数据分布于私有、特定领域或权限受限的客户端时，这种集中式假设具有很大限制性。

Method: 1) 在语言解码器每一层，为公共FFN增加客户端特定的私有FFN专家以获取领域知识；2) 提出公共锚定的专家组合阶段，仅更新路由器和轻量级私有投影适配器，在本地客户端数据与公共数据的混合上训练，并通过各向同性正则化损失减少客户端间的表示漂移，实现免重放的跨客户端组合；3) 推理时进行公共与私有专家间的模块化路由，实现无显式领域标签的token级领域组合。

Result: 在多个视觉-语言基准上的实验表明，DistMoE实现了灵活的专家重用、有效的领域适配和有竞争力的性能，同时保持对客户端特定知识的模块化控制。

Conclusion: DistMoE证明了一种无需集中式数据访问的分布式视觉指令微调范式，通过模块化专家路由实现跨客户端知识组合，兼顾了性能、灵活性与隐私保护。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have shown strong multimodal instruction-following ability, but adapting them to diverse visual-language domains typically assumes centralized data access and costly joint training. This is restrictive when data is distributed across private, domain-specific, or permission-limited clients. To this end, we propose DistMoE, a mixture-of-experts (MoE) approach for distributed visual instruction tuning. In each layer of the language decoder it augments the public feedforward network (FFN) with a client-specific private FFN expert, with the goal to acquire domain-specific knowledge. However, independent expert training causes the private FFNs to learn representation of different scale and magnitudes, making merging the experts difficult. To reduce client-specific drift, we introduce a public-anchored expert composition stage that updates only routers and lightweight private projection adapters on a mix of local client data and public data, via an isotropic regularization loss, therefore making it cross-client rehearsal-free composition. During inference, DistMoE performs modular routing over public and private experts, enabling token-wise domain composition without explicit domain labels. Experiments across diverse visual-language benchmarks show that DistMoE enables flexible expert reuse, effective domain adaptation, and competitive performance while preserving modular control over client-specific knowledge. Codes are available at https://github.com/mainaksingha01/DistMoE.

</details>


### [261] [Beyond Hazard Resemblance: Contrastive Event Adjudication for Training-Free Video Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09908)
*Wenti Yin,Xiang Wang,Huaxin Zhang,Hanqing Wang,Hongbo Shao,Changxin Gao,Nong Sang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CEAVAD，一种无需训练的视频异常检测方法。它将推理单位从孤立的异常概念转变为可证伪的事件假设，通过构建危险-良性事件对比，在推理时建立可解释的判别边界，实现异常的时间定位和基于证据的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有监督方法需要大量领域内标注数据；已有的无训练方法虽利用预训练模型的语义知识和推理能力，但缺乏直接的异常判定准则——更丰富的异常描述只能捕捉危险相似性，无法解决异常判定问题。

Method: CEAVAD包含三个步骤：(1) 利用公共安全知识构建危险-良性事件对比，将每个危险机制与通用正常描述和机制特定的良性对照配对；(2) 判断目标视频区间更支持危险解释还是良性竞争者，产生可修正的对比边界提议；(3) 在竞争解释之间进行裁决，判断危险假设是否在视频证据下成立，同时支持时间定位异常和基于证据的解释。

Result: 在三个广泛使用的VAD基准数据集上，CEAVAD在无需训练范式下达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 将推理单位从概念转向可证伪的事件假设，并通过竞争解释与视频证据的交互建立推理时的解释性边界，是一种有效的无需训练的视频异常检测方案。

Abstract: Video anomaly detection (VAD) aims to identify and temporally localize abnormal events in videos. Supervised methods learn anomaly decision boundaries from target-domain annotations but require substantial in-domain data. Existing training-free methods leverage the rich semantic knowledge and reasoning capabilities of pretrained models to interpret visual content, yet these capabilities do not directly define an anomaly decision criterion: richer anomaly descriptions better capture hazard resemblance without resolving abnormality. To this end, we propose Contrastive Event Adjudication for training-free Video Anomaly Detection (CEAVAD), which shifts the unit of inference from isolated anomaly concepts to falsifiable event hypotheses and establishes an inference-time explanatory boundary through the interaction between competing explanations and video evidence. Specifically, CEAVAD first uses public-safety knowledge to construct hazard-benign event contrasts, pairing each hazard mechanism with a generic normal account and a mechanism-specific benign counterpart. It then determines whether the target interval better supports a hazard explanation or its benign competitor, yielding a revisable contrastive boundary proposal for the target. Finally, CEAVAD adjudicates between the competing explanations to determine whether the hazard hypothesis survives the video evidence, supporting both temporally localized anomaly detection and evidence-grounded explanations. Experiments on three widely used VAD benchmarks demonstrate that CEAVAD achieves state-of-the-art performance under the training-free paradigm.

</details>


### [262] [Learning How the World Evolves: Extrapolative Video World Models via Latent Dynamics Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09926)
*Haodong Li,Shaoteng Liu,Tianyu Wang,Chongjian Ge,Sihui Ji,Jiahan Zhang,Xin Lin,Haolin Lu,Zhe Lin,Manmohan Chandraker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出潜在动力学推理（LDR），将视频生成的潜在状态转移显式建模为运动学积分，只让模型回归三阶及以上的残差项，在结构化潜在空间而非稠密卷积特征上运行，从而实现远超视频扩散模型的外推能力，首次实现视频世界模型在训练分布之外的动力学外推。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有顶尖视频扩散模型大多只是拟合像素，没有显式建模像素随时间的转移规律（动力学），因此虽然能生成视觉上合理的帧，但不能准确遵循物理定律。作者希望纯粹从像素中提取并学习真实动力学，实现超越训练分布的外推。

Method: LDR将潜在空间中的状态转移显式建模为运动学积分：低阶动力学通过数值积分处理，模型仅回归驱动推演的三阶及更高阶残差。该积分在结构化潜在空间（而非稠密卷积特征）上运行以获得更好的外推效果。验证采用PhyWorld白盒物理基准，涵盖匀速运动、抛物线、碰撞、反弹、逼近五种任务。

Result: 在单任务和多任务训练（256²分辨率）下，LDR的分布内外误差差距比视频扩散基线小20倍以上，同时参数减少26倍、推理快143倍。LDR能在严重分布偏移下泛化，例如仅用红球从左到右训练，能正确预测蓝方块从右到左的运动。

Conclusion: LDR是首个能在训练分布之外外推所学动力学的视频世界模型，证明了显式运动学积分与结构化潜在空间的结合能有效捕捉真实物理动力学。

Abstract: The world evolves following its dynamics, i.e., its laws of motion. However, leading video diffusion models largely fit the pixels without modeling how the pixels transit over time. Thus, they render visually plausible frames but may not accurately obey the laws. To capture the dynamics purely from pixels, we introduce Latent Dynamics Reasoning (LDR). LDR casts the latent transition as an explicit kinematic integration, where the lower-order dynamics are integrated numerically and the model regresses only the third- and higher-order residual that drives the rollout. For this integration to extrapolate better, LDR runs it on a structured latent rather than dense convolutional features. Following PhyWorld, we validate LDR on a controlled white-box physics benchmark spanning five tasks (uniform motion, parabola, collision, bouncing, looming), focusing on out-of-distribution scenarios that reveal whether a model has truly learned the underlying dynamics. LDR extrapolates the learned dynamics far better: the gap between its in- and out-of-distribution error is over 20$\times$ smaller than the video diffusion baseline's, under both single- and joint-task training at 256$^2$ resolution, while using 26$\times$ fewer parameters and running 143$\times$ faster. LDR can even generalize under severe shift: for example, trained only on red balls moving left-to-right, it correctly predicts the motion of a blue square moving right-to-left. To our knowledge, this is the first video world model that extrapolates learned dynamics beyond its training distribution. Project page: https://lat-dyn-reason.github.io/

</details>


### [263] [Multimodal Model Diffing for Feature Discovery and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09928)
*Hunar Batra,Lachin Naghashyar,Ashkan Khakzar,Philip Torr,Christian Schroeder de Witt,Constantin Venhoff,Ronald Clark*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MMDiff框架，通过训练多模态稀疏自编码器（SAEs）并将其转化为特征级接口，实现对多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）内部特征的发现、审计与控制。MMDiff支持特征隔离、任务特定特征检测和特征级控制三种用途，在三个MLLM家族上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型展现出强大的视觉理解能力，但其内部导致这些行为的特征难以识别、审计或控制。现有的SAEs虽然可以分解隐藏状态为可解释的特征方向，但既不能轻易隔离多模态训练改变了哪些特征，也不便于直接用于目标控制。

Method: MMDiff框架包含三个核心用途：(i) 特征隔离——通过对比基础LM的SAE与其多模态适配版本的差异来识别因多模态训练而改变的特征；(ii) 任务特定特征检测——通过逐token对比激发分析来隔离因果特征；(iii) 特征级控制——通过因果移除或引导发现的特征方向进行控制。作者在LLaVA-MORE、PaliGemma 2和InternVL3.5三个MLLM家族上训练多模态SAE。

Result: MMDiff发现稀疏且因果特定的特征，移除这些特征可选择性降低目标行为，空间任务平均下降12%、OCR下降17%，多模态安全攻击成功率降低24%，且不影响VQA性能。引导这些特征相比标准单层引导基线，空间和OCR准确率平均提升+3.6%和+1.8%。

Conclusion: 多模态SAEs不仅可作为可解释性工具，还可作为审计、引导和控制MLLM行为的机制，帮助实现更安全、更强大的生成。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) exhibit strong visual understanding, yet the internal features that cause these behaviors remain difficult to identify, audit, or control. While applicable to post-hoc inspection, hidden states that are decomposed into interpretable feature directions using sparse autoencoders (SAEs) neither readily isolate which features are changed by multimodal training, nor are they directly useful for targeted control. We introduce MMDiff, a multimodal model-diffing framework that trains multimodal SAEs and turns them into feature-level interfaces for discovering and controlling multimodal behavior. MMDiff supports three uses: (i) feature isolation, by diffing a base-LM SAE against its multimodal-adapted counterpart to identify features altered by multimodal training; (ii) task-specific feature detection, via per-token contrastive firing analysis that isolates causal features; and (iii) feature-level control, by causally removing or steering the discovered feature directions. We train multimodal SAEs for three MLLM families, LLaVA-MORE, PaliGemma 2, and InternVL3.5, and evaluate on visual-spatial understanding, multimodal safety, and OCR. MMDiff discovers sparse, causally specific features whose removal selectively degrades target behaviors by an average of 12% on spatial tasks and 17% on OCR, and reduces attack success rate by 24% on multimodal safety attacks, with no impact on VQA performance. Steering these features improves spatial and OCR accuracy by +3.6% and +1.8% on average over a standard single-layer steering baseline. These results show that multimodal SAEs can serve not only as interpretability tools, but as mechanisms for auditing, steering, and controlling MLLMs behavior toward safer and more capable generations.

</details>


### [264] [Perception Before Supervision: Self-Contained Visual Distillation from Counterfactual Blind Spots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09931)
*Shravan Venkatraman,Omkar Thawakar,Ritesh Thawkar,Abdelrahman Shaker,Rao Muhammad Anwer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CVPD（对比反事实视觉过程蒸馏），这是第一个完全自包含的MLLM密集、on-policy、token级视觉自蒸馏框架。它通过识别模型的视觉盲区（放大区域改变答案分布而移除区域不影响整体行为），将这些区域转化为密集对比监督用于自蒸馏，无需外部标注或更强模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的自我改进通常依赖基于奖励的方法，只能提供粗粒度的标量反馈。蒸馏虽然能通过密集token级监督提供更丰富的替代方案，但在视觉领域通常依赖外部注释、工具或更强模型构建的特权上下文。需要一个完全自包含的框架来实现视觉自蒸馏。

Method: CVPD通过对比反事实分析识别视觉盲区：放大某个区域会改变并锐化模型的答案分布，而移除该区域时全图行为基本不变。这些区域包含模型能编码但无法在全图条件下一致利用的感知信息。论文提出三门反事实准则直接从模型自身响应中识别这些区域，并将其转化为密集对比监督用于自蒸馏。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct上，CVPD在12个基准上超越6种自进化基线（包括依赖外部GPT-4o监督的方法），且无任何回退。在OCRBench上提升+3.60，MMStar细粒度感知提升+3.38，MMStar逻辑推理提升+3.08，同时保持或提升更广泛的多模态基准性能。

Conclusion: CVPD验证了完全自包含的视觉自蒸馏框架的有效性，通过识别和利用模型自身的视觉盲区，无需外部监督或更强模型即可实现显著的性能提升，为MLLM的自我改进提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Self-improvement for multimodal large language models (MLLMs) is typically driven by reward-based methods that provide only coarse scalar feedback. Distillation offers a richer alternative through dense token-level supervision, but in the visual domain it usually depends on privileged context constructed using external annotations and tools, or stronger models. We introduce \textbf{CVPD} (Contrastive Counterfactual Visual Process Distillation), which, to the best of our knowledge, is the first fully self-contained framework for dense, on-policy, token-level visual self-distillation for MLLMs. CVPD identifies visual blind spots where zooming into a region changes and sharpens the model's answer distribution, while removing the same region leaves the full-image behavior largely unchanged. Such regions reveal perceptual information that the model can encode but fails to consistently utilize under full-image conditioning. We propose a three-gate Counterfactual Criterion that identifies these regions directly from the model's own responses and converts them into dense contrastive supervision for self-distillation. On Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct, CVPD outperforms six self-evolving baselines across twelve benchmarks, including methods that rely on external GPT-4o supervision, without a single regression. It achieves gains of $+3.60$ on OCRBench, $+3.38$ on MMStar Fine-Grained Perception, and $+3.08$ on MMStar Logical Reasoning, while maintaining or improving performance on broader multimodal benchmarks.

</details>
